Absolute Regression
===================

Chapter 101: Only I Can See It
------------------------------
Cheongmyeon chose the fastest route to the Baekgye Merchant Guild. As a result, camping out became unavoidable.

They had to hunt and make dinner, start a campfire—Ian was busy with one thing and another, and in the end, unable to keep watching, I stepped in.

“If you want a campfire to burn well, you set the wood up like this.”

Surprisingly, Ian had never once started a campfire before. It was her first time coming this far from the cult.

“Now, this time I’ll teach you how to dress the meat.”

With practiced hands, I dressed the pheasant and rabbit we had hunted.

“Young Master, you’re really good at this. When did you learn all that?”

“I’ve always been good with my hands, haven’t I?”

Geugak Soma, sitting with his back against the carriage wheel, watched us in silence. Cheongmyeon stood a short distance away from him, keeping guard over Geugak Soma.

I skewered the dressed meat on branches and began roasting it over the campfire.

“Keep a close eye so it doesn’t burn. You may let an assassin get away, but you must never burn the meat!”

“Yes!”

Perhaps she found roasting meat fun, because Ian grilled it with great enthusiasm.

I sincerely hoped that, tense as she must be because of Geugak Soma and Cheongmyeon, even a moment like this would become a good memory for her. It was her first journey, after all.

Once the meat was done, I offered some to Geugak Soma.

“Please join us.”

“I’m fine.”

“Aren’t you hungry?”

“I’m all right.”

After Ian cut the meat, rubbed it with salt and seasoning, and wrapped it in paper, she brought portions to Geugak Soma and Cheongmyeon respectively.

“Please keep it and eat it later if you’d like.”

After handing the meat to the two of them, she came back.

She was terribly afraid of Geugak Soma, but she was still the sort of person who took care of what needed taking care of.

“Then we’ll eat first.”

Ian and I cut up the meat and ate.

We also drank the liquor we had bought when we stopped by a village.

“I’m all right.”

Even when I told her to have a cup, Ian adamantly refused. It seemed she truly did not even want to imagine getting drunk in front of Geugak Soma.

Watching that, Geugak Soma suddenly said,

“No matter how I look at her, she isn’t pretty. What exactly is supposed to be pretty about her?”

Ian flinched at once. Just from those words, Ian would be able to tell what kind of conversation had passed between Geugak Soma and me.

It was a rude thing to say to her, but in a way, it was also a remark made for my sake. It was as good as informing her that I had said she was pretty.

Instead of answering, I looked far off into the darkness.

“Over there, under the rock thirty zhang away. Do you see the weasel?”

Not only Geugak Soma, but Ian and Cheongmyeon as well looked where I pointed, but there was only pitch-black darkness spread out before them.

“If you find it hard to believe, go check.”

Geugak Soma sent Cheongmyeon a look. Cheongmyeon shot off toward that place.

A short while later, Cheongmyeon returned carrying a weasel.

Geugak Soma was startled.

“You truly saw that?”

“My eyes can see what you cannot, Lord Soma.”

Of course, rather than that, I had noticed it because the qi emission training I had learned from Father had reached a certain level.

“The beauty of my guard is visible only to my eyes.”

Ian quickly shook her head as if to tell me not to say such things, then lowered it deeply in embarrassment.

Geugak Soma said with a sneer,

“For a grown man, you are quite good at saying embarrassing things.”

“If something ought to be said, then it should be said. Not left unsaid because it’s embarrassing, not left unsaid because you’ve already said it once. Not left unsaid because saying it too often might become a burden. Not left unsaid because you’re busy. Not left unsaid because it might make you look pathetic.”

That last part, about looking pathetic, was aimed at Geugak Soma. After all, he had described eating beneath a mask as looking pathetic.

“Isn’t a man of character someone who says what must be said?”

As I said that, I looked at Ian. Whether because of the campfire or because she was embarrassed, her lowered face was burning red.

Be proud, Ian!

Without you, my life would not even have begun.

* * *

Two days later, the carriage arrived at the Baekgye Merchant Guild.

As he got down from the carriage, Geugak Soma asked me,

“Have you finished thinking about how you will stop me?”

“The answer was not inside the carriage, nor was it inside my head. I suppose I must find the answer in there.”

I looked up at the large signboard of the Baekgye Merchant Guild.

Cheongmyeon and Ian took the lead, while Geugak Soma and I followed behind them.

I wore a mask here as well. Going around with Geugak Soma while wearing a mask did have its conveniences. If I wore one alone, I would stand out too much, but with three of us wearing them, people would simply assume we were the sort who originally went around in masks.

When Cheongmyeon knocked on the door, a man who looked to be a steward opened it from inside.

One might expect him to be startled at the sight of us in masks, but he was calm, as though he had known we would come.

“The guild master is waiting. Please come this way.”

I had thought that perhaps hundreds of martial artists would be waiting in the training yard, but there was no one there.

I emitted my qi to examine the surroundings, but there was not a single ambusher. It was a wise choice. If they clumsily provoked Geugak Soma, it would only have produced needless victims.

The man guided us to the main hall.

There, nine people were waiting for us.

First, the head of this place offered a greeting.

“I am Seo Jeongtae, guild master of the Baekgye Merchant Guild.”

“Geugak.”

Geugak Soma treated Seo Jeongtae with the utmost coldness. He had never liked members of the unorthodox factions to begin with, so how displeasing must the Seo Jeongtae before him appear in his eyes?

“It is an honor to meet a Demon Venerable of the Divine Cult.”

Seo Jeongtae, on the other hand, was courteous. Calm and composed, he did not fear Geugak Soma, and it was clear that the reason lay with the eight experts lined up behind him. They also explained why there were no other martial artists present.

All eight experts had pulled bamboo hats low over their heads to hide their faces, but as if to prevent unnecessary conflict from the start, they were wearing clothing that revealed their identities. On their chests was drawn a wolf with its savage fangs bared wide.

The fierce wolves of the Evil Dao Alliance, said to tear dragons apart and rip tigers to shreds.

They were none other than the Thirteen Wolves of the Evil Dao, absolute experts of the Evil Dao Alliance, of whom it was said that any two together could face a single Demon Venerable. And as many as eight of those Thirteen Wolves of the Evil Dao had come.

Even after recognizing them, Geugak Soma was not frightened in the least.

Rather, he exuded a faint Lesser Evil Heart and seized control of the surroundings.

When that Lesser Evil Heart began to affect Seo Jeongtae, the other side did not stand idle.

As if by prior agreement, the eight experts all released their auras at once. Their evil energies instantly pushed back the Lesser Evil Heart and erected a wall before Seo Jeongtae. Their evil energies were rough, yet orderly. Without truly entangling, they seemed to interweave, complementing one another and revealing an impregnable momentum.

A contest of pride through force had begun.

Though he pressed forward with the Lesser Evil Heart, the wall of evil energy instead advanced. No matter how formidable Geugak Soma was, he could not bear the internal energy of eight of the Thirteen Wolves of the Evil Dao.

I did not want to see Geugak Soma being pushed back by martial artists of the Evil Dao Alliance either, so I secretly emitted demonic energy.

When my vast internal energy was added to the Lesser Evil Heart, the power struggle between the two sides settled into an even standoff.

When Geugak Soma did not yield, they pressed with even stronger internal energy, but as I drew up more of my martial power, we instead pushed inward once.

Just as they, angered, were about to raise their internal energy even further—

Because it would not be easy for us either if things went any further, Geugak Soma and I withdrew our demonic energy at the exact same time, as if we had agreed upon it.

Ending it the moment we had briefly won—Geugak Soma had achieved a perfect victory in the battle of momentum. Indeed, irritation could be felt from the eight experts.

Geugak Soma sent a sound transmission.

—I had no idea the Second Young Master’s internal energy was this profound.

—It must be because the Lesser Evil Heart is so formidable. My demonic energy merely lent a hand.

—If you lend a hand twice, those wolves will be wagging their tails. As expected, it was you who killed the Soul-Subduing Demon Venerable.

—Lord Soma, look ahead, not at me. Our enemies are not over here; there are nine of them right before us.

Geugak Soma’s gaze turned toward Seo Jeongtae, the guild master who had first introduced himself.

“You seemed to have been waiting for me, so you must know why I came. You had best choose your words carefully.”

“If the Demon Venerable desires my life, then I should gladly offer it, but at the very least, please hear me out first.”

“I am listening, so speak.”

“I will be direct. This guild intends to expand its business in Guizhou for the next eight years. And in the eighth year, we will close all our businesses and withdraw.”

Two years had already passed, and if eight more were added, in other words, the Evil Dao Alliance meant to hold him responsible for the massacre and make money by doing business in Guizhou for ten years.

“You mean to gnaw at my flesh and blood for ten years?”

“The ones the Demon Venerable killed have already had their flesh and blood rot away, leaving only white bones.”

Though displeasure filled his expression, Geugak Soma did not act rashly. It was not solely because of the eight Thirteen Wolves of the Evil Dao.

When it came to handling matters, he was not a rash man. One could tell from the fact that, after leaving the cult, instead of venting his desires, he went straight to meet the guild masters first.

At last, Geugak Soma said to Seo Jeongtae,

“You, too, belong to an organization, so fine. I will make a concession as well. From this very moment, exactly one year. If, after one year, you are still loitering around Guizhou and doing business there, I will take it to mean that you wish to go visit your friends who have become white bones.”

Even in the face of that blatant threat, Seo Jeongtae firmly shook his head.

“One year is a condition we absolutely cannot accept.”

“Then decide it by fighting, martial artist to martial artist. If you’re afraid, you may all come at me together.”

Geugak Soma knew. The Thirteen Wolves of the Evil Dao were also men with pride, and would not attack all at once.

Seo Jeongtae appeared to be lost in thought, but with the Divine Eye Art, I noticed his lips trembling faintly. He was conversing by sound transmission with one of the eight experts behind him.

At that moment, Geugak Soma also sent me a sound transmission.

—You must be disappointed. We should have had a proper clash.

—Disappointed? I get to witness Lord Soma negotiating with people of the Evil Dao Alliance firsthand? This is an experience I will not have again in my lifetime. This sight is a hundred times more thrilling than watching you exchange blows with them. If the world you see right now seems boring, then the original world must have been one that was asleep.

Geugak Soma laughed through sound transmission. It was the first time I had heard Geugak Soma’s laughter through sound transmission, and it sounded different again.

—What will you do if they do not accept?

—I suppose I will kill them all and be confined for about ten years. You take three. I will take four. Let Cheongmyeon and Ugly Heart take the remaining one.

I laughed through sound transmission as well. Of course, the part about fighting was a joke, but what made the joke funny was not that—it was the fact that he called Ian Ugly Heart. He was subtly the type to hold a grudge.

Then Seo Jeongtae spoke firmly.

“It is absolutely impossible.”

Of course, I had known they would come out like that. The unnecessary emotional sparring ended here. Now it was time for me to step forward.

“I would like to hear it directly from the one who decided that it was impossible.”

When I, who had remained silent until now, stepped forward, everyone’s gaze focused on me.

“What do you mean by that? And who are you, to step forward in such an important meeting?”

At Seo Jeongtae’s question, my gaze turned toward one of the men standing in the back.

“Stop playing leader from the shadows and come forward to settle the situation.”

The person I spoke to was the man standing in the center of the eight martial artists.

“You must know Lord Geugak Soma’s temperament well, so it would not be strange no matter what happens here. Will that be all right? Even if the successor of the Evil Dao Alliance is swept up in a fight and dies in a place like this?”

At my words, not only Geugak Soma but even Ian and Cheongmyeon were shocked.

Chaaang!

The seven men, excluding the one I had pointed out, all drew their swords at once and formed a formation. It was a sword formation meant to protect the man.

As expected of peerless experts, they revealed an overwhelming force. It was an aura incomparably more intense than when they had built the wall of evil energy earlier. In terms of our cult, it was almost as though four Demon Venerables were protecting the man.

The man standing in the middle of the sword formation slowly removed his bamboo hat. He was a young man whose face was covered in scars. If Jangho’s scars were impressive, this man’s scars were hideous.

Cut, crushed, distorted. His appearance alone was enough to send a chill through one’s heart, and his atmosphere and aura were likewise dark and heavy. Amid all that gleamed a pair of cold eyes.

“How did you recognize me?”

Perhaps his vocal cords had also been injured, for his voice sounded like a nail scraping against metal.

He was none other than Bi Sain, the official successor of the leader of the Evil Dao Alliance. Since he had been wearing the same clothes as the Thirteen Wolves of the Evil Dao, the fact that I recognized him was tantamount to saying that a secret had been leaked.

“Who are you?”

At Bi Sain’s cold question, I answered,

“I am someone whose death would be a problem, just as yours would be.”

I lifted my mask up onto my head and said,

“It is our first meeting. I am Geom Mugeuk, second son of the Heavenly Demon.”

Chapter 102: You Should Be the One to Survive.
----------------------------------------------
At the words that I was the Heavenly Demon’s son, not only Bi Sain but Seo Jeongtae and the seven members of the Thirteen Wolves of the Evil Dao were shocked.

“Are you truly Geom Mugeuk?”

Bi Sain’s face twisted even further, and his voice sank lower.

“I am.”

Bi Sain asked Extreme Evil Soma for confirmation.

“I will ask the Demon Venerable. Is it true?”

“What man in this world would dare impersonate the Cult Leader’s bloodline in front of me?”

That settled it. Unless Extreme Evil Soma had gone mad, he would never call a fake the real thing.

“Why are you here?”

The Evil Dao Alliance had learned that Extreme Evil Soma had left the cult, but they had failed to discover that I was accompanying him.

“Probably for the same reason you are here. To resolve this matter without bloodshed. Is that not why you came out as well?”

Instead of answering, Bi Sain asked another question.

“How did you know it was me?”

Because it had happened in the past.

But I could not answer that way.

“It is said that two of the Thirteen Wolves of the Evil Dao are skilled enough to take on one Demon Venerable. And yet eight of the Thirteen Wolves came to deal with a single Demon Venerable? No matter how important this matter is, don’t you think even by your own standards you brought too many?”

“That alone does not seem like a basis for finding me.”

“Of course not. That is why I observed you. Important people are usually placed in the center, so I examined the person in the middle. And there were clearly differences.”

“What differences?”

“Even though your clothes were the same, you failed to pay attention to your shoes. Your shoes are of exceptionally fine quality, difficult to obtain in the Central Plains. The sword at your waist was no ordinary sword either. Not only that, when you released your aura earlier, the way you blended with the others was different. Since you have likely had few occasions to release your aura together, it was only natural that you stood out.”

“You truly sensed that difference?”

I nodded, and Bi Sain’s expression hardened. Merely having one’s aura read did not necessarily prove an obvious gap in skill, but it was not a situation that would put him in a good mood.

Pretending not to notice, I continued speaking calmly.

“There is another reason. I noticed the merchant guild master sending a sound transmission. Sending a sound transmission at such an important moment means he was asking someone for a decision. A person who is guarded by seven of the Thirteen Wolves of the Evil Dao, wears precious shoes and carries a valuable sword, and can give orders to this merchant guild master. With that much, should I not have guessed it was you?”

Still wearing a stiff expression, Bi Sain slowly nodded.

At that moment, Extreme Evil Soma and Ian sent almost the same sound transmission at the same time.

—The Second Young Master is a genius.

—Young Master, you’re a genius.

I sent a sound transmission to Ian.

—You just sent the same transmission as Extreme Evil Soma.

—That means both good and evil have acknowledged you, Young Master.

—Which side are you on?

—Young Master!

If it had been just the two of us, I would have burst out laughing and continued joking, but I kept my expression under control and stood calmly.

If the two of them had been pleasantly surprised, Bi Sain’s mood had sunk in the opposite direction.

Bi Sain, now is not the time for you to be offended. You have just met the only chance to change your fate.

“I envy you.”

“What do you mean?”

“Have you not already been confirmed as successor? I am still struggling.”

Bi Sain’s mouth twisted. Even when he wore only the faintest bitter smile, his entire mouth distorted, creating an intensely striking expression.

I knew why he made such a face in a situation where he should have been proud.

Though he had already been chosen as successor, fierce schemes and secret struggles still continued within the Evil Dao Alliance.

He had already suffered three ambushes, and one of them had been truly dangerous. So many people coveted the position of successor that they had yet to discover who had been behind them.

That was also why he was currently traveling with as many as seven of the Thirteen Wolves of the Evil Dao.

But in my life before regression, he was eventually assassinated.

Would his fate change because he had met me?

That was something even I could not know. Whether what was meant to happen would still happen, or whether he would live a new fate.

At that moment, one of the Thirteen Wolves guarding Bi Sain removed his bamboo hat and stepped forward. He was an aged master with white hair.

“This old man is the one who leads the Thirteen Wolves of the Evil Dao.”

First Wolf, Baek Cheolgi.

He was the leader of the Thirteen Wolves of the Evil Dao and the confidant of the Evil Dao Alliance Leader.

When Bi Sain’s face was injured in childhood, Baek Cheolgi had spent his entire life feeling guilty for failing to prevent it. And when Bi Sain was assassinated by the hidden faction, Baek Cheolgi died alongside him while trying to protect him.

Was that why? I felt goodwill toward him. In him, who had died trying to protect his leader, I saw Ian overlap.

“I had heard that the Divine Cult’s Second Young Master had recently begun to distinguish himself, but indeed, your reputation is well deserved.”

“I merely guessed through observation.”

“For a martial artist, a discerning eye is a talent more important than any other.”

“Thank you for looking kindly upon me.”

After exchanging such courteous words, First Wolf brought up the main issue.

“The reason the Young Alliance Leader personally came today was not merely to resolve the matter of the merchant guild.”

“Then what was it for?”

“Someone wishes for Extreme Evil Soma to cause another bloodbath.”

When he was mentioned, Extreme Evil Soma stepped forward and asked.

“Who are they?”

First Wolf shook his head.

“We do not know either.”

At that, Extreme Evil Soma looked at me as though seeking an answer. I met his expectations.

“They must be people aiming for the position of successor to the Evil Dao Alliance.”

“What connection do they have to me?”

“Suppose Lord Soma had slaughtered the Baekgye Merchant Guild today. Another uproar would have broken out, would it not? The people behind this matter are probably trying to use the conflict and dispute between our cult and the Evil Dao Alliance to plot something.”

When I guessed it at once, not only First Wolf, who had been speaking with me, but even Bi Sain was surprised. It was because I had grasped in an instant what they already knew.

Bi Sain joined the conversation again.

“How did you know that?”

“As you said, Young Master Bi personally stepped forward today. If it were not something directly tied to your own affairs, you would have sent someone else rather than come yourself.”

“Since you are this intelligent, I suppose there is no need to explain why you must return. Please go back to your cult now. That would be best for both of us.”

“We also have a demand for Young Master Bi.”

“What demand?”

“Withdraw the Baekgye Merchant Guild from Guizhou immediately. And compensate Lord Soma for all the losses he has suffered over the past two years.”

Everyone was shocked by the unexpected words. Most likely, even Extreme Evil Soma himself was surprised by my proposal.

The first to flare up was Seo Jeongtae, the master of the Baekgye Merchant Guild.

“What are you saying right now? Withdraw immediately? And compensation on top of that? Does that make any sense?”

I looked coldly at Seo Jeongtae.

“It seems there is no hierarchy here. A subordinate dares to interrupt when the Young Alliance Leader is speaking.”

I felt a little sorry saying that. Our cult was a mess too, after all.

In place of the indignant Seo Jeongtae, Bi Sain spoke coldly.

“We even gave you a warning, so you ought to be grateful and extend the period. What nonsense is this?”

“Young Master Bi is the one speaking nonsense.”

“I am?”

“How long has it been since you were made the official successor?”

“Two years.”

“The people who orchestrated this matter must have been working to become successor since the time Young Master Bi was chosen, or even before that. Is that not so?”

“Let us say that is true.”

“Then do you have proof that they were not involved in the bloodbath Lord Soma caused two years ago?”

“What?”

Bi Sain looked bewildered. He had likely never considered that part.

“You saw how Lord Soma handled matters today. Even in a situation where he was angered, he struggled to control himself. Then why was it different two years ago?”

I looked at Extreme Evil Soma. Extreme Evil Soma spoke with an expression of disbelief.

“Surely not. Are you saying there was some sort of hidden force behind that incident as well? They used me? This Extreme Evil Soma? There was a reason those bastards back then charged at me as if they were desperate to die?”

With each word he spoke, a cold demonic energy thickened.

Soon, Extreme Evil Soma began to giggle like a madman. It was the laughter he gave when he was truly furious.

“Second Young Master, I once joked that I should sweep them all away and be locked up for ten years, but at this rate I may have to be locked up for twenty.”

Sensing that Extreme Evil Soma’s reaction was genuine, Bi Sain could not speak for a while. He too must have begun to suspect that the bloodbath two years ago might have been part of their scheme.

“Even so, I have no intention of withdrawing the Baekgye Merchant Guild.”

“Very well. Then let us add one condition.”

“What condition?”

“Lord Soma and I will help uncover the mastermind behind this matter.”

It was a succession of surprises. Bi Sain had probably never been this shocked so many times in a single day.

“You told us to return, but we will not go back. Lord Soma is not the sort of person who returns just because you tell him to, is he?”

Extreme Evil Soma backed me up with a remark.

“If someone tells me to leave, I am precisely the sort who insists on staying.”

I smiled faintly and continued.

“As you can see, he is that kind of person. So we will help. If they are dragging Lord Soma into this, we cannot simply endure it either.”

“Are you confident you can uncover the mastermind?”

“It may be impossible to completely expose people who have been operating in secret for years, but I will find you a definite clue that can lead to them. If I provide that clue, will you accept the demands I made earlier?”

The reason I made such an offer was simple.

I already knew who was behind it.

Bi Sain would accept in the end.

He must have seen something extraordinary in what I had shown today, and more than anything, profits in Guizhou or punishing Extreme Evil Soma were nothing compared to finding and eliminating the people trying to kill him.

Bi Sain and First Wolf, then Bi Sain and Seo Jeongtae, exchanged sound transmissions.

I gave them time.

At that moment, Extreme Evil Soma sent me a sound transmission.

—Will they accept? The amount they stand to lose will be enormous.

—They will definitely accept. Shall we make a wager? Loser buys a meal! What do you say?

—You are fixated on meals and my mask again. I refuse.

I laughed through the sound transmission, but he did not.

At last, Bi Sain finished the discussion he had conducted through sound transmission.

“Very well. I accept. However, once you give us the clue, you will withdraw.”

“Good. Let us do that.”

I drew the Black Demon Sword and held it out to him. Bi Sain drew his sword and lightly struck it against mine.

Clang.

That clear sound of sword against sword was the seal upon our promise.

Through the crossed blades, we looked at one another.

My gaze as I looked at his crushed and distorted face was utterly calm.

I had no particular fondness for him, but I did harbor ill will toward the next successor. Because an absolute piece of trash would take his place.

Of course, if this life flowed the same way as the past, it would mean nothing. That bastard, too, would end up dying at Hwa Mugi’s hands.

But if we all survived?

Then it would have meaning.

You might as well survive.

Because that wounded face of yours will be far more useful to the murim than the handsome face of that smarmy piece of trash.

“Then we shall meet again.”

“Let us.”

And so we left the Baekgye Merchant Guild without shedding a single drop of blood.

In the carriage on the way back, Extreme Evil Soma asked me.

“Was it dull?”

“Meeting the successor of the Evil Dao Alliance while he was surrounded by seven of the Thirteen Wolves of the Evil Dao? Discussing the mastermind trying to kill him? Which part of that was dull, exactly?”

Extreme Evil Soma laughed. It was his habitual laugh, but I felt it had changed somewhat.

“How did you know he would accept?”

“His life and the position of Evil Dao Alliance Leader are at stake. I thought he would accept any condition.”

“Are you confident you can catch them?”

“That is a question I should be asking you, Lord Soma. Are you confident? They will be attacking you, not me.”

Extreme Evil Soma laughed even louder.

“My dull world is becoming interesting.”

“It was never dull from the beginning. The murim is this thrilling, but perhaps you and I have stayed too much within our cult.”

“Then where do we find them?”

“There will be no need for us to find them. They will come to us. We simply have to do what we must.”

Extreme Evil Soma looked at me with sincere admiration. We were going to be attacked anyway, and if we could obtain a clue in the process, the troublesome issue in Guizhou would be resolved all at once. And we would even recover all past losses in the bargain.

To his admiration, I added one more stirring sentiment.

“I have no interest in whether the successor of the Evil Dao Alliance lives or dies. The only thing I am interested in is your world, Lord Soma. In seeing that world.”

This journey was about seeing Extreme Evil Soma’s world, and by the time that sightseeing came to an end, we would know much more about each other.

After quietly gazing at me, Extreme Evil Soma spoke with an intriguing light in his eyes.

“This time, I suppose I must take you to confirm why the Ugly Heart is ugly.”

And so the carriage once again began racing toward some part of his world.

Chapter 103: Everyone Has a Story.
----------------------------------
The place our carriage arrived at was Qianhua Lou (千花樓), the most famous pleasure house in Guizhou.

It was not famous because it actually had a thousand courtesans, but because it was famously expensive.

Sure enough, dozens of luxurious carriages were lined up in Qianhua Lou’s vast courtyard.

It seemed the joke that every wealthy person in Guizhou could be found here at Qianhua Lou was not entirely a joke.

We continued farther inside by carriage. Befitting a large pleasure house, martial artists of considerable skill had been stationed here and there, and those guarding the innermost courtyard were men who had reached the ranks of experts.

“Are we here to play? Or to work?”

At my question, Geugak Soma answered as if it were obvious.

“Does one not come to a pleasure house to play?”

“Is it all right to play at an important base of the Valley of Villains?”

At my words, Geugak Soma was startled.

“What do you mean?”

“The martial artists lying in ambush are no ordinary men.”

A strange light flashed through Geugak Soma’s eyes, deep in their sockets. He looked as if he wondered whether there could still be more to surprise him, yet the surprises kept piling up.

In the end, Geugak Soma admitted the truth.

“People’s tongues tend to loosen over drinks.”

Through Qianhua Lou, Geugak Soma was gathering all manner of information from the Central Plains. This place was practically an external branch of the Valley of Villains.

“Doesn’t the quality of information change according to the money spent?”

At my words, Geugak Soma merely laughed out of habit and did not elaborate.

Since magnates and people in key positions of the martial world came here often, a great deal of important information must be collected. That was how important this place was to Geugak Soma. Instead, he told me this much.

“Shall I tell you one surprising fact? Qianhua Lou earns more than the Daeho Merchant Guild you saw the other day.”

“I had no idea it would be to that extent.”

While we spoke, the carriage stopped before the innermost building.

Judging by the fact that there was not a single carriage here, this did not seem to be a place that received ordinary guests.

Geugak Soma got out, and I put on my mask and followed him down.

After we waited briefly in an extravagantly decorated room, a woman with a slender figure came inside. Her makeup was heavy, so it was difficult to tell her exact age, but she had a peculiar air about her.

“Welcome, Brother.”

The moment I heard that, I burst out laughing.

“Why are you laughing?”

“Because I’m present for a historic moment in which Geugak Soma is called Brother.”

The woman looked at me cautiously and asked,

“I don’t believe we’ve met.”

Quick-witted as she was, she must have guessed from the way I spoke casually to Geugak Soma that my status was no ordinary one. Perhaps I was the first. The first person to mention Geugak Soma so casually in front of her.

She was the first to greet me respectfully and introduce herself.

“I am Lü Qing, the one in charge of Qianhua Lou.”

Qianhua Lou’s mistress, Lü Qing (呂情).

I knew her well.

Not only Qianhua Lou, but she was also secretly operating dozens of pleasure houses throughout the Central Plains. Her dream was to become the empress of the night who ruled over every man in the Central Plains.

The very fact that she dealt with a perilous man like Geugak Soma showed just how great her ambition was.

I lifted the mask up onto my head and greeted her.

“I am Geom Mugeuk.”

The mistress of Qianhua Lou was startled. She had recognized who I was from my name alone. The fact that Geugak Soma had come with me must have confirmed it even further.

“A truly honored guest has come.”

She bowed to me on the spot. It was understandable that she recognized me, but to bow the instant she did was an extremely clever way to conduct herself.

“I might be someone who disappears forever after today.”

At my joke that if I lost the succession struggle, she would never see me again, she smiled and said,

“Conversely, you may rule this martial world for a hundred years.”

Geugak Soma said in a pleased voice,

“Now, prepare a proper drinking table for us. And today, I intend to show our Second Young Master what true beauty is, so bring them in as you see fit.”

“Yes. Please speak among yourselves for a moment.”

When she left, Geugak Soma asked with a laugh,

“Just from looking at the mistress, do you not look forward to how many beauties this place must have?”

“I truly do.”

“Truly? I did not think the Second Young Master would be fond of women.”

“Not at all. I like women too. What man dislikes women?”

“Let us see, then.”

As he said that, Geugak Soma looked at Ian, who stood by the door with Cheongmyeon.

I could feel his will. That nasty will of his, wanting to hear from my own mouth that Ian was not beautiful.

‘It won’t be easy.’

The meal was set out first.

Since this was a table for entertaining the finest guest at the finest pleasure house, it was filled with expensive and precious dishes.

I said to Geugak Soma,

“Would it be all right if our Heart came and ate a little? You two won’t eat anyway.”

“Do as you wish.”

Ian, standing by the door, made a face that said there was no need, but I was already beckoning her over.

When Ian came and sat beside me, she sent me a voice transmission.

—Young Master! How am I supposed to swallow food while sitting at the same table as Geugak Soma?

—There are too many things you like here for you to give up.

—That is true, but still.

—If you die, die as a ghost who ate before dying.

I picked up my chopsticks and said to Geugak Soma,

“Then we’ll eat first.”

Ian, too, decided that since things had come to this, she might as well stop caring, and began eating.

Ian did not regret that choice.

“It’s delicious. How can everything be this good?”

“Let’s kidnap the cook here. Has Cook Im been getting old lately? His seasoning has been off.”

“Cook Im would be hurt if he heard that.”

“My tongue is hurt too.”

“Endure it! And these days, you eat at the Pungryu Tavern all the time anyway. When are you going to abandon that owner?”

“When the food starts getting salty?”

“Young Master!”

Geugak Soma and Cheongmyeon silently watched our informal exchange.

We were also a leader and subordinate, just like them, but the sight must have been completely different from theirs. That said, I did not think our relationship was better than theirs. Some people might prefer a relationship in which trust passes back and forth without words.

I paused my chopsticks for a moment and looked at Geugak Soma.

Before I could speak, Geugak Soma spoke first.

“I am fine, so eat.”

“It is simply a pity. If we shared something delicious, we might become closer.”

“When one shares something delicious, it makes one angry. We would probably end up on worse terms.”

I laughed, and Geugak Soma did not, as if to say it was no joke.

Just then, the mistress of Qianhua Lou entered. Four courtesans followed her in.

The women were truly beautiful. Perhaps these four were something like the Four Great Beauties of Qianhua Lou.

“Please take your time looking and choose the women who please you.”

At the mistress’s words, I answered at once.

“They are all beautiful, so tell them all to sit. Is there any need to choose?”

The mistress of Qianhua Lou looked at Geugak Soma. When Geugak Soma nodded, she had all the women sit.

“A fine choice.”

“It is the way for everyone to win.”

At my answer, the mistress smiled.

The woman seated beside me poured me wine.

“To have such beautiful ladies pour my wine—I suppose I will have to drink quite a bit today.”

I accepted and drank a cup from each of the women.

“Ian, you have a cup too.”

“I am fine.”

“No. The chance to receive wine from the beauties of Qianhua Lou will not come again in your lifetime.”

“Yes, then I will accept just one cup.”

The courtesan nearest to her poured her wine. Ian drank only a little, then set the cup down again.

As Ian and I treated them with courtesy, the looks the women gave us naturally softened.

Geugak Soma, leaning against the mistress of Qianhua Lou, was watching me.

He had wanted me to play wildly with the courtesans, so this situation displeased him.

“You said you knew how to enjoy yourself.”

“I am enjoying myself. This woman here is named Zhuhua, and she is said to be good at singing. I intend to hear a song from her later.”

“What use is it to act like a gentleman at a drinking party? Shall I have them grind ink for you?”

When I acted like a bookish gentleman, Geugak Soma dragged Ian into it for no reason. He said to the mistress of Qianhua Lou,

“The Second Young Master said that guard over there is the greatest beauty. What does our mistress think?”

Then, as though there was no need to think, her answer came immediately.

“I believe that is true.”

“True?”

Geugak Soma, who had been leaning against her, sprang upright.

“Yes. I thought the same from the first time I saw her.”

The mistress of Qianhua Lou stared intently at Ian.

“She is truly a beautiful woman. If only she lost weight.”

As expected of a woman who had seen countless women all her life, she recognized at a glance the beauty hidden beneath the flesh and swelling.

Geugak Soma, on the other hand, was astonished.

Having been told that I could see what he could not, he had been trying somehow to prove that Ian was not beautiful.

But when the mistress of Qianhua Lou responded like this, he was at a momentary loss for words.

With a genuinely regretful expression, the mistress of Qianhua Lou asked Ian,

“You are truly beautiful, so why did you gain so much weight?”

Ian said to her,

“I have a large appetite and was born lazy, so there was nothing I could do.”

But the mistress of Qianhua Lou clearly sensed that was not the case.

“Everyone has at least one story they cannot tell, do they not?”

As she said that, the mistress smiled. At that smile, Ian also gave a faint smile. For some reason, those words seemed to comfort Ian, as if the woman understood her pain.

“Thank you for looking kindly on me.”

“Not at all.”

The mistress of Qianhua Lou was certainly no ordinary woman.

Well, that was why she had become close enough to call Geugak Soma Brother, and why she was nurturing the ambition to become the empress of the night.

“Now, let us drink together!”

I raised my cup high, and Ian and the courtesans toasted with me.

After drinking and enjoying ourselves for a while, I stepped out into the inner courtyard to get some air.

It seemed I truly could not become a hero among heroes. After all, the scent of rouge, of all things, was enough to give me a headache.

Just then, someone called me from behind.

“Young Master.”

I turned and saw the mistress of Qianhua Lou.

“If the courtesans are not to your liking, please say so. Our house has many courtesans.”

“It’s all right.”

“You are truly proper, Young Master.”

“I have to be proper when Lord Geugak Soma is present. If I go out to play alone somewhere, I am a complete wreck. You could not bear to watch with both eyes open.”

The mistress of Qianhua Lou smiled meaningfully.

“Is it not because of your guard?”

“Does it seem to be because of my guard?”

“You seem to cherish her greatly.”

“Is it that obvious?”

“Yes, very.”

“When you go back in, please be sure to tell my guard that.”

We looked at each other and laughed.

“To be honest, I was surprised. You recognized at a glance that my guard was originally beautiful.”

“I have been good at reading people since I was young. You might say I have good intuition. In particular, when I looked at women, I would get a feeling. That woman would be beautiful if she wore her makeup like this. She would be pretty if she dressed like that. That woman’s temper will be dreadful. That woman will like men. Strangely enough, I was often right. People even said I was possessed by a ghost.”

“What about my guard?”

“Her beauty is one thing, but more than that, she seemed like someone who would accomplish great things. Someone who would become precious.”

As expected, she had an accurate eye for people.

“What about me?”

“I only know how to read women.”

She smiled slightly. Perhaps her intuition applied to men as well.

But maybe, because she knew that carelessly flaunting that intuition could endanger her life, she had limited that talent to women and carved out a new destiny for herself.

“Then shall we go back in?”

“Let us.”

She had just taken the lead and started walking.

Shwik.

A masked man who had released his concealment appeared and stabbed at the mistress of Qianhua Lou with a dagger.

She did not even have the leeway to scream.

In that urgent instant,

the fastest move I could unleash was executed.

As I surged forward with the Nether King Steps, the Black Demon Sword was drawn from my hand.

It was the fifth form of the Flying Heaven Sword Art at the eleventh star, the Quick Sword Form, the Cangtian Form.

Shaaaaaak.

Blood sprayed across her face.

When the mistress of Qianhua Lou opened her eyes, the masked man who had tried to kill her lay collapsed on the ground, his face pierced through.

When she, drenched in the masked man’s blood, turned back in shock, my sword was floating in the air. Her eyes widened.

Before her eyes, the sword was splitting apart.

Chararararak!

Four swords.

They were not real swords, but sword qi I had created. Because they were in the shape of the Black Demon Sword, it looked as though the sword had split into four.

The seventh form of the Flying Heaven Sword Art, the Youtian Form (幽天式).

At this astonishing sight, the mistress of Qianhua Lou’s mouth fell open.

The moment the energy stretching out from my body in all directions like a spiderweb detected every hidden enemy—

Swaaaaaaaa!

The four sword qis shot out like light in different directions.

At that radiant sight, an exclamation burst from the mistress of Qianhua Lou’s lips. That exclamation was followed by the sound of flesh tearing and screams cut short.

Then came silence.

A moment later, blood erupted from the empty spaces the swords had pierced.

Puaaaaaak!

The four masked men who had been hiding revealed themselves. Blood gushed from the holes pierced by the sword qi, and they collapsed at the same time.

Boom!

The next instant, someone descended there as if plunging down from above.

It was Geugak Soma.

“What happened?”

“It was an ambush targeting the mistress.”

At my words, Geugak Soma looked at the mistress of Qianhua Lou, who stood drenched in blood.

At the fact that they had targeted not him, but the mistress of Qianhua Lou, Geugak Soma laughed coldly.

A low laugh, like a beast growling.

I could feel his anger. Because he had been laughing at the drinking table only moments ago, that anger felt even greater.

It is always like this. The most cowardly and base methods are precisely the ones with such great effect.

In a voice that had instead settled into calm, Geugak Soma said to me,

“Second Young Master, since I failed to prove that Heart is ugly, would you now like to see my true world?”

Yes. This is why bloodbaths happen.

Chapter 104: Even If It's a Bit Silly.
--------------------------------------
I did not stop Geugak Soma.

“Do as your heart leads you.”

When one is angry, one ought to be angry. If he was not about to rush off and kill someone this very instant, there was no reason for me to forcefully stop him now.

In my experience, most mistakes happen when the heart is in a hurry. It was the same now. If I stepped forward to stop him when there was no need to rush, that would mean I was the one whose heart was impatient.

At times like this, you simply leave it be. Until that surging rage dies down. Don’t judge, don’t try to persuade—just stay still.

While Ian and Cheongmyeon, who had come out a step late, examined the corpses, the mistress of Cheonhwaru stood before Geugak Soma. Bloodstains still remained on her face.

“Elder.”

Instead of calling him older brother, she had changed to addressing him as elder.

“What is it?”

“Please avenge me properly.”

Words I had not expected came from her mouth.

“Make it so I can sleep with both legs stretched out in peace from now on.”

“Very well. I’ll show the murim what happens when they touch the mistress of Cheonhwaru.”

The mistress of Cheonhwaru bowed at the waist. I knew she was no ordinary woman, but I had not known she was the kind of woman who would say such a thing in a situation like this.

I did not mean that in a bad way. I was admiring her. Because I thought that perhaps she, too, was using the finest method to calm Geugak Soma’s rage.

If she stepped forward at a time like this and said something that pricked at his pride, like, “I’m worried that you might be put in danger because of me, older brother,” Geugak Soma would only go even more mad and rampage.

What a fitting pair had met.

Just then, Cheongmyeon discovered something on one of the corpses.

“This was found in their robes.”

What Cheongmyeon held out was a Willow-Leaf Flying Dagger the size of a palm.

“It is a dagger used by the Guizhou Branch of the Sado Alliance. If you look here, you can see their unique mark.”

“You mean they’re men from the Guizhou Branch?”

“No. At their level, they could not have infiltrated this place.”

Cheongmyeon looked at me, seeking confirmation.

I nodded, agreeing with his opinion.

“That’s right. They were certainly more skilled than the people guarding Cheonhwaru.”

After hearing my answer, Cheongmyeon carefully made a conjecture.

“My guess is that they intended to assassinate the mistress, then drop this Willow-Leaf Flying Dagger at the scene before leaving.”

“They were going to pin it on the Sado Alliance? Do they take me for a fool? Did they really think such a shallow trick would work?”

As irritation at having been underestimated was added to his original rage, the air around us grew even heavier.

“Perhaps that method might have worked.”

At my words, Geugak Soma’s gaze turned cold, but I calmly said what needed to be said.

“Because right now, the mistress is not dead. If you had discovered this beside her corpse, your state of mind would have been completely different from what it is now. Would you not have first rushed to the Guizhou Branch of the Sado Alliance to find the owner of this Willow-Leaf Flying Dagger?”

Geugak Soma could not refute me.

“They likely had something prepared on that side as well. The owner of this Willow-Leaf Flying Dagger might have offended Lord Soma’s temper, or perhaps they had prepared something else.”

It takes only an instant for a person to go mad and rampage. One must never take lightly the excitement and rage brought by the death of someone they cherish.

“If they are not men of the Sado Alliance, then who are these people?”

At Geugak Soma’s question, Cheongmyeon asked me,

“What martial arts did they use?”

Cheongmyeon was a man known to be intelligent even in the Valley of Villains. Not only was he quick with sums and calculations and capable of handling account books, he also knew a great deal about martial artists, martial arts, and the state of the murim.

“The first one to reveal himself used a dagger.”

I explained while recreating the exact motion with which the first masked man had stabbed the mistress of Cheonhwaru.

“And these men were masters of stealth. Until he revealed himself to attack the mistress, I had no idea he was there.”

At that, Cheongmyeon inferred someone.

“Judging from the stance the Second Young Master showed me, the fact that they were experts in stealth, and the fact that there were five of them, these men must be the Five Blood-Hand Shadows.”

“The Five Blood-Hand Shadows? I’ve heard of them.”

At Geugak Soma’s question, Cheongmyeon answered,

“They are experts belonging to White Night Valley.”

White Night Valley was a sect belonging to the evil faction and was a place with a considerable ill reputation in the murim. It committed every sort of vile act imaginable, and even killed people for money. The Five Blood-Hand Shadows were the representative experts of White Night Valley.

“They likely accepted the commission without knowing the relationship between Cheonhwaru and us.”

Cheongmyeon had guessed not only their identity, but even the background behind why they had dared commit such an act.

He had seen it accurately. Even in my life before regression, the Five Blood-Hand Shadows had been deeply connected to Geugak Soma. In the end, they had died by Geugak Soma’s hand.

But at the very least, they had not attacked the mistress of Cheonhwaru here today.

In other words, because Geugak Soma moved with me, their fate had changed. They could not escape the result called death, but the time and place had changed.

Geugak Soma did not hesitate.

“We go to White Night Valley.”

“Yes.”

Without another word, Cheongmyeon hurried to prepare for departure.

Geugak Soma even took care of the mistress of Cheonhwaru’s safety.

“You should move with me for the time being.”

Once she had become a target, it was a wise choice for her to stay with him until this matter was over.

“I will follow your wishes, Elder.”

The mistress of Cheonhwaru obediently followed Geugak Soma’s words.

She glanced at me. I could feel the emotion contained in her gaze. I leave stopping Geugak Soma to you.

Once preparations to leave were complete, everyone climbed into the carriage. Seeing that I alone did not board until the very end, Geugak Soma asked from inside the carriage,

“Are you not getting on?”

“I was wondering how I could make Lord Soma get off the carriage.”

“Let us hear your reason for stopping me.”

“The incident has already happened, and we know whose doing it was. If we storm in now, the only thing that will happen is a fight. If Lord Soma and I join forces, we may be able to annihilate them. You might even gain a new epithet: Soma of Annihilation.”

“Are you mocking me?”

“No. I dislike White Night Valley. They commit every sort of filthy deed and even kill people for money, so they deserve to die. However, there is one better method than staining our hands with blood.”

“What is that?”

“To entrust the matter to someone else.”

“To whom are you suggesting we entrust it?”

“Lord Soma, you are so agitated right now that you have forgotten one fact. We made an agreement with Bisa-in. If we give them a clue about the mastermind, they will withdraw from Guizhou and compensate us for the losses of the past two years. Right now, we have that clue.”

Even though it was advice for his sake, Geugak Soma spoke sarcastically to me.

“To be calculating even in this situation—our Second Young Master is truly extraordinary. Extraordinary indeed.”

I did not flare up, but calmly persuaded him.

“If I were truly extraordinary, I would be able to stop Lord Soma somehow. Would you give me just a little more opportunity to do so?”

“Try.”

Looking into eyes that said, If you can, then go ahead, I began to persuade him.

“No matter how much pressure we apply, the Valley Master of White Night Valley will never reveal the person who commissioned them. No, he cannot reveal them. The moment he reveals the mastermind, no one will ever pay White Night Valley to entrust them with work again.”

Geugak Soma surely knew that much as well. When it came to villains, he was someone who knew them even better than I did.

“Most importantly, annihilating White Night Valley will become an even greater problem than annihilating the Guizhou Branch of the Sado Alliance.”

“Why is that?”

“Three years ago, the daughter of the Valley Master of White Night Valley and the son of the vice alliance leader of the Sado Alliance were married. The two families are in-laws.”

Geugak Soma, who had not known this fact, flinched in surprise and asked Cheongmyeon,

“Is that true?”

At his question, Cheongmyeon, who was on the driver’s seat, answered loudly,

“It is.”

“Why did you not tell me beforehand?”

“I only just remembered it myself.”

Geugak Soma asked me with a surprised gaze,

“How did the Second Young Master know?”

“There are many assassin organizations in the jianghu, so I became suspicious as to why they had specifically entrusted the work to White Night Valley. While thinking about that, I remembered the matter.”

Geugak Soma had experienced several times that my mind was unusually sharp, so he would not find it strange that I had remembered it.

The one important phrase that would persuade him was this:

“It is a double scheme.”

“What do you mean?”

“The mastermind who planned this incident arranged for both possibilities. It would be fine if they left behind the Willow-Leaf Flying Dagger and pinned it on the Guizhou Branch of the Sado Alliance, and it would not matter if the ambush failed as it did now and White Night Valley took the blame. The scheme to cause a clash between Lord Soma and the Sado Alliance would succeed no matter what.”

Geugak Soma’s gaze settled coldly. The fact that the laughter had disappeared from his eyes meant his rage had subsided to some degree.

“Is there any need to move according to that bastard’s intentions? Please leave this matter to me.”

The mistress of Cheonhwaru seated beside Geugak Soma, and Ian and Cheongmyeon on the driver’s seat, all listened to our conversation in silence.

Fortunately, Geugak Soma was not a foolish person who would stubbornly hold to a decision once made merely because others were watching.

“Then would I not become far too frivolous a person? I acted as though I would show the Second Young Master my world turned into a sea of blood.”

Since his words already contained the meaning that he would yield, I smiled and said,

“No one would think you frivolous, but even if they did, what is wrong with becoming a little frivolous?”

Geugak Soma laughed. His habitual laughter contained a faint snort of amusement.

“Neither I nor anyone here would want to see a sea of blood. Especially since our mistress has already been drenched in a basinful of blood.”

Geugak Soma looked at the mistress of Cheonhwaru.

As if she had been waiting, she said,

“Avenge me! Go and kill them all!”

Geugak Soma laughed again.

“You are saying something you do not mean.”

Only then did the mistress of Cheonhwaru reveal her honest feelings.

“Yes. That’s right. I’m tired today, so I want to rest now. Beside you, older brother.”

Her form of address had returned to older brother. She, too, had sensed that Geugak Soma’s rage had completely subsided.

Geugak Soma’s deliberation did not last long.

He said to Cheongmyeon,

“Go and tell Bisa-in I wish to meet.”

“Yes.”

Cheongmyeon leapt down from the carriage and disappeared in a flash.

Geugak Soma and the mistress of Cheonhwaru got out of the carriage.

I said to the mistress of Cheonhwaru,

“You must be tired, but until this matter is concluded, please stay glued to Lord Soma’s side. It is the safest place in the world.”

I had taken care of her safety and Geugak Soma’s mood as well, but Geugak Soma did not let it pass.

“The Second Young Master’s Divine Art of Flattery seems to be the greatest from ancient times to the present.”

It was an admiration that was not quite admiration, half mockery and half sincerity.

“It is the strongest technique I use often these days. But it is not ordinary flattery. It is the Divine Art of Flattery performed persistently, with effort to understand the other person, and even with one’s life on the line. And when the other party is Lord Soma, what is wrong with a little flattery?”

At my dignified response, the mistress of Cheonhwaru, who had been listening, could not hold back her laughter.

“I’m sorry for laughing. It’s just that, at this level, I think it really may deserve to be called a divine art.”

The gaze the mistress of Cheonhwaru directed at me contained goodwill beyond gratitude.

The woman I knew was someone who was clear about debts and grudges, and I was certain that saving her today would be of great help on my path going forward.

After the two of them went inside, Ian, who had been sitting on the driver’s seat like a stone statue, let out the sigh she had been holding back.

“How in the world do you endure this kind of pressure?”

The question Seo Daeryong had once asked was now being asked by her.

“If I don’t endure it? I promised to take responsibility for every accident Geugak Soma causes before coming out, didn’t I?”

“Persuading Geugak Soma when he’s furious to the tips of his hair? I would have run away without looking back.”

“You would have persuaded him better than I did.”

“You overestimate me.”

“It’s not my overestimation.”

“Then?”

“The mistress of Cheonhwaru said so. She said you seemed like someone destined to do great things.”

“Not someone destined to cause a great disaster? She probably just said it to make you feel good, Young Master.”

“If she wanted to make me feel good, she would have said I was the one destined to do such great things.”

“That’s true.”

Ian got down from the carriage.

“You feel like drinking, don’t you?”

“Yes.”

“Let’s go in and have another cup. We can ask them to sing the song we were about to hear earlier, too.”

“They’re all gone, aren’t they? Do you think those women are still waiting?”

“I suppose not.”

“…Shall I sing for you instead?”

“I decline. It was already exhausting enough.”

“I sing well. You’ve never heard me sing even once, have you?”

“One does not need to see an opponent draw their sword to know they are an expert.”

“Here you are, failing to recognize another hidden master. Don’t be shocked when you hear me later.”

As I was about to follow Ian into the building, I suddenly looked up at the night sky.

Seeing the stars scattered thickly across it, I suddenly missed my people back at the main cult. I wanted to lie alone on the beach through the Space-Time Transposition Art and bask in the warm sunlight. I wanted to hear Ian sing.

Chapter 105: It's Not Easy.
---------------------------
“You never know someone’s place while they’re there, but you feel it when they’re gone. Without Martial Artist Lee, it really does feel empty.”

Jang Ho agreed with Seo Daeryong’s regret.

“Her absence is keenly felt.”

They were on their way to the Pungryu Tavern in Maga Village.

Jang Ho was huge, while Seo Daeryong was small, so the two made such a striking contrast that passersby glanced at them at least once.

“To think I’d miss Martial Artist Lee more than the Pavilion Master.”

They used to drink as a trio, including Ian, but last time only the two of them had drunk together. It had definitely been less fun than when the three of them drank.

Ian and Seo Daeryong had led the mood with all manner of chatter, but with her gone and only the taciturn Jang Ho left, the atmosphere was bound to settle down.

Of course, there were good points as well. In place of the fun they had given up, a seriousness had entered, giving them a chance to learn about each other. After all, it was only this time that Seo Daeryong first learned Jang Ho’s childhood dream had been to become a painter.

“Martial Artist Lee must be doing well, right?”

At Seo Daeryong’s question, Jang Ho was certain.

“Of course she is. Think of who she went with. Don’t worry.”

“I’m not worried. I’m jealous. All sorts of situations I could never even imagine must be unfolding before Martial Artist Lee. I envy her. I want to be there too, being surprised together with her!”

“Do not forget that the Extreme Evil Soma is beside her as well.”

“Now that I think about it… perhaps having Lord Jang by my side is better.”

Jang Ho gave a faint chuckle. Seo Daeryong thought that manly laugh alone was enough to make this drinking session worthwhile. If nothing else, he wanted to resemble Jang Ho in the way he laughed.

Thus, sharing this and that in conversation, the two arrived at the Pungryu Tavern.

Just as they were about to go inside,

the owner, Jo Chunbae, came out and made a shushing gesture.

“You mustn’t go in right now.”

“Why?”

“The Demon Venerables are here.”

“Who?”

Seo Daeryong poked his head in slightly and looked toward the second floor of the tavern.

At the very place where they usually drank, his own master, the Blood Heaven Blade Demon, and the Demon Buddha sat facing each other, drinking.

Startled, Seo Daeryong hurriedly withdrew.

“We’ll drink somewhere else today.”

“Yes, please do.”

After sending the two away, Jo Chunbae went back into the tavern.

He looked up at the second floor. Even from a distance, he could feel the strange tension flowing between the Blood Heaven Blade Demon and the Demon Buddha.

“Why did you ask to see me?”

At the Blood Heaven Blade Demon’s question, the Demon Buddha gave no answer.

It was unlike the Demon Buddha as he usually was. Instead of his prickly temper, there was an indefinable weariness about him. The Demon Buddha emptied his cup alone.

“It isn’t as though you and I can’t share a drink, Senior.”

“Senior? After going around badmouthing me as a traitor, what ‘Senior’?”

“A traitor is a traitor.”

“Aren’t you the traitor now? Seeing as you left the First Young Master and came to find me.”

“You’ve always acted as though you knew everything since the old days. That’s why you looked down on me so damn much.”

The corners of the Blood Heaven Blade Demon’s mouth curled up. But instead of shooting back a retort, he emptied his cup. Until not so long ago, that was exactly how it had been. Whenever he saw the Demon Buddha, he found him pathetic. A tiny little man, yet full of nothing but greed.

But that arrogance had changed after meeting Geom Mugeuk.

“It isn’t easy, is it?”

At the Blood Heaven Blade Demon’s words, the Demon Buddha raised his head. He searched for the intent behind them, but the Blood Heaven Blade Demon merely filled his cup.

It did not seem like something said to mock him, so the Demon Buddha unwittingly let the words slip out.

“…It isn’t easy.”

The moment he said it, the Demon Buddha regretted it. He had given the Blood Heaven Blade Demon an opening to say something mocking. Sure enough, the Blood Heaven Blade Demon prodded at his nerves.

“You’ve lived all this time thinking you were the First Young Master’s right arm, but you’re not, are you? You’re more like a fish he’s already caught and kept in a basket. A golden carp, perhaps.”

“Even having a pleasant drink with your junior is hard for you, isn’t it? That petty, twisted heart of yours won’t even allow that much, will it?”

“You should have behaved better normally. And if you’re going to call me Senior, call me Senior to the end. If you’re going to call me ‘you,’ then call me that from the start.”

“You’re a bastard to the end!”

Bang!

The Demon Buddha slammed the table. The table shattered, and everything on it fell to the floor.

“Next time, don’t break things. On our side, we follow a demonic path that doesn’t break tables.”

“What are you talking about? Have you gone senile?”

The Demon Buddha left in irritation.

‘Damn it!’

The moment he broke the table, he had already lost. Why had he come to see the Blood Heaven Blade Demon, just to put on a good show?

He hated that the Blood Heaven Blade Demon had seen through his troubled heart. The thought that the Blood Heaven Blade Demon would look down on him even more from now on enraged him.

‘Fine, you dried-up old bastard. Look down on me all you want.’

Watching his retreating back as he left the tavern, the Blood Heaven Blade Demon also rose from his seat.

But there was not the slightest trace of the contempt or mockery the Demon Buddha had imagined in him. Rather, the sigh-laden words the Demon Buddha had uttered earlier now flowed from him as well.

“Yes, it isn’t easy.”

As the Blood Heaven Blade Demon left the tavern, he gave Jo Chunbae money. It included the price of the liquor and the cost of the broken table.

“It’s all right.”

When the Blood Heaven Blade Demon glared at him, he quickly accepted the money.

“Do not tell the Second Young Master about the broken table.”

“Pardon? Yes! Of course, don’t worry.”

And so the Blood Heaven Blade Demon also left.

Jo Chunbae looked after him for a moment, then went upstairs to clear away the broken table.

Even without the Second Young Master, people were still coming. Not long ago, the One-Flame Sword Venerable had come alone and sat for a while before leaving. He had ordered liquor, but had not drunk a single cup.

Jo Chunbae knew.

They did not come to his Pungryu Tavern because the liquor tasted good, or because the food was delicious, or because the atmosphere was pleasant. They were all missing someone.

* * *

I was sitting on the cart I had parked in the clearing, looking up at the sky, when I saw Bisa’in and seven of the Thirteen Wolves of the Heretical Dao walking over from afar.

They had chosen the meeting place this time as well.

An open clearing, exposed on all sides. I could tell they were worried about an ambush.

I understood him. When you kept receiving threats to your life, every nerve would be on edge. Yes, he had to be careful. Even after being this careful, he had died in the end.

Seeing that I had come alone, Bisa’in looked puzzled.

“You came alone?”

“I did.”

The Extreme Evil Soma had entrusted this matter entirely to me. I had left Ian behind when she said she would come along.

Bisa’in’s expression stiffened slightly.

It seemed his pride had been hurt. Though he was not yet the formal successor, he was still of the Heavenly Demon’s bloodline, and yet his opponent was going about alone like this. Meanwhile, he himself was moving around with seven of the Thirteen Wolves of the Heretical Dao, so of course he must have felt like a coward.

I wanted to tell him. That paltry pride is all useless. Feeling wounded in your pride is itself something that wounds your pride. This is all because you’re young.

“Surely you haven’t already found a clue?”

“I have.”

He must have come expecting it to some degree, but when I answered that it was true, Bisa’in could not hide his surprise.

“How did you find it so quickly?”

He asked with suspicion in his eyes.

“I didn’t find it. They came to me.”

I lifted the straw mat covering the cart. Five corpses lay loaded inside.

“As Young Master Bi warned me, there was a surprise attack.”

I did not tell him they had targeted the master of Cheonhwaru. Whether they knew of Cheonhwaru’s existence or not, I judged it was not something I needed to deliberately mention.

“These are the Blood-Hand Five Shadows of White Night Valley.”

“How did you learn that?”

“Do not underestimate our cult’s intelligence network. We know as much as we need to know about you people.”

Bisa’in’s face twisted. He had only frowned slightly, but his expression warped as though he were angry. The scars on his face were too deep and hideous.

“White Night Valley took on this commission. If you go find them, you should be able to learn who is behind it. As for what method you must use to make the Valley Master of White Night reveal the client, that is for you to think about. What do you say? Is this much not sufficient as the clue I promised?”

Bisa’in gave no answer for a while. He must have wanted to say that this much was not enough somehow, but would I allow that?

“Surely what you wanted wasn’t for me to find the one behind it and bring you their head?”

“There is no need to provoke me like that. This is enough. As of today, the Baekgye Merchant Guild will withdraw from Guizhou. I will also calculate and send compensation for the losses over the past two years.”

“As expected of the successor to the Heretical Dao Alliance! Well then, let us meet again next time.”

Just as I was about to turn away, Bisa’in’s voice transmission reached me.

—Second Young Master.

But the transmission ended there. As though I had misheard, as though it were the voice of a ghost carried on the wind, no further words came.

Bisa’in had tried to say something to me in secret, then changed his mind.

I stared at Bisa’in’s face. I did not look at his scars. I looked only at his eyes.

Because of the scars on his face, he looked far more vicious and strong than the heart he truly possessed, but I could see it. The figure of a young man struggling desperately to survive.

Speak, Bisa’in. Whatever it is, if you want to change your fate, speak.

But Bisa’in turned away without saying anything to the end. The Thirteen Wolves of the Heretical Dao pulled the cart and followed behind him.

I watched his back for a moment, then left as well.

* * *

When I returned, the Extreme Evil Soma was walking through the courtyard of Cheonhwaru. He seemed calm, but I could feel it. He was still angry, and he was trying to calm that anger.

I walked alongside the Extreme Evil Soma.

“The Baekgye Merchant Guild has agreed to withdraw from Guizhou. They also agreed to compensate for the losses over the past two years.”

“Second Young Master, you resolved in a few days what had been troubling me for two full years.”

“They merely accepted our proposal because they fear you, Lord Soma.”

“There is no need to be humble. Thank you, Second Young Master.”

After expressing his gratitude, the Extreme Evil Soma stopped walking.

“I will kill the one who gave the order to kill the master of Cheonhwaru with my own hands. Even if it means I am confined for several more years because of it.”

The Extreme Evil Soma looked at me. To the look in his eyes that asked what I thought, I conveyed my thoughts.

“Of course you should kill them. They tried to kill someone you care for. Naturally, you must kill them with your own hands. I would kill them too.”

As though it was an answer he had not expected, the Extreme Evil Soma’s eyes shone pleasantly.

“Do you not want to return to the cult? Then you could escape responsibility for whatever trouble I cause.”

“I was so absorbed in looking around your world, Lord Soma, that I forgot why I came out here in the first place.”

“Shall we go back?”

“No. Show me more of your world, Lord Soma. Show me more, kill the bastard behind this, and then let’s return.”

I was knocking endlessly on his door. I did not hope for that door to be flung wide open, as I did with the Blood Heaven Blade Demon.

I only hoped it would open halfway, so that we could face each other at that point where we were neither friends nor enemies.

I know who you are. As you died, you regretted your life. That empty life filled with evil.

Are you now the same as you were then?

“Second Young Master, there is nothing more to see in my world. This is all there is. Now I must go meet the ones who manage the gambling houses, and meet the heads of the underworld gangs attached to us. You dressed up my world grandly, as though there were something more to it, but in the end, my world is about frightening people and receiving tribute money. In the process, I sometimes cut off the arms of those who embezzle money, and I sometimes kill those I dislike.”

You have changed. In the life before my regression, you were never once this honest.

The you I met before my regression were old, and older still, someone who could no longer change.

How had I been able to become your friend?

Perhaps I, at that time, had been the same as you.

Hardened like a fossil by the single-minded obsession of obtaining the materials for the Great Art, my life and I—perhaps you felt a kinship with that version of me, and that was why you liked me so much.

“There is only one way for the Extreme Evil Soma’s world not to become dull.”

“What is that?”

“Only when war breaks out. The world will become a sea of blood, and you, Lord Soma, will be flying through the skies. I do not want to see that sort of world. I still want to see this world. How you deal with the gambling house managers, how you deal with the underworld bastards.”

In the Extreme Evil Soma’s eyes, a certain passion I had never seen before flashed and vanished.

I walked a few steps ahead, then looked back at him and said,

“Now then, shall we go? To cut off the arms of those who skim money.”

Chapter 106: When the Sleeping Blade God Awakens.
-------------------------------------------------
For the time being, I had the Mistress of Cheonhwaru hide in a safe house used by the upper echelon of the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult. It was operated under such a secretive and ingenious system that there was no need to worry about her safety.

After saying farewell to Extreme Evil Soma, she did not forget to ask after Ian.

“Please take good care of your guard.”

Seeing her emphasize it again, it seemed certain that what she had said about Ian before had not merely been empty praise.

“My guard should be the one taking care of me.”

At my joke, the Mistress of Cheonhwaru smiled. I could feel that my connection with her was only beginning now.

After parting with her, the carriage we rode in began racing off somewhere once again.

“Lord Soma, they may launch another surprise attack. Please be careful.”

“You should be careful, Second Young Master.”

“Would there be any reason for me to be attacked?”

“The person they could kill to make me furious is gone now, so perhaps they’ll try killing you next.”

“If I die, will you be furious?”

“Out of consideration for the Cult Leader, I suppose I would have to pretend to be. It would actually be a good thing. No matter what I did afterward, the Cult Leader would forgive me.”

He spoke as if joking, but he was lava that could erupt at any moment. And those bastards were trying their best to make that lava explode.

I leaned out the window of the moving carriage. Beyond the trees rushing past, I could see a sheer cliff in the distance. That was a place I had climbed before, too. Back when I had searched for materials for the Great Art, there had been nowhere in the Central Plains I had not gone.

Back then, when I imagined my life after regression, I thought I would mostly be training in martial arts at the practice grounds, yet here I was passing through this place again like this. Life after regression seemed even busier.

As I let the wind hit me and thought back on my previous life, I suddenly looked toward the driver’s seat.

“You two haven’t said a word to each other, have you? Why not talk a little?”

Then her sound transmission flew in.

—Please don’t. I’m already uncomfortable enough as it is.

—So? Are you just going to stay uncomfortable forever?

—It’s more comfortable to just go in silence.

That was when Cheongmyeon abruptly asked Ian,

“How is guard duty?”

His question surprised me, and it surprised Ian as well. Of course, it was probably not because he was curious about Ian, but because he was being courteous to me.

“As you can see, he is not exactly an easy person to serve.”

Cheongmyeon gave a slight nod, as if he understood.

This time, Ian asked,

“How about you, Cheongmyeon?”

“I like it.”

If they had been even a little closer, Ian would have said, “If you answer like that, what does that make me?”

But the awkward conversation ended there.

Good. If it had begun like this, that was enough. Now that the channel had been opened, perhaps one day I would see the two of them chatting away together.

I pulled my body back inside the carriage.

“Is it proper for a grown man to be so meddlesome?”

He knew how I would answer if he said something like that, yet he went on again about being a grown man.

“It is not because I am meddlesome. It is a matter of personality. I simply cannot stand seeing something frustrating.”

“Is your attempt to remove my mask also because of that frustration?”

“Perhaps that is part of it.”

“Second Young Master. That is all meddling.”

After speaking curtly, Extreme Evil Soma turned his head toward the window. Soma, even when I meddle this much, you are still the one who remains an impregnable fortress.

I chuckled and looked out the opposite window. The cliff I had climbed before my regression was growing distant.

After running and running like that, the carriage arrived at a marketplace crowded with people.

The merchants doing business nearby flinched in surprise when three masked martial artists stepped down from the carriage.

Cheongmyeon walked in front, and we followed behind him.

“Second Young Master, do you have a lot of money?”

“I do. Why do you ask?”

“If you have a lot, I was thinking of making you squander it all at the gambling den.”

“If you provoke the sleeping God of Gambling, the gambling den may go bankrupt.”

Extreme Evil Soma laughed as if the idea was ridiculous.

Beyond a small door at the end of the alley was an entirely new world.

The gambling den was large and extravagant. Without exaggeration, it was ten times bigger than I had expected. It was far too large, larger than the main cult’s grand training ground, and it was packed with so many people I could not even count them.

Hundreds of tables were crowded with gamblers, and slender beauties in revealing clothes moved among them carrying bottles of liquor.

Everyone was so absorbed in gambling that no one paid any attention to us in our masks. They had no interest in who came in or went out.

“It is far larger than I imagined.”

“Shall I tell you something even more surprising? This place earns more than Cheonhwaru.”

He had said Cheonhwaru earned more than the merchant guild, and now he was saying that this gambling den earned even more than Cheonhwaru.

“The Evil Dao Alliance wants to establish a gambling den in this region by any means and split the profits, while the Martial Alliance wants to get rid of this place altogether. Those orthodox bastards curse us, saying we ruin people by dragging them into gambling, but the truth is they just hate that money is flowing to us. Now then, what do you think after coming to this gambling den?”

“Seeing them obsessed with gambling in broad daylight makes me want to beat them all senseless until they come to their senses.”

“Then from now on, only grass will be served at your table.”

“It is each person’s own life, so I should not interfere, should I?”

That was the bad part about those lives. They did not ruin only themselves.

Borrowing money, stealing, deceiving. After tormenting their families and everyone around them like that, they endured until the very end before finally collapsing. That was how they ended.

When Extreme Evil Soma made an unannounced visit, the managers came running out in a panic. At the fact that Extreme Evil Soma had come in person, they trembled like aspen leaves.
