As we walked toward their office, there was one spot where an unusually large crowd had gathered.

When Extreme Evil Soma stopped, a manager quickly reported,

“That man is the son of a local notable. He occasionally wagers large sums like that. It seems his luck has turned today, because we have been losing continuously.”

Sure enough, I could tell from the expression of the gambler facing him that he had been completely overwhelmed.

“I’ll wager everything on high.”

The man bet all the money placed before him.

The amount at stake was so large that the dealer’s expression completely stiffened.

“No need to see how it turns out.”

At Extreme Evil Soma’s words, the manager hurriedly said,

“I will replace the dealer.”

“Wait.”

Stopping him, Extreme Evil Soma looked at me.

“Would you like to try?”

“Are you planning to cut off my arm if I lose money?”

“What happened to that confidence about bankrupting the gambling den?”

“Very well. But just one round.”

I went to that seat in place of the dealer. When I, wearing a mask, stepped forward, everyone flinched in surprise, but replacing the dealer was the gambling den’s right.

I rapidly shook the cup containing seven dice.

At my skillful hand movements, an interested gleam appeared in Extreme Evil Soma’s eyes.

Tak.

I set the dice cup down. When the dice dancing inside came to a stop, I lifted the cup.

The sum of the dice barely fell on the low side.

A sigh burst from the man’s mouth. It was the moment his run of luck ended. The dealer who had been losing to him let out a sigh of relief.

I immediately left the seat and walked over to Extreme Evil Soma.

“Fortunately, I was lucky.”

“Was it luck?”

“What else could it be, if not luck?”

“Or perhaps the sleeping God of Gambling awakened.”

“It was not, so put the God of Gambling back to sleep.”

But with an inscrutable smile, I only made him even more curious.

Once again, guided by the manager, we left through a passage in a corner of the gambling den.

There were several rooms along the corridor, seemingly the rooms where the martial artists guarding this gambling den waited and resided.

After passing through that area and entering through a secret door, we arrived at the manager’s office.

The manager brought over the ledgers and handed them to Cheongmyeon, then explained the gambling den to Extreme Evil Soma. He reported roughly how many people came each day, how much they earned, and in what manner they were attracting new customers.

Of course, they reported to the main cult every month, but when Extreme Evil Soma came like this, they explained and reported directly.

I silently watched the scene, and even as Extreme Evil Soma listened to the report, he observed my reaction.

To be honest, what sentiment could this process possibly stir in me?

Even so, I did not show that I was bored. I tried to understand his life. I made that effort visible. I am watching your world with this much interest.

Quite a long time passed in reports and ledger reviews. He had said we were going to cut off arms, but the gambling den was running smoothly without any problems.

Extreme Evil Soma offered the managers a few words of encouragement.

Of everything that happened today, this was the key part. As he had done with the merchant guild leaders earlier, Extreme Evil Soma personally encouraged the managers. He made them aware that the most frightening person was watching them. This seemingly insignificant act was the core that kept his businesses from wavering.

At least when it came to work, Extreme Evil Soma did it better than anyone else. Would Blood Heaven Blade Demon or Ilhwa Sword Venerable have this sort of meticulousness and diligence? I thought probably not.

Together with Extreme Evil Soma, I left that place and walked across the gambling den. Behind us came Ian and Cheongmyeon, and behind them came the managers.

Just as we were crossing that place filled with noise and heat and walking toward the entrance—

Shiiiik! Puk! Peoeok!

Starting with the sound of a blade cutting through the air, the sound of flesh being torn and a heavy thud rang out almost simultaneously.

Two men were standing on either side of us.

The dagger of the man on the right had been aimed at Extreme Evil Soma’s neck. But before the dagger could touch his throat, half of the man’s face had already been blown away by Extreme Evil Soma’s palm strike.

My sword was embedded in the heart of the man who had charged in from the left.

The two men’s combined attack had been truly exquisite.

The man on the left had been a man turning around with a cheer, claiming he had won money, and the other had been a man running over from the opposite side to congratulate him. They were assassins targeting us. They had launched their surprise attack while aiming for the instant their paths crossed ours.

The gazes of the surrounding gamblers all turned toward us at once.

But soon their gazes returned to where they had been. Though two corpses had appeared right beside them, not a single person left the gambling den. Instead, they urged the dealer to hurry up and roll the dice. I found them more frightening than the assassins.

“Are you all right, young master?”

At Ian’s worried question, I praised her.

“Your skills really have improved a great deal.”

Earlier, when the assassin had attacked me, her sword had nearly been drawn all the way. In the past she would not have been able to react, but now she had almost drawn her sword.

“If they had attacked me, I would have died.”

“That is not true. The fact that you nearly drew your sword even from behind means that if you had been in my place, you would have cut your opponent down. Your skills have risen to that level.”

Ian wore an expression that said she still could not quite believe it.

After speaking briefly with Cheongmyeon, Extreme Evil Soma walked over to me.

“I did not think they would target even you, Second Young Master.”

“They probably did not know it was me. I was wearing a mask. They likely thought I was one of your subordinates guarding you, Lord Soma.”

Even if they had known about me, they might still have given the order. The mastermind behind this incident truly wanted the main cult and the Evil Dao Alliance to go to war. In their reckless operation, would the bloodline of the Heavenly Demon be an exception?

“They have changed their operation.”

“How?”

“They intend to move the main cult by killing you, Lord Soma. It does not matter even if they fail. The enraged Lord Soma will eventually carry out a slaughter. It seems the one behind this is determined to use your departure from the cult in any way possible to achieve their goal.”

I could feel Extreme Evil Soma’s lava beginning to boil even more fiercely.

The bastards behind this were clever. If he had not come out with me, that lava would already have exploded in one way or another. What they had failed to take into account was my existence.

Then Ian carefully asked,

“If war breaks out like this, will they not be unable to remain safe either?”

I shook my head.

“That would be the case if it became a war of mutual annihilation. But realistically, that sort of war will not break out. Even if war erupts, it will end after a certain degree of sacrifice. The defeated side will pay enormous compensation and conclude a ceasefire. To them, war is also a business.”

“They are madmen.”

“They are extremely selfish bastards. They are people who do not care even if thousands are sacrificed, as long as they can seize power.”

I spoke to Extreme Evil Soma again.

“The fact that they are going this far means they do not have much time either. It means the situation within the Evil Dao Alliance is urgent.”

At my words, Geukak Soma spoke, radiating killing intent.

“They certainly don't have much time left.”

It was the will of Geukak Soma to slaughter them all. He had already declared that the masterminds would die, and this only made that certainty absolute.

What I needed to do was guide him to eliminate only those behind this incident, without touching the other martial artists of the Sado Maeng.

If that came to pass, Bisain, the successor, and the Sado Maeng itself would be grateful to us instead.

Just then, one of Geukak Soma's subordinates came, delivered an urgent missive, and left.

Cheongmyeon, having confirmed its contents, reported quickly with a tense gaze.

“It's urgent intelligence from Tongcheon Gak. They say your name has appeared in the Salsaengbu.”

The Salsaengbu was a mysterious organization shrouded in secrecy, a type of intermediary that connected assassins and clients. It made money by taking a cut of the commission, and rumors abounded that its profits were astronomical—such was the endless resentment in the world.

“The commission is one million nyang. It is said that Heuksal has accepted this contract. Those who attacked earlier were likely assassins belonging to Heuksal as well.”

“So they accepted this knowing it was me? Even if they succeed in killing me, it will eventually come to light, and they'll be annihilated by the main sect, won't they?”

I knew why Heuksal had taken this commission.

The leader of Heuksal had joined hands with the mastermind behind this incident. He intended to use this opportunity to quit the assassination trade and disband the organization. He didn't mind sacrificing every single one of his subordinates.

He had been promised not only the enormous sum of one million nyang, but also a new identity that would place him in a key position within the Sado Maeng.

“Given that the assassination has already begun, Heuksal's assassins must be spread throughout Guiju by now.”

Despite Cheongmyeon's report, Geukak Soma seemed delighted.

“What shall we do? Didn't our Second Young Master say he despises seas of blood?”

“I can swim through a sea of assassins' blood with a smile on my face. Let us go out.”

There was no pressing need to take Ian with me, but I spoke to her.

“You're coming with me, aren't you?”

“I was planning to wait here and gamble for a while.”

“That won't do, Guard.”

“Then it can't be helped.”

A glint of joy flashed at the corners of her eyes. I knew it. I knew how badly she wanted to come along. She simply hadn't been able to bring herself to speak first, afraid she might become a burden.

I had no intention of raising her in a greenhouse. Nothing made a martial artist grow more than real combat.

And the reason I could confidently take her out wasn't simply because I would be with her.

It was because the hilt of her sword had been worn smooth by grueling training. Because of the calluses on her palms.

Perhaps afraid that I might worry, Ian spoke in an impassive tone.

“Please, don't worry about me. After all, a heart is meant to beat on its own, isn't it?”

Chapter 107: For Someone So Ugly.
---------------------------------
The moment I opened the gambling house door and stepped outside, sunlight streamed down, and hidden weapons flew in with that light.

Shhk! Shhk! Shhk! Shhk!

I lightly batted away the incoming hidden weapons with my Black Demon Sword. Thanks to the Divine Eye Technique, my eyes were not dazzled even when looking directly at the sun.

The heads of the assassins on the opposite roof who had thrown the hidden weapons burst in rapid succession.

The palm of Geukak Soma, extended toward them.

His unique martial art, the Demonic Extremity Berserk Palm, had been unleashed.

The moment his outstretched palm folded, and his index finger pointed at something.

Finger wind shot out from his finger like light.

Fwing! Thud!

Another assassin who had been flying over the roof to charge at us fell, a hole drilled through his chest.

It was his other unique martial art, the Blood Calamity Finger.

He had risen to the position of Demonic Lord with that palm and that finger.

I believe the nickname "Extreme Evil" was also influenced by these two martial arts. The sight of enemies' heads and bodies bursting with dull thuds, and holes being blown through them, was far more gruesome than being pierced by a sword.

This was the first time I'd seen his martial arts properly since we left the sect together. I know better than anyone how terrifying these two arts are. When he starts rampaging like a madman, hell itself unfolds from his fingertips.

Geukak Soma's finger slowly turned toward me. A tension flowed from the tip of his finger, as if he were about to unleash the Blood Calamity Finger at any moment. Dodging the Blood Calamity Finger at such close range would not be easy.

The next moment!

Fwing! Thud!

The Blood Calamity Finger grazed past my face and pierced through the forehead of a masked figure charging from behind.

I did not move an inch.

"Not moving just now was harder than dodging."

"Is that so?"

"You have to suppress the reflex to dodge, which is actually more difficult than dodging itself. For an expert like the Second Young Lord, even more so. So it was you who killed Seophon Majon after all."

Flash.

This time, my Black Demon Sword split the air. A strand of sword qi shot from my blade, brushing past Geukak Soma's face before cleaving through the torso of the masked figure rushing at him from behind.

"I did not kill Seophon Majon. Just how long will you keep asking?"

"I won't ask anymore. Because I'm certain now."

There was no leisure to answer "no" amid another assassin's charge. My sword qi and the Blood Calamity Finger pierced through yet another assassin's body.

"There are many enemies."

"Let's go."

Geukak Soma and I walked together. At this moment, I was feeling a slight excitement within. Was Geukak Soma the only one suppressing the urge to cut loose?

The Wind God Steps, the Heavenly Demon Guardian Divine Art, and the Soaring Sword Technique that had reached the Eleventh Star. I, too, had been forcibly suppressing the desire to unleash my strength.

If my opponents were assassins trying to kill me, I could display my martial arts without any hesitation.

As we walked down the alley, four masked figures leaped over the wall and attacked all four of us simultaneously.

I cut down the man trying to stab me with movements three times faster than his and looked back. I had no interest in watching Geukak Soma or Cheongmyeon fight. I only had eyes for Ian's battle.

She was only slower than me, but she dealt with the enemy the same way I did. The mistakes that might have followed such blood-soaked training did not manifest.

She dodged the incoming sword as if brushing past her shoulder and cut him down with a single stroke.

Shhk!

The assassin collapsed on the spot, his jawbone sliced clean through.

She was neither flustered nor tense.

You're doing well, Ian.

We emerged from the alley onto the main road where a carriage was waiting.

As if completely unaware of the ambush in the alley, people were each busy with their own affairs.

A yawning shop owner. A clerk arranging goods before him. An old woman sweeping the road with a broom. A child running past her. An old man sitting by the roadside. Across from them, a middle-aged peddler hawking with a display stand. A man and woman who looked like a married couple quarreling. A woman hurling abuse from an upstairs window while looking down, and a man standing below with his head hung low.

An ordinary moment of daily life unfolded like a painting.

At the very moment when we, too, were naturally becoming part of the painting—

an inexplicable sense of unease and crisis surged in my heart, and the Heavenly Demon Guardian Divine Art activated.

"Watch out!"

In that very moment as I was warning them, the attack began.

Every single one of those we had seen was an assassin.

Shhk! Shhik!

The man and woman who had been quarreling with the peddler wielded daggers and rushed at me. Executing a perfect pincer maneuver, the man aimed for my left, the woman for my right. My eyes could even see the thick, lethal poison smeared on the blue gleaming blades.

The Black Demon Sword quickly and accurately cut down its mark.

As I cut from the man's shoulder to his chest, the woman's dagger grazed past my ear.

Judging by their swift movements, they were undoubtedly among the Black Kill's finest assassins, yet my eyes could even see her eyelashes trembling.

Feeling the Black Demon Sword flying at her from an unseen angle, her eyelashes trembled even faster. In the moment she thought to react—srrk!

As I cut through her waist and turned around, Geukak Soma was facing the peddler.

What the peddler, who had been carrying a display stand, threw at him was poison powder.

As Geukak Soma waved his hand, the poison powder returned to the peddler who had thrown it. Covered in poison, the peddler shrieked and rolled across the ground.

At the same time, the woman who had been throwing hidden weapons from upstairs died pierced by the Blood Calamity Finger, and the man sending sword qi from below was blown to pieces by the Demonic Extremity Berserk Palm.

Neither had I been merely spectating. While knocking away the hidden weapons the woman had thrown in all directions, I simultaneously neutralized the sword qi the man had launched with my own sword qi. Geukak Soma and I had, in effect, executed a perfect combined offensive.

The child among the assassins rushed at Ian.

Cut him down, Ian! That one is no child!

He only had the appearance of a child; in reality, he was a grown adult of advanced years.

Fortunately, Ian was not as soft-hearted as I had feared.

The moment she twisted her body to evade the child's attack and calmly cut him down, the old man sitting by the roadside had already charged in and was stabbing a dagger toward her face. It was a dagger coated with poison that would kill with even a scrape.

Even in that life-or-death moment, I only watched. Hoping Ian would block it herself, I didn't launch any sword qi.

Thrust!

The old man's dagger stopped before Ian's eye as she bent her waist backward. Meanwhile, Ian's sword was lodged in the old man's heart. She had finished off the old man by a hair's breadth. It was thanks to her plump yet flexible waist.

I was more startled than she was, but not helping had been the right decision in the end. This one experience would make her grow more than years of training.

While she dealt with the two, Cheongmyeon had dispatched the shop owner and clerk. In the process, had Geukak Soma not killed the old woman sweeping with a broom using the Blood Calamity Finger, Cheongmyeon would have died by her hand.

One could find the reason Cheongmyeon had devoted his entire life to Geukak Soma in that very sight.

All of this occurred simultaneously in the span of a single breath.

It was only because Geukak Soma's and my skills were so outstanding that we had passed through unscathed; their ambush, designed as a perfect combined offensive that wouldn't interfere with each other's movements, was truly terrifying.

Ian spoke with a sigh, her face flushed.

"Ah, I killed a child and an old person too."

It was said half in jest, but it still eased her mind.

"He's a child older than you, and how many do you think that one killed before becoming an old man?"

"My mind was put at ease in an instant."

After easing her mind, I entered a nearby shop.

The merchant who had originally done business there was collapsed in a corner, his acupoints sealed. While we had been in the gambling house, they had orchestrated this entire murderous scheme.

And the reason the assassins hadn't killed the merchants was to avoid the smell of blood. Otherwise, they would have all been exterminated to the last.

The very moment I was turning around after unsealing the merchant's acupoints and blood points—

Shhk.

An attack flew in from behind.

Srrk!

I turned like lightning and cut the neck of the man I had just unsealed. The man collapsed, astonished at my reaction speed. He was another assassin prepared for when the assassination outside failed.

This ambush was truly unexpected and dangerous. Had it been anyone but me, they would have fallen for it.

The real merchant was collapsed in an inner room, his blood points and demon points sealed. I unsealed him and left the shop.

I went around the surrounding houses one by one and unsealed the original owners who had been held captive there.

Because I already knew there might be assassins mixed among them, another assassin disguised as one of them lost his life to my sword without even managing to swing his blade once.

When I finished unsealing the person at the last shop and came outside, another assassin was being blown apart by Geukak Soma's Demonic Extremity Berserk Palm.

It was an assassin hiding in a box in front of a building that Geukak Soma was passing by.

Even though the outside had already been cleared and the chances of a successful assassination were slim, they were ones who would attack unconditionally when the appointed time came.

Thoroughly trained, they revealed themselves and attacked even while knowing they would die. They were not humans but beings raised as killing tools with fear eliminated.

"I will fetch the carriage."

Cheongmyeon walked to get the carriage across the street.

Geukak Soma spoke to me.

"If they put up a commission fee of a million nyang for assassins, the one behind this must be no ordinary fellow."

"Yes. By our main sect's standards, it would be someone of Demonic Lord rank. Right now they're using blades from the outside to avoid getting caught, but if the main figure is revealed, experts of a caliber we cannot handle may emerge."

Geukak Soma's eyes shone with the desire for victory. Among the Eight Demonic Lords, he was second to none in belligerence. The stronger the enemy, the more he burned.

It was right as we were having this conversation.

Swoosh! Srrk!

A streak of sword qi cut through the passenger section of the carriage.

The one who had launched the sword qi was Ian. Sensing an assassin hiding behind the carriage targeting Cheongmyeon as he sat in the driver's seat, Ian had sent out the sword qi.

Inside the diagonally cut passenger compartment, the assassin lay fallen, sliced apart.

Cheongmyeon got down from the carriage. Even if it had been an assassin who had concealed his presence perfectly, failing to detect him was an obvious mistake.

Cheongmyeon bowed his head deeply to Ian. Ian, too, bowed her head to him.

Well done, Ian. Having someone like Cheongmyeon owe you his life was a good thing.

Seeing Ian's flushed face grow even redder with excitement, I spoke playfully.

"You might be the first to break Soma-nim's carriage."

At my words, Ian started in surprise and then hurriedly bowed her head to Geukak Soma.

"I apologize!"

Geukak Soma indulged my joke.

"That carriage was very expensive."

Ian quickly replied.

"I will compensate."

"How?"

"I will buy the same carriage with my monthly salary."

"It would take several years' worth of your salary, wouldn't it?"

Then Ian, pondering for a moment, spoke in a desperate voice.

"Please lower the price!"

In that instant, Geukak Soma burst out laughing. I had made him laugh many times before, but this was the biggest laugh yet.

"Ugly as you are, you have a talent for being funny."

Though she had been called ugly outright, Ian didn't show any sign of displeasure.

"If you lower it more, I'll be even funnier for you!"

Then Cheongmyeon came over and said in a low voice.

"I will add my monthly salary to that as well."

This time, I laughed. Knowing Cheongmyeon wasn't the type to say such things, laughter came out naturally.

After relieving the tension like that, Geukak Soma gave an order to Cheongmyeon.

"Mobilize all available clandestine networks and find out where the Black Kill Lord is hiding."

"Yes!"

Then I spoke to Cheongmyeon.

"Use the Tongcheongak contact network. Given the gravity of the matter, I'm certain Strategist Sama will understand."

It was Tongcheongak that had sent information regarding the Black Kill earlier. If it was the Sama Gunsa I knew, he would have already given permission for us to use the information.

"Understood."

"And take Ian with you."

"I shall."

Separate from the life debt, it was because I wanted to let her properly experience various things through this opportunity.

"You have to pay for breaking the carriage, right?"

"Yes. I will go and return, Young Master."

Ian left that place following Cheongmyeon.

I calculated the damages incurred by the merchants today and gave them some money before returning.

"A show of generosity for appearances?"

"It is generosity, but who am I showing it to? To Soma-nim? To someone it would never work on?"

"To those merchants. Or perhaps to yourself."

I nodded as if conceding and spoke calmly.

"Perhaps. But so what if it's for show or not? What's important to those people is whether they receive compensation or not."

"You really are persistently pretending to be kind."

"Even living while pretending to be kind like this, my sword is stained with blood again today."

Geukak Soma didn't answer at all.

I slowly walked over and untied the horses tied to the broken carriage.

"The weather is nice, so shall we ride horses until the next village?"

"Very well."

I mounted a horse first, and Geukak Soma mounted one as well. Leading the remaining horses one by one, we began to move slowly.

When we didn't gallop even after going quite a distance, Geukak Soma asked me.

"Aren't we too relaxed?"

I looked up at the clear sky and said.

"What do we have to be busy about? It's always the side that failed that's in a hurry."

Chapter 108: If You Lose, You Have to Do What You Don’t Want
------------------------------------------------------------
After letting the horses graze, we were sitting on a hill and resting.

My hair fluttered in the soft breeze. But in the heart of Extreme Evil Soma, it seemed a bloody gale was still blowing.

“How did you kill the Soul-Seizing Demon Venerable?”

I could only let out a wry laugh.

“You told me I was stubborn in pretending to be good, didn’t you? Your persistence is no less than mine, Lord Soma.”

“Do you not wish to boast? At the Second Young Master’s age, one would want to brag to the whole world. I killed the Soul-Seizing Demon Venerable! So lower those arrogant eyes in front of me! Do you not wish to stand above all others?”

“I do have a bit of bravado, but not to the point of pretending I did something I didn’t. That’s right. I’m strong. Strong enough to draw such suspicion. But I did not kill him.”

I denied it to the end. Saying something that could become a weakness to a man like Extreme Evil Soma was no different from handing him that weakness. If I spoke while swept up in emotion, I would regret it for a long time.

“Very well. Then let us have a match.”

In the end, I knew those words would come.

After seeing my skills against the assassins, he must have been seized by the desire to fight me.

As he killed the assassins, he had become a beast that had caught the scent of blood. The desire he had suppressed and suppressed had raised its head.

“I refuse.”

“Why do you refuse?”

“Because fighting among ourselves when we have a powerful enemy ahead is foolish.”

“Can’t we just fight lightly enough not to get hurt?”

“Once people start fighting, they are bound to get excited.”

“I will not get excited.”

“I will. If, as you suspect, I truly have the skill to kill the Soul-Seizing Demon Venerable, could you handle me once I’m excited?”

“That makes me want to fight you even more.”

He was boiling over, lava bursting out from within. If I wanted to stop him from causing a major disaster, I had to let a little of that lava flow out today.

“Let’s do it!”

“I refuse.”

“Many assassins died today. Would they attack again before even a day has passed? Realistically, it’s impossible.”

“There’s another reason I don’t want to.”

“What is it?”

“Whether I win or lose, I lose.”

“What does that mean?”

“If I win, you’ll want to kill me. If I lose, your interest in me will plummet.”

“What good does it do for me to be interested in the Second Young Master?”

“My purpose is simple. If you become my ally, Lord Soma, it will be a decisive force in making me the successor. I wouldn’t care if all the other Demon Venerables became my enemies.”

Extreme Evil Soma snorted.

“You say that now, but next time you’ll try to draw in another Demon Venerable, won’t you?”

“Is it that obvious? How did you know?”

“It’s obvious.”

He had a deep distrust of people.

“I’m sorry, but I cannot get into the same boat as the Second Young Master.”

“Why is that?”

“You know, don’t you? The demonic path the Second Young Master dreams of is not my demonic path. So there is no need to try to get on my good side. That Flattery Divine Art of yours is nothing but a waste of mental energy.”

“I’ll still try harder.”

“Why?”

“Because there’s nothing for me to lose by doing so.”

Extreme Evil Soma laughed. It seemed to be an answer that suited his heart perfectly.

“A very good reason. If you had spouted nonsense like ‘because I like you’ or ‘because I respect you,’ it would have had the opposite effect.”

How nice it would be if Extreme Evil Soma were a man of only this much, exactly this far.

But this was nothing more than his clothing. The shoes he wore, the mask he had on. His true nature began after this.

“So let us fight. In any case, we are people who will never become close.”

The places he had shown me and called the world were not truly his world.

I knew the true world of Extreme Evil Soma.

The moment his eyes rolled back and he could see nothing, the moment that madness burst forth so that no one could stop him—that was his true world. Only in that moment did he feel alive.

Right now, he was merely holding himself back because of his curiosity about me, because of my peculiarity in pushing him in ways he had not expected.

Originally, he was not the kind of person who pestered someone to have a match. He was the kind who charged in to kill and ended up fighting. I could not let him become aware that he was enduring too much.

“Very well, let’s fight.”

“!”

Extreme Evil Soma was startled by my words. It seemed he had not expected me to really agree to a fight.

“Good! Let us fight at once!”

Extreme Evil Soma laughed loudly. He was genuinely pleased.

“Lord Soma.”

“What is it?”

“Do you see that tree at the very end over there?”

“I see it.”

“First, let us compete in qinggong.”

Before disappointment could flicker in Extreme Evil Soma’s eyes, I quickly added,

“If you beat me in qinggong, I’ll spar with you.”

“Very well.”

A look of contempt crossed Extreme Evil Soma’s eyes. Since he was confident in qinggong, he naturally believed he would win.

“If I win, please grant me one request.”

“Say anything you like.”

“Have a drink with me tonight. You don’t have to take off your mask. You can just lift it slightly like this and drink.”

I lifted an imaginary mask and mimed drinking.

“Second Young Master.”

“Yes.”

“You want me to lift it slightly and drink? You, Second Young Master, are the one who is slightly mad. How can you be this persistent?”

At those words, I laughed like a madman. This was the method I had chosen to deal with a madman. He called it persistence, but this was steadfastness.

Strike, and strike, and strike the same place again. Where I was striking was his mask.

“Very well. If I lose, I will drink.”

“Then shall we begin?”

We stood shoulder to shoulder. It had truly been a long time since I had competed in qinggong.

Extreme Evil Soma asked me,

“Have you ever had a qinggong contest before?”

“Yes. Exactly once.”

“With whom?”

With you.

“I have. An old friend.”

“The Second Young Master is young, and yet you have an old friend?”

“There is one bad bastard.”

“If I saw him, I would have liked him.”

“Perhaps you would have. Or perhaps you would have disliked him because he resembled you too much.”

At my cryptic smile, Extreme Evil Soma looked at me strangely.

“May I run first?”

“Go ahead.”

“I’m going!”

As I ran out first, Extreme Evil Soma leisurely followed behind me.

Extreme Evil Soma caught up to me in an instant. As if teasing me, he ran ahead. Running far in front, he looked back and said to me,

“You challenged me to a qinggong contest with only that level of skill?”

“It isn’t over yet.”

I was unable to close the distance until we rounded the tree we had chosen as the turning point.

Of course, Extreme Evil Soma was wary of me. He knew well that I was not someone who would propose a contest I was bound to lose.

But no matter how he looked at it, it seemed he did not think he would lose to me.

With the finish line not far away, Extreme Evil Soma indulged in one last bit of leisure.

“So the Second Young Master had a weakness. Now you seem a little more human.”

“What did I seem like before?”

“A monster. You are so young that you keep making people lower their guard, but when one looks closely, you are a monster.”

“Like this?”

Just as he turned his head, wondering what I meant—

Whoooosh.

I suddenly accelerated and shot forward. In an instant, I caught up to Extreme Evil Soma. I had unleashed the Swift Step of the Four Steps of the Wind God, which I had hidden until now.

My Swift Step, powered by my extreme internal energy, was literally swift.

Belatedly, Extreme Evil Soma drew up his internal energy and chased after me, but the distance only widened further.

“Stop right there!”

Piiing!

Extreme Evil Soma fired a Blood Calamity Finger from behind. Since it was meant as a warning, it passed beside me.

When I had nearly reached the finish line, Extreme Evil Soma unleashed the Demonic Extreme Berserk Palm. Mad with the contest, he sent it so that it grazed my head, but even then, he could not bring himself to hit me.

The tree in front of me shattered and collapsed. As if congratulating me on my victory, leaves showered down over my head.

Extreme Evil Soma arrived late, killing intent rising from his body. He strode toward me as if he might strike at any moment.

“Are you going to lose twice in one day?”

Because I spoke calmly instead, it was effective.

Though Extreme Evil Soma was a wicked man, he was not petty.

Instead, he vented his anger at the sky. The Demonic Extreme Berserk Palm, filled with irritation, was fired into the air one after another. Thunder rumbled again and again in the clear sky.

I waited until he calmed his anger.

After he had relieved the surge of rage in one burst, Extreme Evil Soma asked me,

“What exactly is that qinggong?”

“It is qinggong I’ve kept hidden as my trump card.”

“I’m asking you what it is.”

“I cannot tell you. It’s my trump card.”

He refused to accept the result.

“Let us compete again. From start to finish with that qinggong.”

The fact that he had lost was one thing, but it seemed what angered him more was that he had lost because he let his guard down.

“Next time.”

I lightly mounted my horse and set out first.

Behind me, killing intent surged once more, then soon subsided.

Clop, clop.

Extreme Evil Soma followed a little distance behind me.

I could feel heat from behind. It was the heat I had wanted. Extreme Evil Soma’s lava was overflowing toward me.

Yes, your madness. Let even a little of it out that way.

That night.

Extreme Evil Soma and I made camp. Since we had traveled so slowly, the next village was still quite far away.

Beyond the campfire, Extreme Evil Soma watched me prepare the animal I had hunted. Since midday, he had not said a single word.

At least the good thing about a campfire was that it made no silence feel awkward.

I roasted the prepared meat and handed it to him.

“Please go into the carriage and eat.”

At that, Extreme Evil Soma finally opened his mouth.

“Why are you not telling me to drink? Is it because, even if you say it, I’m trash who won’t keep a promise anyway?”

I responded calmly to his thorny words.

“I will.”

At the unexpected answer, Extreme Evil Soma could not reply for a moment.

“Of course I will. I ran so hard and won, after all.”

“Then why are you not saying it?”

“I was going to have us eat first and drink afterward. You don’t seem to like alcohol all that much. You dislike drinking with meals, don’t you?”

“If you know I don’t like it, why did you ask me to drink?”

“Because it’s a penalty. If you lose a contest, you have to do something you don’t want to do.”

Extreme Evil Soma stared silently at me, then looked down at the meat I had handed him.

Then, suddenly, he lifted his mask slightly and began to eat.

I was startled.

I had not expected him to lift his mask so easily and eat in front of me. I had expected I would have to prod his pride a little before he would eat. This unpredictable caprice of his was beyond my control.

“It’s bland.”

“The salt is here.”

As I reached out, the salt lying on the spread leather rose into the air. At the sight, Extreme Evil Soma’s eyes flashed open. He knew that lifting countless grains like salt with Void Object Seizure was far more difficult than lifting a single object.

Shaaaarrrk.

The salt flew through the air and sprinkled onto his meat.

Extreme Evil Soma’s eyes trembled as he looked at me. The desire to fight me was rising in him once again.

“It’s still bland.”

“Without even tasting it?”

To him, who wanted to see more of my secret arts, I said with a smile,

“They say eating bland food helps you live long. The Demon Physician said so, so you may believe it.”

“Second Young Master, you truly are…”

Extreme Evil Soma started to say something, then stopped. What had he been about to say? A madman? Someone who irritates people? Or perhaps…

Extreme Evil Soma began eating the meat again. I watched him for a moment, then began eating as well.

When my hunger had been somewhat filled, I brought out the liquor. Since we often camped outdoors, our baggage was never without alcohol.

This time as well, Extreme Evil Soma lifted his mask slightly and drank. He had said he disliked it, but he drank just fine.

“Not even taking off the mask properly. It looks rather pathetic, doesn’t it?”

“It doesn’t look impressive.”

“You are finally speaking honestly.”

“That’s why I like it. Being with someone who tries too hard to look impressive is uncomfortable. You can tell just by looking at my elder brother.”

Extreme Evil Soma suddenly used the meat bone he was holding to draw a long line on the ground. Then he drew a vertical line at the place I had marked before.

“Where am I now? You said you would spare me if I came this far. Have I come this far?”

Chapter 109: Is It You?
-----------------------
Geugak Soma was asking what kind of relationship we had.

Finally, within his understanding of human relations, the concept of a relationship had taken form.

I approached him, looked down at the line that had been drawn, then picked up a fallen branch nearby and drew a line.

I drew the line far short of where he had previously promised to spare my life.

“About here.”

“We haven’t gotten as far as I had thought.”

“Though we have been through much since leaving the sect, I feel my relationship with Lord Soma has been going in circles. The only reason we have advanced even this far is because we shared a meal and drinks together today. It was the first time we have eaten and drunk together.”

“We got this far just from eating and drinking together? Then what must we do to get to here?”

“To cross the gap between us, truly many experiences and memories would have to accumulate, one upon another. And to get to here... shouldn’t we have saved each other’s lives at least?”

“Isn’t the score too harsh?”

“The score is not harsh. It is Lord Soma who is too harsh in accepting the hearts of others.”

Perhaps no longer wishing to talk, Geugak Soma lay down.

“It’s all futile. We are merely strangers to one another.”

I gazed at his stubbornly turned back and drained the remaining alcohol.

Soma, it is not that people do this because they don’t know how futile it is. But it is still a path we must walk. We live by holding our temper.

I reclined halfway back and looked up at the sky.

Geugak Soma, who had been lying turned away, glanced at me before looking up as well. The countless stars covering the night sky shone equally upon the wicked man who claimed everything was futile and upon the one who stubbornly pretended to be good.

After staring at the stars in silence for some time, Geugak Soma suddenly spoke.

“You and I must fight.”

At times like this, I couldn’t help but feel sorry for him.

Even beneath these beautiful stars, the bloody wind howling in his heart showed no sign of abating.

“Let it be so. Someday.”

* * *

The next day, too, we did not hurry.

We had to travel the appointed path and wait until Ian and Cheongmyeon returned from finding Heuksalju’s hideout.

Since Tongcheongak possessed the most outstanding information network in the Central Plains, if they set their mind to finding something, they would surely find it.

On the night of the third day since Geugak Soma and I had set out together, the Cheonma Body Protection Art woke me from sleep.

I sensed someone approaching us from all directions. From their covert movements, they were assassins.

I slowly sat up and drank water from the canteen beside the campfire.

As I rose, Geugak Soma spoke while lying down.

“As expected. You rise like a ghost when enemies approach.”

“But Lord Soma awoke first, did you not?”

“I was not sleeping.”

Come to think of it, Geugak Soma had never slept well at night. Most of the villains I had known slept soundly with their legs stretched out, but Geugak Soma was a villain who could not sleep soundly.

When we awoke and began conversing, the approaching assassins halted their movements, but they did not retreat. It seemed an absolute order to kill us had been given.

Geugak Soma rose from his place.

“I will handle them.”

Perhaps excited at the thought of seeing blood, his voice was slightly feverish.

“Is it alright in the dark?”

“I rather prefer it.”

Geugak Soma walked into the darkness.

From where he had gone, the sound of Hyeolangji being fired rang out.

Fwing!

At the tail of that sharp sound came the noise of flesh being pierced.

Thwok!

With that, the true battle began.

The sounds of Mageuk Gwangpokjang and Hyeolangji being fired rang out in succession.

From all directions, hidden weapons flew toward Geugak Soma.

Dozens of hidden weapons split the night air.

That Geugak Soma still lived could be known by the sounds of Hyeolangji and Mageuk Gwangpokjang being fired.

The sounds came from the left, then from the right. Each time, screams unfailingly followed.

I sat and imagined how Geugak Soma was fighting based on sound alone.

Thanks to the Divine Eye Art, I could have seen if I wished, but I closed my eyes and imagined the movements of Geugak Soma and the assassins based solely on the sounds I heard,

The assassins were no ordinary fighters. They were experts on par with the assassins who had ambushed us in front of the gambling hall earlier.

The sound of daggers slicing through the air, hidden weapons flying about, movement through the trees with lightness arts, flesh tearing and bones being crushed.

All manner of sounds filled the darkness. With my eyes closed, I continued to imagine based on sound alone.

How would I move? How would Geugak Soma move? And if I were one of the assassins, how would I move then?

Within the darkness, my body was also moving. I took Geugak Soma’s side, and then the assassins’ side. When I took the assassins’ side and attacked him, I predicted how he would move.

Even so, the assassins continued to die.

The darkness, which had always been on the assassins’ side, betrayed them.

Tonight, the darkness was on Geugak Soma’s side.

Fwing!

When I instinctively turned my head to the side, a Hyeolangji grazed past my ear.

A scream erupted from where the Hyeolangji had flown.

He hadn’t needed to attack this way; he had definitely fired it in my direction to test me. Had I failed to dodge, it would have taken off my ear. Such a nasty temperament.

Then, one desperate assassin fell in front of me.

“Did you come here to hide from him? Or did you come to kill me too?”

Perhaps even he himself did not know.

“Die!”

The assassin charged at me.

How could the darkness be on Geugak Soma’s side alone? The true master of this darkness should be me.

Shk!

The charging assassin’s chest was cut through to the bone and he collapsed. I closed his wide-open dead eyes.

The screams echoing from all directions gradually faded, and soon silence fell.

After a moment, Geugak Soma returned to where I was.

I held out alcohol to him.

Geugak Soma drank deeply from the canteen instead of the alcohol. He no longer felt any hesitation about raising his mask to drink. The sweltering heat radiating from him was not solely due to the smell of blood on his body. He still hadn’t had his fill of blood.

“This time, they seemed like men who had come to commit suicide.”

I corrected Geugak Soma’s words.

“They were not suicides. They were murdered.”

“Murdered? Do you mean by me?”

“No. By their own leader. No matter how great the sum of one million nyang, the moment they accepted the contract to kill the Demon Venerable, they should have been prepared for annihilation. If they succeeded in killing Lord Soma, they would face our sect; if they failed, they would fall to Lord Soma. Then why did they accept the contract?”

“Perhaps they were coerced?”

“I think the opposite. It means they were guaranteed something besides money by the mastermind behind the Sadomaeng who commissioned this job. He probably promised them key positions within the Sadomaeng.”

Geugak Soma nodded in agreement with my words.

“Was this reckless attack today also because of that?”

“Yes. Heuksalju is currently cleaning up his organization. He is eliminating all the assassins who know his face so that no one can recognize him. He is throwing away assassins who earned him money and served him loyally for so long. And he will live on under a new identity.”

“He’s more of a villain than I am? This feels quite unpleasant.”

“With this choice, he has fallen from a villain to trash.”

“Second Young Master, you have seen through all of this at a glance.”

I felt a little sorry for him. It was only because I had already experienced such things that I knew; how could I possibly understand the background of such matters in a single go otherwise?

“Second Young Master, when you become the Heavenly Demon later, please spare me.”

Geugak Soma spoke as if joking, but I knew that this was rather a moment when I should be tense.

He must have thought this in that instant.

With an opponent like this, shouldn’t I kill him before he becomes the Heavenly Demon?

That’s right, Soma. If I become your enemy, of course you must kill me first. It is to prevent that situation that I am accompanying you on this journey.

I spoke in a voice filled with conviction.

“Let us work together to reach the survival line.”

When I called the line we had drawn the “survival line,” Geugak Soma burst into laughter, perhaps because it struck him as amusing.

“The smell of blood is overwhelming. Now, shall we leave?”

“Is it not a finer scent than women’s perfume?”

“Not to me.”

Just as we were packed up and about to leave, Ian and Cheongmyeon arrived. Ian was startled by the corpses strewn about, but relieved to see me unharmed.

“Tongcheongak has found Heuksalju’s hideout.”

Cheongmyeon showed us a piece of paper with the location written on it. It was not far from here.

Had we found out even slightly later than we did now, Heuksalju would have hidden himself in the embrace of the mastermind behind the Sadomaeng.

But if it was just now, at the point where the assassination had failed, there was a chance he had not yet left his hideout. Because he wouldn’t dream that we could uncover his identity and find his hideout this quickly.

“Let’s have a proper lightness skill race this time.”

Without waiting for my answer, Geugak Soma shot forward first.

“This time, I’m starting first.”

Cheongmyeon was startled by the sight of his back as he flew off first. This was Geugak Soma, who would normally yield leisurely and send me ahead first, so she was surprised to see him running off like a cheat. Though the more surprising thing was likely my composure.

I even exchanged greetings with Ian.

“How did it go?”

“Yes. I was incredibly busy. Running here and there. I learned a lot this time.”

She bowed her head to Cheongmyeon in greeting. Cheongmyeon also returned a polite bow. I could feel their relationship progressing little by little.

“But shouldn’t you be going? It seems you’re having a lightness skill race.”

“I should.”

I had to win and tease him again.

“You two pack up the gear and follow slowly.”

In a flash, my figure vanished from that spot in an instant.

* * *

Heuksalju was burning everything he owned in an empty courtyard.

All secret documents, clothes, and items related to the contract. Everything related to him, he burned.

The assassins who knew his face were all dead. No, he had killed them all.

Now he could leave light and free. He would leave behind the assassin life that everyone pointed fingers at, and now live an upright life before others. He would enjoy wealth and fame in the bright daylight. Now, a new life began.

Just as he was about to leave his hideout, he casually glanced at the wall and was startled out of his wits.

Someone wearing a white mask was sitting astride the wall. What was startling was that he had no idea how long the man had been there.

“Geugak Soma?”

His voice trembled. If it was Geugak Soma, he was a dead man.

“No. I am the victor of the duel.”

The one who pushed his mask up onto his head and smirked was none other than Geommu Geuk.

He had no idea what nonsense he was babbling, but Heuksalju felt secretly relieved that his opponent was at least a young man.

Geommu Geuk spoke to him.

“You could have just left.”

“What are you talking about?”

“If you wanted a fresh start, you didn’t have to go this far. You could have left the Heuksal organization to a trustworthy successor and lived anywhere with your skills. Even without that one million nyang, you’ve earned plenty of money until now, haven’t you?”

When I hit the mark on his situation exactly, Heuksalju flinched in surprise.

“Are assassins so diligent that they would expose a former leader’s secrets just because he’s starting fresh? Everyone’s busy just trying to stay alive.”

Geommu Geuk sensed a hidden killing intent from Heuksalju. He realized Heuksalju was about to launch a surprise attack, but pretended not to notice and continued talking.

“But you couldn’t do that. The moment you close your eyes, you probably see your subordinates betraying you. You can’t live with that anxiety. Do you know why? Because you’re that kind of person. If it were you, you would have threatened or killed your leader and taken everything.”

Heuksalju listened docilely, but his mind was entirely focused on his sleeve. The moment the secret hidden weapon, the Wind Wheel, concealed in his sleeve slid down into his hand, Heuksalju smiled a triumphant smile. At this distance, it was a certain kill.

“Done with your nonsense?”

“I’m finished, but be careful. I lost a match earlier, so I’m very angry right now.”

“What the hell are you babbling about!”

At the very moment Heuksalju tried to throw the Wind Wheel!

Fwing! Thwack!

A finger wind flying like light pierced Heuksalju’s shoulder. It was too fast to avoid.

Soon after, someone who had soared through the sky fell and landed, stomping down onto Heuksalju.

Boom!

A horrific scream erupted from Heuksalju’s mouth.

The one standing on Heuksalju’s pierced shoulder, crushing it, was Geugak Soma.

He vented his anger on an undeserving target instead of Geommu Geuk. His Hyeolangji punched holes all over the surrounding walls.

“Why the hell are you so fast! Why!”

The pain in his crushed shoulder was so great that only screams flowed from Heuksalju’s mouth. Overwhelmed by his opponent’s momentum, he couldn’t even think of counterattacking; instead, pleas came out on their own.

“Ugh! It hurts! Please! It hurts! Aaaargh!”

Only then did a cold gaze from within the white mask look down at Heuksalju.

“You? The bastard going around doing more evil deeds than me?”

Chapter 110: Since They're Your Subordinates, You Take Them
-----------------------------------------------------------
The instant Heuksalju’s eyes met those of the Extreme Evil Soma, goosebumps rose all over his body.

They said a villain recognized another villain, and the chill and terror were so great that he forgot even the pain in his shoulder, which had been agonizing enough to kill him.

“I asked if it was you.”

Heuksalju had even forgotten what the question was, and the Extreme Evil Soma did not wait for an answer.

Piiing! Puh-eok!

When the Extreme Evil Soma unleashed the Hyeorang Finger, his other shoulder was pierced through.

“Uaaaaaagh!”

Heuksalju let out a scream full of pain. No matter how much of a martial artist one was, the pain of living flesh being pierced was difficult to endure.

Watching the scene, I lightly jumped down from the top of the wall and walked over to the Extreme Evil Soma.

“Please don’t kill him. I have something to ask.”

I looked down at Heuksalju and asked.

“Who was it that offered you a position in the Sado Alliance?”

Of course, I already knew who he was. But I had to hear that name in front of the Extreme Evil Soma, so I asked.

Heuksalju clenched his molars tightly, as if he would never answer.

“Please move your foot.”

The Extreme Evil Soma was still trampling his shoulder with his foot.

When he moved his foot away, I saw the shoulder, riddled with a hole and crushed. Forget martial arts—he would not even be able to use chopsticks anymore.

I pressed the acupoints on both his shoulders to stop the bleeding and reduce the pain. I made sure he understood that I was not treating him out of goodwill.

“You can keep your mouth shut and just die here. But since you’ve inconvenienced us, you’ll die in agony. Tell me, and I’ll spare you.”

Heuksalju glared at me with eyes mixed with pain and distrust.

I did not hurry him. He had ended up like this because his opponents were me and the Extreme Evil Soma, but this man, too, had been through every hardship imaginable. He was not someone who would open his mouth easily.

I picked up the hidden weapon he had dropped while trying to launch it. It was his signature hidden weapon, the Wind Wheel.

Its owner might have been pathetic, but this Wind Wheel was an excellent hidden weapon.

I threw the Wind Wheel toward the wall.

Chwararararararak!

The Wind Wheel flew out, spinning rapidly, scraped along the wall, and then returned to my hand. A deep mark remained on the wall, as though some monstrous bird had raked it with its talons.

When I handled the Wind Wheel perfectly in one try, Heuksalju was shocked. The Wind Wheel was a dangerous hidden weapon that could sever the fingers of anyone who touched it carelessly without knowing how to use it.

Heuksalju asked in a trembling voice.

“Who are you?”

“Looks like you’re feeling well enough now. You even have the leisure to ask my name.”

“I asked who you are!”

Heuksalju shouted. He had already judged that he would not survive anyway.

“Geom Mugeuk.”

When he heard my name, Heuksalju was astonished. Since I had come with the Extreme Evil Soma, he had no choice but to believe my identity.

I showed the Wind Wheel to the Extreme Evil Soma and asked.

“May I have this?”

“I wouldn’t take that kind of hidden weapon even if it were given to me.”

“Thank you.”

I put the Wind Wheel away. I could use it against people not worth drawing my sword for, or give it as a gift if I met a subordinate who used hidden weapons. In any case, it was a weapon worth keeping.

I squatted beside Heuksalju again. I quietly looked down at him. His face was full of despair and emptiness. Until just moments ago, he had been dreaming of a brilliant future, only to fall into the abyss in an instant—how great must that sense of loss have been?

“As long as you’re alive, chances will come again. Who knows? Tomorrow, you might undergo rebirth and become an absolute master, or you might meet a peerless beauty and have your life changed. Just give me the name.”

How could he not want to live? Even if his shoulders were shattered, it was human nature to think there might still be some way to make a comeback.

“If I give the name, he’ll kill me.”

“If you give the name, we’ll kill him.”

“How can I trust that you’ll spare me?”

“Sorry to say this to you, but you have no value to me. What does it matter whether you live or die?”

If anything, that seemed to sound more persuasive. I could feel Heuksalju wavering. Then he gave a name.

“Naengyeop.”

Naengyeop, leader of the Gwisudan, the Sado Alliance’s elite unit. He was the most prominent expert in the Sado Alliance.

It was a lie. A lie born from Heuksalju’s desire for revenge, hoping we would die trying to kill Naengyeop.

“Fine. Lying is your freedom, and tearing off your arm is mine.”

I sprang to my feet, stepped on his shoulder, and began pulling his arm.

“Uaaaaaaaagh!”

I pulled with the genuine intent to rip it off.

Dudududuk.

No matter how ruthless Heuksalju’s heart was, he could not withstand the pain of his arm being torn off while still alive.

“Stop! Please stop! Uuuuugh! Seok Gwanchu! It’s Seok Gwanchu!”

This time, a proper answer came out. The one behind this affair was Seok Gwanchu, the Great Elder of the Sado Alliance.

“Is it really Seok Gwanchu?”

Heuksalju squeezed his eyes shut and said nothing.

Seok Gwanchu was a respected master of the previous generation in the Sado Alliance, and having spent so long in the council of elders, he was practically a forgotten existence. It was unimaginable that he would have entered a power struggle.

Heuksalju, too, would never have decided to fold up his organization and throw himself into the Sado Alliance if not for persuasion from someone of Seok Gwanchu’s stature.

“Keep your promise to spare me.”

“Of course…”

At that moment!

Puh-eok!

Heuksalju’s head burst apart as though a watermelon had been smashed against the ground.

The Extreme Evil Soma’s palm was aimed at him. He had unleashed the Mageuk Gwangpok Palm and killed him.

I finished the words I had been about to say to Heuksalju.

“Of course I can’t keep it. The person stepping on you is the Extreme Evil Soma.”

The Extreme Evil Soma did not say a word. The dead Heuksalju was not even in his thoughts.

When I searched Heuksalju’s clothes, a thick envelope came out. Inside were high-value notes, and when I counted them, they totaled one million nyang. Seeing that he had received the full amount in advance, it seemed the success or failure of the assassination had not mattered anyway.

I gave the money to the Extreme Evil Soma.

“This is money received for a commission to kill you, Lord Soma, so it belongs to you.”

Then the Extreme Evil Soma took only five hundred thousand nyang from it for himself and gave me the envelope containing the remaining five hundred thousand nyang.

“We nearly died together, so we should split it in half. Don’t refuse. Take it.”

“Then I will accept it gratefully.”

The Extreme Evil Soma was not a man excessively greedy for wealth. If he had been someone who possessed greed on top of everything else, he would be standing before me as an entirely different person.

The luggage Heuksalju had intended to take with him was full of treasures. Since he was in that line of work, it seemed he had not entrusted his money to a money house, but had exchanged it for precious treasures and kept them himself.

“If we sell these, they should fetch a decent sum. When Cheongmyeon comes later, let’s have him sell them.”

“You should take those, Second Young Master.”

“You should take these, Lord Soma.”

“I lost in lightness skill, so consider it the prize money.”

“It is far too large a prize. Treating me to a drink would be much cheaper.”

“One drink with me is more expensive than that.”

I smiled pleasantly. When he said he would give them to me, I did not force myself to refuse.

Long ago, Gowol had told me that money did not come with name tags. Its value was determined by how it was used in the moment.

The Extreme Evil Soma asked me.

“Do you believe for certain that Seok Gwanchu is behind this?”

“Isn’t that why you killed him without asking further?”

“No. I killed him because I simply couldn’t stand the sight of him.”

Thinking that he really had done just that, I let out a small laugh.

“From what I saw, he didn’t seem to be lying.”

“Seok Gwanchu, is it. That is truly a name I haven’t heard in a long time. He must be around ninety. I cannot understand why that old, decrepit man would covet the position of Sado Alliance Leader.”

“Seok Gwanchu has one grandson whom he dotes on. From what I’ve heard, that grandson is extremely ambitious.”

“Because of blood ties?”

“The affection an old master nearing death has for his bloodline is likely not something we can understand. In any case, if Seok Gwanchu is behind this, it will not be easy to deal with him.”

As if he agreed, the Extreme Evil Soma nodded. But the fact that the opponent was formidable only seemed to make him happier. I could read great joy in those small eye holes.

Half an hour later, Ian and Cheongmyeon arrived there.

“You must be tired from coming all this way, but let’s handle this first and then rest.”

I took Ian out to the marketplace.

There, after visiting several places, I sold all of Heuksalju’s treasures.

Even though I sold them below market price, they came to a staggering four hundred thousand nyang.

“If he had just lived well for the rest of his life with this money, that would have been enough. Why was he so greedy? Even this much money is more than enough to spend without using it all before dying.”

“Human greed has no end, does it?”

“Ian. Later, if it looks like I’m getting greedy, make sure you stop me. Say, ‘If you were going to be like this, why did you criticize Heuksalju back then?’ and laugh at me as hard as you can.”

“I might be too busy being greedy myself.”

“You?”

“I feel it while training in martial arts. That I’m a person with a truly great deal of greed. Every day, I’m tormented by the desire to quickly achieve mastery and fight powerful opponents.”

“That isn’t because of your greed. It’s because of the Bicheon Sword Art.”

“Because of the sword art?”

“Supreme martial arts like the Bicheon Sword Art tempt and stimulate the person practicing them. You know what happens if you’re devoured by that, don’t you?”

Ian nodded. She was so clever that she must have understood what I meant.

I took her to the Daeryuk Money House.

Of the total nine hundred thousand nyang we had gained this time, I deposited six hundred thousand under my name and gave Ian three hundred thousand nyang to deposit. Three hundred nyang was already a large sum, but when I gave her three hundred thousand nyang, she was horrified and startled.

“Young Master, I absolutely cannot accept this money!”

“I’m not giving it to you so you can buy liquor.”

“Then?”

“When you run the Gwiyeongdae later, there will be times when you have to spend money for your subordinates. Use it then.”

“It’s too much money.”

“You need to know how to handle large sums as well.”

“This is the first time in my life I’ve ever seen this much money.”

“Now, if someone wants to bribe you, they’ll need at least three hundred thousand nyang, won’t they?”

“No matter how little money I have, I won’t fall for that amount.”

“Then how much would it take?”

Ian seemed to ponder for a moment, then held up three fingers.

“Three million nyang?”

Ian shook her head.

“Oh, thirty million nyang?”

Still, Ian shook her head.

“…Thirty thousand nyang? Surely not three thousand nyang?”

Only then did she smile and say,

“Three hundred million nyang. Bring me that. If you want to buy me with money, all you need is three hundred million nyang.”

“Even if I gathered all the money in the world, it wouldn’t be that much.”

“That is what I am worth.”

We looked at each other and laughed softly. To be honest, I felt good. Because someone who could not be bought with money was staking everything for me.

“If there’s money left over, buy yourself something tasty too.”

“I’ll leave a lot left over.”

“You can leave all of it.”

On the way back, I casually asked Ian.

“What do you think of Cheongmyeon?”

“I think he’s a good person.”

“No, I mean, how would he be as your subordinate?”

“What?”

Ian was startled.

“My subordinate?”

“Cheongmyeon, leader of the First Squad of the Gwiyeongdae. He’s reliable, so I think he would suit it. The organization’s name means ghostly shadow, and he’s Cheongmyeon, who wears a blue mask. It fits, doesn’t it?”

I said it because I knew what Cheongmyeon’s loyalty was like.

“This isn’t a matter of whether it suits him or not. Cheongmyeon is someone aiming to become the next Demon Venerable. In reality, he’s also the strongest candidate.”

But he would never become the Demon Venerable in his life. Because the Extreme Evil Soma would survive for far too long.

Rather than spending his life taking care of that ill-tempered Extreme Evil Soma, wouldn’t a life that began as a squad leader of the Gwiyeongdae be much better?

He would become a squad leader, later become a unit commander, and who knew what else after that?

His current life was fixed toward a stifling conclusion, but a new life would have an open ending.

“If it feels too burdensome, you can ask him to serve as squad leader only in the early stages, until the Gwiyeongdae settles into place.”

“Would he do it?”

“There’s no reason he wouldn’t.”

“Come on. Still.”

“Ian. Do you think the position of a Gwiyeongdae squad leader is lower than that of the Demon Venerable’s successor?”

“…Of course it’s lower, isn’t it?”

“Ian!”

“Yes!”

“You, my direct subordinate and the commander of the Gwiyeongdae, stand in a position higher than the Demon Venerable’s successor. I am the one who will become the Heavenly Demon.”

Sensing that I was speaking sincerely, Ian lowered her head.

“I’m sorry. I absolutely didn’t say that because I was disregarding you, Young Master. It’s just that when I hear ‘Demon Venerable,’ it still feels too overwhelming.”

“I know. From now on, we’re changing the standard. Then taking Cheongmyeon as a squad leader won’t be such a burdensome matter.”

“Yes.”

“And right now, there’s a high chance Cheongmyeon will refuse. It isn’t something we can force. The Extreme Evil Soma won’t allow it easily either. We’ll have to work at it slowly.”

You still do not know how difficult it is to win a single person’s heart.

“He’ll be your subordinate, so you work hard to win him over.”

“Yes.”

And so we returned to Heuksalju’s hideout.

The Extreme Evil Soma, who had been waiting for me, asked about what came next.

“What do you intend to do now?”

“If we face Seok Gwanchu directly, a war may break out between our cult and the Sado Alliance. So I’m thinking of putting one person forward to fight.”

“And who is that?”

“Bi Sain.”

If it was Bi Sain, the successor of the Sado Alliance, he was the ideal person to put forward against Seok Gwanchu.

I gazed at the Extreme Evil Soma and said in a low voice.

“You and I, Lord Soma, will interfere in the decision of the Sado Alliance’s successor.”

Chapter 111: Because I Didn't Know.
-----------------------------------
Extreme Evil Soma was delighted.

He may have lived through all manner of experiences as a Demon Venerable, but he had surely never been involved in deciding the successor of the Sado Alliance. No—he probably had never even imagined there would be any reason for him to be involved.

“Are you truly saying we should decide the successor?”

“Yes, that’s right.”

“How, exactly?”

“If Bi Sain, the current successor, seems acceptable, we help him hold on to his position. If someone else seems more advantageous to us, we seat that person instead. I may meddle in many things, but when it comes to the successor of the Sado Alliance, regardless of each individual’s character, I believe we must install someone who will be thoroughly beneficial to our cult.”

Of course, he had to be beneficial not only to our cult, but to me as well.

“Then we should seat the most worthless bastard there is.”

“No. If we did that, the state of the murim would become unstable instead. At the very least, we need to put someone there whose actions can be predicted. In any case, if we handle this matter well, we’ll have rendered a great service to our cult.”

Extreme Evil Soma laughed loudly. He was already pleased at the thought of facing a formidable foe like Seok Gwanchu, and if that deed also earned merit, it would truly kill two birds with one stone.

“Will I finally get to see the Second Young Master’s world now?”

A glimmer of anticipation passed through the eyes within his sockets.

“No. This world is still your world, Lord Soma.”

“My world?”

“If you were not here, this would never have been possible in the first place. Do you think I would have even considered facing the Great Elder of the Sado Alliance alone?”

“Are you employing your Divine Art of Flattery again?”

“Why else would it be called a divine art? One must hone and polish it for a lifetime.”

At my joke, Extreme Evil Soma was amused. He found the turn of events interesting.

I sent Ian and Cheongmyeon to the safe house where the Lord of Cheonhwaru was staying.

I ordered them to protect the Lord of Cheonhwaru, but there were two other reasons.

First, it was dangerous. Now that it had been revealed that our main enemy was Seok Gwanchu, the Great Elder of the Sado Alliance, there was no telling what kind of master might appear next.

Second, I wanted Ian and Cheongmyeon to spend time together. I had told Ian: Your subordinate is for you to obtain. Whether she could manage that would depend on Ian. I could only hope Ian’s vessel was large enough to contain Cheongmyeon.

Before leaving, Ian said only one thing to me.

“If anything happens to you, Young Master, I will die too.”

“Very well. I’ll fight as though I have two lives.”

Cheongmyeon watched this, then bowed politely to Extreme Evil Soma. Extreme Evil Soma said nothing.

I had never seen the brusque Extreme Evil Soma and Cheongmyeon exchange any conversation apart from official commands.

After sending the two of them to the safe house, Extreme Evil Soma and I sped off using qinggong.

“Where are we going?”

“To Baegya Valley, to meet Bi Sain.”

Previously, I had informed Bi Sain that those who had tried to kill Extreme Evil Soma were the Bloody Hand Five Shadows of Baegya Valley.

Since there had been no further news from Bi Sain or Baegya Valley afterward, it meant Bi Sain had yet to resolve the matter of finding the mastermind in Baegya Valley.

* * *

Bi Sain was drinking in a tavern near Baegya Valley.

His face was red. Not from the alcohol, but from anger. The deeper his worries grew, the more hideous the scar on his face became.

He still had not uncovered the mastermind. It was valuable information he had obtained in Guizhou, in exchange for withdrawing the Baekgye Merchant Guild and paying a tremendous sum of money, but things had not gone as he had expected.

Until he came here, he had intended to pressure the lord of Baegya Valley by any means necessary and make him confess that he had ordered the Bloody Hand Five Shadows to kill Extreme Evil Soma.

If that failed, he had intended to force a confession through strength. With himself and seven of the Thirteen Wolves of the Sado Alliance, neither the lord of Baegya Valley nor the experts of Baegya Valley would be able to do anything.

But when they arrived at Baegya Valley, the lord of Baegya Valley had already gone somewhere. He had been here until the day before their arrival, yet had suddenly left the valley in a hurry with only his close aides.

Bi Sain had tried to frighten his subordinates into revealing the valley lord’s whereabouts, but they did not know.

He wondered if the lord had heard in advance that he was coming. Secrets kept leaking out.

Bi Sain emptied another cup of wine. Suddenly, Geom Mugeuk came to mind. Whenever he felt cornered, the man would come to mind without his realizing it.

‘Pointless.’

Just as Bi Sain was about to drink again, as though to shake Geom Mugeuk from his thoughts—

A man wearing a bamboo hat pulled low strode toward the seat where he was sitting. The seven Thirteen Wolves of the Sado Alliance seated around him leaped to their feet and drew their swords.

Then the approaching man removed his bamboo hat and revealed himself.

“It has been a while.”

The man who had come to find him was none other than Geom Mugeuk. Since Geom Mugeuk had appeared just as Bi Sain was thinking of him, Bi Sain was truly startled.

“How did you know I was here?”

Bi Sain glared at Geom Mugeuk with a face full of suspicion.

Geom Mugeuk, on the other hand, was calm.

“The information came from me, so it was only natural to guess you would have come to Baegya Valley. And with so many of you moving together, how could I fail to find you?”

Geom Mugeuk looked at the Thirteen Wolves of the Sado Alliance around him. He meant that with eight martial artists moving as a group, finding their location had been easy.

“May I sit?”

“Do as you please.”

Geom Mugeuk sat down before Bi Sain.

Bi Sain ordered the Thirteen Wolves of the Sado Alliance to take their seats. Everyone sat back down tensely. First Wolf Baek Cheolgi sat beside Bi Sain, ready to draw his sword at any moment.

“Why have you come here?”

He asked bluntly, but inwardly, Bi Sain was glad to see Geom Mugeuk. He should not feel such emotions, yet he kept finding himself wanting to rely on him.

“I came because I have important information to tell you, Young Master Bi.”

“What is it?”

“I have discovered who is behind this matter.”

In that instant, Bi Sain was shocked. Not only he, but Baek Cheolgi beside him and all the other Thirteen Wolves of the Sado Alliance were shocked as well.

“Who is it?”

Geom Mugeuk did not answer immediately.

“I can’t simply hand over such important information for free.”

The next moment, Bi Sain looked at Geom Mugeuk with a sense of dismay.

‘Second Young Master, you have made a grave mistake.’

He should not have mentioned such an important fact in this place.

The reason was the Thirteen Wolves of the Sado Alliance.

Bi Sain suspected that among these seven Thirteen Wolves of the Sado Alliance who followed him, there was a spy planted by the person trying to kill him.

Many things had not gone well recently, and he believed that was because the spy had been leaking information to the other side in advance.

When he arrived at Baegya Valley this time and saw that the lord of Baegya Valley had vacated his seat, his suspicion turned to certainty. Someone was definitely passing information to that side.

In a situation like this, such important information had now been revealed, so it was only a matter of time before this fact reached the mastermind.

Previously, the reason he had started to send a voice transmission to Geom Mugeuk and then stopped was because he had considered telling him this and seeking his help, but in the end, he could not bring himself to ask.

He regretted sending the voice transmission at all. The successor of the Sado Alliance seeking help from the blood of the Heavenly Demon? He had reproached himself endlessly for nearly doing something so absurd.

“I came from far away, so my throat is dry.”

Geom Mugeuk held out his cup.

Bi Sain poured wine into Geom Mugeuk’s cup.

At that very moment, Geom Mugeuk’s voice transmission reached Bi Sain.

—Let’s talk about the details after we catch the rat first.

—!

Bi Sain’s hand, which had been pouring the wine, paused for a moment before he finished pouring.

Bi Sain could tell. Geom Mugeuk understood exactly what situation he was in. At the thought that the problem he had agonized over all this time might finally be resolved, Bi Sain’s heart began to beat rapidly.

It was a change that had come after meeting Geom Mugeuk. This man kept making his heart race. He kept making him compare himself, kept making him feel shabby.

‘Damn it!’

He made him hope.

That night, a masked man silently landed on the roof of the largest inn in the village.

He hid something beneath the roof tiles at the edge of the roof. There was a set rule for sending secret letters. No matter what village one went to, if one hid a secret letter at the corner of the roof of the largest inn in that village, there would be someone who retrieved it and sent it by messenger pigeon. If it was in the mountains, there was a designated tree for hiding it; if it was in the fields, there was a designated place to bury it.

Just as he hid the secret letter and was about to hurry away, someone spoke from behind him.

“Geom Mugeuk has discovered the mastermind’s identity.”

The masked man flinched in shock and turned around. Before he knew it, Geom Mugeuk had approached silently and was taking out the secret letter he had hidden and reading it.

Geom Mugeuk had been drinking with Bi Sain at their lodging in Baegya Valley, and the masked man had confirmed that he had fallen asleep before leaving.

“I’m a light sleeper, you see. While I was sleeping, I heard someone sneaking out like a thieving cat.”

In that instant, the masked man realized this entire thing had been a trap. He had thought the information he needed to report was too important, and his haste had been his undoing.

Just as the masked man was about to flee—

“I’ll run after you and tell everyone. That the person among the Thirteen Wolves of the Sado Alliance who is absent right now is the traitor. I’m confident in my qinggong, at least, so you won’t make it back before me.”

The masked man’s figure froze, and Geom Mugeuk provoked him.

“You’d be better off killing me here and now. Then at least your identity won’t be exposed.”

The masked man turned toward Geom Mugeuk and drew his sword.

The masked man still did not say a word. Since he did not know how things would turn out, he had no desire to let his voice be heard unnecessarily.

Since things had come to this, there was only one method left. Kill him to silence him, then return quietly and pretend to know nothing.

The masked man immediately rushed toward Geom Mugeuk.

Swish!

As befitted a master said to be stronger than a Demon Venerable when paired with another, the masked man’s movements were extraordinary.

Swiish.

The masked man’s figure closed the distance in an instant and flew at Geom Mugeuk.

Clang!

The moment their swords clashed for the first time, the masked man’s heart sank. In the instant he nearly lost his grip on his sword, he realized it. Geom Mugeuk possessed internal energy far superior to his own.

And it was not only his internal energy that was outstanding.

Clang! Clang!

The techniques with which he had easily killed enemies until now did not work at all.

Every attack aimed at vital points was invariably blocked by Geom Mugeuk’s sword. He was using all his strength, yet it felt as though his opponent was blocking half-heartedly.

Feeling the clear gap in their abilities, the masked man threw himself in the opposite direction after barely a dozen exchanges. All he could think was that he had to flee far away if he wanted to survive.

At that very moment—

Piiing!

Thud!

A blast of finger wind shot through his thigh.

With a short scream, he rolled across the ground, then sprang back to his feet. The person who had descended to block his path was none other than Extreme Evil Soma.

“A man turning his back in the middle of a fight? Are you not ashamed?”
