“By the way, you said my brother is approaching the Poison King and the Fist Demon, so why did you leave out the Great Drunken Demon?”

When I mentioned the Great Drunken Demon, the smile vanished from the Extreme Evil Soma’s face, and he abruptly asked,

“Do you like alcohol?”

“I drink in moderation.”

“The Drunken Demon is someone who has seen alcohol through to the end. It is not easy for a sober person to deal with a drunkard.”

“Do you know about the Drunken Demon?”

I also knew as much as one could know about the Demon Venerables, but that information was focused more on the future than the present. I knew their major deeds, how they had lived, what they had been like around the time they came looking for materials for the Great Art, and their ends.

But I did not know well what kind of person the Drunken Demon was at this point in time. The Extreme Evil Soma, who lived in the same era, was likewise the same.

“I do not know him well. Only, when I see him, I feel unpleasant.”

“If even the Extreme Evil Soma feels unpleasant looking at him, doesn’t that mean he is a good person?”

“Perhaps. Or…”

“Or?”

“He is an even worse bastard.”

“That is a little frightening. Worse than the Extreme Evil Soma, you say.”

“The smell of alcohol on him has always put me in a foul mood.”

With those words, the Extreme Evil Soma said no more about the Drunken Demon.

A strange light gleamed in the two eyes looking at me. It was as if he found it interesting to see how I would deal with him.

He clearly knew something more about the Drunken Demon. His nasty nature had stirred again, and he was choosing to enjoy himself rather than tell me.

Soma, what do you know about the Drunken Demon?

Chapter 128: If I Could Ask Only One Question.
----------------------------------------------
Jang Ho was being led by Seo Daeryong toward Pungryu Tavern.

“Didn’t we agree to hold our drinking gatherings after Martial Artist Yi returned?”

“We did.”

“Then why are we going to drink?”

The day’s work was not even over yet, but Seo Daeryong had insisted they had to go, practically dragging him along. Since this was something that had never happened before, Jang Ho quietly went along with Seo Daeryong’s wishes.

Their drinking gatherings had been suspended because Ian was away, but before that, the two of them had shared all sorts of conversations. The awkward and uncomfortable atmosphere of their first meeting alone had, by the final day, become a fairly friendly relationship.

“There’s someone I want to introduce to Lord Jang today.”

“Who is it?”

“You’ll know when we get there. This person is important to me, so I really wanted to introduce you.”

“Very well.”

For some reason, Jang Ho saw a childish playfulness in Seo Daeryong’s expression.

But since Seo Daeryong had never once played a prank on him all this time, he could not imagine what sort of occasion today’s meeting was supposed to be.

And so the two entered Pungryu Tavern.

The owner, Jo Chunbae, welcomed them warmly.

“Ah, it’s been a long time. If I’d known you two were coming, I would have kept your seats open.”

In the second-floor seat where they always sat, a veiled woman was seated with her back turned to them.

“It’s all right.”

Seo Daeryong took Jang Ho up to the second-floor seat where the woman was sitting. She had ordered wine and a few simple side dishes, but because she was wearing a veil, her face could not be seen clearly.

When Seo Daeryong sat down before her, Jang Ho finally realized that the person he meant to introduce was this woman.

‘Is this the woman the Second Young Master mentioned?’

He had once seen the Second Young Master teasing Seo Daeryong about a junior he liked. But if she was an inspector of Hwangcheon Pavilion, she would not be meeting him while wearing a veil, would she? Especially not at a meeting like this, where she was being introduced.

Just as Jang Ho was wondering inwardly, the veiled woman greeted him first.

“I’ve heard a great deal about you, Lord Jang.”

Of course, she was Ian. This time as well, she spoke with her voice altered.

It was then.

Jang Ho recognized Ian.

“Martial Artist Yi?”

In that instant, not only Ian but Seo Daeryong beside her were startled.

“How did you know it was me?”

At Ian’s question, Jang Ho hesitated for a moment before speaking quietly.

“I am extremely sensitive to sound. I remember and recognize the unique qualities in a person’s voice and intonation very well. So if it is a voice I have heard often, no matter how much it is altered, I can discern the original voice.”

“Ah!”

Ian and Seo Daeryong exclaimed at the same time.

“The reason I was able to become the Demon Army Lord was also because this sensitivity to sound was of great help in martial training and actual combat. This is the first time I have revealed it.”

The fact that his childhood dream had been to become a painter had also been something he had first revealed in front of Seo Daeryong.

When Jang Ho had first begun attending these drinking gatherings, he had truly never imagined he would reveal his personal history here. He had started with the thought that they were people close to Geom Mugeuk, so there was no harm in becoming friendly with them, but now he was telling these two all manner of things.

Ian removed her veil.

“It’s been a while, Lord Jang.”

“Welcome back, Martial Artist Yi.”

Jang Ho greeted Ian gladly. Watching him, Seo Daeryong inwardly marveled. Even in the face of Ian’s beauty, Jang Ho did not waver in the slightest. Compared to him, had he not made every possible fuss under the sun?

‘How can he be like that?’

He was even worried that Ian might feel embarrassed.

Of course, Ian did not mind at all. Rather, she seemed more at ease because Jang Ho treated her exactly as he had before.

“Now then, today is all on me. So order as much as you like!”

The three of them talked about many things.

The star of the day was, without question, Ian. She spoke of how difficult it had been to undergo the Divine Poison Purification Art, what had happened on her travels, and how amusing the first wedding she had ever seen had been. She told them all sorts of stories.

Seo Daeryong and Jang Ho listened to her and chimed in at the right moments.

Then, when she stepped away for a moment, Seo Daeryong asked Jang Ho,

“Lord Jang. Isn’t Martial Artist Yi’s change astonishing?”

“It is astonishing. She has become very beautiful.”

“Is that all?”

“Pardon? Is something else necessary?”

“No, not exactly, but you don’t seem very surprised. I even started to suspect that the two of you had already met beforehand and were teasing me.”

“That cannot be. We have not.”

Seo Daeryong lowered his voice and spoke insinuatingly.

“Then there’s no need to hold back on purpose. It’s just us here.”

She’s beautiful, isn’t she? Please say she’s beautiful, please! Seeing those emotions written plainly all over Seo Daeryong’s face, Jang Ho finally burst into laughter. They had become close enough for the word “us” to come naturally.

“I don’t know if this might be disrespectful to Martial Artist Yi, but I attach no meaning to a person’s appearance. Perhaps I am even more like that because of the scar on my own face. I suppose there is also a feeling of, with this face, who am I to judge anyone?”

At Jang Ho’s honest words, Seo Daeryong also conveyed what he had always thought.

“Lord Jang, that scar of yours is impressive.”

“Impressive? It is hideous.”

“No, it is impressive. Truly. Sell it to me!”

At that, Jang Ho did not hesitate as he reached to draw a dagger.

“I can make you one exactly like it.”

Seo Daeryong was startled and hurriedly stopped him.

“What are you doing!”

“Then speak honestly. Say it is hideous.”

“It’s impressive because there’s a story behind it. I’m saying I want to buy that story too.”

“There is no great story. I was injured while fighting enemies.”

“For a martial artist, that is a great story. I do not want to get a scar from a friend’s dagger while drinking.”

Seo Daeryong did not even realize that he had used the word friend.

“Let us have a drink.”

Jang Ho lifted his cup.

Seo Daeryong might not know how he felt, but Jang Ho found this place comfortable. In front of the Demon Army, he maintained a flawless, unshaken bearing, but when he came here, he could finally relax and feel as though he were resting.

Just then, Ian returned.

“Where was I?”

Jang Ho said to her,

“Martial Artist Yi, this night is long. You may start again from the beginning.”

* * *

At that time, I was meeting the Ilhwa Sword Venerable.

And from her, I heard something unexpected.

“I know the Drunken Demon well.”

Unexpectedly, the Ilhwa Sword Venerable was on friendly terms with him.

“We even drink together now and then. We drank a few days ago, too.”

“You drank a few days ago?”

“He sometimes comes to see me and drinks.”

At first glance, it was hard to imagine, but thinking about it again, it was only natural that among the Eight Demon Venerables who held power, there would be exchanges of their own.

“What sort of person is the Drunken Demon?”

“Someone pleasant to drink with.”

The Ilhwa Sword Venerable’s assessment was the complete opposite of the Extreme Evil Smiling Demon’s.

“When did you become close?”

“I don’t know. When was it? It feels like it’s been quite a long time. We just became close while drinking.”

“What is the Drunken Demon’s personality like?”

“Unpretentious.”

If he truly were an unpretentious person, the Extreme Evil Smiling Demon would never have described him as he did. In other words, the Drunken Demon showed completely different sides depending on the person he faced.

“How could a Demon Venerable be unpretentious? He must simply be hiding his claws.”

“That may be so. But why are you asking about the Drunken Demon?”

“This time, someone from the Drunken Demon’s side caused trouble. Hwangcheon Pavilion has locked him up, but I thought the Drunken Demon might make a move.”

“Or perhaps the Second Young Master intends to use that as an excuse to make a move?”

“As expected, you are sharp. The Drunken Demon is the only one who has yet to decide where he stands between my elder brother and me.”

The Ilhwa Sword Venerable stared at me, then smiled as she said,

“I have not decided where I stand either. I have not yet decided to stand on the Second Young Master’s side.”

How could I not know that this was how she felt? That was why I spoke to her in an exaggerated manner.

“Ah! And here I was, firmly believing you were on my side.”

“Of course, I like our Second Young Master. Honestly, comparing you to the Eldest Young Master is absurd to begin with. But being close is a private matter, while the issue of succession is a public one, is it not?”

“I understand.”

“That does not mean I will choose the Eldest Young Master either, so do not feel hurt.”

“I would never feel hurt by you, Sword Venerable. No, I cannot.”

“Why is that?”

“Do you remember what I told you when I met you before? I threw a rather unreasonable tantrum and told you to prove that you, Senior, would be more useful to me than Elder Blade Demon.”

The Ilhwa Sword Venerable gave a faint smile. Her expression said she remembered everything.

“Now it is your turn, Senior. ‘Prove that you are more useful to me than the Eldest Young Master!’ It would be your turn to say that.”

The mere fact that she had not reacted that way was proof that she was treating me with goodwill.

“I told you then, did I not? Dreams, ideals, loyalty… I said I despise those who try to gain the hearts of their subordinates or juniors for free with such things. I have no intention whatsoever of gaining your heart with things like that, either. I must grant what you want as much as I can, while at the same time showing you that I possess the qualities of the Heavenly Demon.”

At my words, a look of admiration flashed across the Ilhwa Sword Venerable’s face.

“As expected! I chose my sparring friend well.”

The fact that she brought up sparring at this moment meant that sparring was included among the things she wanted. She still possessed the desire to become stronger.

“I look forward to the day we spar again.”

* * *

After meeting the Ilhwa Sword Venerable, I returned to my residence.

Gowol, who had been staying with me, was not at home. He had already left the cult to carry out my order to create an intelligence organization like Tongcheon Pavilion.

Several letters he had sent had arrived at my residence. They were reports saying he was traveling throughout the Central Plains with the money I had given him, building an organizational network.

I was not worried about him. As if to prove that he was not someone who would make me worry, he had gone out together with the leader of the Pungcheon Cult. Imagining the two of them bickering as they carried out this task together made me smile of its own accord.

At night, Ian stopped by for a moment.

I had expected her to come thoroughly tipsy from drink, but she was not as drunk as I thought she would be.

“I chattered so much that I sobered right up.”

“Did you enjoy it?”

“They say a journey is not about leaving, but about returning. After talking to those two to my heart’s content, it feels as though the journey has finally been completed.”

Whether because of the opening of the Ren and Du meridians, or because she had realized something this time, she definitely gave off a different impression than before she had left the cult.

“There was something I resolved during this journey. It may sound as if I’m showing off, so it feels a little awkward, but since I’ve brought it up, I’ll tell you. I was pleased that the attention directed at me was excessive, but it was also a great burden. And I came to the conclusion that the only way to escape that burden is for me to truly become strong.”

Ian was preparing herself to train fiercely again. Since she put in effort like this, I believed that in the end, she would accomplish something, whatever it might be.

“But that does not mean I will shut myself away in the training hall. Like the Blood Heaven Blade Demon, I will read many books and meet people as well. It will not be only my martial arts that grow stronger; I, as a person, will grow stronger. Only then will I be able to make Cheongmyeon and Seojin my squad leaders, right? Those people will not come to me simply because my martial arts are strong.”

I believed she would become a true master. Because the direction of her effort was aimed toward becoming a true master.

In martial arts, talent and effort are important. What martial art one trains in is important, and who one’s master is matters. Real combat experience is important as well.

But the moment martial arts, rushing toward the extreme, surpass a certain level, she will come to realize it, just as I did.

That the end of martial learning is ultimately a battle over what kind of person I am.

I consider the process of meeting and persuading the Demon Venerables to be part of the process of becoming stronger. I even regard my efforts to stop Gwiryeongja’s marriage as part of that process of becoming stronger.

To perfectly understand someone is nearly impossible. But I had finally realized that the broader one’s understanding of people and the world becomes, the closer one draws to the essence of martial arts.

“Then I’ll be going. I’m completely sober now, but my head is pounding.”

I asked her as she turned to leave.

“Ian.”

“Yes.”

“Suppose you ended up drinking with the Drunken Demon.”

“Suddenly, with the Drunken Demon Venerable?”

“You’re no slouch in the world of drunkards yourself, are you?”

“Well, that’s true.”

“I want to know what kind of person the Drunken Demon is, but I can only ask one question. What would you ask?”

Ian seemed to think it over carefully, then came up with one thing.

“That person must have drunk a truly great amount of wine by now. If so, I would ask this.”

As if the Drunken Demon were standing before her, Ian asked,

“When, and with whom, did the wine taste best to you?”

Chapter 129: What Dream Are You Having While Drunk?
---------------------------------------------------
I arrived at Hwangcheon Pavilion the next day.

The investigators and enforcement warriors welcomed me back with fervor after my long absence. In particular, morale at Hwangcheon Pavilion had soared once again after I brought in one of the Great Drunken Demon’s subordinates.

My office was as neatly arranged as it had been when I left. Seeing the flowers in the pot by the window growing well, I could tell Seo Daeryong had been taking good care of the office too.

“Bring me everything that’s piled up.”

“I thought you would say that, so I brought it already. These are the matters you must handle personally, Pavilion Master.”

I had expected the work to have piled up, but when I looked at the documents Seo Daeryong handed me, there weren’t as many as I’d thought.

On closer inspection, aside from the matters that absolutely required my attention, he had meticulously finished everything himself. It reaffirmed for me that when it came to Hwangcheon Pavilion’s work, he was better at it than I was. And since he was learning martial arts from the Blood Heaven Blade Demon, I had nothing to worry about there either.

‘He’s growing up well, our next Hwangcheon Pavilion Master.’

Of course, Seo Daeryong had no idea I was thinking that.

Just then, another investigator entered carrying a box.

“This is an item delivered for you, Pavilion Master.”

“Who sent it?”

“It was sent by the Drunken Demon Venerable.”

He sent something after I captured one of the Drunken Demon’s men? I had asked the Demon Venerables about the Drunken Demon, but he had made a move before I did.

The investigator set the box down and left.

Just as I was about to open it, Seo Daeryong suddenly shouted.

“Be careful!”

“You startled me! Why?”

“I was about to say, what if poisonous smoke bursts out? But that can’t be it. He wouldn’t kill you over some bastard who drank and drove a carriage into people.”

“Of course not. I nearly died because you startled me instead.”

When I opened the box, there was a single liquor bottle inside.

“He sent liquor.”

The moment I picked up the bottle, I flinched in surprise.

“No, he didn’t send liquor. He sent a liquor bottle.”

The bottle was empty.

“Could it be precious porcelain?”

“It’s just an ordinary liquor bottle you’d find in the marketplace.”

“Why would he send you a cheap liquor bottle, Pavilion Master? Isn’t he asking for a fight?”

He must have known we would react this way. So why send an empty bottle?

“This is the Drunken Demon’s warning. He wants his man released.”

“If that were the case, he would have sent a broken liquor bottle. Or a blade.”

“Then why did he send it?”

“Is there any need to agonize over it?”

I sprang to my feet.

“What are you going to do?”

“What else would I do with the Drunken Demon?”

I took the liquor bottle and left the office.

“I should go have a drink.”

* * *

Holding the liquor bottle in one hand, I entered Daechwi Forest, where the Drunken Demon resided. Since I had sent word ahead that I would visit, a warrior was waiting to guide me.

Following the warrior’s lead, I went deeper into Daechwi Forest.

Houses had been built all throughout the place, ranging from huts to grand estates. All kinds of homes stood there, giving off an impression that was both beautiful and free-spirited.

In particular, breweries large and small were scattered throughout Daechwi Forest. Drunkards sat on raised platforms drinking, and some were simply lying there, dead drunk. If you looked kindly on it, it seemed free. If not, it looked like utter chaos.

But I knew. Compared to the followers of the other Demon Venerables, their numbers weren’t great, but those gathered here were relatively skilled. That was why the gazes of those who appeared drunk were anything but ordinary.

There was a lake in the center of Daechwi Forest.

In the middle of that lake was a small island, and on it stood the pavilion where the Drunken Demon resided.

I headed there in a small rowboat.

The warrior who brought me dropped me off on the island and returned.

I slowly walked toward the pavilion. At the entrance, a woman welcomed me with the door open.

“Welcome, Young Master. I am Yeo Bin.”

With her graceful impression, she was, like Gu Mayeong of the Three Great Drunkards whom I had met before, an expert counted among the Three Great Drunkards.

“The Demon Venerable is waiting for you. Please come this way.”

I slowly went inside.

The place she led me to was a pavilion called Chimong Pavilion.

When I went up into the pavilion, the Drunken Demon was there.

He was asleep, reclining at an angle in a cushioned floor chair covered with soft fur.

The place reeked of liquor and was a complete mess. Dozens of liquor bottles lay scattered everywhere. Some had toppled over, while others stood upright.

But I could feel it. These bottles were not simply lying around. If a fight broke out here, I instinctively sensed that all of them would become his weapons. They might even activate as a formation. It was chaos, yet not chaos at all.

The Illhwa Sword Venerable had called him unpretentious, and the Extreme Evil Young Demon had called him unpleasant. Now he was showing me yet another side of himself: sleeping in a drunken stupor.

Drunken Demon, what kind of dream do you dream when drunk?

I stood for a moment, gazing past the Drunken Demon at the sunlight sparkling on the lake, then sat about ten paces away from him. I set the liquor bottle I had brought down in front of me.

At the sound of another presence, the Drunken Demon slowly opened his eyes.

The Great Drunken Demon, Song Sahyeok.

He was young and handsome. Even within our cult, he was considered one of the finest-looking men, and he had a youthful face that made him look far younger than his age. It seemed he had not shaved for several days, yet the reason he looked manly rather than shabby was because he was handsome.

As soon as he opened his eyes, he drank from the liquor before him and asked me,

“Will you have a cup?”

Over his face appeared the final image of him I had seen before my regression. His emaciated form, reduced to skin and bones.

When I returned to our cult to obtain the materials for the Great Regression Art, the Drunken Demon was still alive. But he was merely clinging to life; it was not a life truly lived.

He had been bedridden. They said he had suffered from illness for a very long time. At the time, the demon physician diagnosed that the illness had come from drinking too much liquor.

The Drunken Demon dying from a liquor bottle?

This shocked everyone and became a source of mockery for those who disliked him.

—Will you have a cup?

Those had been the first words he said to me as he lay sick in bed. Then and now, the first words he spoke to me were the same.

Those words echoed in my heart, and over the face of his sickly self overlapped the Drunken Demon before me now, full of vitality.

“It’s liquor you’ll like.”

“I’m all right.”

As if he would not force me, he drank the liquor he was holding by himself.

“Why have you only come now? Am I that unpopular among the Eight Demon Venerables?”

“The protagonist always appears at the end.”

“Am I the protagonist of your story?”

“The protagonist of my story is me. You, Lord Drunken Demon, will either become my helper or my adversary. What about in your story? What role do I play? Is my brother the helper? Or am I?”

The Drunken Demon stared intently at me, then asked instead of answering.

“Do you like liquor?”

“There was a time when I did.”

“And now?”

“I drink enough to match the mood at a drinking party.”

“You’re still green with youth, yet you speak as if you have a story.”

At his amused response, I asked back boldly,

“Are the stories of the young not stories?”

The Drunken Demon laughed. Seeing such a handsome man laugh, my first thought was that he must have made plenty of women cry.

“What kind of liquor do you like?”

“I haven’t drunk enough to develop a preference yet. I prefer something easy to drink over hard liquor.”

He was probing me through questions about liquor.

His gaze turned to the liquor bottle placed in front of me.

“What liquor did you fill it with?”

“Why are you so sure it’s liquor? It might be water. It might be poison.”

At my answer, he smiled and poured liquor into his cup. He did not ask me to pour for him, and I did not offer.

“Why do you think I sent an empty liquor bottle?”

“I don’t know. I wondered if you sent it so I’d come ask you that.”

“How did you feel when you received it? Be honest.”

“Honestly? You’ll be offended.”

“It’s fine.”

“Never mind. You’ll definitely get angry.”

“I promise I won’t. If I get angry, I can just drink. Don’t we have liquor? Now, say it.”

“I thought, what the hell is this bastard trying to pull?”

For an instant, the Drunken Demon flinched, then drank the liquor before him.

“Excellent!”

By the time he set the bottle down, his expression had improved.

“This is why liquor is good. Once you drink, you forget everything and feel better.”

He spoke as if he had drunk an entire bottle after only one cup.

“If it annoyed you that much, why did you come?”

“Because it is a chance to learn what kind of person you are. Whether you are someone I can accept or not. Whether you are someone I can accomplish a great undertaking with, or merely a drunkard.”

The Drunken Demon’s demonic energy, subtly flowing out, was unlike anything I had ever felt in my life. As though alcohol qi were mixed into it, the moment his demonic energy enveloped my body, my head spun as if I were drunk.

I activated the Heavenly Demon Body-Protecting Art. The dizzying energy vanished, and my mind regained its calm.

When my gaze did not waver, surprise flickered across the Drunken Demon’s face.

“Lord Drunken Demon, do you like liquor?”

When I endured his pressure and even posed a question, the Drunken Demon withdrew his demonic energy.

“Do I like liquor? Is that something you ask the Drunken Demon?”

“Isn’t it precisely because you are the Drunken Demon that I should ask?”

“What do you mean?”

“You should ask a martial artist whether he likes martial arts, ask a musician whether he likes music, and ask a painter whether he likes painting. You should ask the owner of Pungryu Tavern what he thinks of selling liquor. But isn’t that a question you ought to ask such people at least once? Now, I’ll ask again. Do you like liquor?”

“!”

The Drunken Demon openly showed an expression that said, Look at this brat.

“You really are different from the rumors. I admit it. Among all the people I’ve met, you are the furthest from the rumors about you.”

“I hear that far too often. I don’t know who is spreading rumors about me, but I think I should drag them in and scold them. If they’re going to spread rumors, they should at least do it properly.”

“To deal with you, I’ll have to drink. It would be difficult sober.”

“Isn’t that something you should say in your heart?”

“What is so special about drinking?”

He drank several cups in succession.

“What kind of liquor is that? It must be difficult to find outside.”

“That is one of the preconceptions people have about me. I drink any liquor well.”

As if to prove it, he sent his wine cup flying toward me.

The cup slowly flew through the air and hovered in front of my face.

I smelled the liquor.

“Ah, this liquor is?”

“That’s right. It’s liquor I brought from the Pungryu Tavern you frequent.”

It meant he had been watching me.

“I’ll cast aside my preconceptions.”

He was the one who retrieved the cup as well. The cup flew back, tilted in midair, and he opened his mouth beneath it to drink.

The emptied cup dropped onto the table. It moved so smoothly that not a sound could be heard.

Just by watching him use Void Object Manipulation, I could gauge his skill. It was such a clean technique that it made me wonder how a drunken man could manage it.

“You’re impressive too. To know it was Pungryu Tavern’s liquor just from the smell.”

“It’s because I’ve only been drinking that tavern’s liquor lately. Aren’t you the one who can identify the taste of every liquor in the world by smell, Lord Drunken Demon?”

“How could I possibly know all the countless liquors in the world?”

Contrary to his modest words, the Drunken Demon was famous for identifying liquor by scent.

The Drunken Demon drank again. The more liquor went in, the redder his face became. I had thought that someone like the Drunken Demon could drink great quantities without his complexion changing, but his face reddened enough to show.

“When I drink, I feel better. Is it not the same for you?”

“It depends on who I drink with.”

“Liquor tastes best when you drink alone.”

“I don’t know that world well yet.”

“Oh dear! You do not know such paradise-like pleasure!”

That was a lie. When I lived as a wandering swordsman, I drank a lot alone. Back then, liquor was my friend, my refuge, and my resting place. And it was a demon tempting me toward ruin.

“You should have learned liquor from me! From this Drunken Demon.”

“That’s true. My father taught me liquor wrong. I’ll tell him when I see him tomorrow.”

Knowing it was a joke, the Drunken Demon laughed and drank.

The more drunk he became, the more his body tilted. He behaved like an ordinary person drunk on liquor. While drinking, he even spilled some. It was hard to tell how much was real and how much was an act.

He was defenselessness itself, but I did not think he was truly defenseless. Rather, this was probably the most dangerous moment to fight him. If one wished to fight him, one would first have to sober him up.

“Come to think of it, that’s true. It was also a preconception that because you are the Drunken Demon, you would drink endlessly.”

“Precisely because I am the Drunken Demon, I drink in moderation. Only those who know nothing of liquor drink until they lose themselves.”

“You’re right.”

“Second Young Master, have you ever fought after drinking?”

“No.”

“Try it. It is truly magnificent. It loosens your tension and makes you less afraid. Even when you’re hit, it hurts less. It will raise your full strength by three-tenths. In my opinion, all the masters who left their names in the history of the murim fought after drinking.”

He looked at the liquor in front of me.

“So drink. Liquor will give you strength. Liquor will soothe you.”

Only then did I pick up the liquor bottle I had brought. Then I slowly opened its stopper.

“That will not be the case.”

The moment the fragrance of the liquor spread out—

The Drunken Demon, who had been sitting at an angle as though lying down, sprang upright. It was a liquor he knew.

Without drinking, I set it back down before me and spoke calmly.

“Because I, too, know very well… the heart of a person drowning in liquor.”

Chapter 130: You Are Prejudice Itself
-------------------------------------
Silence flowed.

It was a silence rather long and heavy for two people to bear in the same space.

The liquor I had bought was the liquor he had asked for just before he died. It was sold at a brewery near the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult, and no one knew why, of all things, he had wanted to drink this particular liquor. The Drunken Demon drank this liquor and died.

“Why did you buy that liquor?”

His voice was trembling as much as his eyes.

Judging by his reaction, I could be certain there was some story tied to this liquor.

Pretending not to know, I made an excuse.

“Because I was in a foul mood. You must have been expecting some extraordinary famed wine, but I just stopped by anywhere and bought it. If you sent me an empty bottle to test me, you should have been prepared for some common liquor from nearby. Did I not tell you how I felt when I received that empty bottle?”

At my answer that I had bought it without any particular meaning, the Drunken Demon glared at me for a moment.

“What is it? Was I not supposed to buy this liquor?”

If I wanted to lessen his suspicion, I had to ask back instead. I had to press in without avoiding it. I had to ask openly.

“Does this liquor have some special meaning?”

When I looked at him with a suspicious gaze of my own, the Drunken Demon quietly changed the subject.

“You have a temper.”

“Who doesn’t? Everyone just endures it and lives on.”

The Drunken Demon relaxed from the posture he had shot up into and leaned back against the backrest again.

“You said you understood the heart of someone drowning in liquor? How?”

“Because I once drowned in it myself.”

At that, he openly snorted.

“Is it that a young drunkard is not acknowledged as a drunkard either?”

It was a continuation of my earlier retort to him—was a young person’s story not a story?

“I understand. It is an age when everything burns fiercely.”

“Do not mock me.”

“I am not mocking you. I mean age cannot be helped. I do not want to act like some obnoxious senior who only shows off and tries to lecture others either.”

“You are doing exactly that right now.”

“So I said age cannot be helped, didn’t I? If you are angry, drink that liquor.”

After saying that, as if he were the one growing angry, he gulped down his liquor.

“Damn it!”

He set the cup down with a clack. The force of it shattered the cup. When he lifted his hand, the broken pieces rose into the air and flew off to a corner. There, broken cups were piled high.

Pouring liquor into a new cup, the Drunken Demon said,

“I told you to have a drink too.”

“I am angry, but I will not drink.”

“Why?”

“Because I came here today intending not to drink. If I drink while dealing with the Drunken Demon, what do you think the result will be?”

“A plausible thought, but have you not considered the opposite? How can you deal with the Drunken Demon without drinking?”

“Then please promise me one thing. Today, we only drink.”

“Isn’t that what I should be saying? Please do not get drunk and ask me to help you.”

“Very well.”

I drank straight from the bottle I had brought. Even after only a little, my throat burned.

“It is strong. Would you like to try this liquor?”

The Drunken Demon shook his head.

Holding the bottle, I walked over to the Drunken Demon. Then I sat across from him at the drinking table.

“I have to eat side dishes when I drink.”

“A drinker with no style.”

“Health over style. And I am not a drinker.”

I took out a silver needle and checked the dishes for poison. At the sight, the Drunken Demon snapped.

“Do I look like the sort of person who feeds poisoned food to a guest?”

“That is why I am checking. To see whether you are that sort of person or not.”

The Drunken Demon snatched up the dish I was about to test with the silver needle and ate it.

“What do you take me for? There is no such thing! Just eat!”

“I refuse. How am I supposed to trust you?”

When I stuck the silver needle into another dish, he ate that one first too.

“Trust me. I hate those bastards who put poison or drugs in liquor and snacks! I am the sort of man who chews them alive.”

I spoke calmly to him as he grew thoroughly furious.

“You eat snacks well. Please keep eating them like that from now on. Think of the sincerity of the person who made them too. Are snacks flowers? Something to just look at?”

To be honest, I had deliberately come and eaten the snacks just to say this to him. There would be no one among the people around him who would dare tell the Drunken Demon to eat his snacks.

Listen, man. If you keep going like this, you will die of drink. You like liquor this much and take so much pride in it, so you cannot die because of that liquor, can you?

This was separate from whether he would become my ally or my brother’s. Even if he committed evil deeds and died by my hand, I would rather he be stabbed by a sword and die.

If a person liked and enjoyed one thing his entire life, would it not be too wretched to die because of that very thing?

If he saw his future self, I could guarantee it. He would kill himself.

“Now, have a drink.”

“If you do not eat the snacks, I will not drink.”

“Are you satisfied? Are you satisfied now!”

“Good.”

We drank together. He gave the impression that if he drank any more it would be hard on him, that it would be hard on him, and yet he kept drinking. He grew more and more drunk.

In the midst of that, my Flattery Divine Art was attempted without fail.

“Among the Eight Demon Venerables, you are the most handsome.”

“That is something anyone would acknowledge.”

The art of flattery requires the skill of pushing and pulling. If you have said something good, you must also say something bad for the good words to shine brighter—like this.

“To be honest, you are so sculpted that it actually makes you feel distant.”

“Tell the women of the world. Please keep some distance from me.”

“Ah! I cannot endure this. I need to drink.”

Who in the world would dislike being told they were handsome? The Drunken Demon also drank in good spirits.

“At one time, you see… women truly lined up for me. Can you imagine a righteous sect heroine and an unorthodox faction female master fighting over me? Ah, I really remember those days.”

Perhaps suddenly stirred by excitement, he sprang to his feet and took off his upper garment. Then, before I could stop him, he launched himself into the lake.

He swam all over like a madman for a long while before climbing back up to the pavilion. His body, dripping with water, was firm and muscular, but there were many scars all over it.

Come to think of it, I had never seen the bodies of the Demon Venerables. Perhaps the bodies of the other Demon Venerables were covered in scars like this as well? Scars demanded by their positions, just as my body had been battered to pieces while I gathered materials for the Grand Art.

“Ah, now I am finally sobering up a little. Now, let us drink again.”

He returned far too normally for it to be called drunken antics. He roughly threw on his clothes and began drinking again.

I drank slowly. He drank without pause, but he did not force me to drink. That one thing was truly good.

“Shall I tell you honestly why I called you?”

“Please tell me.”

Then the Drunken Demon gave a truly unexpected reason.

“Because no one came looking for me. Why does neither the Eldest Young Master nor you come looking for me? When you go around meeting the other Demon Venerables so eagerly? Are you looking down on me? Is that it?”

“If I were, I would not have come today.”

“Then why? Spare me that nonsense about the main character appearing later.”

“If I had to give a reason, it would be that, for some reason, you felt like someone far removed from the succession struggle.”

“I did?”

“Are you not?”

“Not at all. What relationship is there between my liking liquor and my trying not to be pushed out of the power struggles within the cult? Are they not completely separate matters? What is this? If someone likes liquor, must he like people, have no desire for power, and have no greed? Must it be that way? Who decided that?”

“It seems this was also a prejudice. Truly, you are prejudice itself, Drunken Demon.”

“Good grief, what a tiresome world.”

The Drunken Demon drank again. Whether he was pleased, angry, or felt any emotion at all, the moment he felt it, he drank. Drinking like this, it was only natural that he died of illness from drink.

After several more cups of liquor, I asked the question I had been waiting to ask.

“Did you send a liquor bottle to my brother as well?”

“I did.”

“Did my brother come to see you?”

At that, the Drunken Demon shook his head.

“He sent this instead.”

When he stretched out his hand, a box in the corner flew over.

When I opened the box, inside was a liquor bottle identical to the one he had sent me. And the bottle was broken.

“Do you know what I thought when I saw this?”

“I do.”

“You do? What did I think?”

“What is this bastard trying to pull?”

When I repeated the words I had said upon seeing the empty liquor bottle the Drunken Demon had sent me, the Drunken Demon laughed as if he would die and drank.

“The Second Young Master is amusing. How about we become sworn brothers? If I had a younger brother as entertaining as the Second Young Master, it would be truly fantastic.”

“I sincerely hope my brother thought the same.”

The Drunken Demon laughed even louder and drank.

I found it strange.

Since three Demon Venerables had interacted with me, my brother was in a situation where even one Demon Venerable mattered. And yet he had refused the Drunken Demon’s proposal this blatantly?

If this was true, I needed to find out why my brother had made such a choice. There had to be a reason.

“That is unlike my brother.”

“You may be seeing the Eldest Young Master incorrectly.”

“There is probably no one who knows my brother as well as I do.”

“Listen, Second Young Master.”

“Yes.”

“Do you know this? Those closest see the least.”

He seemed to think it was a rather fine thing to say, but in the middle of it, the Drunken Demon spilled his liquor. Perhaps feeling needlessly embarrassed, he tried to go swimming again.

“I need to sober up a bit. Those arrogant fish bastards! They must have many prejudices against me too. I will go teach them a lesson.”

I stopped him as he staggered to his feet.

“You should stop drinking. I must be going now.”

To be honest, I wanted to drink more. Was it because I was drinking with the Drunken Demon? Or had the liquor bug sleeping inside me awakened? I enjoyed this drinking party.

But because this was a seat with the person I had to be most careful of in this murim, at least when it came to drinking, I intended to end it after emptying only the liquor I had brought.

“I will take my leave for today.”

Unable to steady himself, the Drunken Demon leaned against the backrest and asked me,

“Will you drink with me again?”

“Anytime you call me.”

“We should become sworn brothers, should we not?”

Instead of answering, I asked him. It was the very question Ian had mentioned.

“Drunken Demon, when was the liquor you drank the most delicious and enjoyable?”

I thought this question was an excellent one that could offer a glimpse of what kind of person he was.

Then came an unexpected answer.

“Now.”

“You mean this drinking session with me? This is an honor.”

“Our Second Young Master has some rather ambitious dreams.”

With unfocused eyes, the Drunken Demon looked at the lake.

“The liquor I drink right now is always the most delicious. Whether I drink alone or with some stranger passing by. The liquor I drink at this very moment is the most delicious. Even if you leave and an assassin comes to kill me, the liquor I drink with him will be the most delicious.”

With that, the Drunken Demon emptied his last cup. Now that the drunkenness was rising too much, he leaned against the backrest almost as if lying down and closed his eyes. He gestured for me to hurry and go, speaking with a twisted tongue.

“What memories could there be… in liquor that has already gone out as piss? Farewell.”

With those words, he fell asleep.

I quietly turned and left.

Yeo Bin of the Three Great Drunkards, who had greeted me earlier, rowed the ferry.

When we had crossed about halfway across the lake, I looked back.

Unable to support himself, the Drunken Demon was draped over the railing of the pavilion like laundry, staring at me.

It was far too distant to see his expression, but thanks to the Divine Eye Art, I could see his face clearly. On his face, red as if it would burst, his two eyes were… indifferent.

They were empty eyes, enough to make me wonder what all the liquor, jokes, and time we had shared until now had even been.

He was not easygoing as the One Flower Sword Venerable had felt, nor was he unpleasant as the Extreme Evil Lesser Demon had felt. He seemed like a drunkard, yet was not a drunkard; he seemed easy to deal with, yet was not easy at all.

I still did not know what kind of person the Drunken Demon was.

* * *

After leaving the Great Drunken Forest, I went straight to see the Blood Heaven Saber Demon.

The Blood Heaven Saber Demon was training in saber techniques again today.

“Why have you been so absorbed in training lately?”

The Blood Heaven Saber Demon drove the Heaven-Destroying Great Saber he had been swinging into the ground and leaned his body against the blade. His body was drenched in sweat.

“You may believe you can end the succession fight without seeing blood, but I do not.”

It meant that a bloody struggle might break out.

“Will you fight for me?”

“I will fight for myself.”

“That is the same thing.”

“You drank.”

“I drank with the Drunken Demon.”

“With the Drunken Demon?”

“Yes. He approached me first.”

How, indeed, did the Drunken Demon appear to the Blood Heaven Saber Demon?

“The Drunken Demon is an insidious man. Do not get close to him.”

An insidious man. It was the moment another side of the Drunken Demon was revealed.

“Why do you only associate with bad ones?”

“There would have to be someone who is not bad.”

The Blood Heaven Saber Demon merely smacked his lips.

“If you had to choose only one between the Extreme Evil Lesser Demon and the Drunken Demon?”

He could not easily choose one.

“Are you saying he is that insidious?”

“I dislike those who drown in liquor. Different when drunk, different when sober. Drunken fits, making a nuisance of themselves, shameless forgetfulness. The one standing at the extreme of that lunacy is the Drunken Demon.”

But the reason the Blood Heaven Saber Demon disliked the Drunken Demon was not merely that.

“Have you ever seen him sober?”

“No, I have not.”

“Neither have I. Perhaps no one has. That is why. That is the reason he is an insidious bastard.”

The decisive reason why the Blood Heaven Saber Demon disliked him had emerged.

“He has never once shown himself not drunk.”

Was his drunken self truly his real self? Or was the sober one real?

“Be careful. An insidious bastard has begun to move.”

I do not know with what intent he sent me that liquor bottle. But perhaps, Drunken Demon, it is finally time for you to sober up at least once in your life.

Chapter 131: The Drunkards I’ve Encountered.
--------------------------------------------
The next day, when I entered the Hwangcheon Pavilion office, Seo Dae-ryong was waiting for me.

The moment he saw me, he let out a sigh of relief.

“You’re safe.”

“Why? Were you afraid I’d fallen into the Drunken Demon’s wine jar and drowned?”

“You can’t hold your liquor, can you?”

“I can drink just fine. I was only being careful when drinking with you lot.”

“Why?”

“In case my drunken habits came out.”

“You have drunken habits, Pavilion Lord?”

“I do.”

“What kind?”

It was a drunken habit I could not tell Seo Dae-ryong about. My mood would plummet without end, and I would become unbearably depressed. If I had not met Seojin during my days as a wanderer, I would either have become a ruined drunkard, or eventually, in a drunken stupor, gone to kill Hwa Mugi. I would never even have met Hwa Mugi, and would have been killed by those who supported him.

I said the opposite of my real drunken habit.

“I drink, sing, dance. It becomes a whole commotion.”

“I can’t imagine it!”

“And I nag my right-hand man endlessly.”

“That, I don’t want to imagine.”

“Now do you understand why I restrain myself from drinking?”

“No. Drink. Let your drunken habits out, at least in front of us. You need to vent your pent-up anger too, Pavilion Lord. This right-hand man of yours will endure your nagging for half an hour!”

Suddenly, I remembered the moment I first met Seo Dae-ryong. The sight of him entering my room, dragging that gloomy aura with him. He had been the very picture of depression and crookedness, and now he was comforting me like this.

“We’re gathering again tonight. You should come too, Pavilion Lord. Lord Jang will want to see you.”

“Fine. I’ll see you tonight.”

“Yes. I’ll just water the flowerpot and go.”

Seo Dae-ryong watered the flowerpot by the window.

“Now that number one has returned, you are number two from now on.”

“What are you talking about?”

“There are sorrows that number one can never know.”

Just then, Seo Dae-ryong, who had been looking out the window absentmindedly, gasped in surprise.

When I went over and looked, someone was entering Hwangcheon Pavilion. Just as the Extreme Evil Smiling Demon had once crossed that training ground, another Demon Supreme was walking in.

Everyone could tell who it was. The wine bottle dangling at his waist revealed its owner.

Astonishingly, the Drunken Demon had entered Hwangcheon Pavilion. A woman followed behind him, and she was none other than Yeo Bin, one of Daechewrim’s Three Great Drunkards, who had given me a ride on the ferry.

At the Drunken Demon’s appearance, the investigators and enforcement warriors passing by held their breath.

Ever since I became the Lord of Hwangcheon Pavilion, Demon Supremes I had truly never seen here before had been appearing far too often.

Seo Dae-ryong looked at me and asked,

“How is it that whenever you go to meet Demon Supremes, they come looking for you here? Just what did you do yesterday?”

“I suppose the alcohol he drank yesterday wasn’t enough.”

“He’s staggering as he walks over here, though?”

Indeed, even from a distance, it was obvious he was drunk. The Drunken Demon truly remained drunk all day long.

“Go greet our guests.”

A little while later, guided by Seo Dae-ryong, the Drunken Demon and Yeo Bin entered.

“Welcome, Lord Drunken Demon.”

“We meet again, our Second Young Master.”

He even used the word our as he greeted me familiarly.

“After drinking together like that yesterday, shouldn’t at least a few days pass before we meet again?”

“Today, I came on official business.”

“Have you come to meet Doho, by any chance?”

“That’s right. He may have caused trouble, but isn’t he still my man? I should see him once before sending him to the dungeon.”

It was a reason for his visit I truly had not expected.

“I won’t be the one meeting him. This person here will.”

“Very well.”

I said to Seo Dae-ryong,

“Guide her to Doho.”

“Yes.”

Seo Dae-ryong took Yeo Bin and left.

When only the two of us remained, I said to the Drunken Demon,

“You seemed very drunk yesterday. Is your stomach all right?”

“It’s not all right. So let’s have a drink to cure the hangover.”

The Drunken Demon lifted his wine bottle and shook it.

“I’m still working, so I can’t.”

“Didn’t I tell you? Everything goes better if you do it after drinking. Drink, then throw them all into the dungeon!”

“Wasn’t it getting carried away like that that made Doho cause trouble? If only he had died, that would have been one thing, but the problem is that he’s perfectly fine while innocent people died.”

“What will happen to Doho?”

“He’ll have to be locked in the dungeon for a very long time. I intend to apply the maximum sentence.”

“It will be hard for him to live without alcohol.”

“Then he shouldn’t have caused trouble. But did you really come here yourself because of Doho?”

“That was just an excuse. Have you forgotten? We agreed to address each other as brothers.”

From yesterday until now, he had used that talk of brotherhood three times. Even the look he gave me now was quite serious. Serious enough that one might believe he truly meant it. But I did not believe the Drunken Demon’s words, nor that look in his eyes.

The drunkards I had experienced were more earnest and sincere than anyone when they were drunk. To the point that I would wonder why they were going so far with what they said.

But once the drinking party ended, I realized those words had not been all that important. There had been many lies, and plenty of bluster. Because their insides were empty, I had often felt that they were adorned with all manner of stories.

Drunken Demon, what about you? Are you the same sort of person as them? Are you truly someone standing precariously at the edge of that hollow emptiness? Are you truly nothing more than the final chief of those drunkards? If not… why are you so drunk?

“Why do you want to become close to me?”

“It is less that I like you, and more that I dislike the First Young Master. He broke my wine bottle and sent it back, so he must pay the price.”

“Why do you think he broke the wine bottle and sent it back? He must have expected that you would be offended like this.”

“How would I know what is in his mind? Perhaps I would if we shared a drink.”

“Do you come to know someone if you drink with them?”

“I do.”

“What kind of person am I?”

He looked at me, but his eyes were drunk, so it was not easy to read his thoughts.

“That judgment has no meaning. You yesterday and you now are different people.”

“How does a person change in a single day? The me then and the me now are the same person.”

“Are you truly certain of that? Does the Second Young Master then look at me with the same heart as the Second Young Master now?”

I could not answer that question. Since I had heard from the Blood Heaven Blade Demon that the Drunken Demon was a sinister fellow, strictly speaking, I could not be looking at him with the same heart.

“That is why I am saying we should drink again today. Another us, drinking on another day. Let us create another history.”

Fine. If fate was pushing me so forcefully into a drinking party with you, then fine. Let’s drink again.

“I’ll be drinking at the Pungryu Tavern this evening. Come there. But several people will gather on my side, so bring the female martial artist who came with you earlier.”

I intended to learn about the Drunken Demon through Yeo Bin. I also meant to keep the Drunken Demon from taking control of the atmosphere alone.

“Good. I shall bring her.”

Once the drinking appointment was set, the Drunken Demon truly looked pleased. Was it the alcohol he liked? Or was it drinking with me? He was impossible to know.

Just then, the door opened, and Seo Dae-ryong and Yeo Bin returned.

The Drunken Demon rose from his seat.

“Then I’ll see you later.”

“I’ll tell you in advance, there is nowhere to swim at the Pungryu Tavern.”

I was telling him not to act out while drunk, but the Drunken Demon said he would swim even if he had to fall into a wine cup, and left the office while miming swimming.

After the two left, Seo Dae-ryong asked urgently,

“What does that mean?”

“I told him to come to the Pungryu Tavern. And to bring Yeo Bin with him.”

“No, why?”

“He said he wanted to drink.”

“Aren’t you worried about us, Pavilion Lord?”

“Who should I worry about? Lord Ma? The Blood Heaven Blade Demon’s disciple? My guard Ian? Who should I be worried for?”

“When you put it that way… there really isn’t anyone to worry about.”

“Are you scared? If you’re scared, you don’t have to come.”

Seo Dae-ryong sighed.

“As you know, I was someone who would never have fallen for such provocation. If it were the old me, I would have backed up all the way to that door, then waved and told you to have a good trip. My liver was the size of a bean, after all.”

“And now?”

“That bean has swollen up and is sticking out of my belly. Even so, it’s only the size of a walnut, but right now I feel as if I could even sit across from the Drunken Demon and drink with him. When confidence in martial arts gives rise to this kind of clumsy arrogance, that’s exactly the time one is best suited to die, isn’t it?”

“You already know everything, so there is nothing for me to teach you. You may descend the mountain now.”

“The old me would have taken that ‘descend the mountain’ you just mentioned as departing this world. I would have grabbed your trouser leg and said I couldn’t go down. But do you know what I’m thinking now? What’s so special about the martial world? What’s so special about the Drunken Demon? A man just has to face it head-on. Worst case, what more can happen than death? That’s the insane thought that comes to mind first. Please stop me. Have I fallen into qi deviation?”

Though he seemed to be joking, Seo Dae-ryong was guarding himself against his own arrogance. This was why intelligent people survived longer.

“What did Yeo Bin and Doho talk about in the interrogation room?”

“They didn’t say much. The female martial artist asked if she could give Doho a cup of alcohol, so after checking whether it was poisoned, we allowed it.”

Was it a custom of Daechewrim? Or was the liquor of some other significance?

It was at that very moment.

An urgent report came from outside.

“Pavilion Lord, Doho has killed himself.”

Startled, I ran to the interrogation room with Seo Dae-ryong. Doho lay collapsed on the floor, his head smashed.

“He rammed his head into the wall and killed himself.”

At the report from the subordinate who had been guarding the place, Seo Dae-ryong blamed himself.

“Should I have stopped her from giving him the alcohol?”

I shook my head.

“It wasn’t because of the alcohol. Yeo Bin must have sent him a sound transmission telling him to die.”

“Why on earth would she order him to die? He was going to suffer for a long time in the dungeon anyway.”

Suddenly, I remembered what the Drunken Demon had said.

It will be hard for him to live without alcohol.

Did you really kill him for that reason? To lessen your subordinate’s suffering?

“Will you still come tonight?”

Seo Dae-ryong looked angry. It was probably because he felt as if he had guided the Grim Reaper to Doho.

“I’m going. What’s so special about the martial world? What’s so special about the Drunken Demon? Worst case, what more can happen than death? Don’t try to stop me.”

And this was why intelligent people sometimes died sooner.

“I think you’re in qi deviation.”

* * *

Before going to the Pungryu Tavern, I went to see my elder brother.

When I entered his office, he was in the middle of work.

Being of the Heavenly Demon’s bloodline did not mean one could simply laze around and enjoy life. Just as I had taken charge of the duties of the Hwangcheon Pavilion Lord, my brother too was handling work handed down from the Heavenly Demon Hall.

The enormous organization known as the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult required countless people to carry out their duties in their respective positions, and my brother was better than anyone at managing and operating an organization.

Even though he knew I had entered the room, Geom Muyang did not lift his head from the heaps of documents piled up before him.

“Have you been well? I assume those documents piled up there don’t say that when someone comes in, you should greet them first?”

Geom Muyang ignored me and focused only on his work.

“Fine. I know you’re someone who can do several things at once, so listen while you work. Why did you reject the Drunken Demon? Even sending him a broken wine bottle?”

For an instant, Geom Muyang’s hand stopped, then began moving again.

“I filled the wine bottle he sent with alcohol and went to see him. He was extremely pleased with me, you know? And very angry with you.”

As I spoke, I carefully observed my brother’s reaction. Using the Divine Eye Art, I watched the responses of his face and body. He probably thought he showed no particular reaction, but there were extremely subtle ones.

“Do you know what the Drunken Demon said about you?”

At that, Geom Muyang raised his head and looked at me.

“So you’re curious about that.”

“What did he say?”

“Meet him yourself and ask. Ah, will he refuse to meet you?”

I saw Geom Muyang’s lips twitch ever so slightly. I realized he had something to say, but was holding it back.

‘There’s no reason for my brother to reject the Drunken Demon. Even if there were a reason, he would have endured it and accepted him anyway. There is definitely something behind this matter.’

Geom Muyang’s reaction made me even more certain.

“Thanks to you, I obtained the Drunken Demon for free. He came looking for me today too. Said he wanted to become close. We even agreed to drink together tonight.”

Geom Muyang’s gaze returned to the documents.

“If you’ve said all you came to say, leave.”

If I had come like this to tease him, my brother would normally have made at least one cold remark. But he held back until the end. As if, should he say even a single word, a wine bottle would fall and shatter.

Just seeing that reaction was enough to accomplish the purpose of today’s visit.

* * *

A short while after Geom Mugeuk left, the Demon Buddha entered.

“I heard the Second Young Master came by.”

“He came to probe me. He wanted to know why we rejected the Drunken Demon.”

“Did he know? About the wine bottle?”

“No, it seemed he did not.”

Then an astonishing statement flowed from the Demon Buddha’s mouth.

“Then the Second Young Master must continue not to know. That it was not we who rejected him, but the Drunken Demon who rejected us.”

The one who had originally sent the broken wine bottle was not Geom Muyang, but the Drunken Demon. The empty wine bottle the Drunken Demon had sent to Geom Muyang had been broken.

Geom Muyang had taken it as a sign of rejection, that the Drunken Demon would not join hands with him.

“Why did the Drunken Demon choose Mugeuk? He could have gone back and forth between Mugeuk and me, weighing us against each other.”

“I cannot know that either.”

“We must uncover the reason and make the Drunken Demon our man.”

“There is no need to hurry.”

The Demon Buddha, rather, considered this an opportunity.

“The Drunken Demon is an unpredictable man. Once he begins stirring things up, even the Second Young Master will not be able to deal with him easily. On top of that, the Blood Heaven Blade Demon and the Extreme Evil Smiling Demon dislike each other, but they dislike the Drunken Demon even more. In the end, the Demon Supremes the Second Young Master has gathered with such care will split apart. If he gains the Drunken Demon, he will lose one of the other Demon Supremes. Perhaps he may even lose all three.”

The Demon Buddha’s eyes shone with conviction as bright as his golden body.

“This is, rather, an opportunity to overturn an unfavorable board.”

Geom Muyang held back what he wanted to say.

‘And what will you do if the Drunken Demon simply ends up joining Mugeuk without any trouble?’

But in any case, from the moment he received the broken wine bottle, he had had no choice.

He could only hope that the Drunken Demon’s drunken antics would be even greater than they expected.

Chapter 132: Today the drinks are on me.
----------------------------------------
The owner of Pungryu Tavern, Jo Chunbae, welcomed me. He was so glad to see me, as if a dead ancestor had come back to life, that I almost felt sorry for him.

“You truly have returned at the perfect time. You have no idea how much I’ve wanted to see you.”

“Owner, have you made a lot of money in the meantime?”

“I have indeed. Thanks to you, Pavilion Lord, business has been good.”

Thanks to the Hwangcheon Pavilion’s small branch, which I had thought would only last for a while before disappearing, Maga Village had been cleansed considerably. In particular, martial artists who got drunk and caused trouble or left without paying had nearly vanished, making business far easier.

“You should prepare plenty of wine today.”

“Because it’s a reunion after so long?”

“That’s not why. A new drinker will be coming today.”

“A drinker?”

“You’ll see later.”

I went up to the second floor.

Only Seo Daeryong and Jang Ho were there. Jang Ho sprang to his feet and greeted me.

“It has been a long time.”

“Have you been well?”

I gripped his thick hand firmly.

“The Demon Army has settled into place now, so the work is easier than before. If there is anything you wish to entrust to me, please say so at any time.”

The look in Jang Ho’s eyes as he gazed at me had not changed.

Even if I came back after a year, or even after ten years, that look would probably remain the same.

A person who repaid one favor with ten, and even after repaying ten still felt he had not repaid enough—that was exactly the kind of man Jang Ho was.

That was why I would protect this noble loyalty well. I wanted to raise him into a towering tree and rest beneath its dense shade. I had not the slightest intention of sitting on a lonely stump and merely reminiscing about the past.

After exchanging greetings with Jang Ho, Seo Daeryong spoke.

“I told Troop Lord Jang and Martial Artist Lee who would be coming later. A little while ago, Martial Artist Lee said she had urgent business and left.”

Even without being told, I knew why she had excused herself. Since I was here, nothing would happen, but she must have been worried about even the slightest possibility. For now, her consideration for me was still greater than her desire to enjoy herself. She was probably sweating in the training hall right about now.

“And I also told Troop Lord Jang about what happened earlier in the day.”

I could still feel heat radiating from Seo Daeryong. Hotter than before, even. Emotions left unresolved had a way of growing inside the heart.

“You told me to drink and make a drunken scene, didn’t you? You should let out all your feelings today too.”

“What if I die doing that?”

“Then Troop Lord Jang here will become the second-in-command.”

With a faint smile, Jang Ho filled Seo Daeryong’s cup to the brim. I knew that wordless support was a great source of strength for Seo Daeryong. The reason Seo Daeryong took Jang Ho as a model was probably because that aspect of Jang Ho’s character was so appealing.

After we all emptied a cup to celebrate our reunion, I said the one thing Seo Daeryong needed to hear.

“It’s natural to be afraid. If you’re scared, then be scared. You have to fear what is frightening for your instincts to work properly. You need to know exactly what you’re afraid of, when and how you react, and where your limits are.”

As we were talking about this and that, two people entered the tavern. It was the Drunken Demon and Yeo Bin.

Jo Chunbae, recognizing who the guest was, was truly astonished. And he was happier than he would have been at the visit of any Demon Venerable. As a man who sold wine, welcoming the Drunken Demon as a guest meant more to him than mere honor, and his joy showed it.

“It is an honor that you have come, Great Drunken Demon!”

He bowed at the waist.

When the surrounding patrons realized that the guest who had entered was the Drunken Demon, they held their breath.

The Drunken Demon swept his gaze around and said,

“You are the people I like most in all the world. Drink to your hearts’ content! Today’s wine is on me.”

At his words, the drinkers erupted in cheers.

Watching from the second floor, I shouted,

“So handsome! The most handsome of all the Demon Venerables!”

The Drunken Demon looked up at me and laughed.

“Give that table twice the wine and food!”

At those words, shouts of “handsome” poured out from every direction.

“That table too! And that one! Give it to them all!”

Applause burst forth, and the drinkers cheered. The distance between a Demon Venerable and the drinkers of Pungryu Tavern, a gulf as vast as a sheer cliff ten thousand zhang high, was closed by the Drunken Demon in an instant.

The Drunken Demon even went to a table on the path leading to the second floor and poured wine for them. The drinker received it with trembling hands, but it was a moment that would become a lifelong boast. Envious exclamations and cheers rose from all around.

Truly, seeing how he blended in with the drinkers in the blink of an eye, I thought: the Drunken Demon was the Drunken Demon indeed.

After sweeping through the first floor in a commotion, the Drunken Demon came up to the second floor.

“Second Young Master, I’m here.”

“Welcome.”

As promised, the Drunken Demon had brought Yeo Bin with him.

“This is my subordinate, and one of the Three Great Drunkards of the Great Drunken Forest, Yeo Bin.”

Seo Daeryong and Jang Ho greeted the Drunken Demon and Yeo Bin.

After those greetings, we sat together at one table.

“I heard that the Second Young Master’s guard had grown beautiful, but I don’t see her today. What a pity. I was going to show her this handsome face!”

The Drunken Demon knew a great deal about me. I thought perhaps he might also know a great deal about Seo Daeryong and Jang Ho.

“It seems you truly are prejudice itself, Drunken Demon.”

“What do you mean?”

“People usually think those who like wine won’t be well-informed, don’t they?”

“Since I spend so much time drunk, I have to be even better informed!”

“You’re right, but most drinkers don’t live that way.”

A thought suddenly came to me. If I peeled away his prejudices one layer at a time, perhaps only then would I begin to see the person called the Drunken Demon.

“Martial Artist Yeo, can you hold your liquor?”

“Yes, I like drinking.”

She answered my question briefly. She was a little stiffer than when she had welcomed me at the Great Drunken Forest. It seemed she was being cautious because she was in front of the Drunken Demon.

I glanced at Seo Daeryong. He was tense, watching the Drunken Demon and Yeo Bin’s reactions.

I sent Seo Daeryong a voice transmission.

—When are you going to step forward?

—Is there any reason to ruin the mood right from the start of a drinking party?

—If you’re going to do it, do it now. If you say it after getting drunk, you really might die.

—Please don’t pour wine over my grave. Just plant the flowers I’m raising.

I did not know whether Seo Daeryong would hold himself back or end up speaking in a drunken impulse. Whether he endured it or brought it up, I believed it would become a helpful experience for him.

Just then, Jo Chunbae came up with wine. It was a bottle I had never seen before.

“This is wine I specially saved so that I might offer it to Lord Drunken Demon if he ever visited our tavern.”

At that, the Drunken Demon smelled it and widened his eyes.

“Fenghuang Dong Wine?”

“Oh! You recognize it by scent alone! Truly amazing.”

“How could I not know a peerless famed wine brewed from over two hundred medicinal herbs? How did you acquire something so precious?”

“I once worked at a brewery in Guizhou. When we parted, the master who taught me gave it to me as a gift.”

“Is it all right to give such precious wine to me?”

“I am a man who sells wine in the front yard of the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult. Who else would I give this wine to? I have been waiting for this day.”

“Thank you.”

I looked at Jo Chunbae and shouted, pretending to be sulky.

“Owner! I came here so often, and you never gave it to me!”

Jo Chunbae smiled and said,

“This is wine for someone who truly understands wine.”

It seemed that, at least when it came to this wine, he had kept it aside for the Drunken Demon, with whom he was bound to cross paths one day. Then he expressed his gratitude to me.

“Thanks to you, Pavilion Lord, I have fulfilled a lifelong wish. Thank you.”

The Drunken Demon poured the Fenghuang Dong Wine and filled a cup for each of us. He even poured a cup for Jo Chunbae, who repeatedly refused.

“Take the remaining wine downstairs and share a little with everyone.”

The moment I heard those words, I was inwardly moved by the Drunken Demon. I did not know what kind of person he was, but at the very least, I thought the heart that so readily shared such precious wine with other drinkers was remarkable.

“Thank you. Everyone will be delighted.”

Sure enough, cheers erupted from downstairs a short while later.

“Now, let us drink as well!”

The Drunken Demon raised his cup, and everyone in the tavern toasted together.

Whether because it was a famed wine, or because of the atmosphere, the taste was superb.

“Thanks to you, I’ve had good wine.”

“How is it thanks to me? It’s thanks to you, Second Young Master.”

“What do you mean?”

“If not for you, I would never have had occasion to come to this tavern. So in the end, it is thanks to you.”

He knew how to make people feel good. The reason I did not often use my Divine Art of Flattery on him was because he made me feel good first.

When the Drunken Demon had drunk three cups in succession, I said,

“Please have some food with it.”

The moment my words ended, the chopsticks in front of him rose slightly into the air. I had lifted them with Void Grasp.

Then the chopsticks descended again. This time, the Drunken Demon had lowered them again with Void Grasp.

The chopsticks rose and fell, and our contest of internal energy began.

Seo Daeryong, Jang Ho, and Yeo Bin watched the situation with faintly tense expressions.

The Drunken Demon’s internal energy was stronger than I had expected. Of course, I could have defeated him with internal energy, but I yielded and withdrew at a certain point.

The chopsticks were set down on the table.

“Second Young Master, your internal energy is impressive.”

“Didn’t you say you grow three-tenths stronger when you drink?”

After drinking the wine before me, I lifted the chopsticks again.

This time, the Drunken Demon did not block them with his internal energy. As if admitting defeat, he picked up the chopsticks and ate some food.

“Is that enough?”

“It is.”

“Last time too, and now again. Why is a manly man so obsessed with side dishes?”

“This isn’t a question of men or women. Nor is it a question of being a great man or a petty one. It’s simply because of health.”

“If I eat food with it, I end up drinking less wine.”

“That’s exactly why I’m telling you to eat it. At the very least, for every three cups of wine, please have a bite of food.”

In that moment, I saw it. Yeo Bin slightly bowed her head toward me. It was an expression of thanks. From that gesture, I could feel that she truly cared for the Drunken Demon.

The Drunken Demon asked me,

“Why do you care for my health so much?”

After a brief pause, I said to him,

“Because I don’t yet know what kind of relationship I’ll have with you, Drunken Demon. What if, by some chance, we end up on good terms, and then you die of drink?”

“What? Me? Die of drink?”

He laughed louder than he ever had since meeting me, but I did not laugh.

“You really are a strange man.”

“In what way?”

“In my entire life, you are the only person who has ever worried that I might fall ill from drink. No one I meet from now on will ever worry about such a thing either.”

“There must be others around you besides me. They simply can’t say it because they find you difficult to approach.”

“Fine, let’s say that’s so. Then what if we become enemies? Wouldn’t it be better if I died early from drink?”

“I wouldn’t want to see you using that as an excuse. If you die, you should die by my sword. You said we’d be enemies, didn’t you?”

The somewhat violent words froze the atmosphere for a moment, but the Drunken Demon was not offended in the slightest. Instead, he laughed and drank. Then, as if to show me, he ate some food. Is that enough? Yes!

The atmosphere at the drinking table grew even better. As we drank several more cups, Seo Daeryong sent me a voice transmission.

—From now on, if you mock me as a coward, I’ll admit it. Add “coward” to the words you use to introduce me.

He had decided to hold back. It could not have been easy to step forward and throw cold water over this cheerful atmosphere led by the Drunken Demon.

—I keep thinking only of reasons why I shouldn’t be brave. In any case, a bastard who deserved to die died. I wonder whether this is really something worth stepping forward over. Martial Artist Yeo was only following orders too. If I’m going to argue, I should argue with the Drunken Demon, but if I do that, I think I might die.

—Hey, coward.

Seo Daeryong, who had been looking down at his cup, raised his head and looked at me.

I held out my cup. What need was there for words at a time like this? People grew by going through this thing and that.

Seo Daeryong smiled faintly and clinked his cup against mine.

Watching us, the Drunken Demon said to Seo Daeryong,

“You’re the disciple of the Blood Heaven Blade Demon, aren’t you?”

“Yes, that’s right.”

He was not on good terms with the Blood Heaven Blade Demon. How would he respond? He might make a sarcastic remark about the Blood Heaven Blade Demon or mock Seo Daeryong.

But the words that came out overturned such expectations.

“I like your feel.”

“Pardon?”

“As a martial artist, you have a good feel about you. Do well from here on.”

“Ah, yes. Thank you.”

Seo Daeryong, who had been bracing himself for what might be said, was flustered. He had not expected the Drunken Demon to give him such praise. Still, perhaps because praise felt good, his expression brightened.

He was grinning foolishly as he drank when our eyes met, and an embarrassed voice transmission came flying in.

—Yes, I’m that kind of bastard! From now on, after my master, the Demon Venerable I like best is the Drunken Demon. That’s the kind of bastard I am! What am I supposed to do when I like it?

—I didn’t say anything.

We drank again. As expected, drinking with the Drunken Demon loosened the heart. Seo Daeryong, of course, and even Jang Ho were pleasantly drinking while chatting about this and that with Yeo Bin.

One must not forget. The Drunken Demon was a man who knew how to change the atmosphere of a drinking party with a single joke or gesture, and we were sitting at that drinking party. One must not forget that he was a man who had built his history through wine.

Then, when we were laughing and talking and the mood was at its very best, the Drunken Demon asked me,

“Well then, what do you say? Shall we call each other brothers now?”

At his words, the gazes of the three people present turned toward me and the Drunken Demon. A Demon Venerable proposing brotherhood to the blood of the Heavenly Demon was not something to be taken lightly.

Drunken Demon, with what heart are you doing this to me? Is it the empty sincerity of a drunkard that will vanish when the wine wears off? Or is it a thoroughly calculated plan?

At that moment, the raucous tavern fell silent.

Looking down, I saw someone entering the tavern.

A person even I had not expected to come here, someone exuding a cold aura as he entered with a great blade at his waist—it was the Blood Heaven Blade Demon.

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon slowly made his way up to the second floor, sat down between me and the Drunken Demon, and said,

“Give me a cup of wine too.”

Chapter 133: People Don't Share My Feelings.
--------------------------------------------
The atmosphere changed in an instant with the appearance of the Blood Heaven Blade Demon.

Seo Daeryong, Jang Ho, and Yeobin rose from their seats and respectfully cupped their fists.

“We greet the Demon Venerable.”

I poured the Blood Heaven Blade Demon a drink.

“How did you know to come here?”

“I was passing by and stopped in.”

As if that could be true. He must have been watching my movements and come here after hearing that I was drinking with the Drunken Demon.

He had come for my sake. Worried that I might be swayed by the Drunken Demon. The reason he had been training so diligently lately was likely an extension of the same concern.

After drinking the wine I had poured him, the Blood Heaven Blade Demon said to Seo Daeryong, Jang Ho, and Yeobin,

“You lot, step outside for a while.”

Whose order was this that they could refuse it? The three of them bowed politely and went out.

Now only the Blood Heaven Blade Demon, the Drunken Demon, and I remained there.

The Drunken Demon greeted him first.

“It has been a long time, Senior.”

He greeted him with an amiable smile, but the Blood Heaven Blade Demon looked at him with displeasure in his eyes.

“I’ll dispense with the formalities and get to the point. Withdraw.”

“What do you mean?”

“I mean withdraw from the Second Young Master.”

“I have merely been drinking with the Second Young Master.”

“I’m telling you not to do that.”

“You are telling the Drunken Demon not to drink?”

“Drink with someone else.”
