The Drunken Demon laughed and drank. The Blood Heaven Blade Demon still looked at him with cold eyes. He had no close acquaintances even among the Demon Venerables, and there was no way he would look favorably upon the Drunken Demon, whom he considered sinister.

“Now, calm your temper and have a cup from me.”

The Drunken Demon filled the Blood Heaven Blade Demon’s cup. Even in front of the Blood Heaven Blade Demon, he was not intimidated in the slightest.

“I respect you, Senior, but I do think this is going a little too far.”

“I thought a man drunk every day would have no Dao to speak of.”

“I have the Dao of Wine. Senior, please do not hate me too much.”

He smiled as he said it, with the face of a good-natured man, but the Blood Heaven Blade Demon had come today with his mind made up.

“Distance yourself from the Second Young Master. Then I will have no reason to interfere in your life.”

At that, the Drunken Demon picked up his cup, rose from his seat, and sat down in the place farthest from me.

“Will this much distance suffice?”

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon’s expression hardened at once.

“Do I look as though I’m joking right now?”

“No. You spoke so seriously that I made a little joke. My apologies.”

As he said that, the Drunken Demon looked at me. His eyes seemed to be asking if I had nothing to say at this point, but I said nothing.

The Drunken Demon’s gaze turned back to the Blood Heaven Blade Demon.

“Senior, you were not this sort of person, were you? Were you someone who immersed yourself this deeply in another?”

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon snorted with a look of contempt.

“Aren’t you the one who has no interest in others?”

“Me?”

“Were you not a man who cared only for his own drunken mood?”

“That’s right. I am that kind of selfish bastard.”

The Drunken Demon drank and set his cup down. The sound it made was louder than before.

Before I knew it, the air around us had turned cold. The auras that naturally flowed from them reflected the mood of the two men.

“Stop drinking and answer with that mouth of yours. Say that you will no longer meet the Second Young Master.”

But the Drunken Demon drank again. Beyond the cup, his expression had hardened. Having only ever seen him laughing and chattering, the sight of his serious face felt unfamiliar.

“I refuse.”

At the Drunken Demon’s refusal, demonic qi erupted from the Blood Heaven Blade Demon’s body. At the same time, demonic qi also surged from the Drunken Demon’s body. When the Blood Heaven Blade Demon’s demonic qi and the Drunken Demon’s demonic qi mingled, an energy I had never felt in my life swept over us.

“Are you doing this because you trust the unwritten rule that Demon Venerables must never fight one another?”

“Are you saying you will break that unwritten rule?”

“Is there any reason I cannot?”

“Good. I have always preferred being on the side that breaks things. Surely you do not think I fear the breaking of an unwritten rule?”

“I wonder if you will remain so relaxed when your wine gourd breaks.”

The demonic qi of the two men grew even denser. Though it thickened by the moment, I did not stop them. I merely protected my body with the Heavenly Demon Protection Art and quietly watched.

“Fine. Then let me at least ask the reason. Why are you stopping me?”

“Because you will be an obstacle on the path the Second Young Master walks.”

“Not because I will be an obstacle to you, Senior?”

The corners of the Blood Heaven Blade Demon’s mouth curled upward coldly. His momentum was no less than that of the relatively younger Drunken Demon, but the problem was that the Drunken Demon knew his weakness well.

“A few days ago, I drank with the Sword Venerable.”

When the Ilhwa Sword Venerable was mentioned, irritation surged over the Blood Heaven Blade Demon’s face.

“She was doing well. Still beautiful, still dignified. We spoke about the last Eight Demon Assembly, and about this and that. Ah, she did not mention you once, Senior.”

Flames blazed in the Blood Heaven Blade Demon’s eyes. It was a precarious situation, as though he truly might swing his Heaven-Destroying Great Saber at any moment.

The Drunken Demon’s unique weapon was the wine gourd shaped like a bottle hanging at his waist. That wine gourd, said to be impossible to cut even with a treasured sword, was named Blood Tears. I did not know why he had given it such a name, but one thing was certain: when its stopper was opened, bloody tears would flow from someone’s eyes.

Even before my regression, I had never seen the Heaven-Destroying Great Saber or Blood Tears used. What would happen if those two truly clashed?

Even in this hair-trigger situation, the Drunken Demon continued to provoke the Blood Heaven Blade Demon.

“You told me to withdraw? Are you not the one who should withdraw, Senior? So long as you remain with the Second Young Master, the Sword Venerable will not help him. But if it is me, the Sword Venerable would gladly help the Second Young Master. What do you think? Do you believe you are better than the two of us combined?”

Now that he had dragged the Sword Venerable into it, the Blood Heaven Blade Demon could not offer a rebuttal. The Ilhwa Sword Venerable was his weakness.

Moreover, the Demon Venerables were all similar in ability, so it was not easy to boast that he could fulfill the roles of two people by himself.

At that moment, the one who answered was me.

“He is better.”

The Drunken Demon and the Blood Heaven Blade Demon looked at me.

“Elder Blood Heaven Blade Demon is better than the two of you combined.”

My eyes met the Blood Heaven Blade Demon’s as he looked at me. What flickered in those small, sharp eyes was gratitude, and emotion.

The Drunken Demon smiled and drank the wine before him.

“Could you say that even if the Sword Venerable were here?”

“I could.”

“Could you say that even if the Extreme Evil Soma were here?”

The Drunken Demon knew that I had shared a deep emotional exchange with the Extreme Evil Soma. As expected, his information was quick, and he understood the situation better than anyone.

“I could.”

“You boast because they are not here.”

The Drunken Demon snorted, rose, and went to the window. He shouted to Yeobin, who was waiting across the street.

“Go and bring the Ilhwa Sword Venerable and the Extreme Evil Soma.”

“Yes.”

Yeobin shot away and vanished in an instant.

The Drunken Demon returned to his seat.

“Must I really go this far? I even offered to become sworn brothers with you, yet are you not being too much?”

“I am grateful that you regard me favorably, but I do not become sworn brothers with someone after drinking with him only twice. If you wish to do so, get in line. Your turn has not yet come, Drunken Demon.”

Perhaps I might become far closer to the Drunken Demon than I was now. Perhaps the Drunken Demon might become of great help in the grand undertaking to come.

Even so, the Blood Heaven Blade Demon came first.

I had diligently knocked on the door to his heart, and he had willingly opened it to me.

No matter how dazzlingly the Drunken Demon tried to bewitch me or how many good words he offered, they could not compare to my gratitude toward the man who had struggled to open that massive door, a door that had been shut for so long it had rusted and grown stiff.

Then the Blood Heaven Blade Demon sent me a sound transmission.

—When the Sword Venerable comes later, say that the Sword Venerable is more important.

—I refuse.

—Even if you say that, I will take your side.

That cantankerous old man who had poked me in the side and spoken of misfortune had, before I knew it, come this far. A man who was as good as dead without his pride was trying to cast it aside because of me.

—I know you would.

—Then why refuse?

—I do not want you to be humiliated in front of others, Elder.

—I understand. I know your feelings now, so do as I say. The greater cause is more important than personal feelings. You know that, don’t you?

How could I put someone else first when he was like this?

—That is precisely why I cannot.

—Why?

—Because the Ilhwa Sword Venerable will notice that I am lying. You know, do you not? She is quick-witted. Even for the sake of the greater cause, it is better to speak honestly.

To that, the Blood Heaven Blade Demon could say nothing.

By mentioning the Ilhwa Sword Venerable, the Drunken Demon had made this seat deeply uncomfortable for him.

He must have wanted to storm out several times, yet he remained seated for my sake. He must have thought he had to settle this matter somehow.

The first to arrive was the Ilhwa Sword Venerable.

She must already have asked Yeobin who was here, for she showed no emotion at all even when she saw the Blood Heaven Blade Demon.

“Welcome, Senior.”

“Second Young Master, why are you drinking with people who are of no help to your life?”

At her joke, the Drunken Demon laughed and said,

“Friend, how can you say something so hurtful?”

By calling her friend, the Drunken Demon flaunted his closeness with her. The Ilhwa Sword Venerable had described him merely as easygoing, yet the Drunken Demon called her friend.

It was also a moment that made me realize anew that when the Demon Venerables explained one another to me, they did not tell me everything.

“Stop drinking so much.”

“Your sword training and my drinking are one and the same.”

“Then you should drink alone too. Since training is also done alone.”

“Drinking with the Second Young Master is far too enjoyable.”

“That much I acknowledge. But why did you call me, when I cannot drink well and am not entertaining either?”

At that, the Drunken Demon looked at the Blood Heaven Blade Demon and said,

“Senior Blade Demon was trying to discipline his junior. And you have Senior Blade Demon firmly under your thumb, don’t you?”

Ordinarily, he would have pushed me to make a choice in front of the Sword Venerable, but the Drunken Demon did not.

He was clever, and he knew me well. He knew that doing so would only hurt my feelings and bring him no benefit.

He also knew. He knew that just this much would be enough for the Blood Heaven Blade Demon to step forward first. Especially when it came to the Ilhwa Sword Venerable, he reacted all the more stiffly.

“I told the Drunken Demon to withdraw from the Second Young Master.”

At the Blood Heaven Blade Demon’s words, the Ilhwa Sword Venerable responded coldly.

“By what authority?”

“I have no authority. It is simply my intuition that he will not be of help to the Second Young Master.”

“I have already confirmed that your intuition is dreadful.”

“You know nothing about me.”

“I do not wish to know.”

There had been a time when, after seeing each other often, the edge between them had lessened somewhat, but perhaps because the Drunken Demon was here, they reacted irritably to one another again.

The Drunken Demon once more struck at the Blood Heaven Blade Demon’s weakness.

“Good. Let us suppose the Second Young Master chooses you, Senior, and the Sword Venerable and I withdraw. Then are you close with the remaining Extreme Evil Soma?”

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon could make no reply. He was not close to anyone.

I did not step forward. I knew well that if I clumsily took his side at a time like this, it would only inflame the Blood Heaven Blade Demon’s anger further.

The Drunken Demon was striking precisely at the Blood Heaven Blade Demon’s weakness. I could not blame him alone. The one who had barged into this gathering today was the Blood Heaven Blade Demon.

The Drunken Demon said to me with drunken eyes,

“Now you will hate me. Since I have driven Senior Blade Demon, whom you like, into such a corner. Understand me. I merely showed you reality.”

“I will not hate you.”

“Truly? Why?”

“Because I have no intention of sending either you or Senior Sword Venerable away. I will make all of you my people.”

For an instant, the Blood Heaven Blade Demon glared at me. But with his heart in turmoil, he could not stop me. He had been pushed into a situation where he had to do the work of three people all by himself.

“Even if you look at me like that, it cannot be helped. I will take all of them with me.”

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon shook his head.

“You harbor the illusion that if people make an effort, everyone can become close and harmonize. Perhaps you can. You certainly possess a tolerance that does not suit your age. But in the end, you too will come to know. That people are not as your heart wishes.”

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon looked at the Drunken Demon and the Sword Venerable, then turned his head toward the window. At the open window, the Extreme Evil Soma, wearing a white mask, was floating in the air with his arms crossed, watching what was happening inside. No one knew when he had arrived.

“They may pretend to harmonize on the surface because of you, but behind your back, they will fight, hate one another, and cause an uproar. Amid all that chaos, someone will be hurt, nurture hatred, and betray you at the decisive moment. Using the wound they received then as their reason.”

The other Demon Venerables also looked at me with eyes that agreed with those words.

“That may be so.”

“Then why?”

“If you ask why we must go together, I will answer you.”

I looked at the four Demon Venerables and released my presence. Until now, I had never properly shown them my presence.

But now, I showed them clearly what my presence was. It pushed away the presence they had created and took control of the surroundings.

It was not a display of strength. I imbued my presence with the greatest dignity I could muster.

Looking at them, I spoke in a low voice.

“Because from now on, I am your Heavenly Demon.”

At my words, they all flinched in surprise. They likely had never imagined I would say such a thing.

“This is the decision of the one who will become your Heavenly Demon in the future. Whoever I bring, accept them. So long as you do not kill one another, fight, hate, or betray as you please. But in exchange, just one thing!”

I looked at the four Demon Venerables and spoke as though making a proclamation.

“Do it within my fence.”

A brief silence flowed.

They were looking at me. Drunken eyes looked at me, and gentle eyes looked at me. Small, sharp eyes looked at me, and eyes behind a mask looked at me.

I hoped they would follow my will. I hoped someone would step forward first with a positive word.

Then the Demon Venerables spoke at the same time.

“I don’t want to.”

“No.”

“I refuse.”

“I won’t.”

At that moment, they were of one heart.

Unable to hold back, I burst into a small laugh. Good grief, my adorable and utterly dreadful Demon Venerables!

I stuck my head out and shouted loudly to Jo Chunbae on the first floor.

“Please prepare a new drinking table up here!”

Chapter 134: A Truly Villainous Duo.
------------------------------------
“Why don’t you like it?”

The Demon Venerables, who had been fighting so fiercely just moments ago, united as one.

The first to answer was the Drunken Demon.

“I can’t stand being shut up inside a fence and all that. Just thinking about a fence makes me feel suffocated.”

The one who took up his words was the Ilhwa Sword Venerable.

“You say I should accept anyone? That would be difficult. Even when going for a simple stroll, one must be of the same mind.”

I could tell who she had in mind when she said that.

Next, the Blood Heaven Blade Demon spoke.

“Did you not hear what I said? People’s hearts are different from my own!”

The last reason was given by the Extreme Evil Smiling Demon.

“Why must killing be forbidden?”

It was a joke about my words that they could fight and hate one another within my fence, as long as they did not kill.

Everyone’s reasons were a mix of jest and sincerity, but the surprising thing was that all four of them had entirely different reasons.

The Drunken Demon mocked me, imitating what I had said earlier.

“Did you see the Second Young Master putting on airs earlier? ‘This is the decision of the man who will become your Heavenly Demon in the future.’ The Cult Leader should have seen that.”

I waved my hands exaggeratedly.

“Please don’t say such dangerous things. Now then, since we’ve gathered like this, let’s have a drink today.”

The Extreme Evil Smiling Demon, who had been outside the window, came inside as well. This was the first time the Demon Venerables who had formed connections with me had gathered in one place like this.

It was truly a historic day, and that applied not only to me but also to the tavern owner.

Jo Chunbae, who brought over the wine and food, was the very picture of emotion.

“Are you that happy?”

“How could I not be? I must be the first tavern owner ever to experience something like this. I could die with no regrets.”

“This may only be the beginning. Who knows who will gather here to drink in the future?”

“Even now, I feel as if my heart is about to burst……”

Jo Chunbae bowed at the waist to the Demon Venerables.

“It is an honor to have you here. I will serve you with all my heart.”

After greeting them like that, he withdrew. On the stairs leading down to the first floor, he turned back to look this way for a moment, as if trying somehow to commit this glorious moment to memory.

“Now then, allow me to pour each of you a cup.”

The Drunken Demon poured wine for everyone.

Since the Extreme Evil Smiling Demon did not drink because of his mask anyway, he merely accepted the wine.

When the Blood Heaven Blade Demon received his wine, he set it aside and poured himself a drink into a new cup.

As if to show the Blood Heaven Blade Demon, the Ilhwa Sword Venerable drank the wine the Drunken Demon had poured for her.

They were the sort of people who created taut tension even with the simple act of pouring a cup of wine.

Once a round of drinks had gone around, the Extreme Evil Smiling Demon asked me,

“But why did you call me?”

He naturally assumed I had been the one to call him.

“Lord Smiling Demon, I was not the one who called you.”

“Then who called me?”

“The Drunken Demon did.”

The Drunken Demon looked at the Extreme Evil Smiling Demon and spoke politely.

“I called you because there is something I wanted to inform you of.”

Whatever they thought of each other inwardly, they were exceedingly courteous to one another.

“He said the Second Young Master values that senior Blade Demon over there more than us, so I called you to come and hear it for yourself.”

As the Drunken Demon poured wine into his cup, he said,

“We are nothing but side branches. The center of the Second Young Master’s heart is Senior Blood Heaven Blade Demon over there.”

“You may be a side branch, Drunken Demon, but I am not.”

“Is that truly so?”

As if he would confirm it himself, the Drunken Demon asked me,

“Second Young Master, if you had to choose only one between Lord Smiling Demon and Senior Blade Demon, whom would you choose?”

“Lord Smiling Demon.”

The Drunken Demon blinked, then asked again.

“That is not what you said earlier. Did you not say that even if the person himself were in front of you, you could say Senior Blade Demon was better?”

“I did? When?”

I opened my eyes wide and flatly denied it.

“How could you say such a thing, seeing the bond between Lord Smiling Demon and me? Lord Smiling Demon, do not be deceived. Right now, the rolling stone is scheming to dislodge the stone already embedded.”

At my words, the Drunken Demon instead laughed loudly as if amused and drank. Setting down his wine cup, he said,

“Do not look down on a rolling stone. I do not believe the length of time matters between people. There are people one cannot forget for a lifetime even after seeing them only once.”

I stared at him for a moment, then suddenly asked,

“Drunken Demon, did you truly have someone like that?”

The Drunken Demon’s hand, which had been about to bring the wine to his lips, stopped in midair.

“What do you mean?”

“I was wondering if such a person really exists. I cannot imagine someone you meet only once yet never forget for the rest of your life. Do all of you have someone like that?”

After meeting the eyes of each of the four Demon Venerables in turn, I continued.

“Even if you were close enough to die for one another, if enough years pass without seeing each other, you find you’ve become like strangers. Even when you think you’ve grown fairly close, if there is a gap, it becomes awkward, as though you have to start all over again from the beginning. That is how it is for me. Ah! I admit it if it is someone as beautiful as our Sword Venerable. Seeing someone like her and being unable to forget is understandable.”

The Drunken Demon emptied another cup of wine.

“It feels strange to hear someone as young as you say such things.”

“There you go, looking down on me again. You looked down on young stories, and you looked down on a young drunkard too.”

The Drunken Demon gazed at me with a peculiar look in his eyes. Since I remembered everything he had said like this, my words might have displeased him, yet at the same time, they must have made him feel good as well.

“Very well. I keep forgetting. That our Second Young Master’s youth is vastly different from ordinary youth.”

“Of course. Please do not forget that I am an old man in a young body.”

When I lifted my cup, the Drunken Demon toasted me.

With the Demon Venerables gathered, the atmosphere had become more rigid than at first, but even so, drinking with him was somehow enjoyable. He had the skill to lead the conversation so that it did not break off and to stir the pleasure of a drinking party.

“Now then, let me say it again. I choose the Extreme Evil Smiling Demon.”

As if to toast him, I raised my cup and drank alone.

The two eyes inside the eyeholes of his mask were smiling happily. Seeing that smile after so long put me in a good mood as well.

It is good to see you again.

Perhaps because of the bloody battle against Baek Manggi, I had developed an indescribable sense of camaraderie with the Extreme Evil Smiling Demon. This was an emotion separate from matters such as whether he was a Demon Venerable or I was a successor.

At that moment, the Blood Heaven Blade Demon sent me a voice transmission.

—Yes, you are doing well. Take care of the others first.

—Does your pride not hurt?

—Why would my pride be hurt over something like this? Just remember one thing. You must never trust the Extreme Evil one or the Drunken Demon.

The more I came to know them, the more I understood Father’s difficulties. Four of them were already this hard, yet he had to lead eight, and on top of that, handle the countless matters a Cult Leader must deal with. Father, it must truly have been difficult.

The atmosphere at the drinking table was good.

Since everyone had goodwill toward me, none of them went out of their way to reveal their prickliness or tempers here. Thankfully, all of them were holding themselves back well. To think I would have such an ordinary yet pleasant gathering with the Demon Venerables!

As expected, the one drinking the most was the Drunken Demon.

As if to show me, the Drunken Demon made sure to pick up some food after every three cups of wine he drank.

Our eyes met once then, and I nodded with a smile.

The Extreme Evil Smiling Demon watched that with a subtle look in his eyes.

I knew. Though not all the emotions contained in that gaze were jealousy, a part of it certainly was.

The one who had first pestered me to become friends was the Extreme Evil Smiling Demon. And now, the Drunken Demon was pestering me from that same position to become sworn brothers.

Two people who seemed similar, yet were completely different.

Would the day ever come when I could be together with the Extreme Evil Smiling Demon without his mask and the Drunken Demon when he was sober?

The Extreme Evil Smiling Demon had said that smelling the Drunken Demon’s alcohol made him feel unpleasant.

But in this place, he did not show that in the slightest. If he had not told me beforehand, I would never have known he was thinking such a thing. Only the courtesy between Demon Venerables passed back and forth between them.

“I heard that you left the cult with the Second Young Master and returned.”

“I did.”

“Then you must have learned a great deal about the Second Young Master.”

The Extreme Evil Smiling Demon glanced at me and said,

“The Second Young Master is someone far removed from you or me.”

“Very far?”

“Very far.”

After telling me that I was a villain like him, the Extreme Evil Smiling Demon was giving the Drunken Demon different information.

“If anyone, it is the Blade Demon who is the closest to the Second Young Master.”

At the Extreme Evil Smiling Demon’s words, the Drunken Demon showed an unexpected reaction.

“Senior Blade Demon? Not our Sword Venerable?”

Then the Extreme Evil Smiling Demon looked at the Ilhwa Sword Venerable and said something none of us had expected.

“Is the Sword Venerable not the same as us? If you were friends, you would have known.”

For an instant, silence fell.

The Ilhwa Sword Venerable’s expression stiffened slightly.

What had the Extreme Evil Smiling Demon seen in the Ilhwa Sword Venerable to say such a thing? Among the Eight Demon Venerables, the Ilhwa Sword Venerable was known as the one least like a Demon Venerable.

At that moment, the Blood Heaven Blade Demon burst out.

“What nonsense are you spouting! The Sword Venerable is entirely different from the likes of you.”

In the end, the one who stepped forward for her in such a situation was the Blood Heaven Blade Demon.

“What right does someone who goes around with a mask slapped over his face have to judge anyone? She is far better than you.”

After holding himself back several times throughout the drinking party, he had finally exploded.

Even at his fierce reaction, the Extreme Evil Smiling Demon did not get angry and merely smiled. That sight made the Blood Heaven Blade Demon even angrier, and he gave off the air that he might swing the Heaven-Destroying Great Saber at any moment.

Then the Ilhwa Sword Venerable spoke in a low, cold voice.

“Do you think I will like it if you step forward like that?”

How could the Blood Heaven Blade Demon have stepped forward without knowing she would react this way? The Blood Heaven Blade Demon simply drank the wine before him in quick succession.

Then the Ilhwa Sword Venerable said something unexpected.

“But…… those words may be right.”

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon looked down at his empty cup and said,

“Yes, they may be.”

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon had not stepped forward blindly just to defend her.

“We may be more hypocritical and wicked than what is behind that mask. That is true.”

At the Blood Heaven Blade Demon’s honest words, the Ilhwa Sword Venerable’s eyes trembled slightly.

“What is so great about us that we can say this person is this way and that person is that way? Still, at least in front of those people, do not let yourself be seen through. I am telling you not to be toyed with by mask-wearing and drunkard bastards!”

At that, the Drunken Demon murmured,

“Why are you dragging me into this when I was staying still?”

“You are the same! You sinister bastard! You hypocritical bastard! You may fool everyone else, but you cannot fool my eyes. You two bastards! Get away from the Second Young Master at once!”

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon had been unable to hold back and poured out everything in his heart. Though rigid and strict, he was a man with deep schemes, but because it concerned me and the Sword Venerable, his emotions had become inflamed.

On the other hand, seeing the two of them smiling cheerfully even after being cursed at and sipping their wine, I thought they truly looked like a pair of villains.

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon rose from his seat and expressed his apology to me.

“Second Young Master, I am sorry for ruining the mood. At least today, I had no intention of being the first to leave.”

“No, sir, you are not leaving first.”

“What do you mean?”

“Today’s drinking party ends here.”

I looked at the remaining three Demon Venerables and said,

“Let us stop here for today.”

The Drunken Demon emptied his last cup and said,

“Are you hammering down the embedded stone so it will not come loose?”

“Of course. What sort of drinking could I do when my elder is leaving in anger? Elder Blade Demon is my wing. If my wing flies away, I go with it.”

I made it clear that an embedded stone could not be dislodged so easily. It was my way of showing gratitude to him for coming all the way to this drinking party for my sake.

—Thank you, sir.

Perhaps his heart was moved by my voice transmission, for the Blood Heaven Blade Demon turned his head and looked out the window.

The Drunken Demon smiled and said,

“I will give up on being your wing, so become brothers with me.”

At that, the Extreme Evil Smiling Demon said,

“And friends with me.”

The Ilhwa Sword Venerable did not stay out of it either. Though her mood had sunk, she could not have missed this moment.

“I am already a special friend.”

Everyone looked at her with puzzled expressions, but she merely looked pleased by herself and did not boast that we were sparring friends.

“Now then, I will say one final thing.”

I once again emitted the bearing I had shown earlier. Releasing an aura full of dignity and grace, I spoke to them.

“As the future Heavenly Demon who will lead you all, I say this. As I stated earlier, within my fence, you may fight as much as you please. Hate, envy, fight, and…… Ah! Truly, this is too much!”

The Demon Venerables had flung themselves away and left the place. And they had not simply left; each of them had used their own lightness skill and vanished in an instant.

“At least listen to the end before you go! Lord Blade Demon! Even you, sir!”

But they had already disappeared.

Smiling, I sat back down. The seats were empty, but it still felt as though their heat remained.

When I looked down at the first floor, Jo Chunbae was holding the bank draft the Drunken Demon had tossed him against his chest, gazing wistfully in the direction where the Demon Venerables had disappeared.

“Owner, have a drink with me.”

“Gladly, Pavilion Master.”

Jo Chunbae came up and poured wine into my cup. I also poured wine into his.

We emptied our cups together. Dealing with the Demon Venerables had consumed a great deal of mental strength, but drinking like this with Jo Chunbae put my heart at ease.

Jo Chunbae glanced at my expression and asked carefully,

“Are you tired?”

“Do I look tired?”

“I was thinking how exhausting it must be to deal with those Demon Venerables.”

“Yes, it is tiring.”

When I answered honestly, Jo Chunbae grinned. The wrinkles on his face, carved by the hardships and sorrows of life while dealing with all kinds of customers, looked especially fine today.

“When I am having a hard time, do you know what my wife whispers in my ear while hugging me?”

“What does she say?”

“‘You weakling.’”

I burst into loud laughter. I knew well just how much that affectionate couple loved each other. I also knew well that sometimes, a joke like that could give more strength than heartfelt consolation.

Jo Chunbae said playfully,

“Shall I do it for you once?”

“No, I do not think you need to. The person who will do it has arrived.”

“Pardon? Who?”

In that instant, Jo Chunbae felt an overwhelming presence behind his back. Every hair on his body stood on end, and it must have been a feeling on an entirely different level from when he had been with the Demon Venerables. Jo Chunbae carefully turned around.

“Good heavens!”

Jo Chunbae was startled out of his wits and prostrated himself flat on the floor.

Father, having come upstairs at some point, was standing behind him.

Father sat down before me and said,

“You weakling.”

Smiling, I said to Jo Chunbae,

“Owner, close the shop and prepare another drinking table here!”

Chapter 135: Will You Never Fight in a Tavern Your Whole Life?
--------------------------------------------------------------
"How did you come to be here?"

At my question, Father answered with his usual line.

"I was passing by."

As if. Father had been accurately tracking the movements of the Demon Lords, and he must have come after hearing the news that the four Demon Lords and I had gathered in one place.

What state of mind had he come in? From what point had he seen and heard?

Though I had grown much closer to Father, I wasn't yet close enough to ask such sensitive questions so brazenly.

"For the first time, I shared a table with four Demon Lords."

"How was it?"

"If you hadn't come, Father, without a doubt I would have sought you out the moment I left the tavern. I would have gone to ask how I should deal with and handle the Demon Lords from now on. Ah, I suppose it shouldn't become a habit—seeking answers from you whenever I feel frustrated."

To my complaint, half sincere and half flattery, he would normally have just snorted and taken a drink, but today, for some reason, he spoke a word. And it was something that struck my heart.

"You can worry about that in ten years or so."

"Will you indulge me until then?"

It was at that moment that Father snorted.

"Didn't know it would be this hard?"

"I didn't."

"You were making such a fuss about whipping things into shape, so I thought you knew it all."

"If I had known, I wouldn't have said such things. The way I look from the outside is different, the way I am treated is different, and when entangled with others, it's different again."

I deliberately played up my misery. Because Father was the type to enjoy it more the harder I struggled.

"That's right. I've become terribly weak."

"At least you've improved greatly compared to before."

Because even when I hunted with Father right after the regression, I had been different from now.

As we spoke, a modest drinking spread was laid out.

Father had the strongest liquor from the tavern brought out with a simple vegetable stir-fry. Jo Chunbae must have stir-fried those vegetables with every ounce of skill he possessed.

"Let's have a drink."

I drank with Father.

"Kuh, that's strong."

"You drank with Chwima without batting an eye, and now you fear this swill?"

"He seemed like a nice person, though?"

"With eyes for people like that, it's a marvel you're still alive."

What kind of person did Chwima appear to be in Father's eyes?

"What kind of person is Chwima?"

"He is someone who toys with others while laughing and enjoying himself. So it's best not to carelessly open his liquor bottle."

"They say you should never ignore your elders' words. Hyeolcheon Doma also warned me to be careful of Chwima, calling him a sinister one."

"Not knowing he himself is the sinister one."

I burst out laughing at Father's words. I made a note to tease Hyeolcheon Doma about this later when I saw him.

Pouring Father a drink, I asked,

"Have you ever drunk alone with Chwima?"

"I have."

"How did he act toward you?"

"He doesn't talk much to me."

"That talkative Chwima?"

That was unexpected. I would have thought he would talk even more in front of Father, acting familiar.

"Could it be because he thinks he can't toy with you no matter what?"

"Who knows. How could I know what goes on inside that man's head?"

"That only makes me more curious."

"Poisonous things are naturally the most splendid, after all."

In other words, don't be swayed by Chwima.

"Don't try to dig into a person's heart. Judge only by what is revealed. That leads to fewer mistakes."

"Yes. I will take it to heart."

I emptied my drink with Father. Refilling the cups, I asked him,

"What do you talk about when you drink with Brother?"

"Why do you ask?"

"I'm simply curious. Aren't you teaching him all my weaknesses?"

"It's a weakness anyone can see. Is there any need to bother teaching it?"

"What is my weakness?"

"That cheap sentimentality perfectly suited for getting yourself killed."

I smiled brightly.

"I consider that my strength, and I live by it. Since we're at it, let's go pleasantly and have fun. The road ahead is long and treacherous. Threatening, oppressing, killing—let's not do that, and just have fun instead."

"Do you think 'fun' suits our work? You'll die seeking enjoyment."

Father, I should be allowed to seek some. Not for a single moment has Hwamugi left my mind. I cannot fall asleep even lying down. The image of dying at Hwamugi's hand springs to mind, and I leap up and run to the training grounds.

"You too should live a little more enjoyably and cheerfully now, Father."

"If the Cheonma's authority and terror disappear, they will come to destroy us under the banner of some trifling agreement."

I suddenly thought that perhaps Father, too, in his own way, had been unable to sleep at night. I shouldn't let myself be fooled by those floral pajamas.

"I will strive to grow stronger."

"You should have already grown stronger. Only then does today's meeting with the Demon Lords hold meaning."

"Then in that spirit, please grant me a bout."

Since my last spar with Father, I had achieved new accomplishments in martial arts. A bout with Father now would provide tremendous stimulation and influence upon my martial studies.

Father rose from his seat and stood in the space beside the table.

"Here?"

"Do you plan on never fighting inside a tavern your whole life?"

"That's not it. If we fight in this cramped space, everything will break."

Then Father said something unexpected.

"Who said to fight like low-level hoodlums, smashing everything in sight? The one who breaks a single thing loses."

It meant we were to fight without breaking anything here.

I looked around the space where we were. It was narrower than the first floor with a low ceiling. Tables and chairs filled the space.

Spar with Father here? Without breaking anything? Truly, it would be a more difficult spar than any I had ever fought.

I looked down at the first floor. After Father arrived, people had been sent out and the doors closed, so only Jo Chunbae remained in the tavern.

"Proprietor, go into the kitchen for a moment."

"Yes!"

"No matter what happens, do not be surprised!"

"I have been surprised so much today, I haven't any heart left to be surprised further."

Jo Chunbae scurried into the kitchen.

I stood facing Father, several paces apart.

"Didn't you say it was hard?"

"Yes."

"It's because you are weak."

When Father unleashed his aura, my breath caught. His aura—which even the Cheonma Hoshin-gong strained to endure—was as heavy as a great mountain, as suffocating as a swamp, and as sharp as a blade.

Father slowly drew the Cheonma Sword.

"A Cheonma must be someone who can kill when he wishes to kill. The sole reason my opponent remains alive before me is that I have permitted it."

*Chaeng!*

The Cheonma Sword and the Black Demon Sword clashed in the air. Their speed remained the same, but the force behind the blades was restrained.

*Chaeng!*

The blades clashed once more in the air. I understood. Father was speaking through the force loaded in his sword.

This force was the standard.

Like tuning an instrument, he was saying we should fight using only this exact amount of force.

This fight was not one where you lost because you were weaker than your opponent. It was a fight where you lost if you failed to control your power.

*Chaeng!*

The tuning lasted only three times.

*Chaeng-chaeng-chaeng-chaeng-chaeng-chaeng-chaeng!*

The Cheonma Sword and the Black Demon Sword clashed brilliantly.

They wielded their swords using only the promised force. Since each technique originally used a set amount of power, ignoring that power and fighting with only this current force demanded immense concentration and mental exertion.

In that moment, I placed no restrictions on my abilities.

Every fight since the regression until now had been restricted.

*I should only show this much skill.*

But now, I fought without such awareness. I was certain that it was permissible to reveal my full strength.

You told me not to trust people, but at least I trust you, Father.

I fought to my heart's content, I fought freely. It was a fight possible only because it was Father.

The more we fought, the faster my body adapted to the battle.

Then Father raised the level of the fight.

Now, it was no longer simply about not breaking things—it had become a fight where we must not even touch the objects surrounding us.

Avoiding Father's attacks while moving between tables and chairs without touching them? I exerted the highest level of concentration I was capable of.

Amidst it all, our clash was beautiful. The sword trails drawn between the tables and chairs were like the masterwork of a painter who had spent a lifetime wielding the brush, and the sound of blades clashing amid the liquor bottles was like the sorrowful lament of an old musician. Then, paper tore, and instrument strings snapped.

The battle grew fiercer and the restrictions stricter.

Reaching the final stage, we fought while even protecting the lanterns hanging on the walls, preventing the flames from being extinguished by the wind of our swords.

And at some point.

*Shwiiik!*

A streak of sword qi flew from Father.

It was sword qi released with restrained force. If internal energy had been properly loaded into it, this single move would have blown the tavern away.

Sword qi also flew from my Black Demon Sword. My sword qi, imbued with restrained internal energy like Father's, neutralized Father's sword qi in midair.

*Fwaang!*

Wind blew, threatening to extinguish the lantern flames, and I threw myself to block it. They flickered precariously but did not go out.

Sword qi flew in continuous succession.

If I missed even one, the sword qi would fly past and destroy the wall. Then would the one who fired the sword qi lose? No. In this case, it became a fight where the side that missed lost.

I fought Father, nearly lost in a trance.

There were many sounds.

The sound of footsteps, the sound of wind, the sound of swords cutting through wind, the sound of blade meeting blade, the sound of sword qi being released, the sound of sword qi dissipating, the sound of sleeves fluttering, the sound of lanterns trembling. Each sound was steeped in the essence of martial arts.

I would surely be able to replay this spar later. There was no need to deliberately memorize it. Every technique was perfect, and the situation was desperate. Because it was a sequence of moves I could never forget, this fight would remain a source of learning for me for a long time to come.

And so the spar ended, and we sheathed our swords simultaneously. As the Cheonma Sword and the Black Demon Sword entered their scabbards at the same time, they produced a pleasant *clack*.

Looking around, not a single chair had toppled. There was not a scratch on the walls.

"It was a draw!"

"No. You lost."

Father came over and held up the chopstick holder on the table in front of me. The tips of the chopsticks inside had been cut away.

"Ah, this is a mark grazed by my sword qi. When in the world did you notice?"

I checked the chopstick holder on Father's table and let out a sigh.

"I lose!"

It was a loss, but not a crushing defeat. Father was truly surprised and impressed by my skill.

"When I heard the news that you and Geukak had killed Baekmanggi, I had a hunch. That you had achieved a great accomplishment."

But he couldn't have imagined it was to this extent.

Father must have been greatly spurred by me.

"What should I do now?"

I asked the way. Where my martial arts should advance. To the only person who could provide that answer.

"From now on, devote yourself solely to training in the Pungsinsabo."

That was Father's conclusion. And there was a reason for this conclusion.

"I stopped training in the Pungsinsabo."

"Why did you stop?"

To my startled self, Father spoke honestly.

"I hit a wall."

"If you have struck a limit, then I can never overcome it."

Father looked at me for a moment, then shook his head.

"Even if I cannot cross it, you can achieve the Great Completion."

"What do you mean?"

"It is a wall created by the Guhwamagong. In a state where one had already learned the Guhwamagong first, the Great Completion of the Pungsinsabo could not be achieved."

"Ah!"

I understood what he meant. There existed compatibility in martial arts as well. Just as there were arts that did not suit one from the start, there were cases where one could not achieve the Great Completion depending on the order in which one learned them.

"If you do not achieve the Great Completion before learning the Guhwamagong, you will never be able to achieve it after."

"I will definitely achieve the Great Completion before then."

When the Pungsinsabo achieved the Great Completion, it would combine with the Bicheon Sword Technique, which had attained twelvefold completion, and display yet another accomplishment. And when combined with the Guhwamagong later, it would show even greater effects.

After gazing at me for a moment, Father asked,

"Still hard?"

"Thanks to your teachings, I have gained confidence."

"Do you still intend to become a cheerful Cheonma?"

"I want to live smiling."

After staring at me for a moment, Father turned away without a word. And when he had gone all the way down to the first floor, he tossed out casually,

"Come play a round of baduk sometime, if you have the time."

Jo Chunbae, who had been hiding under the counter, bowed until his waist folded, then looked up at me.

"Nothing was broken, so don't worry."

"The demonic path I pursue is one that every tavern keeper in the world would love, is it not?"

Jo Chunbae let out a sigh of relief.

"What a long day it has been."

* * *

Jo Chunbae's day had ended, but mine had not.

I went to find Hyeolcheon Doma. He was sitting by the window reading a book. I had expected him to be swinging his saber in anger, but he was calmer than I'd expected.

"No martial arts training today?"

"Why have you come?"

"I came because I wanted to see you, Elder."

Before bringing up what happened at the tavern earlier, I handed him what I had brought first. Because I hadn't come to talk about that.

"What is this?"

"A tonic."

"A tonic?"

Hyeolcheon Doma was startled.

"You are thinner than when I first saw you. It is a medicine that stimulates appetite and nourishes the body. I pestered the physician to obtain it, so you can trust its efficacy."

Hyeolcheon Doma was bewildered. He was more flustered than when I had given him the blood-based poison liquor as a birthday gift.

"Give it to the Lord instead."

"You are the kind of person who would suspect I'd poisoned it and make me eat it all in front of you."

At my half-jest, half-truth, Hyeolcheon Doma burst into laughter.

Lowering my voice to a whisper, I said,

"Actually, this is something Father takes; I pestered the physician to get it. You know how Father is, taking enormous care of his health. So please don't worry and take it."

As I walked out, Hyeolcheon Doma asked from behind,

"Why do you treat me so well?"

I turned and said,

"Because you are good to me, so I am good to you. And because I think that if I treat you this well, you will be of more help than the other three Demon Lords. I'm very selfish and calculating, you see. Well then, sleep well. Make sure to take your medicine. I will check with Investigator Seo!"

To him, who was staring at me with deepening eyes, I gave a deep bow and turned away.

You have to do these things while you can. If you don't do it when it comes to mind, there might not be a next time.

Only then did my long day truly end. The drunkenness belatedly rose.

Chapter 136: Because I’m More Heartless Than I Look
---------------------------------------------------
The following day, I entered closed-door training for a period of ten days.

I left the affairs of Hwangcheon Pavilion to Seo Daeryong, and forgot about the Demon Venerables.

This training was urgent.

Everything has its trigger and its proper time. Just as in a duel, when the moment comes, you must stake everything on victory, there is also a right moment for training.

My trigger was the duel with my father, still vivid in my mind. Before that duel faded from my thoughts, I had to melt it down and make it my own.

I decided not to think about the Demon Venerables.

The Drunken Demon might have come looking for me the next day, or the Demon Venerables might have ended up fighting among themselves. In any case, they were not people I could control as I wished, so I focused only on what I had to do.

I replayed the duel at the Pungryu Tavern, where I had fought in a state of selflessness, exactly as it had happened.

I reproduced my duel with Father down to the smallest movement. When I moved like this, Father moved like that. When I attacked in that way, Father defended in that way. I compared and analyzed Father’s movements and my own.

This duel held great meaning in many respects. There was the fact that I had fought without setting limits on myself, and the immense constraint of not being allowed to touch the objects around us even in the midst of that urgency.

Because of that, there was much I felt, and much I had to learn.

It was not training that exhausted the body, but training of deep contemplation and reflection. I thought only about martial arts, and I sank into them more deeply than ever before.

Ten days later, I emerged from the training hall with a thick beard grown over my face.

At that moment, the sharp smell of alcohol wafted over me, and just as I thought, Surely not, that very “surely not” spoke to me from behind.

“Just who are you trying to kill, training so hard? Me? No, right?”

Astonishingly, the Drunken Demon had been waiting for me.

“Why are you here?”

“Why else? I was waiting for you to come out. The day after we drank at the Pungryu Tavern, I went looking for you, and they told me you’d gone into closed-door training for ten days. The moment I heard that, I came running here.”

“Are you telling me you’ve been waiting here the whole time?”

“Of course! I’ve been waiting since the first day.”

I stared at him, then said with a smile.

“That’s a lie. You must have told Martial Artist Yeo, ‘Let me know when the Second Young Master comes out after ten days of closed-door training.’ Then you went off and happily drank yourself silly, didn’t you? When did you arrive today? Just a little while ago, right?”

“Hm. Has Yeo Bin turned to your side?”

At his frank confession, I couldn’t help but laugh.

The Drunken Demon asked with suspicious eyes.

“By the way, you didn’t go into closed-door training because I was annoying you, did you?”

“To be honest, I enjoy drinking and spending time with you, Drunken Demon. If anything, the problem is that it’s too enjoyable.”

“When I heard you’d gone into closed-door training, I thought the sky had collapsed. If you’d gone into seclusion for a hundred days, I would have become a stone statue waiting for you.”

“Do you not have other drinking companions?”

“Who?”

“You could drink with the Sword Venerable.”

“After suffering such humiliation from that Soma that day, would she want to meet anyone and drink? I have to give her time to cool her anger.”

As if that were the reason. This was a man who had come all the way to wait in front of the training hall. Right now, rather than the Sword Venerable, he was interested in me, so she probably did not even enter his eyes.

“Isn’t it precisely at times like this that one needs a friend?”

“I’m avoiding her because I’m her friend.”

That, too, was true, so I could not refute him.

“Now, let’s go drink.”

“Do you enjoy drinking with me that much?”

“You said you don’t call someone brother after drinking together only twice, didn’t you?”

It was something I had said to him at the Pungryu Tavern. It seemed to have remained in his mind.

“Would you do it if we drank together about two hundred times?”

“If we’ve shared cups two hundred times, wouldn’t the form of address be meaningless by then?”

“No, it’s important. If we drank together two hundred times and still couldn’t call each other brothers, wouldn’t that be a strange relationship?”

“That is true, but…”

“If I asked you to drink tea with me, I doubt we’d even reach five times. But if it’s drinking alcohol, I can reach two hundred.”

“Why go this far just to call each other brothers with me?”

Then an unexpected reason came out.

“It’s simple. Right now, your chances of becoming the Heavenly Demon are half and half, aren’t they? Isn’t this an opportunity to have the Heavenly Demon as my younger brother with a fifty percent chance?”

“You’re under one major misconception.”

“What misconception?”

“If I become the Heavenly Demon, I won’t treat you as an elder brother. Those two hundred drinking sessions will vanish in that instant.”

“Only on the surface, surely?”

“Not in my heart either. Nor behind closed doors. In fact, because I wouldn’t want such a misunderstanding, I might treat you even more strictly, Drunken Demon.”

The Drunken Demon looked at me with a strange gaze.

“I thought that even if you became the Heavenly Demon, you would show an open and unguarded side. I thought you would break formality and live like brothers with the Demon Venerables.”

“That would be a preconception about me. I’m colder and less affectionate than I look. Perhaps I only pretend to be more affectionate in order to hide that. If I become the Heavenly Demon, I will draw a sharp line between public and private matters. If you expected that I would vaguely muddle things along and call you brother, then investing the remaining hundred and ninety-eight drinking sessions in my elder brother would be wiser.”

At that, the Drunken Demon spoke in a meaningful tone.

“Your elder brother must not become the Cult Leader.”

“Why?”

“I’ll tell you over a drink.”

The Drunken Demon quickened his steps and walked off.

I walked in the opposite direction.

The Drunken Demon, who had gone some distance, came chasing after me.

“Now, this is too much. Second Young Master, aren’t you curious?”

“Not at all.”

“It’s the reason your elder brother must not become Cult Leader.”

“Yes.”

“Why on earth not?”

This time, I spoke in a meaningful tone.

“You’re curious why I’m not curious, aren’t you?”

“I am.”

“I only pretended not to be curious so you would become curious, Drunken Demon. Aren’t you the same? You’re trying to make me curious and bait me into drinking with you when there’s no real reason, aren’t you?”

“When a man is too clever, he loses his humanity!”

“I already told you I’m a person without affection.”

As I strode away, the Drunken Demon persistently followed after me.

“I waited ten days. Second Young Master, are you truly going to be like this?”

Fine. If he had gone this far, I had to acknowledge his effort.

“I lose. First, I’ll wash up and rest a bit, then I’ll visit the Great Drunken Forest this evening.”

Only then did the Drunken Demon’s expression brighten radiantly.

“I’ll be waiting. You must come.”

“But you must tell me. The reason my elder brother must not become Cult Leader.”

“You said you weren’t curious.”

“As if that could be true. Then I’ll see you later.”

I returned to my quarters, shaved my beard, washed myself, and changed into fresh clothes.

Then I stood before the bronze mirror.

The me of ten days ago and the me of now had changed yet again.

Through this closed-door training, I had raised the Wind God Four Steps to a new realm.

The Wind God Four Steps, eighth level.

When I entered seclusion, I had anticipated results.

I had brought the Flying Heaven Sword Art to the Great Completion of the twelfth level, and I had gained enlightenment from the words left behind by the Progenitor of All Things. I had faced Baek Manggi in combat, and afterward, I had even had the strange experience of examining that fight again through the eyes of a third party in a dream.

On top of that, the duel with Father had been decisive. As if he had anticipated this outcome, Father had dealt with me throughout the duel using the Wind God Four Steps as his footwork.

Ah, there was one more thing. The desperation to reach Great Completion in the Wind God Four Steps before receiving the Nine Flames Demonic Art, no matter what.

As a result, I had achieved what could, in a way, be called the inevitable feat of bringing the Wind God Four Steps to the eighth level.

Once the Wind God Four Steps rose to the eighth level, the greatest change was that I was overcome with the desire to run somewhere using Rapid Steps. My body itched so badly that I barely restrained myself from rushing straight to the training field. That would not be enough. I needed to satisfy this desire properly.

First, I lay down on the bed and slept. Perhaps because I felt so good, for the first time in a long while, I slept deeply, without a single worry, making up for all the sleep I had missed.

* * *

That evening, when I bought a bottle of alcohol and went to the Great Drunken Forest, the Drunken Demon was lying in a ferryboat, drifting across the lake.

I boarded the ferryboat Yeo Bin provided and slowly approached the boat the Drunken Demon was on.

There was nothing in his boat but bottles of alcohol.

“As expected! When I’m not watching, you don’t eat any snacks with your alcohol.”

“If you drank with me every day, I would eat snacks every day. Here, take a cup.”

“I’ll drink the liquor I brought.”

One thing I liked about the Drunken Demon was that when it came to alcohol, he never forced anything on anyone. No matter what liquor you drank, or whether you merely touched your lips to it and put it down, he did not care. Nor did he force you to get drunk with him. I thought the Drunken Demon was someone who truly loved alcohol.

“I felt this the last time I came, but this lake really is wonderful.”

“There are places in the Great Drunken Forest even more splendid than this. I’ll take you next time.”

“Do you truly think there will be a next time for us?”

“Why do you think there won’t be?”

Instead of answering, I drank.

“Yes, I know what you mean. The Blood Heaven Blade Demon hates me terribly.”

He drank as well.

“True. Then again, am I the only one he hates? Do you really intend to gain the Blood Heaven Blade Demon and lose three others? Isn’t that far too much of a loss in business? That man is someone who would go to the First Young Master at any time if the conditions were right.”

The Drunken Demon slowly began sowing discord and pushing the Blood Heaven Blade Demon away.

“I told you that day, didn’t I? That I would gain all three of you. That is why I came here today.”

“If you fail to distinguish between ideals and reality like this, the First Young Master will become the successor in the end. The successor who will become the Heavenly Demon is not decided simply by who has greater martial arts, or who is more intelligent. It is not determined by such things.”

“Then what is it determined by?”

“Think about it. Choosing a successor is not the same as choosing the greatest demonic sword under heaven. It is choosing the person who will lead our Cult well. A Cult Leader who yields what must be yielded and harmonizes well with the Eight Demon Venerables. If not, our Cult will split apart, and in the end, the Martial Alliance will devour us. Why are you looking at me like that?”

“I did not know you were someone who thought so rationally about the future of our Cult, Drunken Demon.”

“I am the prime victim of misunderstandings and preconceptions.”

“If you dislike that, why not stop drinking?”

“The Drunken Demon who gave up drinking? The whole murim would laugh at me.”

Saying that, the Drunken Demon drank.

“May I ask you one thing? I have wanted to ask for some time.”

“What is it?”

After a brief pause, I asked him.

“Drunken Demon, do you drink in order to forget? Or do you drink in order not to forget?”

“…!”

The Drunken Demon flinched for an instant, then stared at me before answering that it was neither.

“To enjoy it. I drink simply because I like alcohol. Because I like alcohol itself. Assuming I must be drinking because I have some story—that, too, is a preconception.”

With that, the Drunken Demon drank again. I could not tell whether those were sincere words or an excuse. As long as he was drinking, it was not easy to see the truth in him.

I drank the liquor I had brought as well. When I was with him, I found myself drinking again and again. Back when I drank heavily, I had certainly had a reason.

Back then, had I drunk in order to forget? Or had I drunk in order not to forget?

“In that case, you could simply support my elder brother as the successor. Unlike me, he won’t say absurd things like that he must reconcile with Elder Blade Demon.”

At that, the Drunken Demon brought up something unexpected.

“The First Young Master must not become the successor.”

It was the same thing he had said in front of the training hall. The fact that he brought it up first meant it had not merely been bait to drag me to a drinking session.

Could this be the reason this man had approached me? While saying we should call each other brothers?

“The First Young Master… likes the Cult Leader too much.”

“…!”

Words I had never expected came from the Drunken Demon.

“I like Father as well.”

“He likes him beyond comparison to you.”

It was not something I could deny. Whether it was because he truly liked Father in his heart, because he was suppressed by Father’s authority, or because he wanted to become successor, my elder brother was someone who would do anything if Father said it.

“I acknowledge that you are superior to the First Young Master. But even so, I cannot yet be certain that you will become the successor.”

Again, I had to acknowledge it. Father was someone who could duel me today, then tomorrow bluntly say, “The successor is your elder brother.”

“Very well. Let us say that is true. But why is the fact that my elder brother likes Father a reason he must not become successor?”

“Because the First Young Master will not be able to defy the Cult Leader’s will.”

“What will?”

Once again, astonishing words flowed from the Drunken Demon.

“The will to unite the murim.”

The Drunken Demon knew. He knew that Father harbored the ambition of unifying the murim.

I recalled the conversation I had once had with Father regarding Cult Leader Pungcheon.

—The time has not yet come. Know only that.

At the time, I had thought that Father might be dreaming of the unification of the murim.

The Drunken Demon had sensed that ambition of Father’s. Perhaps it was not only the Drunken Demon. Among the other Demon Venerables, someone else might know as well. After all, the Demon Venerables had all avoided mentioning anything related to Father.

“The First Young Master will become the successor and faithfully follow his father’s orders. Without doubt, he will become the vanguard and set out to conquer the murim. But what about you? You would stop the Cult Leader. The you I have seen so far is not someone who approves of war.”

“That is correct. I do not want war.”

“Then you must become the successor and stop the Cult Leader.”

I truly had never dreamed that the Drunken Demon had chosen me for such a reason.

He had chosen me to prevent the war that would occur in the future? This Drunken Demon, who was always drunk? This person who seemed the most thoughtless among the Eight Demon Venerables?

I brought my face close to the Drunken Demon’s.

“What are you doing?”

“I’m trying to determine how much is truth and how much is lies.”

“Everything I have said is the truth.”

“You, Drunken Demon, who spends every day drinking, chose me because of the peace of the murim? Are you telling me to believe that?”

“Call it my final preconception.”

Looking at me with drunken eyes, the Drunken Demon revealed the truth as if confessing.

“That is precisely why I sent the broken bottle of liquor to the First Young Master.”

Chapter 137: This Is Why I Wanted to Come
-----------------------------------------
Amid the flowing silence, Chwima realized one fact.

“Did you already know?”

“I suspected as much. The brother I know is not the sort to send a broken bottle to you, Lord Chwima.”

“I thought you two were on bad terms?”

“Don’t you always end up knowing far more about someone you loathe than someone you’re close to?”

Chwima, who had been staring at me, sprang to his feet.

“Ah, the drink is getting to me.”

With that, he immediately threw himself into the lake.

“You ought to fix your habit of swimming right after drinking!”

Regardless of my concern, Chwima was a strong swimmer. His skill was considerable, but more than that, he knew how to swim with joy. He would dive, then float on his back gazing at the sky; he would speed off into the distance and return; he would leap out like a dolphin and plunge back in with a splash.

After swimming about with such delight, Chwima climbed back onto the boat.

“Now the alcohol is finally wearing off.”

And with that, he drank again.

“You just sobered up to drink again?”

“Didn’t I sober up so I could drink more?”

I shouted to Yeobin, who was standing on the distant shore.

“Bring some snacks over here!”

At that, Yeobin, as if she had been waiting, poled the boat over and brought the snacks. She had prepared them in advance, but it seemed Chwima had brought only the liquor. As she handed over the snacks and turned to leave, Yeobin bowed her head slightly to convey her gratitude to me.

“You should get into the habit of eating side dishes.”

“I’ve never eaten them in my life. Is that easy?”

“Can you simply do only what you want? There are things you must do even if you dislike them.”

“Why are you so concerned about my health?”

I ate the snacks with chopsticks. They were warm. It seemed she had kept them heated, ready to bring at any moment.

“As if I’m the only one who looks after you, Lord Chwima. Here, eat.”

Chwima also reluctantly ate the snacks.

We drank together and then gazed up at the night sky.

“Ah, this is wonderful!”

It truly was as pleasant as Chwima’s cry. With a boat upon this lake, drinking beneath the stars, even immortals could not compare.

Lying at a slant and staring at the stars, Chwima spoke.

“Drinking and lazing about is my dream. If the Demonic-Orthodox Great War breaks out, this very lake could turn into crimson blood. Then how would I ever sober up?”

The unique demonic weapon hanging at Chwima’s waist was named Hyeollu, Blood Tear. He was saying such things while wearing a demonic weapon named Blood Tears.

“Are you serious?”

“I am. I wish to live and die like this my whole life.”

“Is that truly why you wish to become my man?”

“That is correct.”

“Then prove it.”

“How do you mean?”

“Reconcile with Elder Hyeolcheon Doma.”

Perhaps because it was an unexpected suggestion, Chwima flinched in surprise.

“I am serious as well. From now on, you must make an effort to become my man. If you reconcile with Elder Hyeolcheon Doma and maintain a harmonious relationship with Geukak Soma…”

I drained the last of the liquor and spoke to him.

“I will swear brotherhood with you, Lord Chwima.”

Silence fell again.

After staring at me for a moment, Chwima eventually opened his mouth.

“You truly are an unpredictable man.”

“The unpredictable one is you, Lord Chwima. You are surrounded by all manner of prejudice. I, on the contrary, am rational. I am simply exchanging what I want for what you want.”

I drank refreshingly and set down the bottle, asking.

“Was your offer to swear brotherhood with me a joke?”

“It was no joke.”

“It is a matter of forming a brotherhood with someone. You must show at least this much sincerity. Ah, so that person is truly doing something they hate for my sake. Doing something they truly detest. Eating snacks, making peace. What words could I not say then? I could even win the Martial Alliance Leader over to my side.”

“If it were you, you might truly win him over.”

I let out a hollow laugh, and Chwima drank.

“It is not that I do not wish to reconcile.”

“Then what?”

“Reconciliation with Hyeolcheon Doma is impossible from the start. Have you ever seen water mix with oil?”

“They need not mix. They need only float along together.”

“What if someone lights a fire?”

“Then they burn. If you truly detest it, be the water. Let Elder Doma be the oil. He is a fiery person by nature, so let him be the oil.”

He usually laughed at most any jest, but this time he did not laugh. He looked quite unwilling.

Seeing how he acts toward me, one would think he could at least smile outwardly and humor Hyeolcheon Doma.

I could understand even why Hyeolcheon Doma disliked Chwima. He had disliked people who got drunk and made a scene from the start.

But why did Chwima find this reconciliation so burdensome?

“There is another reason you approached me, isn’t there? Put aside such nonsense as the peace of the martial world and reveal your true reason.”

He could well have grown angry at such insolence, but Chwima merely drank with drunken eyes.

“This kind of brotherly relationship is enough with the one brother I have.”

I leaped toward the boat I had come on.

He could have stopped me from leaving like this, but in the end he said nothing. As I docked the boat at the lakeshore, I looked back. Chwima was drinking alone beneath the moonlight.

* * *

The next dawn, I quietly left the sect.

I left a letter for Seo Daeryong saying I would be away for a few days.

Ever since the Wind God Swift Steps reached the realm of Eight Stars, my body had been itching with restlessness, unable to stay still. I had tossed and turned all night wanting to fight strong experts, but the desire even more intense than that was the urge to run.

I know I must control this desire well. A supreme martial art like the Wind God Swift Steps must be handled with even greater care.

There are many cases where mishandling this desire leads to qi deviation or reckless battles ending in death. It often happens when someone whose vessel is insufficient learns a martial art beyond their vessel.

After leaving the sect, I began running with swift footwork. I ran with terrifying speed. There was no need to wear a mask to hide my identity.

My running speed was so fast that one would wonder who I was, or whether what had just passed was a person or a bird, or simply an illusion. It had been fast even at Seven Stars, but this was different from then.

Originally, this speed was only possible in wide open places like plains. But the path I ran on now had obstacles. There were moving carriages and passing people. There were trees and rocks.

However, thanks to the Divine Eye Technique, I could run at this speed on any path. Moreover, upon reaching Eight Stars, the amount of internal energy consumed actually decreased. I had become able to use martial arts more efficiently.

I ran until my internal energy was nearly depleted, then meditated to recover before running again. Never in my life had I run so exhilaratingly. Perhaps it was because my destination was where the people I wanted to see were.

* * *

In a crowded tavern sat the Pungcheon Cult Leader and Gowol. Several people came to their table at intervals, reported something, and left. There were men and women, young and old.

Whenever they came and went, Gowol recorded something on paper.

“What are you writing so meticulously? He’d know you’re working even without contact.”

Gowol ignored the Pungcheon Cult Leader and wrote a dispatch. It was a report to be sent to Geom Mugeuk.

Gowol had received Geom Mugeuk’s orders and was devoted to building an information network in the Central Plains. Because the Pungcheon Cult Leader had accompanied him, the work was proceeding smoothly.

In the process, the Pungcheon Cult Leader once again admired Gowol’s abilities. He was constructing an organization operated differently from the existing Tongcheon Pavilion, making it so that it could later be merged with Tongcheon Pavilion. He had been keeping this in mind until Geom Mugeuk became Cult Leader.

So it was more difficult, but the one who made the process smooth was the Pungcheon Cult Leader.

When the two joined forces, the work truly proceeded in one fell swoop. Of course, Gowol had to endure the Pungcheon Cult Leader’s complaints.

“Send reports for a hundred days like that, see if you get even one reply from the Second Young Master.”

“This is a report. Where is the sense in replying to a report?”

“Why can’t he? If he cared, he would. Didn’t I say? Our debt will become that Cult Leader, that shackle. Just look, that’s exactly our state now. Come out to a foreign land and suffer every kind of hardship.”

“Eating delicacies from every region. Sightseeing scenic spots when you have time. Such hardship indeed.”

“Leaving home is hardship!”

“Where exactly is your home, Cult Leader?”

For a moment, the Pungcheon Cult Leader flinched. Gowol’s gaze shifted from his writing toward the Cult Leader.

“The Cheonma Divine Cult isn’t your home, is it? The Cult Leader’s home is right here in the Central Plains. Didn’t you sing about advancing into the Central Plains? Look carefully as we go around. You have to build your home somewhere here. Open your eyes wide!”

With nothing left to say, the Pungcheon Cult Leader’s arrows turned back toward Geom Mugeuk.

“Hmph! I have to build a home. The Second Young Master has forgotten us. When he sees us back at the sect later, he’ll probably feel a pang of guilt. Ah! Right. Among my many subordinates, there was that foreign dimwit Cult Leader and that shackle fellow. Ah, but what was that shackle’s name again? Well, one way or another, people’s hearts are all the same.”

“Cult Leader.”

“What?”

“Everyone is ‘that so-and-so Cult Leader’ or ‘that so-and-so shackle’ to someone. Only someone like the Second Young Master is special to everyone. Everyone else lives being moderately ignored. And being a special person is a tiresome thing. This kind of life suits you better, Cult Leader.”

“Are you ignoring me again? You’re ignoring me again!”

Gowol pretended not to hear and focused on writing again.

“You used to stare blankly at the Yin Thunder Bells all day and move sluggishly, but look how diligent you’ve become. Just what do you like so much about the Second Young Master that you’re making such a fuss?”

“Then why did you follow me all the way here and suffer, Cult Leader?”

The Pungcheon Cult Leader could not answer.

“It is the same for me. What reason is there to explain? I simply like him. And after the Second Young Master comes you, Cult Leader. So don’t be too disappointed.”

“Hmph! You only have two people around you. If I’m second, isn’t that last place?”

“You noticed?”

“You madman!”

Gowol laughed, and the Pungcheon Cult Leader laughed along.

Such conversation was their joy. Repeating the same stories over and over, slandering Geom Mugeuk, talking about old times, and talking about things to come. Thus they told and retold the same tales every day.

“This is the proof of truly close friends. Being able to say the same things over and over and listen comfortably. You can’t do this with the Second Young Master, can you? It’s uncomfortable, no? That’s proof you’re not close with him. Or rather, the Second Young Master is busy showing off here and there, so he has no time to listen to you, does he?”

Right at that moment, someone suddenly spoke from behind the Pungcheon Cult Leader.

“You insisted on calling him Second Young Master to the very end.”

Right where the surprised Pungcheon Cult Leader turned, I stood smiling.

“So you intended to badmouth me to the very end.”

The Pungcheon Cult Leader and Gowol jumped to their feet in surprise.

“Second Young Master!”

“Second Young Master!”

Still smiling, I spoke to the Pungcheon Cult Leader.

“This is why I wanted to come.”

“Hmph! Did I say anything untrue? So what!”

His unchanged appearance made me glad from the start.

The place I had run to like mad was where the two were working. Because they had sent dispatches each time about where they were and how the work progressed, I had been able to find them like this.

I caught up with the two of them.

What was surprising was that Gowol already knew about the matters with Geukak Soma and Chwima, and the fact that my brother was in contact with Gwonma and the Poison King. It was evident that the information network within the sect had already been properly secured. The external information network was also rapidly expanding throughout the Central Plains.

“What is the financial situation?”

“It is almost exhausted.”

Using capable people and establishing an organizational network required an enormous amount of money. Gowol had taken twelve hundred thousand nyang, but it was already hitting bottom. The initial cost of building an organization was inherently high.

“I will send additional funds soon.”

“Yes!”

For now, funds were sufficient thanks to the money received from the Golden Hall, but to later operate the Ghost Shadow Division as well, more money had to be earned.

Then my eyes met the Pungcheon Cult Leader’s. He flinched in surprise.

“Why are you looking at me while talking about running out of money?”

“I looked because I’m happy to see you after so long. Why? Am I not allowed to look?”

“Doesn’t it feel like silent pressure to lend you money? I see. It’s that look. Exactly that look! The same sinister look you had when you were after my sacred relic before. Not a chance. No, my money is absolutely off-limits!”

I burst out laughing.

“Thanks to that sacred relic, I saved my life.”

At my words, Cult Leader Pungcheon started in surprise.

“Is that true?”

“Yes. I lived thanks to the supreme Heavenly Silkworm Silk.”

“Ah!”

A flicker of joy crossed Cult Leader Pungcheon’s face. Though he was grumbling, the affection for me in his eyes was incomparable to before.

Cult Leader. Forget the money. Let’s just keep going like this. I know well what you sacrificed to come this far. I’m grateful enough that you’re staying by Gowol’s side.

“I’ve seen your faces, so I should head back now. I have to return by the morning after tomorrow.”

“The morning after tomorrow? What are you talking about? Do you know how far it is from here?”

“It took me two days to get here. I’m thinking of cutting down the time on the way back.”

Cult Leader Pungcheon, wearing an incredulous expression, seized the opportunity and said to Gowol,

“See? This is the Second Young Master’s true nature. Didn’t I tell you before that the Second Young Master was a braggart? Now do you understand how well I read people? Will you still look down on me?”

Then Gowol said lightly,

“Is it truly a boast?”

What was on Gowol’s face as he looked at me was unwavering trust.

At that, Cult Leader Pungcheon raised his voice.

“Of course it’s a boast! Even if you ran without rest from here to our cult, it would take ten days. Even if I ran there myself.”

“You’re slow because you’ve gotten fat, Cult Leader.”

“What? You’ve never seen me run, have you? I’m Cult Leader Pungcheon!”

Cult Leader Pungcheon provoked me.

“Two days? If you reach the main altar of the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult within two days, I’ll give you one million nyang. How about it? Make a bet with me for one million nyang. You can’t do that, can you?”

Cult Leader Pungcheon showed his confidence triumphantly. He believed it was absolutely impossible.

“Let’s make the bet.”

“Truly?”

“Yes.”

“Don’t tell me you’re trying to give me money in return for saving your life with the supreme Heavenly Silkworm Silk. You don’t have to. I have plenty of money.”

“I am grateful, but that doesn’t mean I have enough leeway to give you money. As you know, I have a lot of expenses right now.”

“Good, then let’s do it! I’ll say this in advance: I won’t give back a single coin. It’ll be no use crying about it then!”
