“That is what I should be saying. You must not say things like it was medicine money or funds for your old age.”

“I told you, I have plenty of money! I’m the former Cult Leader Pungcheon!”

“Good. Once I arrive, I’ll send an official message from our cult here. Since it’ll be an official message, it will have the date stamped on it, and when you come to our cult later, you’ll be able to confirm when I arrived.”

“Don’t worry. There’s someone who can confirm the date you arrive. Don’t forget. It’s one million nyang, Second Young Master.”

I rose from my seat with a smile.

“I know well how much the two of you are struggling for my sake. I am always grateful. Then I’ll see you later.”

As I was about to turn away, Gowol said casually,

“Congratulations in advance on the one million nyang.”

“Thank you.”

From behind me, I heard Cult Leader Pungcheon grumbling.

“What are you saying? You should be cheering for me! If you’re going to be like this, even if I get one million nyang from the Second Young Master, I won’t give you a single coin!”

After parting with them like that, I began running toward the cult again.

The more I ran, the more accustomed I became to running, and I increased my speed even further. The more I ran, the faster I became, and the more I ran, the greater the freedom I felt. I also felt an intense longing to reach great accomplishment.

And two days later, I was writing a message from our cult.

[Cult Leader Pungcheon, I will make good use of the one million nyang. Thank you as always. Ah, and if you feel regretful, perhaps we could do it once more with a day and a half…….]

Chapter 138: Would I Be That Evil If I Just Let You Go?
-------------------------------------------------------
“Where have you been?”

At Seo Daeryong’s question, I told him where my destination had been.

Seo Daeryong blinked and asked. By now, he did not even look surprised.

“You went there and back in four days?”

“That’s right.”

“Of course, you’re not joking or messing with me, are you?”

I nodded, and Seo Daeryong shook his head.

“I simply won’t ask anymore. I won’t even try to understand. If I trouble myself over it for no reason, I’ll be the only one who ends up looking strange. You’re the sort of person who could suddenly say one day, ‘I’m joining the Murim Alliance tomorrow,’ and it wouldn’t be odd at all.”

“As long as you know. By the way, what about the Drunken Demon?”

“He hasn’t come even once since you left the cult, Pavilion Master.”

I remembered the way he had looked when we last parted. Sitting in a ferryboat, drinking alone.

I had told him to stop spouting nonsense about peace in the murim and tell me the real reason. Perhaps he had been angered by my words.

‘Unless you become honest, you and I will never be able to call each other brothers.’

He might indeed have a desire to prevent war from breaking out. But I could clearly feel that this was not the only reason he had approached me.

“By the way, Investigator Seo, why have you been limping since earlier?”

“Yesterday, Master put me through hell training.”

“All of a sudden?”

“Yes. He seemed to be in a rather bad mood.”

Just then, guided by another investigator, the Blood Heaven Blade Demon entered the office.

Since we had just been talking about him, Seo Daeryong was startled.

“Master!”

To Seo Daeryong, he was always a respected and welcome master.

It would have been nice if he treated him a bit more warmly, but the Blood Heaven Blade Demon merely gave a slight nod and said,

“You, step outside for a while.”

“Yes!”

Seo Daeryong went out. He had been limping so noticeably in front of me, but now he clenched his teeth and strode out with determination.

Investigator Seo, stay strong. Opening the door to your stiff-necked master’s heart will not be easy.

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon’s small, fierce eyes looked even more savage than usual today. Seo Daeryong had said he seemed in a bad mood yesterday, and today appeared to be an extension of that same mood.

“Welcome, Elder.”

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon went straight to the point.

“Yesterday, the Drunken Demon came to see me.”

I could tell. Seo Daeryong’s hell training had been because of the Drunken Demon.

“He brought me a bottle of liquor. A very expensive one.”

His tone was calm, but the Blood Heaven Blade Demon looked displeased by the situation.

“Why did he come?”

“He said he simply wanted to give it to me, handed over the liquor, and left.”

He had begun making an effort to reconcile with the Blood Heaven Blade Demon. It had taken him five days just to decide to go and give him a bottle of liquor.

“It was similar to when the Extreme Evil Little Demon came and told me to pass something on if I went to you.”

“That’s different. That was business with me, and this time, he had business with you, Elder.”

“I still thought it was business with you.”

Truly, his perception was terrifyingly sharp.

“No, this is the first I’ve heard of it. So, did you drink the liquor?”

“How would I know what was in it and drink it? I just poured it out.”

“How could you throw away someone’s sincerity like that?”

“Why do you care about someone else’s sincerity?”

“Because you said it was expensive liquor. You should have given it to me.”

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon glared at me suspiciously.

“It was you, wasn’t it? You did something. Otherwise, there’s no way that drunkard would do something he’d never done in his life.”

I considered telling him the truth, but denied it to the end. If this reconciliation was to happen properly, the two of them had to resolve it themselves somehow.

“No, I know nothing about it. Perhaps he was moved by you that day while drinking at Pungryu Tavern.”

“Moved by what? I did nothing but curse at him.”

“Who in this world would curse at him like that? Perhaps it stirred something in his heart.”

“Do not defend the Drunken Demon. I told you clearly, did I not? The Drunken Demon is a sinister man. Second Young Master, do not give your heart to someone needlessly. If you go too far, the road back becomes arduous.”

Having said only what he wanted to say, the Blood Heaven Blade Demon left the office.

Their reconciliation would depend on how the Drunken Demon acted from here on. In that process, the Drunken Demon would shed yet another layer of prejudice, and it would become an opportunity for me to learn a little more about him.

* * *

That night.

Sitting on the branch of a giant tree far away, I watched Ian train. It was far enough that she would not notice me, but I could see even her expression clearly.

With the most serious face in the world, sweat dripping from her, she was practicing the Flying Heaven Sword Art. She repeated the same form again and again, over and over. Until that repetition upon repetition answered her someday, she would not rest.

Just then, someone entered her training ground.

Surprisingly, the person who had come to see her was Blue Mask, the subordinate of the Extreme Evil Little Demon.

“Martial Artist I.”

“Ah, Sir Blue Mask.”

“I apologize for coming at such a late hour.”

“Not at all. What brings you here?”

“What you said then about asking me to join as a squad leader—does that offer still stand?”

“Until you clearly refuse, Sir Blue Mask, the position of First Squad Leader will always remain empty.”

Ian’s voice trembled. She was flustered by Blue Mask’s sudden visit. Blue Mask, on the other hand, continued with the questions he had prepared.

“What kind of work will the Ghost Shadow Unit do from now on?”

“The Ghost Shadow Unit will operate solely as an organization for the Second Young Master.”

“What if the Cult Leader’s orders and the Second Young Master’s orders conflict?”

Ian merely smiled faintly, as if she had already given her answer.

“Do you know what answer you are giving right now?”

“I haven’t said anything at all, have I?”

Their gazes tangled in the air. From the eyes behind Blue Mask’s mask, I could sense his hesitation.

When this strange woman, who had once been incomparably fat and had become incomparably beautiful, suddenly asked him to take on the role of squad leader, to be honest, at first he had thought, Is she insane?

No matter how she was someone who had saved his life, even to the point of smashing through a carriage, she was telling him to give up his future position as a Demon Venerable and become a squad leader?

At that moment, he had truly thought this: The heavens do not give everything. To be this beautiful and yet insane!

But as time passed, that insane woman kept coming to mind.

Rather than waiting twenty or thirty years to become a Demon Venerable, he found himself thinking that he wanted to roam the murim with her. The more he tried to shake off that thought, the more he thought of Ian.

If she had made the proposal before her transformation, would he have agonized over it like this? He reproached himself as a fool bewitched by beauty, but he could not help thinking of her. The thought that he would have been dead anyway if not for her only strengthened that temptation.

Ian chose honesty over pleasant words meant to draw Blue Mask in.

“I believe in the Young Master. If he looked at the sky and said that it was the earth, then from that day on, I would live thinking of the sky as the earth. If he told me, ‘Die in my place,’ I would die in his place. Sometimes I even practice in front of a mirror in advance, wondering what words I should leave behind then. I’ve prepared several. Some that would make him remember me for a long, long time, some that would let him send me off without burden, and some that would hurt him. I won’t know which ones I’ll say until that moment comes. The organization led by someone like me is the Ghost Shadow Unit. If you join, you should know that before you do.”

The more she spoke, the more her trembling subsided.

“You don’t have to decide right now. For my sake as well, please decide carefully.”

“For Martial Artist I’s sake? What does that mean?”

“The position I offered you is truly important and precious to me. It is the First Squad Leader’s seat in the first organization I have ever commanded in my life. I do not want to see you regret it from that position. I do not want that precious seat to become a place of regret for someone. So take your time and make a decision you will not regret. I intend to leave the First Squad Leader’s seat open until you decide. Even if all the other squad leader positions are filled, I will leave the First Squad Leader’s seat empty.”

Their gazes tangled in the air.

“Even after joining the Ghost Shadow Unit, may I continue wearing this mask?”

“Yes. But in exchange, you must speak to me often in ordinary times. So that if someone else wears that blue mask and appears before me pretending to be you, Sir Blue Mask, I can recognize it immediately.”

After gazing at Ian in silence for a while, Blue Mask bowed his head in a respectful greeting and left.

Only after he disappeared did Ian finally stroke her chest in relief.

“Ugh! I thought I was going to die from nerves. What did I even say? Ahhh! I’m doomed. I rambled on way too much!”

Watching her from afar, I burst into laughter.

Just then, someone flew lightly through the air and landed beside the branch where I was sitting.

Surprisingly, it was the Extreme Evil Little Demon. It seemed he had followed Blue Mask here.

“He agonized over it and lost sleep for a while, and in the end, he came here.”

It seemed Blue Mask’s worries had already entered the Extreme Evil Little Demon’s eyes.

“Foolish bastard! What does he think he’s doing, bewitched by beauty?”

“Isn’t it beauty worth being bewitched by?”

“Second Young Master, you took my mask from me, and now you are taking my subordinate as well.”

“Now, let’s correct that. You gave me the mask as a gift, and Blue Mask is being taken not by me, but by that Ian over there.”

“Then I shall have to take revenge on that pretty heart.”

“Let’s just say I’m the one taking him.”

The Extreme Evil Little Demon laughed quietly. Perhaps because he was in a bad mood, it sounded like a beast growling.

“What do you intend to do if Blue Mask says he is leaving?”

“If I simply let him go, would I be the Extreme Evil Little Demon? I should at least cut off one of his arms before sending him away.”

I could not tell whether he was serious or joking. When Ian had first made the offer to Blue Mask, the Extreme Evil Little Demon, who had been watching, had not said anything in particular. So I had thought that perhaps he might let him go peacefully, but now I thought that might not be the case. I did not know what feelings he had for Blue Mask.

“Would it not be all right to let him go in peace?”

“Are you saying that being a squad leader in that organization with no one in it is better than becoming a future Demon Venerable?”

“In this sense, yes. Becoming a Demon Venerable is a matter of the distant future, while wanting to be a squad leader is the feeling he has now.”

In truth, there had been another reason from the start. Blue Mask would not sit in the position of Demon Venerable even when he grew older.

The current Extreme Evil Little Demon would not know. He would not know that he himself would hold the position of Demon Venerable for such a long time. Nor would he know that Blue Mask would spend all those long years supporting him from behind.

“Lord Little Demon, you and I are bound by the fate we were born with and cannot live as we please, are we not? Let at least that Blue Mask live as he wishes.”

“He will regret it later. What then?”

“What else? He chose it himself, so he will have to regret it. At that time, he may regret it and come looking for you again, Lord Little Demon. Asking you to take him back.”

“Not a chance.”

“Please take him back. There is more than enough reason to do so.”

“What reason would that be?”

“Blue Mask as he is now and Blue Mask after living his own life will surely be different. The Blue Mask who wanders and returns will be stronger. The sword of a man who has known regret will be faster.”

The Extreme Evil Little Demon stared at me.

“Why are you looking at me like that?”

“You were always curious about what was beneath my mask, were you not? I am always curious as well. About what is inside you, Second Young Master.”

I felt a twinge inwardly, but answered calmly.

“Sometimes I wonder that myself. What is inside me that makes me talk as if I’ve already lived an entire life? If I talk this much and act like I’m teaching people, I won’t be popular with women these days, will I?”

“At least you know.”

The Extreme Evil Little Demon laughed, and I laughed along with him.

“Since we are on the subject, let me ask you one thing. If Blue Mask does choose to become a squad leader, and you become so angry that you want to cut off his arm, please come see me first.”

“Why? So you can cut off my arm to stop me?”

“I like you more than Blue Mask, Lord Little Demon. Why would I do that?”

“Then why do you want to see me?”

“For Ian’s sake, I will have to try somehow to stop and persuade you. I mean that sincerely.”

“I will think about it.”

The Extreme Evil Little Demon launched himself lightly into the air and left.

My gaze returned to Ian.

She had calmed her excitement and gone back to her tedious martial arts training.

You are doing well, Ian. In the end, Blue Mask will come not because of your persuasion, but because he is captivated by your skill. I think the Little Demon’s forgiveness will depend on that as well.

* * *

The next day, as soon as Seo Daeryong entered the office with an excited face, he asked,

“Pavilion Master, have you heard the rumor?”

“What rumor?”

Seo Daeryong had brought a truly astonishing rumor.

“They say Master is going to have a bout with the Drunken Demon.”

I was startled.

“Is that true?”

“I’m not sure whether it’s a baseless rumor or the truth. It started from the Southern Blade Sect. But Demon Venerables don’t fight each other—isn’t that an unwritten rule?”

Seo Daeryong seemed worried that the rumor might be true.

“Are you worried?”

“Honestly, yes.”

“What are you worried about? Your master has been training tremendously in martial arts lately.”

“Still, he is advanced in age.”

Did the Blood Heaven Blade Demon know? That his disciple was worrying about him like this. Then again, if he had known that, would he have put him through hell training just because he was in a bad mood?

When I rose from my seat, Seo Daeryong asked,

“Where are you going?”

“To buy liquor. Something very strong today.”

“Ah! Are you going to stop the Drunken Demon?”

As I left the office, I said,

“There will be no duel. Because he’ll die by my hand first.”

I told him to reconcile, and now he is going to have a duel instead? I really cannot live like this. I just cannot.

Chapter 139: Punishment for the Cowardly Drunkard!
--------------------------------------------------
The Drunken Demon was sleeping dead to the world. He must have drunk so much that he did not even notice I had arrived.

“Lord Drunken Demon, Lord Drunken Demon!”

No matter how many times I called him, he did not wake. At times like this, he was nothing but a drunkard.

I lifted the Drunken Demon clean off the ground and threw him into the lake.

Splash!

The Drunken Demon fell into the water, but even after I waited a good while, he did not come up.

“I swear, I can’t live like this. I can’t!”

I jumped into the lake as well.

The Drunken Demon had sunk beneath the lake like a corpse. I took him with me and rose back to the surface.

“Come to your senses!”

The Drunken Demon opened his eyes and only then recognized me.

“Second Young Master?”

I climbed onto the boat first. The Drunken Demon did not climb up, but floated on the water, staring at the sky.

“Aren’t you coming up?”

“I’ll stay here.”

“What are you, a rebellious youth? This is truly unbearable to watch. At least your face is handsome enough that I can let it slide.”

“Stop looking at my handsome face and hand me some liquor.”

“Are you going to show me the wondrous feat of drinking while lying on water now? Which martial art of the Drunken Demon is this?”

When I did not give him the liquor, the Drunken Demon reached out his hand. The liquor on the ferryboat flew through the air by Void Seizing. He drank while lying there.

“The rumors are true, aren’t they?”

The Drunken Demon gave no answer.

“What on earth happened?”

As if he did not want to answer, the Drunken Demon swam away from the boat while still lying on his back.

I rowed after him.

“I told you to reconcile, and now you’re having a duel? This means you want to fight me, doesn’t it? Come out. Let’s have it out.”

“It wasn’t because of me. It was because of that old man. He threw away the liquor I gave him as a gift. And right in the front yard, as if to make sure I saw it.”

The Drunken Demon, sensitive to smells, had realized the liquor had been thrown away. It had gone wrong from the start.

“And do you know what that old man said to me after that?”

The Drunken Demon sat up. He laid the empty liquor bottle flat and sat on top of it. Neither the bottle nor the Drunken Demon sank.

“He asked if it felt good to get drunk and live while running away from reality. Said I was the most pathetic of all the Demon Venerables. So I said, aren’t there seniors who run away from reality even without getting drunk? I asked who was more pathetic.”

“You two are exactly the same. You’re not children. How very mature of you both.”

“He started it!”

“And then?”

“I was going to end it there. But then he went and compared me.”

“To the Extreme Evil Laughing Demon, I presume.”

The Drunken Demon nodded and mimicked the Blood Heaven Blade Demon’s voice.

“You’re worse than that masked bastard. The masked bastard hides only his face, not even his heart.”

He was good at imitation too; if I closed my eyes and listened for a moment, it truly sounded as though the Blood Heaven Blade Demon were speaking right in front of me.

“How dare he compare me! And to the likes of the Extreme Evil Laughing Demon! It nearly popped the lid off my liquor.”

“And then?”

“What do you mean, and then? I got angry and shot back. I said I was better than a petty man who spent his entire life suffering over one woman.”

I shook my head with an expression that said there was no stopping him.

“You didn’t just touch his reverse scale. You tore it right off.”

“That old man gets to be a dragon scale, and I’m what, a fish scale? He touched mine first!”

With a splash, the Drunken Demon went into the water. He must have gone all the way to the bottom, for he surfaced only after quite some time.

With only his head out of the water, I asked him,

“Just go and apologize. Say you were wrong. If he curses at you, grit your teeth, listen, and come back. If you apologize, he won’t insist on fighting.”

“No. If anyone apologizes, it should be him.”

“Then you have to fight.”

“I don’t want that either!”

“Then what do you want to do?”

“I don’t know.”

Again, the Drunken Demon swam away. I did not chase after him and rowed in the opposite direction. Then the Drunken Demon swam after me.

“The reason I’m trying not to fight the Blade Demon is because of you.”

“Because of me?”

Then words I had not expected flowed from his mouth.

“If he fights me, that Blade Demon you like so much will die.”

Leaving him there with that serious expression, I rowed even faster.

“I don’t believe the words of a drunk man.”

The Drunken Demon’s voice came from behind me.

“I’m telling you, he’ll die!”

“I understand, so sober up and wait. I’m going to see Elder Blade Demon. If you’re still drunk then, you’ll die by my hand!”

After leaving the Great Drunken Grove, I went straight to find the Blood Heaven Blade Demon.

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon was training again today. He was executing techniques rougher and more violent than usual. Judging by the atmosphere alone, it was the eve of a decisive battle.

“Why are you training so hard just to catch one drunkard?”

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon withdrew his blade and sat on a rock in the yard. His entire body was soaked in sweat.

“What happens if the unspoken rule that Demon Venerables do not fight one another is broken?”

“Nothing happens.”

“You mean there are no sanctions?”

“That’s right.”

“Then why is the unspoken rule never broken?”

“Because everyone knows one of them will certainly die. There is a high chance of dying in the fight, and even if the loser is not killed by the opponent, his life as a Demon Venerable ends. Who would follow as their leader someone who lost to another Demon Venerable?”

“And knowing that, you still intend to duel?”

“Why? Are you worried I might be driven from my seat as a Demon Venerable?”

“You have lived your whole life as a Demon Venerable. Living as an ordinary demonic practitioner will not be easy.”

Then the Blood Heaven Blade Demon said something unexpected.

“Do I look foolish enough to stake my seat as Demon Venerable because of that sinister fellow?”

“Then why are you trying to fight?”

“For your sake.”

Coincidentally, both of them were saying it was for my sake. One said he would not fight for my sake, and the other said he would fight for my sake.

“It was obvious that he suddenly started treating me kindly because of you. You told him to reconcile with me, didn’t you? I knew that and deliberately provoked him.”

“I heard the Drunken Demon brought up Senior Sword Venerable?”

“What of it? Everyone thinks the Sword Venerable is some great weakness of mine, but in truth, that isn’t the case. Rather, at this age, one learns to make use of one’s weaknesses.”

“So you are saying you did not ask to fight because you were angry, but that you did so intentionally.”

“Yes. I thought you might be able to make use of this confrontation. If a problem like this arose, naturally you would intervene. Would it not make it easier for you to make use of him?”

“You thought that far ahead?”

“Did I judge wrongly?”

“No. You did well.”

“In this matter, at least, I will follow your will. If you tell me to fight, I will fight. If you tell me not to, I will not. This fight is not my fight, but yours.”

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon had seen through the essence of this matter. I was the one who had tried to make them reconcile in the first place, and I was the one now trying to mediate. In the end, the Blade Demon had understood from the start that this was a problem between the Drunken Demon and me. He had probably even expected that I would come to see him like this.

“Do not hope for me to reconcile with him. But I understand that you need him. If it had been anyone else, I would have stopped them to the end, but if it is you, perhaps you can even take that sinister fellow with you. I have decided to trust you, not the Drunken Demon. So use this chance to bring him under your control.”

Suddenly, that thought came to me. From now on, I must not lose any fight. With such a person standing beside me for my sake, how could I possibly lose?

So many things I wanted to say to him rose in my mind that I simply swallowed them all.

“Thank you very much.”

I bowed politely and turned to leave. Behind me, the Blood Heaven Blade Demon spoke quietly.

“I should at least earn the price of the tonic you had made for me.”

You are not earning the price of a tonic, but of a miraculous elixir.

* * *

“It seems you didn’t want to die after all.”

In the meantime, the Drunken Demon had sobered up, changed into fresh clothes, and was waiting at the Drunken Dream Pavilion.

“What did that old man say? I know you’ll take his side.”

“What Elder Blade Demon said is not important.”

“What? Why? Did he curse me again? He did, didn’t he?”

“This matter is between you and me, Lord Drunken Demon. It happened because I asked you to reconcile with Elder Blade Demon, didn’t it?”

“I made an effort.”

“Through this incident, I realized it clearly. Reconciliation between you two is impossible. This is, in the end, a matter between you and me. The two of us should resolve it.”

“How do you propose we resolve it?”

I set down the liquor bottle I had brought in front of him.

The Drunken Demon asked in surprise,

“You want to resolve it with liquor?”

“Let’s drink until we die. What need is there for side dishes?”

The Drunken Demon, who had been staring at me blankly, burst into laughter. It was the heartiest laugh I had heard from him since meeting him.

“Good, that’s it. This is why I like you, Second Young Master!”

The Drunken Demon called Yeobin.

“Bring liquor. Bring every bottle I have saved!”

I began drinking with the Drunken Demon.

Previously, during my spar with Father, I had fought without placing any restrictions on myself for the first time.

This drinking session was the same. Until now, I had drunk carefully so as not to make a mistake. But today, I simply drank. I returned to the days of my wandering years when I had lived steeped in liquor.

“You drink this well. How did you hold back all this time?”

“The cult cannot have two Drunken Demons, can it?”

At my words, the Drunken Demon roared with laughter. When I set my mind to drinking, the Drunken Demon was beside himself with joy.

The liquor drunk in this pavilion had a charm different from drinking on the ferryboat.

The Drunken Demon told all sorts of stories, from the women he had met in his youth to gossip about the other Demon Venerables.

I, too, spoke without hiding my true feelings, from stories of how my elder brother had tormented me in childhood to stories about Father.

And so we gradually became drunk.

I had drunk with bravado, but my capacity could not match the Drunken Demon’s. The Drunken Demon had even drunk twice as much as I had.

“Ah, now I understand why you jump in there, Lord Drunken Demon.”

I jumped into the lake as well. After swimming one lap and coming back out, I felt somewhat alive again.

“That lake is my side dish.”

“I will drink slowly from now on.”

“Stop drinking. You’ve already drunk a great deal.”

“Do you know something? The coolest thing about you, Lord Drunken Demon, is that you do not force liquor on others.”

“What am I to do if something so trifling is the coolest thing about me?”

“Trifling? Do you know how cool that is? It is truly cool precisely because it is you, the Drunken Demon, and no one else, who does that. Ah, I’m drunk.”

I flopped down on my back.

The Drunken Demon did not scold me for my rudeness. He kept drinking, and I lay there, listening to his stories. It was the sort of gathering some people might not have been able to endure, because the Drunken Demon boasted tremendously about himself. But I did not find those boasts particularly grating. To be honest, they were entertaining.

As we drank like that, I made a proposal to him.

“Shall we arm-wrestle?”

“Arm-wrestle? Using internal energy?”

“Of course not. A contest of man against man. What do you say?”

“Good. But it would be boring if we did it for nothing, so let’s make a wager. The winner gets one wish granted.”

“A wish over a mere arm-wrestling match?”

“This is a real contest. Imagine our cult leader, the Martial Alliance Leader, and the Evil Path Alliance Leader all gathering to compete. One arm-wrestling match with the fate of the martial world at stake! Martial artists would find something like that much more entertaining.”

“Very well. Then go into the water once and come back, so you won’t make excuses later that you lost because you were drunk.”

“It’s fine. Someone like you is no problem!”

We cleared the liquor table and arm-wrestled. How could he possibly beat my youthful strength? Just as I was forcing his arm down, I felt the Drunken Demon secretly use internal energy.

“I knew you’d do this! You little drunkard!”

I began using internal energy as well.

Then the Drunken Demon started using his internal energy in earnest. Had all the liquor he had ever drunk turned into internal energy? Contrary to my expectations, his internal energy was fierce and powerful. It was enough to make me realize that his claim that he could kill the Blood Heaven Blade Demon was not mere bluster.

But he could not overcome my internal energy. Today, I hid neither my drinking capacity nor my internal energy.

Crash!

The liquor table broke, and the contest was decided.

“Punishment for the cowardly drunkard! I won!”

I sprang up from my seat and jumped up and down like a child.

“This is invalid. At the start, you were the first to use strength! And I drank more liquor than you, didn’t I? I’ll go into the water and come back, so let’s have a proper match!”

There was no way his sophistry would work when he had used internal energy first.

I let his words pass by and leaned against the railing. The sunset that had just begun to fall was dyeing the whole world red. After gazing at that scene for a moment, I conveyed my true feelings to the Drunken Demon. Since these were words I could not say while looking at him, I spoke while gazing at the red-stained lake.

“Everyone says you are sinister and suspicious, Lord Drunken Demon, but I enjoyed drinking with you.”

“!”

“You know how there are people like that, don’t you? Everyone else says they are no good, but to me, they are good people. You are that kind of person to me, Lord Drunken Demon. I liked the way you showed off, I liked the way you bluffed, and I liked the hollow look you revealed in your eyes from time to time. That was why I also felt anxious. What on earth was your reason for approaching me? To be honest, I thought it would be fine even if your intentions were impure. Who has no secrets, and who has no selfish plans? I thought it was all fine. I only hoped for one thing. Please let it be something I can understand. Please let it not be something that prevents us from becoming drinking companions.”

Sensing that I was speaking sincerely, the Drunken Demon’s eyes wavered.

“Our Second Young Master has a serious drinking habit. Once drunk, he says everything in his heart.”

Only then did I look at him and say,

“Elder Brother.”

At my words, the Drunken Demon’s eyes went wide. He truly looked startled.

“From today onward, let us call each other elder and younger brother. That is my wish.”

“You won, so why?”

“Because it was what I wanted as well.”

There is a kind of surprise and passion brought by an unexpected emotion. All of that was contained in the Drunken Demon’s expression.

“You… you truly must be mad.”

“I hear those words from you too, Lord Drunken Demon—no, Elder Brother. Since I have heard it from all the Demon Venerables, I suppose I really must be mad.”

“That madness of yours is all the more frightening because it is subtle.”

The Drunken Demon straightened his posture, brought over a cup, and poured liquor for me.

“Younger Brother, accept a cup.”

“Yes, Elder Brother.”

I drank the liquor he gave me. This time, I poured liquor for him. He drank as well.

“Please do not be too moved. It is true that my heart is drawn to you, but I am also doing this because I absolutely need you in order to become the successor. So only half of this is pure feeling. As for the other half, well, let us fill it with liquor. Then I will take my leave for today.”

Just as I was about to rise first because I felt unnecessarily awkward, the Drunken Demon abruptly spoke.

“It was because of me.”

Words I had not expected flowed from his mouth.

“The reason the Blade Demon and the Sword Venerable’s relationship worsened was because of me.”

As I stared at him in astonishment, the Drunken Demon confessed something even more shocking.

“I drove a wedge between the two of them.”

Chapter 140: In the Days of the Foolish Beast.
----------------------------------------------
Chwima’s face flushed red.

Whether it was the alcohol, the sunset, or the shame buried deep for so many years, it was impossible to tell.

“I should drink more.”

I, who had been about to stand, sat back down.

I know the reason why Hyeolcheon Doma and Ilhwa Geomjon’s relationship soured. That the two had once liked each other as man and woman and parted on bad terms. But I hadn’t known that Chwima was involved in that breakup.

“From the old days, the Geomjon and I would occasionally drink and talk. One day, the Geomjon asked me. Whether I knew what kind of person Doma was. I asked why she asked. She said Doma had drawn her a picture. I asked to see what kind of picture. It was a beauty portrait of the Geomjon. The moment I saw that picture, drawn more beautifully than the real thing, I could tell. Ah, so Doma likes the Geomjon. I had a hunch right then.”

Them in their youth. Hyeolcheon Doma drawing a picture for a woman! Truly an unimaginable sight.

“As expected, from then on Doma began expressing his liking for the Geomjon. Inviting her to tea, going for walks. The Geomjon told me everything about her meetings with Doma. Do you know how I felt then?”

“Was it bad?”

“Very much so.”

“Did you like the Geomjon too?”

“It wasn’t like that from the start. Because Doma liked the Geomjon, my heart suddenly grew urgent. Until then, I had thought the Geomjon liked the Sect Leader. So I had thought she wouldn’t accept anyone, but that wasn’t the case. She had purely respected the Sect Leader.”

I poured alcohol into Chwima’s cup. He stared blankly at the cup, recalling the past.

“One day, while drinking, I subtly revealed to the Geomjon that I liked her. However……”

Chwima couldn’t continue. I sighed and spoke in his stead.

“You were rejected.”

Chwima slowly nodded.

“She said her heart went out to Doma.”

Suddenly Chwima shouted in anger.

“Does that make sense? Does it make sense?! That scrawny, ugly Doma, even much older than us! I was so furious I couldn’t sleep a wink. I was angry at myself for not confessing more carefully, and even angrier that she had caught my flustered expression at the moment of rejection.”

Without hiding anything, Chwima told me honestly everything of that time.

“These thoughts tormented me. Truthfully, I hadn’t liked the Geomjon that much. So perhaps the thought of not wanting to give her to someone else had spurred me to confess? Making such excuses because I was rejected, the fool! For several days I couldn’t sleep.”

Thinking of the current sinister Chwima, it was a purity truly impossible to imagine.

“Isn’t youth the time of foolish beasts led by instinct?”

“But you weren’t?”

I was too.

“Damn it! Just thinking about that time makes me so embarrassed and annoyed I could die!”

Chwima sprang up and paced back and forth in the pavilion. Then he leaned against the railing and looked at the now-darkened lake.

“From that day, my heart grew twisted. Though I didn’t show it outwardly, I wanted to tear the two apart somehow. But as time passed, the Geomjon’s fondness for Doma only grew. Then came a certain day.”

Chwima lit the lantern attached to the pavilion pillar. Getting angry, speaking, pacing, lighting a fire. His complicated heart was conveyed through his actions. When the lantern lit, the surroundings brightened.

“I was walking down the pleasure house corridor when I heard Doma’s voice. He was drinking with martial artists of the Nam Dojong. I don’t know if they were talking about the Geomjon, but the Nam Dojong martial artists were asking about the Geomjon. How much he liked her, had they kissed? Had they slept together? The kind of things friends say when they gather, but I was furious to the tips of my hair. Those bastards!”

Chwima emptied his drink. I drank as well. Perhaps because of the mood, I didn’t get drunk even after drinking. I felt as if I were standing in that pleasure house corridor with young Chwima.

“I should have just kicked the door open and flipped the table then. You bastards! Shut your mouths!”

“Why didn’t you?”

“The moment I broke that door, I felt like my life would end too.”

Those of us in the present know. That even if he had, nothing much would have happened. Looking back, it would only be remembered as a day of fighting and cursing.

“The next day, when I met the Geomjon, I told her about yesterday. The truth was, I met Doma at the pleasure house yesterday. That alone would have upset her, but I even passed on words he never said. I spoke as if the words those with him were spouting were Doma’s own words. That Doma was talking about kissing you. I still vividly remember my heart racing madly as I said those words. Me, doing such a despicable thing? Me, being such a lowly person? My heart raced and my head reproached me, but my mouth was saying, ‘Men usually put on airs in front of courtesans.’ I don’t even know if there were courtesans in that room that day.”

Crack.

The pavilion railing was torn off in Chwima’s hand. Rage and regret could be felt from his drunken eyes. I could now clearly see where that hollow gaze had originated.

“Some time after that, the Geomjon told me. That she had fought terribly with Doma and broken up. From that day on, the two fought every time they met.”

“Did you continue to drive a wedge between them afterward?”

Chwima shook his head.

“There was no need. Once the relationship twisted, it unraveled uncontrollably. Rather, these days I freely drive wedges. I even drink with the Geomjon while cursing Doma for fun.”

“Have you considered apologizing to the two of them?”

“I have.”

“But why didn’t you?”

“Had it been for some grand reason, I might have apologized to the Geomjon. But to say I did it out of jealousy, I simply couldn’t. Or perhaps I still wished the two wouldn’t do well.”

Chwima looked at me and added.

“I am such a petty and despicable fellow.”

“I know.”

“What?”

“Then did you think I considered you an upright and honest person? Sinister and wicked……”

“Enough!”

It seemed his stomach churned to hear his own slander from another’s mouth after all.

“What did I do so wrong! If they were going to break up over a few words, they were going to break up anyway, weren’t they? Right? It’s true he went to the pleasure house, and it’s true he brought up the Geomjon in the first place, isn’t it? Who told him to go? Who told him to meet me there? Damn it!”

I realized. That for all those long years, it hadn’t only been Doma and the Geomjon who fought. Perhaps Chwima had fought the fiercest of all.

“You’re right. If those two truly loved each other, they shouldn’t have been shaken by such things. Even if left alone, they would have broken up for some other reason.”

“Right? Isn’t that right?”

When I took his side, Chwima came beside me and giggled. Then he suddenly sighed.

“When they said they broke up, I wasn’t the least bit happy.”

Now was the time to ask why.

“Why did you tell me this?”

“Didn’t you keep asking? Why I approached you. Not long ago at a drinking gathering, the Geomjon said. That you want to make Doma and herself your left and right wings.”

“Did you come to cut the wings? Or to mend them so we can fly together?”

Chwima shook his head.

“I don’t know either. Seeing Doma and the Geomjon together around you, I just thought I should jump in too. Though I didn’t expect to end up saying all this.”

“Do you feel better now that you’ve said it all?”

“I’m already regretting it. Why did I have to reveal such pathetic deeds with my own mouth? Like I was possessed by a ghost. Completely caught up in that talk about half-purity or whatever.”

He flopped down on the floor. And looking blankly up at the pavilion ceiling, he spoke.

“You’ll think of this every time you see me from now on.”

“I suppose I will. Sigh, our pathetic older brother.”

At my words, Chwima laughed. The small laugh gradually grew bigger. After a refreshing bout of laughter, he asked.

“Will you reveal the truth to those two?”

I shook my head.

“That’s not for me to decide.”

“I intend never to reveal it to them, though?”

“Do that.”

“You’ll really let me do that.”

“Please do.”

Chwima sprang up and looked at me.

“Don’t you want those two to reconcile?”

“Would those two truly be happier knowing the truth?”

“!”

“Setting aside hatred for you, those two would grieve even more that their love failed for such a reason. Because it cannot be turned back, that regret would only grow. Can those two start a new love now? Is that possible? In the end, all three of you would become more miserable than you are now.”

I think it is a truth that must be buried forever. Hyeolcheon Doma might fly into a rage and try to kill Chwima. Even if he killed Chwima, Hyeolcheon Doma would pay the price as well. And what of the Geomjon’s heart in the midst of that? After all, this happened because Chwima liked her.

“Think of it as using up your one chance to reveal the truth on me right now.”

There will be a great difference between whether you told me or not. Because this past that clings like a specter is not something one can face alone.

“If you free yourself from the obsession that the truth must be revealed, you might instead reconcile the two better. Starting now, try to get closer to Hyeolcheon Doma, and when drinking with the Geomjon, offer praise instead of slander. Then wouldn’t an opportunity to connect those two arise unexpectedly someday? Give them wings even now.”

“You should be the older brother.”

“I refuse. If I’m going to tease our pathetic older brother, it’s more fun if he’s the older brother.”

Chwima’s expression became much more at ease. Having said everything, he looked relieved.

“Do keep one promise.”

“What is it?”

“Cut down on drinking, and eat your snacks. Since it’s come to this, we have to let our pathetic older brother live a long life.”

I don’t know if Chwima’s fate will change with today as a turning point. Even with this, whether he’ll eventually die of drink, or live a different life.

Perhaps changing his fate will make him die even earlier. But that’s fine. Any death is better than the final moments of Chwima dying from alcoholism.

“Hyung, let’s have one last drink. I can’t drink anymore. And…… thank you for being honest with me.”

The expression with which he looked at me was something I had never seen before. Can we truly maintain this expression toward each other until the very end?

“Little brother…… thank you.”

Chwima toasted toward my cup.

* * *

The next day, Father called for me.

When I entered the Heavenly Demon Hall, I sensed the atmosphere was different from usual.

Grand Strategist Sama Myeong was there, and my older brother Geom Muyang was present as well.

After greeting Father and Sama Myeong, Geom Muyang spoke to me.

“You reek of alcohol. The smell of alcohol in a place where you meet Father?”

“I drank a bit too much yesterday. With the person who likes alcohol the most in our sect.”

I deliberately mentioned Chwima. Geom Muyang tried to act composed.

“Yes, I heard you’ve been spending time with Chwima lately. Don’t drink too much.”

Geom Muyang must be feeling displeased. He would have wanted Chwima to come cause problems for me, but instead it must look like the two of us are having a good time.

Even so, Geom Muyang was trying his best not to show his emotions. Truly, my older brother in front of Father becomes a different person.

“Shouldn’t you be more careful, Hyung?”

“What do you mean?”

“I heard you’ve been hanging around with the Poison King these days? Mine is alcohol, but yours is poison, isn’t it? Be careful, Hyung.”

Even in front of Father, a sharp war of nerves unfolded. I deliberately didn’t hide it. From Father, and from my older brother.

As could be seen from Chwima’s case, emotions hidden and hidden again eventually cause problems. Fighting openly like this and engaging in a battle of nerves. This is the right way if I’m going to fight to save my older brother. Because if you’re angry outwardly you curse, but if you’re angry inwardly you send assassins.

Father watched our conversation with an unknowable gaze, then signaled Sama Myeong with a nod.

Then Sama Myeong stepped forward and spoke.

“Today, different missions will be given to the two young masters.”

In Sama Myeong’s hand were two red envelopes.

The inevitable had finally come. The true trial to decide the successor had begun.

“To ensure fairness, please choose whichever one you prefer.”

The moment I saw those envelopes, my heart surged.

Before the regression, today came to mind. The two missions given to us.

My older brother succeeded, and I failed.

By botching this mission, I completely fell away from the successor’s position.

Just as in the past, this time too my older brother gave me the first chance to choose.

“Choose first. Don’t regret it later if you chose wrong.”

“That probably won’t happen.”

Slowly, I reached out my hand and chose one of the two envelopes.

Chapter 141: Make the Right Choice This Time.
---------------------------------------------
I chose the path I knew.

Before the regression, I had chosen the mission on the right, and today, too, I chose the envelope on the right.

I can handle any mission now!

This was not the time to show such confidence. It was an opportunity to humbly make up for my past failure.

Inside the envelope was a single sheet of paper, and four characters were written on it.

Punishment of False Demons (僞魔懲罰).

It was the very order I had been given in the past.

Sima Ming sent me a voice transmission to explain. It was so that neither of us would know the other’s mission.

—Recently, a strange organization called the Tianming Society (天命會) has begun operating in the shadows of the murim. They have raised the banner of creating a new murim under Heaven’s mandate, but in truth they are people seeking private gain. Of late, they have caused several incidents while impersonating the Demon Venerables of our cult, and matters have reached a point where they can no longer be ignored. Second Young Master, find their society leader, execute him, and eliminate the Tianming Society.

In truth, when I heard this voice transmission before the regression, I looked down on them. Not because they had put their skills forward, but because they had gone around impersonating the Demon Venerables of our cult.

But now I took it differently. The fact that they dared impersonate Demon Venerables meant either that they were confident they could handle the consequences, or that some other scheme was hidden behind it. These bastards were not ordinary bastards.

“I shall obey the order.”

Once I accepted my mission, Geom Muyang chose the remaining envelope. Of course, I knew very well what mission was written inside that envelope.

After Sima Ming conveyed the basic information to Geom Muyang by voice transmission as well, he spoke to the two of us.

“You must resolve the mission alone. However, while the two of you are carrying out your missions, you may use all of the information networks of our cult’s Tongtian Pavilion, and you may call upon the Demon Army, the Four Greats of the Demonic Path, or one of the Eight Demon Venerables of your choosing, one time only.”

It meant that while we had to solve it alone, we could receive help from the cult when it came time to subjugate them.

When Sima Ming finished speaking, Father said,

“A frog in a well or a flower in a greenhouse cannot lead our cult. Resolve it properly and return!”

This was my father, who did not offer a single word of caution even while entrusting us with such dangerous missions. I smiled and eased the rigid atmosphere.

“Father, which one of us do you hope returns successful?”

At my question, the corners of Father’s mouth rose slightly. I could sense that he was forcibly holding back something he wanted to say.

In truth, Father’s gaze as he looked down at us was impartial. He did not send either me or my elder brother a personal look.

Before the regression, I had not been looking at Father in this moment. I had been thinking only of completing the mission somehow.

But now, I was looking at Father.

Father, don’t worry. I’ll resolve it safely and come back.

I conveyed that faith with my eyes. This, too, was an effort to become the successor. The more Father trusted me, the closer I would step toward the position of heir.

My gaze, which had been directed at Father, turned to Geom Muyang.

‘Brother, do well and come back like you did before. I’ll do well and come back too. Ah, and don’t look at me so coldly when you don’t even know how I feel.’

* * *

The next morning, as I was preparing to depart, Ian came to see me.

“You called for me?”

“I’ll be leaving the cult for a while and then returning.”

“Where are you going?”

“I can’t tell you.”

I raised a finger and pointed upward. Ian realized that I was leaving to carry out an order handed down from the Heavenly Demon Hall.

“Bring me back something tasty when you return!”

“I’m not going out to play. I’m going to handle something extremely dangerous.”

“I’m busy with martial arts training, so I’ll be going now.”

“Terrifying enemies are going to come pouring out.”

“Have a safe trip!”

Ian, who had run all the way to the door like that, let out a sigh and turned back.

“How nice would it be if I could really leave like this? How dangerous is it? I’ll go with you and help.”

She had changed from Ian joking around to Ian worrying about me day and night.

“It’s something I have to do alone. And I was joking too. You don’t need to worry.”

“Really?”

“Don’t worry for nothing. Just focus on your own work.”

“Actually, Lord Blue Mask came to see me not long ago.”

I had watched the two of them meet, but I pretended not to know.

“Did he say he’d become the squad leader?”

“No, he’s still thinking about it. He just asked me a few things and left.”

“What does your intuition say?”

“Half and half. To be honest, right now I desperately hope he comes, but if he really does, I think I might worry even more. Because he’d be living a new life because of me.”

“Worrying about what comes after is disregarding Blue Mask’s own deliberation. At a glance, it may seem as though you have to take responsibility for him, but that isn’t the case at all.”

“It isn’t?”

“That’s right. Blue Mask will be coming after careful consideration as well. He’ll come because, after working the abacus hard, he judged that coming to you would be more beneficial. If he comes and decides it isn’t right, he’ll go back. So don’t feel burdened.”

At my words, Ian grinned.

“Become strong, Ian. Then nine out of ten problems you’re worrying over will resolve themselves.”

After parting with Ian, I left my residence.

Before leaving, I intended to stop by and see the Demon Venerables one by one. They were people I did not particularly need to look after, but this time, I wanted to see each of them before I went.

The first place I visited was the Extreme Evil Lesser Demon’s Valley of the Wicked.

He had always been in that white room, staring at the wall, but today Blue Mask informed me of his absence.

“The Demon Venerable left the cult several days ago.”

“Can you tell me where he went?”

“He left without telling even me.”

“When he returns, please tell him I stopped by.”

“Yes, I will.”

As I turned to leave, Blue Mask asked me,

“There is one thing I would like to ask.”

“Please speak.”

“You may find this question strange coming out of nowhere, but what kind of organization is the Ghost Shadow Unit to you, Second Young Master?”

Because I knew very well why he was asking this question, I answered honestly.

“It is the organization that will become my main force in the future. It is also the organization led by the person I cherish most. It will probably become the busiest organization in the murim. So much so that you’ll naturally find yourself saying that things were better under the Extreme Evil Lesser Demon.”

I could see the eyes behind Blue Mask’s mask smile.

“Thank you for telling me.”

Blue Mask bowed his head in greeting. I could have said a few words to him for Ian’s sake, but I did not. Only if he judged and decided for himself would even his regrets become worthwhile regrets.

Next, I stopped by the Ilhwa Sword Venerable.

After hearing from the Drunken Demon about what had happened in the past, I found myself thinking about her life at least once.

If things had truly worked out between her and the Blood Heaven Blade Demon, would she have been happier than she was now?

“You seem to have grown even younger these days.”

“Don’t say such nonsense. I’ve been worried lately because I’ve gained so many wrinkles.”

Even as she said that, the Ilhwa Sword Venerable’s face brightened at the rare compliment on her appearance.

“I heard you drank with the Drunken Demon.”

“How did you know?”

“The Drunken Demon came by. He said he’d gotten some good liquor and gave me some. He did something he never usually does.”

“What did he say when he gave you the liquor?”

“He was so proud and pleased, saying that he and you had become sworn brothers.”

“I don’t know if that’s a good thing. If he and I are sworn brothers, the hierarchy of seniority gets all tangled.”

“Then be friends with me as well.”

“Please don’t say such things even as a joke. What I said back then was only a joke.”

The Drunken Demon probably had not come to boast about becoming sworn brothers with me. After confessing everything to me, he must have felt sorry toward her.

How the Drunken Demon would act from here on was something to be seen.

“As you know, the Drunken Demon and the Blade Demon are not on good terms. Will it be all right?”

“You have to help me, Senior.”

“How?”

“Even if you don’t feel inclined, please say good things about Elder Blade Demon too. I know. I know you are displeased with him. But I ask this of you for my sake.”

The Ilhwa Sword Venerable gave no answer. It seemed that even my face was not enough yet to erase her aversion toward the Blade Demon.

Still, I expected things to improve compared to before. If the Drunken Demon stopped speaking ill of the Blood Heaven Blade Demon and began saying positive things, then her feelings toward the Blade Demon might slowly change as well.

Next, I stopped by the Drunken Demon.

But Yeo Bin gave me unexpected news.

“The Drunken Demon has entered closed-door training.”

Because it was so sudden, I was startled.

“Closed-door training all of a sudden?”

“He said he had drunk too much lately and needed to expel some of the wine qi.”

“That’s a good thing, right?”

“Yes. It was something I truly hoped for. Thank you, Second Young Master.”

“Not at all. He promised me that he’d cut down on drinking and eat plenty of food when he drinks, so please take good care of him, Martial Artist Yeo.”

“Yes, I will.”

I had felt it for a long time. This Yeo Bin liked the Drunken Demon. Did the Drunken Demon know of her feelings? I suspected that, like the sly old fox he was, he knew and was pretending not to.

Among the Demon Venerables, the last place I stopped by was the residence of the Blood Heaven Blade Demon.

“Elder, your blade is becoming even fiercer.”

Today, too, the Blood Heaven Blade Demon was immersed in training.

“The more you grow, the fiercer your enemies will become.”

“Are you truly training in order to face my enemies? Do you intend to clash with the Demon Venerables who support my brother?”

“It is not only because of the successor struggle.”

“Then why?”

“Do you remember what I told you before? That when a dragon ascends to the heavens, it brings wind and rain with it.”

“Of course. You said that my people were being swept away by that wind and rain.”

“That’s right. You said you were just a loach stirring up muddy water, but I believe that those waves will awaken the sleeping dragons of the murim.”

“Aren’t you overestimating me too much?”

“Judging by what you have shown so far, even this is an underestimate. Be careful even after you leave. It may seem as though our cult, the Murim Alliance, and the Evil Path Alliance have divided and seized control of the murim, but compared to all martial artists, our numbers are only a small fraction. We do not know where people stronger than us may spring out from. Do not forget. The jianghu is a place where eccentrics and hidden masters are as numerous as grains of sand.”

“I will always judge carefully and move with caution.”

“And, wait a moment.”

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon went into his room and came out carrying a small box. When I opened it, there was a single medicinal pill inside.

“It is a Swift Recovery Pill (速功丹). Use it if you ever find yourself in urgent need.”

The Swift Recovery Pill was a medicinal pill that, when taken after one’s internal energy had been exhausted, allowed one to recover internal energy two or three times faster than usual.

Because it had no side effects, it could only be obtained by paying an enormous sum, and even then, it was so uncommon that it was not easy to find.

“Please keep it and use it yourself, Elder.”

“Are you planning to work this old man so hard that you’ll even make me eat a Swift Recovery Pill? You take it and use it.”

He forced the Swift Recovery Pill into my hand. Feeling his concern and hidden affection for me, my heart swelled.

Elder, don’t worry. I’ll be careful and return safely.

Since I was accepting it anyway, I accepted it in a cheerful mood.

“Is there nothing else you can give me? Please go inside and look for more.”

“There isn’t! There’s nothing left!”

“Seeing how strongly you deny it, there must be something. I’ll look forward to next time.”

I greeted him with a laugh and was about to turn away when the Blood Heaven Blade Demon asked,

“What happened with that sinister fellow?”

“We agreed to be sworn brothers from now on.”

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon first looked dumbfounded, then impressed.

“If you were a loach, you would never have been able to become sworn brothers with that sinister fellow.”

It meant that though he might not like the Drunken Demon, becoming sworn brothers with him was an impressive feat.

“I am doing as I please because I trust you, Elder.”

“And that is why this old body is swinging this heavy blade, is it not?”

After leaving the Blood Heaven Blade Demon’s residence, I left the cult.

Once I reached a deserted place, I began to run in earnest. These days, I had grown to prefer moving alone, if only because of Swift Step.

But even while running, when it was time to rest, I rested properly. My every movement on this mission would be reported to the Heavenly Demon Hall. There was no need to inform everyone that I could run at an unbelievable speed.

I ran while pushing Swift Step to its limit, and once I arrived at a predetermined point, I spent the remaining time there practicing the rest of the Wind God’s Four Steps.

Dark Shadow Step became even more stealthy, Flicker Step became even faster, and Underworld King Step became even more savage. I trained and trained again. I poured in all my effort and concentration. For the time being, my martial arts goal was unconditionally the complete mastery of the Wind God’s Four Steps.

According to the materials sent by the Tongtian Pavilion, the Tianming Society had caused several incidents throughout Hunan.

The two places where recent incidents had occurred were Yongxing (永興) and Dongkou (洞口).

In the past, I had gone to Yongxing and investigated the incident there while tracking the Tianming Society. But in the end, the Yongxing incident turned out not to be the work of the Tianming Society. It had been committed by people imitating the Tianming Society.

Because of that, I wasted a great deal of time in the past, and only belatedly went to Dongkou to follow their traces. As a result, my mind grew impatient, I missed clues I should have found, and in the end I failed the mission.

Of course, this time I made the proper choice.

After arriving in Dongkou, I went straight to Yuefeng Sect (月風門), where the incident had occurred.

Chapter 142: The Answer Lies with You.
--------------------------------------
Chusaeng (秋生), the master of the Wolpung Sect, was struggling to contain his boiling rage.

“Please do not worry too much. Several sects in Hunan have promised to cooperate with us.”

It was precisely such words that angered him. And hearing them from Seok Hyeong, a man he trusted, only made him angrier.

“Cooperate? How? Tell me specifically. How many men did they say they would send, when, and where?”

Seok Hyeong could not answer. They had only said they would help; not a single sect had come forward with such concrete terms. Despite all their efforts to keep it secret, rumors were already spreading that the Demonic Cult had become involved.

“Put yourself in their place. Do you think they would send you to deal with the Demonic Cult bastards? Outwardly, they’ll pretend to be concerned, but they’ll be too busy locking their own doors.”

Chusaeng sprang to his feet. At the force in him, as though he might smash something at any moment, Seok Hyeong tried to stop him.

“Please calm yourself.”

“How am I supposed to endure this when I’m furious? Those Demonic Cult sons of bitches! I’ll chew them alive!”

Boom!

Chusaeng thrust out a palm and punched a hole through the wall. Since the incident had occurred, more than twenty holes had already appeared in the walls. They patched one up, and he broke through another; patched that one, and another was broken.

Chusaeng felt so wronged and enraged he could hardly bear it. He could not contain the fury rising within him. And at the root of all those emotions was fear.

Fear that the bastard would return.

Chusaeng could not sleep at night, and his nerves had grown strained to the utmost.

“Even now, you should ask the Murim Alliance for help.”

The Wolpung Sect had originally been a sect that stood between the righteous and unorthodox paths, belonging neither to the Murim Alliance nor the Evil Dao Alliance.

“If I do that, we’ll be at the mercy of the Murim Alliance bastards from now on. If they resolve this matter, they’ll use it as an excuse to force us to obey the Murim Alliance unconditionally. And even if they fail to resolve it, they’ll use one pretext or another to bring us under their control.”

Sects belonging to the Murim Alliance had to pay a fixed sum to the alliance every year. The same was true of the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult and the Evil Dao Alliance. In return, when trouble arose, they could receive help.

Money was one thing, but Chusaeng did not want to belong to the righteous faction.

It was then that someone spoke his thoughts exactly.

“You wouldn’t want to serve under bastards who do nothing for you in ordinary times and yet never fail to wring money out of you, would you?”

Startled, Chusaeng and Seok Hyeong turned around. At some point, a young man had entered the main hall.

Seok Hyeong stepped between the sect master and the man, drawing his sword.

“Who are you?”

The fact that they had not noticed someone enter like this put both men on edge.

Then the young man said,

“I have come from the Divine Cult.”

The one who had startled the two of them was none other than me.

I could have announced myself from the entrance, but I deliberately did not. At present, the Wolpung Sect was in a chaotic state, with countless martial artists swarming from the gate all the way inside.

“Forgive me. I dislike crowds, so I came in quietly.”

Chusaeng had Seok Hyeong step back. He had acknowledged that if I had intended to ambush them, they would already be dead.

“I am Chusaeng, master of the Wolpung Sect.”

Having lost his father early, he was among the youngest heads of sects in Hunan Province, being in his late twenties.

I did not reveal my identity to him.

“I have come to deal with the incident that occurred in your sect. Circumstances prevent me from revealing who I am, but you should recognize this.”

I showed them the divine token engraved with the evil spirit that symbolized our cult.

Naturally, Chusaeng began with suspicion.

“A divine token can be forged. I cannot trust you on that alone.”

“How did someone as suspicious as you get deceived by them?”

“You go too far!”

Just as Seok Hyeong took a step forward, I stretched out my hand.

Swaaaah.

The demonic energy emitted from me constricted Seok Hyeong’s body.

With a brief groan, he found himself unable to move. As his breath was cut off, his face turned bright red.

“Sometimes, one’s ability can stand in for one’s identity, can it not?”

With the demonic energy dominating the surroundings, Chusaeng spoke urgently.

“Stop! I lost several subordinates this time. I cannot lose another. Release him.”

When I withdrew my demonic energy, Seok Hyeong gasped for breath and was able to move again. He glared at me with fearful eyes, but he still stood in front of Chusaeng to protect him.

“I, too, have loyal subordinates like you. If I were to lose them, I would truly feel as though my heart had been torn out. The same must be true for Sect Master Chu here, so the two of you should not act rashly.”

Though I said it to Seok Hyeong, in truth, it was also meant for Chusaeng. Quick to understand, Chusaeng sent Seok Hyeong out.

“You wait outside.”

“Yes.”

Seeing him leave obediently in this situation, it seemed Chusaeng had sent him a voice transmission. Since the two of them alone could not deal with me anyway, he had likely ordered him to go out and gather all the experts of the Wolpung Sect.

Even after only the two of us remained, Chusaeng was composed.

“You people caused the trouble, and now you come claiming you will clean it up. Just what are your intentions?”

“Not clean it up. Investigate. The previous incident was not our doing.”

Chusaeng snorted. He had been struck by the Cheonmyeong Society, which had impersonated one of our cult’s Demon Venerables, and he firmly believed it was our doing.

Several similar incidents had occurred recently, but not all the sects of the Central Plains were aware of this yet.

In other words, it was still a time before the Cheonmyeong Society had fully surfaced.

“Tell me what happened. If I had come to harm you, would I be standing here trading words like this?”

Perhaps those words reached him, for Chusaeng finally began to speak of the past incident.

“Not long ago, someone came here, claiming to be from the Divine Cult.”

“Who did he say he was?”

“He was Geugak Soma.”

I had known they had impersonated a Demon Venerable, but I had not known the one they chose was Geugak Soma.

A hollow laugh escaped me. Of all people, why would they go around pretending to be Geugak Soma? What did they plan to do if they were caught by the real Geugak Soma?

“What did he look like?”

“He came wearing a white mask.”

Then again, since he wore a mask, Geugak Soma was probably the easiest Demon Venerable to impersonate.

“You believed him just because he wore a mask?”

At that, Chusaeng spoke with conviction.

“He was the real Geugak Soma.”

“Why do you believe that?”

In that instant, Chusaeng was swept up in anger.

“He killed the experts of my sect in an instant. His finger wind pierced holes through their faces, and his palm art shattered their bodies into pieces. Among the dead was one of the top three experts of our sect. If he was not Geugak Soma, then who in the world was he?”

It seemed they had even imitated Geugak Soma’s unique demonic arts, the Mageuk Gwangpokjang and the Hyeorangji. With the situation like that, Chusaeng had no choice but to believe it.

“After killing those who tried to protect me and turning the place into a sea of blood, Geugak Soma forced us to submit to the Demonic Cult and swear loyalty.”

The bastard had committed unnecessary slaughter solely to frighten his opponent. That act alone made him an evil deserving of death.

“And do you know what else he did? It would have been less absurd than this if he had simply massacred us. I would have thought we had just been unlucky enough to run into him and die.”

“What did he do?”

“As he left, he stole our money. He told us to hand over all the money we could provide right then. He cleaned out all the sect’s funds. And yet thieves like you dare—!”

Whatever insult might have followed, it was a situation I could understand.

“How much money did he take?”

“About sixty-two thousand nyang.”

Only then could I understand Chusaeng’s rage. I also understood why there were so many holes in the walls.

“If we had fought for some other reason and lost, I would not feel so wronged. Submit to the Demonic Cult? Don’t make me laugh! That was just an excuse. He killed my subordinates like that so he could steal our money. Do you understand what I am saying? My subordinates died for mere money! If he wanted money, he should have asked for it from the beginning!”

Unable to hold back, he roared.

“Uaaaaaah! I am so furious I cannot endure it. I will not die a natural death.”

Listening to Chusaeng, I thought he must ordinarily have been a pleasant and cheerful person. At this rate, he would never return to who he had been. The families of the dead, their friends, the survivors. The number of victims had yet to be determined.

“Right now, Sect Master Chu, you are so angry that you cannot make use of your strengths.”

“And what exactly are my strengths!”

I took a sheet of paper from my robes and read it.

“Chusaeng, master of the Wolpung Sect. Clear-headed and possesses sound judgment. Strong pride, with a hot-blooded side.”

“What bastard made that assessment?”

“The head of our cult’s Tongcheon Pavilion Hunan branch. Would you like to meet him?”

Chusaeng stared at me with eyes that seemed torn between believing and disbelieving, then rebuked himself in a drained voice.

“What good are a clear head and sound judgment? I was only helplessly victimized like this.”

“Even so, should you not take revenge?”

At the word revenge, strength returned to Chusaeng’s eyes.

“I will. I absolutely will.”

There had been a time when I felt the same.

Revenge when I slept, revenge when I ate, revenge when I looked at people. A time when my mind was filled only with thoughts of how I would take revenge, and I could not live a normal life.

Chusaeng was the same. The enemy before him was not one he could face by ordinary means.

But fortunately, just as I had met Seojin, he had met me.

“I will help you with that revenge.”

Chusaeng went to the table and gulped down the cold tea straight from the pot.

“Was it truly not your doing?”

“Doesn’t something seem strange to you as well? If the real Geugak Soma had been after your money, do you think he would have taken only sixty-two thousand nyang and left? He would have pulled up the very pillars of this place.”

“What if it was merely a kind of amusement?”

“If he enjoyed such amusement, he would die by the hand of the Cult Leader, the Heavenly Demon himself. Let us say it was amusement. If that had been the case, who here would have survived?”

“To be honest, I found it strange as well. That is why I thought Geugak Soma had gone mad.”

If he stared at white walls like that long enough, he might someday go mad.

“He is still sane. I met him not long ago.”

At my words that I had met Geugak Soma, Chusaeng flinched. But because of the ability I had shown him, he did not seem to think I was bluffing.

“Are you saying Geugak Soma is truly in your cult?”

For a moment, I hesitated to answer. When I had set out, Geugak Soma had not been in the Valley of Villains.

“He was.”

I did not answer that way because I wanted to avoid an argument with Chusaeng. It was because I was certain this was not Geugak Soma’s doing. Father says one can never know what is inside another person, but there are still things one can know. No matter how wicked Geugak Soma may be, he is not the kind of person to commit such an act.

“If that man was a fake, then the one in your cult must be fake as well.”

It made me wonder just how overwhelming the figure he had seen had been for him to say such a thing.

I slowly walked around the main hall and looked about. I tried to recall the scene from that time. The scene of the Wolpung Sect’s martial artists being killed and their money stolen.

Why in the world had they done this? For money? Or to draw us in? In this incomprehensible incident, I had to find a clue about them.

“Fine. Let us say he was a fake, as you claim. Then why did that fake bastard do something like this? Was he trying to sow discord with the Murim Alliance? Was he trying to start a war?”

“If that were the case, he would have touched a righteous sect belonging to the Murim Alliance.”

“Then what in the world is going on?”

“That is why I came. To uncover what is going on.”

After staring at me for a while, Chusaeng opened the door and went outside. Outside, Seok Hyeong and dozens of Wolpung Sect martial artists who had gone ahead were waiting in formation.

Chusaeng went out alone and spoke to Seok Hyeong.

“Have all the men withdraw, and clean out a guest room for him. We will be treating him as an honored guest for the time being.”

“Yes, understood.”

Seok Hyeong looked relieved. Though he had gathered the martial artists, he seemed to have judged that they could not handle me.

Chusaeng came back inside.

“The thought that you may be in league with that bastard still will not leave my mind. Even so, I intend to try trusting you. Do you know why? Because right now, I have to grasp at even a straw.”

Then he suddenly looked up at the ceiling and shouted,

“Father! Grandfather! Please help me! Please do not let me be toyed with any longer! Watch over this sect! Save my subordinates!”

Considering his fate had led him to meet me, his ancestors were already helping him plenty.

After shouting once, he revealed his true feelings. He lowered his voice so his subordinates outside would not hear.

“Because you came, I am ruined. If, as you say, he was a fake Geugak Soma, then I cannot stop seeking revenge. If it had been the real Demonic Cult, I might have been able to give up, telling myself there was nothing I could do. Because of you, I have stepped onto a bridge from which I can never return. I will have to cross this endless bridge of revenge for the rest of my life!”

“I will help you cross that bridge.”

In the past, I had arrived too late, and in my urgency, I had left this place without even investigating properly.

In those days, I did not look at Father, and I did not look at Ian. It was a time when I looked only at myself and not at others, so naturally, I did not look at Chusaeng either.

“There are countless sects in murim, yet there must be a reason they chose your sect in particular. Why you? Young, clear-headed, hot-blooded, and proud. Aside from what is written on this paper, what else is there about you?”

I approached him, stared intently into his face, and said,

“There must be a reason they chose you. Now give me the answer!”

Chapter 143: The Reason You Appeared Before Me.
-----------------------------------------------
“If I knew the answer, would I be in this state?”

Chusaeng shouted.

He certainly was hot-blooded. There couldn’t be many people who could shout like this at a demonic practitioner, knowing full well that the other party’s martial arts were far superior to their own.

“My apologies. These days, the vessel of my patience is filled to the brim and sloshing over. Drop so much as a pebble into it and it spills.”

“I understand.”

“What do you understand?”

He flared up again, then soon apologized.

“I’m sorry. This isn’t something I should be taking out on you.”

“It’s all right. In a situation like this, wouldn’t it be stranger to remain calm?”

Chusaeng said to me in a slightly softened tone.

“What should we do now?”

“Do as you normally would. Pretend I’m not here and proceed with whatever you meant to do.”

“How am I supposed to pretend you’re not here when you are?”

“Focus only on yourself. In a situation where your life hangs in the balance, what can’t be done?”

I took out the paper on which information about him was written and handed it to him outright.

“Keep that with you at all times, and when you feel confused or your confidence falters, unfold it and read it. Especially don’t forget the part about you being sharp. You are a sharp man. So use that clever head of yours and judge well.”

Chusaeng clenched the paper he had been reading. It seemed even that paper was about to make his vessel of patience overflow. He could not bring himself to tear it up, and instead tucked the crumpled sheet into his robes.

“How do you intend to move now? If I hadn’t come, would you have sent word to the Murim Alliance?”

At my question, Chusaeng pondered for a moment, then shook his head.

“I would sooner have joined hands with the Sado Alliance than call the Murim Alliance.”

“Then will you join hands with the Sado Alliance?”

“Not them either.”

He was answering honestly. Even before the regression, he had not joined hands with either the Murim Alliance or the Sado Alliance.

“Then how were you planning to resolve this?”

“I don’t know.”

At that very moment, I saw it. The slight tremor in his eyes. To deceive me, he would have to be capable of deceiving even himself, and he was still far too young.

‘So there is something he is hiding from me.’

But I did not pressure him. It was only natural that he would not trust someone he had just met. When the other party is trying to hide something, pressing them only gets you lies or excuses. At times like this, one must respond calmly.

“Let us rest for today.”

* * *

Seokhyeong guided me to the guest room.

Good food and wine were brought out. I checked each dish with a silver needle to see if it had been poisoned, then finished my meal.

Afterward, I trained in the Wind God Four Steps in my room. If the space was narrow, I trained accordingly; if it was wide, I trained accordingly. Training in a room was an extension of the spar I had shared with Father at the inn earlier, so it was highly effective.

Now that I had set the goal of reaching great completion before receiving instruction in the Guhwa Demonic Art, my training would not stop.

* * *
