When the moon hung bright and full in the sky, I went to see Chusaeng.

Despite the late hour, he had not fallen asleep and was lost in thought in his room.

“Did you rest well?”

“I rested well, but Sect Master Chu seems not to have slept a wink.”

“I haven’t been sleeping properly lately.”

Indeed, Chusaeng’s eyes were bloodshot red.

“If you cannot sleep, you cannot make proper judgments.”

“What can I do when sleep will not come even if I want it to?”

“Try sleeping properly for even one day. Shall I help you?”

“How? Are you going to press my sleeping acupoint?”

“There is a secret method for falling asleep when sleep won’t come that I learned from our cult’s demon physician.”

At the words that I had learned it from a demon physician, Chusaeng seemed intrigued.

“Now, there’s nothing to lose, so lie down on the bed.”

“This is embarrassing for a grown man.”

Contrary to his words, Chusaeng must have been suffering greatly from his sleeplessness, for he obediently lay down on the bed like a new bride.

“Do as I say. First, relax the muscles of your face. Next, let the tension out of your shoulders and arms. More comfortably. Now, this time, relax your thighs and both legs entirely. And imagine that you are lying on a sandy beach beneath warm sunlight. Good. Then raise a strand of qi from your dantian and slowly move it as I instruct. From the Shuifen acupoint to the Zhongwan acupoint, from Zhongting to the Yutang acupoint, slowly. And then again…”

* * *

When Chusaeng opened his eyes, it was morning.

Awakening on the bed, he was bewildered. He had not slept properly in recent days, yet the throbbing headache was gone, and his body felt light enough to fly. He truly had slept so deeply it was astonishing.

“Ah! That secret method!”

It seemed he had fallen asleep without realizing it while following the method taught to him by that man from the Demon Cult whose name he did not even know.

Chusaeng climbed down from the bed and hurried out of the room.

Geom Mugeuk was in the main hall where they had first met yesterday, staring at the marks on the wall.

“You’ve come?”

“Thanks to you, I slept well. I don’t know how long it’s been since I slept that deeply.”

“Sect Master Chu seems like a man suited to the righteous faction.”

“What are you suddenly talking about?”

“I thought the reason Sect Master Chu couldn’t sleep was because of guilt over failing to protect your subordinates.”

“What nonsense are you spouting? I was simply angry at that man. I couldn’t sleep because I was so furious and resentful I couldn’t master my own temper.”

Chusaeng disliked being treated as part of the righteous faction. This was largely due to his father’s influence. Ever since he was young, his father had taught him that if one was kind, one would be taken advantage of.

“Do you think I could have survived in this jianghu until now with such half-baked feelings? Ridiculous. Don’t irritate me with pointless words.”

“Understood.”

When he accepted it too readily, Chusaeng grew angry in his own way again. He had come to thank him for helping him sleep well, yet for no reason he was only raising his voice.

Then Geom Mugeuk turned and pointed to the wall as he asked.

“This hole here.”

“Ah, I made a few of those because I was angry. I’m on the verge of making another one even now.”

“Not those. This one.”

There was a small hole where Geom Mugeuk pointed.

“Is this the hole made by the finger wind he fired?”

Chusaeng’s expression stiffened.

“That’s right. It pierced through my subordinate and went on to pierce that wall. I deliberately told them not to patch this place. So I could look at it again and again and remember what happened that day.”

Geom Mugeuk’s expression was grave as he looked at the hole.

“What is it?”

“It looks exactly like the real Hyeorang Finger.”

“Are you perhaps referring to the martial art of the Vile Little Demon?”

“That’s right. I have seen his martial arts before.”

At that, Chusaeng grew excited.

“See? Wasn’t I right? It really is the Vile Little Demon!”

“It is not the Vile Little Demon.”

“What are you saying now? Didn’t you say it was the real Hyeorang Finger?”

“I meant that the technique was that profound. It was not the work of the Vile Little Demon.”

“You seem to trust the Vile Little Demon far too much.”

“I do trust him.”

“That is exactly why I trust you even less. How can I trust someone who trusts the Vile Little Demon this much?”

“Whether you trust me or not is your freedom, but trust me this time.”

Chusaeng silently stared at Geom Mugeuk. He was a truly strange person. He said he had come from the Demon Cult, yet he did not feel like a demonic practitioner. He lacked that unique rough, cruel, sinister feeling of a man of the demonic path. Rather, though only a few days had passed since they had met, he had a peculiar power that somehow made one keep wanting to trust him.

‘If this man is evil, he will become a truly great evil.’

Hiding that thought, Chusaeng asked Geom Mugeuk.

“Then why did you tell me, knowing I would suspect you like this?”

“Because I should. Since we decided to join forces, there is nothing more foolish than acting while hiding one’s true thoughts. If we were going to do that, we should not have joined hands in the first place.”

Those words stabbed straight into Chusaeng’s heart, for he was hiding something.

“If you cannot sleep tonight either, use the method I taught you. Your complexion is much better.”

* * *

The next morning as well, Chusaeng came to see me.

“I slept well again yesterday. Perhaps you were right. The better I sleep, the more apologetic I feel toward those who died.”

“Think of the other disciples Sect Master Chu must protect. What can be done? The living must live on and move forward.”

It was a trite consolation, but perhaps it served as comfort nonetheless, for he rose with a somewhat easier expression.

“If there is anything inconvenient, tell me.”

“I will.”

Like someone with something to say, Chusaeng looked around the room here and there.

“How long are you going to stay here? I don’t think anything will change by you remaining like this.”

“Until you tell me the truth.”

“!”

Chusaeng flinched for an instant and turned toward me.

“Because I slept well and my head cleared, my thoughts about you changed too. I thought, there must be a reason that man is staying by my side. Someone so extraordinary would not be here for no reason.”

I spoke to him calmly.

“That’s right. I had noticed that you were hiding something from me.”

Around the time we first met, he would have flatly denied my words. But the Chusaeng of now did not.

“Then why do you not force me? With your martial arts, you could find out even if you had to torture me by force.”

“This may sound strange to you, but I will say it. My demonic path is a demonic path that eliminates the absolute evils of the world. My demonic path does not break tables, and my demonic path does not torture those who are not evildoers.”

“That truly is a strange thing to say.”

But his expression differed from his words. He was beginning to understand, at least a little, what kind of person I was.

“I believe the reason they chose you is because of whatever it is you are hiding right now. If my guess is correct, you will eventually die to them because of it. You may not believe it, but I want to save you.”

“You may not believe it… but I believe your words. There is no reason I should believe you, yet I keep wanting to, and that makes me hesitate even more. I wonder if I am being deceived by you. My father said never to trust another person’s words. That was his dying wish.”

Chusaeng looked up at the sky. What answer was his father giving him now?

He had done well until now, but this trial that had befallen him before he had even reached thirty was too difficult for him to overcome by his own strength.

Tell him to open his heart a little, for your son’s sake. I will make sure your son lives.

Did that thought of mine reach him?

After agonizing for a long while, Chusaeng revealed what was in his heart.

“After this matter occurred, I kept thinking of one method. I continued to agonize over whether I should tell you about it or not. My head told me I must never reveal the secret, but my intuition told me to reveal it. It told me to trust you.”

“I will contact the Hunan branch of Tongcheongak and tell them to change their records about you. His intuition is good, but he is not sharp. Like that.”

Chusaeng laughed. It was the laugh of a man who had now made up his mind.

“I was planning to ask one person for help.”

“Who is that?”

“I do not know who it is either.”

Chusaeng took out something he had kept carefully hanging from the cord of his necklace and showed it to me.

It was a coin cut exactly in half.

“It is an Eunwon Coin.”

An Eunwon Coin was a kind of token made by cutting a coin in half and giving it to a benefactor, so that even if a descendant later brought the coin, the favor would be repaid.

“They say the owner of this Eunwon Coin was injured and hovering between life and death when my father saved him. When he handed this down to me, my father repeatedly admonished me to use it only when my life and the fate of the clan depended on it.”

“Do you know who the owner of this coin is?”

Chusaeng shook his head.

“He did not tell me. I do not even know whether that person is still alive.”

“How is this Eunwon Coin used?”

“He said that if I went to any Yellow River Inn in the Central Plains and showed the coin to the innkeeper, someone would come looking for me.”

Using inns to make contact was a method used by our cult and the Murim Alliance. I thought the owner of the coin was likely someone who had once belonged to a large organization.

“Can anyone take it?”

“No. He said only our direct family line can use it. I am not saying this because I fear you might take mine from me. He truly told me that was the case.”

“I believe you. If that were not so, they would already have killed you and taken the Eunwon Coin.”

“Do you truly think he did such a thing because of this?”

“I cannot be certain, but that is my intuition.”

If, as I expected, this was what they were after, then they must be searching for the owner of this coin. Just who could it be?

“When the incident happened, why did you not use it immediately?”

Chusaeng let out a long sigh.

“I agonized over it. Truly, I agonized until my head felt as if it would burst. I was afraid. I was afraid that if I used this, I would become entangled with that man again. I was afraid that if revenge failed, he would come find us and annihilate our sect. I would rather everything simply pass by like this.”

Chusaeng lifted his head and looked at me.

“I believe you appearing before me was my father sending someone to rebuke me for such cowardice. I will stake my fate and that of my sect on you.”

* * *

Four days later.

We were sitting in a Yellow River Inn in Hunan. When we showed the coin to the innkeeper, he merely told us to wait here.

We waited without any plan.

Perhaps afraid that someone would come and leave without meeting him, Chusaeng sat in the inn all day long.

I shut myself in my room and trained in the Wind God Four Steps, then when mealtime came, I ate with Chusaeng, drank tea, and conversed with him.

I told him about the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult. I also told him stories about famous martial artists, sects, and wandering warriors from various regions, and spoke of scenic wonders and delicious dishes throughout the Central Plains. I taught him about hunting and camping as well.

He marveled at my extensive knowledge. No matter what he asked, I was someone who could answer.

At one point, Chusaeng even said this.

“How can someone so young know all this? Are you perhaps… someone who has returned to youth?”

“Even if I had the chance, I would not do it. I will grow old together with my people and die.”

“I am curious what kind of people they are. Who surrounds a person like you? I want to see them.”

“You will regret it if you do.”

“Why?”

I answered with a smile instead. He would not say such things after being poked in the side by the Blood Heaven Blade Demon until he was bruised black and blue.

On the tenth day of waiting there like that, someone finally came looking for us.

The moment he saw him, Chusaeng was so startled he sprang to his feet.

And not only Chusaeng, but I, too, was shocked.

A man wearing a white mask was striding toward us.

Chapter 144: You Look Even More Handsome in a Mask.
---------------------------------------------------
"It's him! That man right there!"

Qiusheng shouted. Upon seeing the white mask, he was half out of his mind.

"Flee! No, we must flee!"

Perhaps thinking I had frozen in fear, Qiusheng screamed as if having a fit.

"Even you cannot stand against that man! Let us flee at once."

When I remained still, Qiusheng frowned and drew his sword.

"Damn it! Let's attack together!"

"As expected, you are a man suited for the orthodox faction."

"Have you lost your mind? What nonsense at a time like this!"

I could understand why his father had left a dying wish not to trust people. Even in this situation, he was a man who did not run and looked after my safety.

Before we knew it, the man in the white mask had approached our table.

The white-masked man stared at us silently.

Qiusheng trembled violently. Though he had sworn revenge, now that he faced his opponent, his body would not move as he willed.

It was I who broke the heavy silence hanging in the hall.

"Why did you not show me this dangerous world before?"

To my surprise, he was the real Ji'e Xiaomo.

When I first saw him, I had naturally assumed he was the fake Ji'e Xiaomo who had come to Yuefengmen.

But watching him walk toward us, I realized he was the real Ji'e Xiaomo. It was an air that only Ji'e Xiaomo could exude.

"Why on earth are you here?"

"Why is the Second Young Lord here?"

At our conversation, Qiusheng's eyes widened in surprise.

He must have been confused. Whether the real one had come, or whether I had been in league with the fake.

I asked Qiusheng.

"Look closely. Is that the man who came that day?"

Qiusheng looked at Ji'e Xiaomo, trembling. After a moment, he exclaimed.

"Ah! He is different. He is definitely different. He is more handsome than that man!"

"But he is wearing a mask?"

"Indeed. He is definitely more handsome."

I smiled and looked at Ji'e Xiaomo.

"It seems this man can see through Lord Xiaomo's mask."

"Or perhaps my mask is simply more handsome."

I could understand the meaning behind Qiusheng's words. Not just anyone could give off Ji'e Xiaomo's feeling simply by wearing a mask.

An air, a feeling, an atmosphere. There was a unique aura belonging solely to Ji'e Xiaomo. Qiusheng had expressed that as 'handsomeness.'

"So this is the real Ji'e Xiaomo."

"Did I not tell you? That man was a fake. Now, greet him. This is Lord Ji'e Xiaomo, the Demon Venerable of our cult."

Qiusheng froze as if struck by lightning. He did not know what to say. He looked back and forth between me and Ji'e Xiaomo in shock and terror.

But the truly surprising matter for Qiusheng began now.

"Before leaving the cult, I visited Eren Gu to see you, but you had already departed."

"I heard there was someone going around imitating me."

Given Ji'e Xiaomo's temperament, there was no way he would let such a person be.

"But how did you know I would be here and come yourself?"

"I did not know the Second Young Lord was coming."

A surprising answer flowed from Ji'e Xiaomo's lips.

"Today, I came to this place to collect the Enyuan Coin."

I was greatly surprised. Rather, I was more surprised than Qiusheng.

"The owner of the Enyuan Coin is you, Lord Xiaomo?"

Ji'e Xiaomo opened his hand, revealing a coin cut in half resting in his palm.

At that sight, the moment Qiusheng took out the necklace hanging from his neck.

"Huh? Wha?"

The coin attached to the end of the necklace and Qiusheng, floating in midair, were suddenly pulled forward. Ji'e Xiaomo had drawn him through the air.

The coin in Ji'e Xiaomo's palm likewise rose to meet the coin hanging from the necklace in midair. The two halves joined perfectly.

Dragged before Ji'e Xiaomo, Qiusheng looked back and forth between the joined Enyuan Coin and Ji'e Xiaomo in shock. He could never have imagined that the man his father saved was Ji'e Xiaomo. It was the same for me.

With a casual flick, Ji'e Xiaomo snapped the necklace cord and reclaimed the other half of the Enyuan Coin.

Speaking in place of the dazed Qiusheng, I said to Ji'e Xiaomo.

"I truly never imagined that you were the owner of the Enyuan Coin, Lord Xiaomo."

"It was a very long time ago."

It felt new to me that there had been a time when Ji'e Xiaomo had such a clear debt of gratitude. Moreover, it was even more surprising that he had appeared here today to keep a promise.

"Seeing you, Lord Xiaomo, is a joyous occasion, but this place feels like a trap today."

Indeed, even before Ji'e Xiaomo appeared, all the guests had left, and even the innkeeper had vanished afterward. Not a single person passed by the front of the inn.

"That person's disguise as Lord Xiaomo does not seem to be a coincidence either."

It seemed not merely that someone wore a mask and impersonated Ji'e Xiaomo, but that they had targeted Ji'e Xiaomo from the start. Did that mean the Tianminghui was targeting Ji'e Xiaomo?

"Is there anyone you suspect?"

After a moment of thought, Ji'e Xiaomo spoke with a perplexed expression.

"Would there be only one? Just those who came to mind number some fifty people."

When I laughed in disbelief, Ji'e Xiaomo laughed as well. Qiusheng remained silent, still seemingly unable to adapt to the situation, merely listening to our conversation.

"Is there anyone else besides Lord Xiaomo who uses the Xueyangzhi?"

"!"

For a moment, I sensed Ji'e Xiaomo flinch.

"Why do you ask?"

"The traces the fake left on Yuefengmen's walls were extremely similar to Lord Xiaomo's Xueyangzhi. It was such an exquisite technique that one might say it was left by you yourself."

Ji'e Xiaomo answered after a brief pause.

"There is another."

I asked no further. Because I knew he had given me that answer only because it was me.

"Perhaps the enemy we face is no ordinary opponent."

Ji'e Xiaomo nodded at my words. He clearly seemed to have a guess as to who was behind this.

When our conversation paused for a moment, Qiusheng asked me.

"But he called you the Second Young Lord. Surely it is not...?"

I returned the question to him with a meaningful smile.

"If not, who else in the world could converse with a Demon Venerable at such a young age?"

"Are you truly the bloodline of the Heavenly Demon?"

"I am."

"Ah! To think the day would come when I see the bloodline of the Demonic Cult Leader and a Demon Venerable in one place!"

He was seeing at once people that an ordinary martial artist might never encounter in their entire life.

"I had suspected you were no ordinary person. In fact, it would not have been strange at all if you had said, 'I am the Heavenly Demon.' I would have believed you were a Heavenly Demon who had returned to youth. You truly are... No, I suppose I should call you Second Young Lord now."

Ji'e Xiaomo smiled softly, as if he understood exactly what was in Qiusheng's heart.

"Do not stand on ceremony with me. Treat me as comfortably as you have until now."

"May I?"

"It is fine. Had I desired special treatment, I would have revealed my identity from the start."

"Understood. No, I understand."

Once our exchange was over, Ji'e Xiaomo spoke to Qiusheng.

"Whatever the situation, a past debt must be repaid. Now, what help do you need? Speak carefully. The Enyuan Coin can be used but once. I am granting this request out of respect for your father, so choose wisely."

Qiusheng, who had been pondering briefly, glanced at me.

"The Second Young Lord will make the request in my stead."

He had entrusted me with the use of the Enyuan Coin.

"Why entrust it to me?"

"Listening to your conversation, this matter seems not to be solely our family's affair. Is this something that ends simply with 'avenge us'? In my humble opinion, it does not seem so. I do not know what request to make of Lord Xiaomo now. That is why I entrust it to you, Second Young Lord."

I thought it a wise decision.

Yes, Qiusheng. Continue to live making good use of that sharp head of yours.

Smiling, I said to Ji'e Xiaomo.

"What do you say? Will you recognize my request in his stead?"

"I had decided to grant requests only from immediate family, but since it is the Second Young Lord, I shall make a special exception."

"Thank you."

After a moment's consideration, I spoke to him.

"Please make me the successor to our cult."

Startled, Qiusheng cried out as if to argue.

"Why are you using my Enyuan Coin to ask for your own affair?"

"You entrusted it to me, did you not?"

"No, but still. I never imagined you would make such a request. You should ask for revenge for my family."

Just as Qiusheng was arguing, Ji'e Xiaomo answered.

"Very well, I accept."

What he accepted was not the Enyuan Coin's request, but my jest.

Qiusheng, on the other hand, was frantic.

"It cannot be! This is invalid! Invalid!"

Ji'e Xiaomo looked at him with cold eyes.

"Who are you?"

Startled by Ji'e Xiaomo's attitude, which said their business was done.

"Who is it that has been hovering before me since earlier?"

Looking back and forth between Ji'e Xiaomo and me, he sighed and reproached himself.

"I suppose my temperament is selectively short-tempered. That vessel of patience that brimmed over even at pebbles does not brim over before this man. It seems it would not overflow even if the sea were poured into it."

At his self-mocking sigh, I smiled and spoke.

"You have lost the Enyuan Coin, but in exchange, have you not gained a future Heavenly Demon?"

At those words, Qiusheng looked at me with a surprised face.

"I apologize for the jest. Now, let me formally introduce myself. I am Jian Wuji, Second Young Lord of the Tianmo Shenjiao. I shall avenge your family upon my name."

Only then did Qiusheng realize it had been a jest, and he was deeply moved. A promise more certain than the Enyuan Coin had been made.

There, Ji'e Xiaomo stepped forward as well.

"This matter is related to me, so I intend to handle it myself. Therefore, do not worry and return home."

"Thank you. Truly, thank you."

"It is deeply regrettable that your family became entangled because of me. Had I known it would come to this, I would never have given the Enyuan Coin to your father."

Though he did not say the words 'I am sorry,' I could tell he felt apologetic.

"No, how could that be Lord Xiaomo's fault?"

"This is no place for you. Return at once."

"Yes."

I asked worriedly.

"Is it safe to send him alone?"

With enemies clearly already nearby, I was worried he might encounter mishaps returning alone.

"You need not worry."

It was an answer that suggested he knew who was behind this scheme.

Qiusheng cupped his hands toward me and Ji'e Xiaomo, then turned. Just as he was about to leave the inn, he turned back at the door.

"I say this because I fear we may not meet again. Perhaps when this matter ends, my main sect may join the orthodox faction. So I say this: should a war between orthodox and demonic ever break out later... please look favorably upon my family."

At his joke, I too reciprocated in jest.

"Then you must save me once. I shall cut a coin for you."

Qiusheng smiled broadly.

"I am certain. My father sent a precious person to me. Second Young Lord, please become a Heavenly Demon who does not start a war between orthodox and demonic. Then I shall look forward to meeting you again someday."

Qiusheng gave a very respectful final greeting and left.

Left alone in the inn, Ji'e Xiaomo spoke.

"Second Young Lord, give up on becoming the Heavenly Demon."

"What do you mean? I spent the Enyuan Coin for this."

"Travel the Central Plains like this. Meeting people like Qiusheng, living while smiling like this. This suits you more."

I could tell. That Ji'e Xiaomo was speaking from the heart.

"Have you perhaps accepted a bribe from my elder brother? Have you sided with him in the time we have not met?"

Ji'e Xiaomo laughed loudly.

Once Qiusheng left, as if they had been waiting, enemies revealed themselves one by one. Indeed, this place today was undoubtedly a trap to lure Ji'e Xiaomo.

The first to appear were three old men.

They were old men of similar age, but peculiarly, they were dressed in matching red armor. From that attire alone, I recognized who they were.

Xuelu Sanjun.

They were once vagabonds who dyed the martial world in terror, accepting any contract as long as money was paid. They killed indiscriminately, women and children alike, and were known to kill even their own parents if paid.

Eventually, their evil deeds became so excessive that they were condemned as public enemies of the martial world and vanished from it, yet today they revealed themselves here.

They had made their notorious names at middle age; now they had grown old. The wickedness they had accumulated through countless trials replaced their diminished muscle. They seated themselves at a table far from us.

Again, a middle-aged woman appeared in the inn, swaying a pink fan. She wore gaudy palace attire with thick makeup, and her figure was voluptuously sensual, as if bursting.

Jile Yaoji.

She was a heinous villainess who had sucked the vital essence of countless men to forge her internal energy. That skin and figure, so ill-suited to her age, had been created by stealing the vital essence and lives of countless men. She too had once ranked first among the public enemies of the martial world. Rumor had it that she had fallen prey to a heinous lecher and died from having her internal energy drained in reverse, yet here she was, brazenly alive.

She revealed her pale legs, crossed them, and sat astride a table.

Next, a middle-aged man entered.

Wearing a single straight blade at his waist, he emanated an air like a beast wandering the wilderness.

Xuelang Dao.

He was the very martial artist who had sought out experts of the Central Plains solely to grow stronger. He did anything to win and forcibly fought even those who wished to avoid combat. Thus, too many experts had died unjustly by his hand.

Eventually, he too was condemned as a public enemy of the martial world, and there were many rumors regarding his end: that he died fighting an elder of the Martial Alliance, that he died challenging a Demon Venerable, that he died at the hands of a nameless hermit expert. Rendering all such rumors meaningless, he gulped down the teapot placed at the entrance.

The last to appear was a man dressed entirely in black clothes and shoes, with a black cloak wrapped around him.

Yanluo Shenjun.

Though he appeared sane now, he was once a mad martial artist who had massacled martial artists in his insanity. He had believed himself to be a messenger sent by the King of Hell, considering himself a god who governed life and death. He too had been condemned as a public enemy of the martial world, stirring up a storm of blood before disappearing.

Villains who had once shaken the martial world had appeared all at once, so rather, it felt unreal. Moreover, the mastermind behind the impersonation had yet to show himself.

It was not the time to act leisurely, but I could not help saying this to Ji'e Xiaomo.

"Just what on earth have you been doing?"

Looking at the villains radiating killing intent toward him, Ji'e Xiaomo replied sheepishly.

"They are all people I know."

Chapter 145: It Looks Delicious Even If It’s a Mess.
----------------------------------------------------
The first to speak was the Paradise Enchantress.

She lightly fluttered her pale pink fan and said,

“Handsome little brother over there!”

A seductive gleam flowed through her eyes as she looked at me.

“How about coming out with me, little brother? It looks like things are about to get ugly here.”

I smiled and said to her,

“What should I do? Madam, you’re not my type.”

“Oh my! Madam? I’m not that old. Just call me big sister.”

I asked the Evil Smiling Demon,

“How old would you say she is?”

“She must be well over sixty.”

At that, the Paradise Enchantress burst out shouting.

“Shut up! You ugly demon bastard. I’m still twenty-eight.”

She was not even afraid of the Evil Smiling Demon. She truly was a fearless wicked woman.

“Didn’t you say you were twenty-eight ten years ago too?”

“You remember that?”

“Even then, you looked over forty.”

The Evil Smiling Demon seemed to know full well that such words would offend her the most.

A bewitching energy flowed from the enraged Paradise Enchantress’s body.

“Today, I’ll finally get to see the face behind that mask. If you hadn’t crawled into the Demon Cult, you would have died by my hand long ago.”

Just from their conversation, it was clear the two were not on good terms.

Then the third of the Three Lords of the Blood Road, who was nearby, unexpectedly took the Evil Smiling Demon’s side.

“That old hag’s demonic aura grows more sinister by the day.”

The second, standing beside him, mocked the Paradise Enchantress while pretending to restrain him.

“Little brother, if you run your mouth carelessly, that monster will suck your vital essence dry too.”

When the Paradise Enchantress turned to look at them with a hardened expression, this time the eldest of the Three Lords of the Blood Road spoke.

“Have my younger brothers’ loose tongues only been a problem for a day or two? Think of it as a passing dog barking.”

The Paradise Enchantress snorted and said,

“Why do those sons of bitches bark like that whenever they see a person?”

Even so, she seemed to have some measure of acquaintance with the eldest. At a glance, the one leading the Three Lords of the Blood Road was the calm-natured eldest.

“Next time we come out, I’ll make sure to put muzzles on them.”

At that moment, the third made a show of whispering into someone’s ear. Of course, his voice was still loud enough for everyone to hear.

“Seeing how he always takes her side like that, don’t you think Big Brother might have spent a passionate night with that monster woman?”

“Then we should treat her as our sister-in-law.”

The eldest closed his eyes gently and said to the Paradise Enchantress,

“Please consider them senile.”

On the surface, it looked as if they were exchanging idle nonsense, but they were ready to strike at any moment.

Even the second and third, who ran their mouths like ruffians, each possessed no ordinary bearing. If that had not been the case, they would have already been torn to shreds by the Paradise Enchantress.

I released several strands of energy like a spiderweb and examined their bearing. Just as a spiderweb trembles when prey is caught, I keenly watched how their presences shifted.

They were relaxed. The atmosphere around them said they believed that if they attacked together, they could easily kill the Evil Smiling Demon. The jokes going back and forth reflected that confidence.

At that moment, Divine Lord Yama, who had entered last, opened his mouth.

“If they’re senile, they should die.”

He deliberately distorted his voice, speaking gloomily as though a reaper were talking.

He took a booklet from his robes and opened it. It was the Death Ledger he carried with him.

“You wicked bastards should have fallen into the Eight Scorching Hells long ago. I’ll send you there now.”

Then the youngest and second of the Three Lords of the Blood Road clicked their tongues and said,

“That madman’s illness still shows no sign of improving.”

“Hey, you bastard, you shouldn’t be writing our names. You should be writing the name of that masked fellow over there.”

Realizing that they knew one another well, and even met often, I sent a sound transmission to the Evil Smiling Demon.

—Do you know where these people came from?

—They came from the Valley of Villains.

Within the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult, the place where the Evil Smiling Demon stayed was also called the Valley of Villains. But the Valley of Villains the Evil Smiling Demon had just mentioned was not the place where he resided.

In the Central Plains, there was a true Valley of Villains where evildoers gathered to live.

No one knew when the Valley of Villains had first come into existence. It had formed as criminals who had committed crimes and fled began gathering there one by one. The terrain swarmed with swamps, venomous snakes, and poisonous insects, and all manner of traps had been installed there, so anyone who entered recklessly would lose their life before taking even a few steps inside.

Even if one safely overcame those dangers and made it inside, all kinds of villains awaited within. A child who offered directions would stab your throat with a dagger, and a kindly-looking old woman would put poison in your rice. That was the Valley of Villains.

The Murim Alliance had attempted to subjugate the place several times, but each attempt had failed. There were simply too many villains, and every time, they suffered heavy losses and were forced to turn back.

Even so, there was one reason the Valley of Villains had never become a single force and burst out into the murim.

Because they were villains.

Whenever someone tried to unite them, someone would inevitably betray them. From the moment they opened their eyes, they stole from one another, fought, and killed, making it impossible for them to unite into one faction.

And yet, people from that very Valley of Villains had joined hands for the purpose of killing the Evil Smiling Demon. If the person behind this had moved not ordinary villains, but figures of this caliber, then that alone was enough to guess that the mastermind was truly extraordinary.

—What is our strategy?

At my sound transmission, the Evil Smiling Demon answered without hesitation.

—Sweep them all away.

—Is that possible?

—It is. There is one crucial thing missing from their calculations.

—Me, of course?

—No. They ignored the fact that I do not enjoy falling into traps.

They had properly provoked the Evil Smiling Demon.

You dare set a trap to kill me? And even use the Silver Coin Hall to do it?

The Evil Smiling Demon was currently very angry. Of course, they were not opponents he could deal with purely according to his mood, so he was likely only watching for an opportunity.

Just then, Blood Wolf Saber, who had been sitting alone, abruptly stood and announced,

“Evil Smiling Demon, you and I shall fight to the death.”

Immediately, Divine Lord Yama stepped forward.

“The Evil Smiling Demon’s name is already written in the ledger. I will take him.”

When he opened the Ledger of Life and Death, the Evil Smiling Demon’s name was written there in red characters.

The Paradise Enchantress licked her lips with her tongue, coveting the Evil Smiling Demon.

“He may be damn troublesome, but he looks like he’d be delicious.”

The Three Lords of the Blood Road were the same.

“If those ones kill him, we’ll have to keep listening to them boast for the rest of our lives. Let us take the lead.”

It seemed they all wanted to fight the Evil Smiling Demon. Well, to them, killing the Evil Smiling Demon would be something to brag about for a long time.

Seeing that, I said to the Evil Smiling Demon,

“You’re popular.”

“This is nothing. If I go over to the righteous faction, it becomes a complete uproar. The line of people wanting to kill me stretches for ten li.”

At his joke, I burst out laughing.

Naturally, the villains’ gazes turned toward me. Judging by their reactions, they still did not know who I was.

Blood Wolf Saber asked me,

“Brat, who are you?”

Instead of answering, I said to him,

“I heard you were originally someone who walked his own path. I didn’t expect you to come kill someone at another’s instigation.”

Blood Wolf Saber, struck where it hurt, looked at me coldly. I did not know how he had come to be here, but I did know that he was a man who had lived for nothing but pride.

“I asked who you are.”

“Geom Mugeuk.”

“Geom Mugeuk?”

Then the eldest of the Three Lords of the Blood Road recognized me from behind.

“The Heavenly Demon’s second son.”

His expression, which had been calm until now, instantly twisted.

“Damn it! That damned bastard! He drove us into a place where the Heavenly Demon’s bloodline is present.”

At that, the second took up his words.

“He’ll probably insist he didn’t know he had come together with the Evil Smiling Demon. But it’s not as if we’re in a situation where we can withdraw either, so this is troublesome.”

I did not know the reason, but it seemed they too had been driven into a situation where they absolutely had to kill the Evil Smiling Demon. Well, if not for that, they would not have dared think of killing a Demon Venerable.

On the other hand, Blood Wolf Saber showed no fear at all even after learning my identity.

“You’re running your mouth because you trust your father. Do you think I fear your father?”

This too was the result of his pride being triggered. He was a man who lived by pride and died by pride. But how could that be called pride?

“A person with nothing to protect naturally has nothing to fear.”

At my words, his momentum surged. In a situation where he looked ready to draw his saber at once, I cut off the flow of that momentum and retreated behind the Evil Smiling Demon.

Blood Wolf Saber did not draw his saber recklessly. Since he would not yet know my true ability, he was likely being wary of the Evil Smiling Demon.

I openly asked the Evil Smiling Demon so everyone could hear.

“The person behind this is someone capable of mobilizing people like them, correct?”

“As you can see, yes.”

“Then why did he send them instead of stepping forward himself?”

“Most likely… because of you, Second Young Master.”

“Because of me?”

“He is hoping that one of them will kill you, Second Young Master. Then the responsibility for killing you will fall on that person.”

“A very cowardly bastard, then.”

I looked at the villains and said,

“Well then, who will be the first to fall for that cowardly intent?”

For a moment, silence flowed. Even if this was the Valley of Villains, there was fear in the name of the Heavenly Demon. If Father truly became enraged, he could attack the Valley of Villains as well. The reason he had left it alone until now was because that place was an even greater burden to the Murim Alliance.

The villains began exchanging sound transmissions among themselves. On the surface, they appeared to be bickering and eager to tear one another apart, but they were villains who had been worn smooth by the jianghu. Because they handled matters where their lives were at stake, they were extremely cautious. It was also the reason they had survived until now despite their countless evil deeds.

After a moment, the Paradise Enchantress stepped forward with a bewitching smile in her eyes.

“Little brother, you truly should come out with me. How could such an esteemed person get tangled up with these filthy villains?”

The instant I saw her eyes, I could feel that she had resolved to kill me. It seemed the mastermind close at hand was more frightening to her now than the Heavenly Demon far away.

The Paradise Enchantress lightly fluttered her fan.

“Little brother, shall we go out?”

The moment a vivid crimson energy flowed from the fan, an aura thick with bewitchment wrapped around me. She had used the Soul-Bewitching Art on me. It was the Soul-Bewitching Art of a woman who had plundered the inner energy of countless martial artists until now.

The instant a crimson light settled in my eyes, the Paradise Enchantress shouted at the Evil Smiling Demon,

“If you move, the Second Young Master dies!”

The Evil Smiling Demon did not budge. In truth, even if she had not warned him, he would not have moved. He was certain that I had killed the Soul-Bewitching Demon Venerable, so how could I fail to handle the Paradise Enchantress’s Soul-Bewitching Art? He must have trusted me that much.

Thinking that I had completely fallen under the Soul-Bewitching Art and lost my reason, the Paradise Enchantress approached me and placed her palm against my dantian.

“Shall I have a taste of my little brother’s fresh inner energy?”

Whoooooong!

The sound of inner energy being drawn from the dantian rang out.

The Paradise Enchantress shouted in a voice full of ecstasy.

“It has been my lifelong wish to suck dry the Heavenly Demon’s vital essence! Since I’m eating his bloodline, I have achieved half that wish now.”

The Paradise Enchantress’s expression changed from moment to moment, containing joy, anger, sorrow, and pleasure. The Evil Smiling Demon, and all the other villains in the hall, simply watched the sight.

The youngest of the Three Lords of the Blood Road ran his mouth at the Evil Smiling Demon.

“You cold-blooded bastard! Even as the Heavenly Demon’s bloodline suffers, you just stand there watching.”

At that, the Evil Smiling Demon calmly asked them,

“You lot. Can you handle what comes after?”

“We can simply hide in the Valley of Villains. We’ll burn your corpses and throw them away, and no one will know it was our doing. Even if it is revealed, do you think your Demon Cult will truly bear such heavy sacrifices to attack the Valley of Villains?”

At the third’s answer, the Evil Smiling Demon said nothing more and simply watched as the Paradise Enchantress plundered my inner energy.

And a moment later.

Thud.

The Paradise Enchantress collapsed where she stood, crumpling to the floor.

Startled, the eldest of the Three Lords of the Blood Road examined her.

“Old hag, what’s wrong?”

The eldest, after checking her, shouted in shock.

“She’s dead! She died because her inner energy was exhausted!”

At that moment, my eyes flashed open.

“Ah, I’m full! She was damn troublesome, but she was very delicious.”

I had instead absorbed all of her inner energy.

I had deliberately pretended to be caught, but sorcery does not work on me. In particular, arts of the Soul-Bewitching type absolutely do not work.

Recently, I had gained insight into martial learning, and on top of that, I had the Blood-Eye Essence, the Heavenly Demon Body-Protecting Art, and the Heavenly Vein Strengthening Art. From the beginning, it had been impossible for her to take inner energy from my body.

In the end, the Paradise Enchantress met a miserable death by having the very method she had used all her life to plunder men turned back on her.

As I stored the inner energy I had gained from her in my dantian, I looked at Divine Lord Yama with eyes that had deepened further and said,

“What are you staring at? Aren’t you going to write our big sister’s name in your ledger?”

Chapter 146: No Matter How Urgent It Is.
----------------------------------------
Divine Lord Yama opened the Ledger of Slaughter.

Then he took out a small vessel of blood, dipped his brush into it, and wrote my name beside Extreme Evil Soma’s.

The look in Divine Lord Yama’s eyes, which earlier had made him seem half out of his mind, changed. It seemed as though he had returned to normal, and yet it also seemed like the gaze of a man who had gone completely mad.

“Since our names are written side by side, I suppose we’ll die together as well.”

At my words, Extreme Evil Soma laughed.

Seeing our composure, the eldest of the Three Lords of the Blood Road, who had been examining the Blissful Enchantress’s corpse, roared.

“You bastards! Don’t laugh!”

He rose from where he stood and glared coldly at me.

“The Enchantress has never once failed in her soul-bewitching arts. So what happened?”

“It seems Elder Sister saw that this younger brother’s inner force was lacking and wished to add to it.”

“Enough of your nonsense!”

He was truly enraged. And I could feel it. That anger was deeply personal. It seemed the second and third of the Three Lords of the Blood Road really had been supposed to call the Blissful Enchantress sister-in-law.

The eldest looked at Extreme Evil Soma and said,

“You knew. You knew that Second Young Master would not fall to her.”

Of course Extreme Evil Soma had trusted me.

Extreme Evil Soma looked at me. Our gazes tangled in midair as we exchanged transmitted voices.

—Now confess with that mouth of yours. Say that I killed the Soul-Bewitching Demon Venerable.

—No such thing happened.

—Someday I’ll hear it from you.

Extreme Evil Soma’s eyes were smiling within the eyeholes of his mask. Seeing us calmly looking at each other and smiling, the eldest of the Three Lords of the Blood Road revealed his fury.

“Fine, laugh. Laugh like that even as you go to hell.”

I said to the eldest of the Three Lords of the Blood Road,

“Did that man tell you this before driving you all to your deaths? That Lord Soma and I killed the Sword Emperor Baek Manggi.”

“What?”

Not only the eldest, but the other villains there were also startled. They knew there was no guarantee they could kill the Sword Emperor Baek Manggi even if they attacked him together. And now even the Blissful Enchantress was dead.

“That man probably never believed you could kill us. He merely used you to wound us or exhaust our inner force.”

The remaining ones looked at one another. This time, I did not give them a chance to form a plan through transmitted voices.

I said to Blood Wolf Saber,

“Blood Wolf Saber, a life-and-death duel.”

I returned to him the exact words he had spoken to Extreme Evil Soma earlier.

“You said you spent your whole life wandering in search of the strong, didn’t you? Well, the man you were looking for has come.”

As I drew the Black Demon Sword, I said to Extreme Evil Soma,

“Please kill anyone who interferes with this spar.”

Originally, this should have been a fight I yielded to Extreme Evil Soma. But now was a situation where no variables could be permitted. We had to deal with them without taking injuries if possible, then leave this place. The true enemy was waiting for us outside.

Extreme Evil Soma also guessed my intent, so he willingly yielded this fight to me.

“I will.”

There were still four left besides Blood Wolf Saber, yet Extreme Evil Soma answered with confidence.

If anything, the one most flustered in this situation was Blood Wolf Saber. After seeing me reverse-absorb the Blissful Enchantress’s inner force, he must have felt fear deep down. The momentum was in my favor. I pressed him even harder.

“If you had truly set out in search of the strong, could you have remained alive until now? If that was truly your heart, why didn’t you go find the Murim Alliance Leader? Why didn’t you come find my father?”

“……”

“You don’t want to fight me, do you? And yet your whole life, you forced duels on people who didn’t want to fight. Now, suffer the same yourself.”

The moment I finished speaking, I charged toward Blood Wolf Saber.

Blood Wolf Saber drew his straight saber.

Though he had been suppressed in momentum at the start of the fight, Blood Wolf Saber was not a man with a false reputation. His saber art was fast, precise, and powerful.

His saber came flying in with enough force to break my sword.

When sword and saber clashed, there was no ringing clang, but thunderous booms. Inner force was infused into both sword and saber, turning it into a contest of internal power. The shame of having been struck at his sore spot rode upon his saber.

He hammered, and hammered, and hammered again.

He pressed me like a madman. If it had been any other martial artist, their sword would already have broken, or their wrist would have snapped.

But I blocked, and blocked, and blocked again.

Beyond the colliding sword and saber, I saw his expression change from moment to moment. The composure he had shown at the start, when he thought he had seized the initiative, gradually turned into impatience. His wrist was likely beginning to ache.

Now, slowly, our positions began to change.

I became the one hammering. The Black Demon Sword began to strike down on his saber. It was not a contest of dazzling techniques, but the very contest of inner force he had intended, and I drove him into it.

Because I struck down with my sword so quickly, he could not dodge, nor could he unleash another technique.

Boom! Boom! Boom! Boom!

Just then, amid the heavy impacts, a wretched scream mingled with the blows.

“Kuaaaargh!”

His wrist, overpowered in inner force, had broken.

But my sword did not stop.

On the next move, his saber, unable to receive inner force, broke, and on the move after that, his body was cut in a long vertical line.

“Wasn’t this the life you wanted? To die fighting someone stronger.”

But I saw it. The resentment and regret reflected in his eyes at the final moment. Yes, words were easy, but truly living such a life could not have been easy.

Blood Wolf Saber toppled forward as blood sprayed in a straight line from the crown of his head to his navel. He had spent his whole life forcing duels on others, and in the end, he died by receiving back the very method he had used.

Divine Lord Yama and the Three Lords of the Blood Road watched this in silence. It was partly because of Extreme Evil Soma, but also because they had found no chance to interfere in this brute-force contest.

They likely had not expected Blood Wolf Saber to lose to me. After all, a head-on contest of inner force was Blood Wolf Saber’s specialty.

I said again to Divine Lord Yama,

“Did you write it down? Blood Wolf Saber’s name too?”

Blood Wolf Saber had died before his eyes, but he was not frightened in the least.

“After writing your name, there wasn’t enough space, so I couldn’t.”

When he stepped toward me, the eldest of the Three Lords of the Blood Road stopped him.

“Madman. Your opponent is over there.”

The eldest of the Three Lords of the Blood Road intended to kill me and avenge the Blissful Enchantress.

With my blood-dripping sword lowered, I walked toward them. Naturally, Extreme Evil Soma faced Divine Lord Yama.

“Did you write your own name in advance?”

At Extreme Evil Soma’s question, Divine Lord Yama shouted in a ringing voice,

“I am the envoy sent by the King of Hell. The King of Hell seeks you!”

Puaang! Kwaang!

Divine Lord Yama’s palm strike collided with Extreme Evil Soma’s Demonic Extreme Berserk Palm.

The aftermath sent a fierce wind rushing over us, and my fight with the Three Lords of the Blood Road began as well.

The three men charged at me at the same time.

The eldest’s sword aimed for my face, the second’s for my chest, and the third’s for my legs.

Clang! Clang! Claang!

I knocked away the incoming swords almost simultaneously. I was fast. No, I had to be fast. Their attacks were at a speed where, if I was not fast, some part of me would inevitably be pierced.

The directions of their thrusts kept changing. This time the eldest aimed for my chest, the second for my stomach, and the third for my face. The next time, it changed again.

It seemed their order of attack had already been set through dozens, hundreds of practices and experiences. This combined assault was perfect, having thoroughly studied the opponent’s mind. Truly, if it had not been me, I would have been stabbed several times by this merciless attack.

Clangclangclangclangclang!

I was continuously pushed back as I knocked away their attacks.

Then, seizing an opening, I attacked.

When the Black Demon Sword stabbed toward the eldest, the second joined in defending, while the third thrust at me.

Because they had fought together all their lives, though their bodies were three, they fought as if their minds were one. It was like fighting a monster with three heads and six arms. They were far more difficult opponents than Blood Wolf Saber, whom I had fought earlier. I was certain. Of the four villains who had come today, they were the most troublesome to face.

Four swords wove together, moving above and below. An exchange continued in which not even an inch of error was allowed.

I had no intention of dragging this fight out. That was why I thought of one method I had never used until now.

At the very moment they were pressing me—

Ssssst.

The startled second hurriedly looked around. The eldest and third had suddenly vanished, and he was standing alone with me in a field.

Through the Space-Time Transposition Art, I had moved only the second to a new space.

To the eyes of the eldest and third left behind, our forms must have vanished in an instant.

The second shouted at me,

“Is this an illusion art? Cowardly!”

“Well, by your standards, I suppose one-on-one is cowardly.”

I charged toward him. Even the three of them together had failed to kill me; alone, he could not possibly face me.

Outside, the eldest and third were looking around in shock.

If only their opponent had disappeared, that would have been one thing, but even the second had vanished with me.

At that moment, the second’s corpse appeared as if dropping from thin air. The instant the third was flustered by the corpse that had suddenly appeared before his eyes—

Swish! Thunk!

The Black Demon Sword pierced through the corpse’s armpit and stabbed into the third’s heart. Considering their skill, it was an utterly empty death, but their opponent was none other than me, who had incorporated the Space-Time Transposition Art into actual combat.

Without even letting out a single scream, the third also collapsed.

I thought the eldest would lose his reason and charge in like a madman.

But what he chose was escape. He tried to break through one wall of the inn and flee, but in a flash, I blocked his path with Flicker Step.

Startled, he turned again and fled. He threw the Blissful Enchantress’s corpse, which lay on the floor, at me. He had intended to kill me to avenge her, and yet—

“No matter how urgent it is, that’s not right, is it?”

“Shut up, you bastard!”

As though he judged the fight had already turned against him, he went berserk in order to live. He shouted and swung his sword like a madman. But there was no way such struggles would work.

Before even five moves had passed—

Thuk!

The Black Demon Sword pierced his heart, and even faster than that, cut off his wrist.

Thud.

In the fallen hand was a pouch. It was a poison pouch he had meant to scatter at me. The instant he was stabbed by my sword, he had tried to spray poison at me and perish together.

I had known from the start that they were such vicious men. I simply thought their end had come far too late. After committing all manner of evil their entire lives, they only died now, in their later years. If I had not regressed, they would not even have died here.

Isn’t this too unfair? That is why everyone laments that even the heavens are indifferent.

When my fight ended, Extreme Evil Soma’s fight had ended as well.

Divine Lord Yama was already dead, his forehead pierced by the Blood Calamity Finger, his face buried in his own ledger. The blood flowing out soaked his Ledger of Slaughter.

“You spent your whole life pretending to be the envoy of the King of Hell, but when the King of Hell actually sees you, he’ll say this: Who are you? When did I ever send you?”

At my words, Extreme Evil Soma laughed and asked,

“Are you hurt anywhere?”

“I’m fine. What about you, Lord Soma?”

“I’m fine as well.”

Though it might have seemed we had won easily, in truth, they were powerful enemies against whom it would not have been strange if either of us had died.

But we handled it well, and once again passed safely through a crisis. With him, unintentionally, I kept crossing the boundaries of life and death like this.

“Your skills have improved again while I wasn’t watching. Just how strong do you intend to become?”

“I intend to go all the way to the end.”

“If you learn the Nine Flames Demonic Art on top of this, there will be no one under heaven who can face you.”

I truly hoped so. Truly, sincerely, I hoped no one would be my match.

Before going outside, I placed money on the counter at the entrance of the inn. It was enough to repair the damaged inn and buy new furnishings. It was money for the innkeeper, who must have fled somewhere. What happened today had likely been forced upon him.

“Now, this sight no longer seems strange.”

“As you know, the demonic path I pursue is a demonic path that does not break tables.”

“Do you believe we can become good people, Second Young Master?”

“I do not wish for us to become good people.”

“Then?”

“I am trying to uphold at least what must be upheld. Shouldn’t we at least become human first, and only above that can there be the demonic path, the orthodox path, or the heretical path? That is all I want.”

Extreme Evil Soma stared at me silently for a moment. I could not know what thoughts he was looking at me with, but at the very least, it was certain that he was gradually becoming accustomed to the person I was.

“Let’s go out.”

“Let’s.”

Together, we walked out of the inn.

Outside, there were no passersby at all. The street was truly empty.

Before us stood a man wearing a white mask.

He was merely standing there, yet I felt an overwhelming presence from him. A presence no less than that of the Sword Emperor Baek Manggi.

I could tell. This man was the fake who had impersonated Extreme Evil Soma, and at the same time, the mastermind behind this trap.

“Have you forgotten your manners now that you’ve been associating with those of the demonic path?”

Extreme Evil Soma stared at him for a moment with complicated eyes, then spoke in a calm tone.

“I greet Senior Martial Uncle.”

He was someone I also knew. He was Liang Qiji, the senior disciple-brother of Extreme Evil Soma’s master. Before my regression, when I had formed a connection with Extreme Evil Soma, he had once told me about this senior martial uncle.

Liang Qiji spoke to Extreme Evil Soma in a commanding tone.

“Take off your mask.”

Chapter 147: Who on Earth Did He Join Hands With.
-------------------------------------------------
“I refuse.”

Geugak Soma flatly declined.

“You dare defy the words of your martial uncle?”

The fact that he could say something so shameless after having tried to release the villains of the Valley of Villains to kill Geugak Soma told me exactly what kind of man he was.

“What is this paltry mask, anyway!”

Yang Cheogi took off his mask. A wrinkled old man appeared, and he was truly an ugly wretch. His face was far too much of a contrast to Geugak Soma’s handsome face that I had seen before my regression.

“Take it off at once!”

“I refuse.”

“Yes, you always looked down on me.”

It seemed Geugak Soma’s relationship with his master had not been bad. The reason someone with his personality was obeying his martial uncle’s words so meekly was likely because the man was his master’s senior martial brother.

“The moment your master died, you lost the right to wear that mask.”

“What do you mean?”

“You were of the White Mask Sect only while your master defended you. You are no longer of the White Mask Sect. I will expel you.”

The sect where Geugak Soma had learned martial arts was the White Mask Sect, a sect of the Evil Path. After that, Geugak Soma threw himself into the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult and became a Demon Venerable. Becoming a Demon Venerable had been his master’s dream. Geugak Soma had fulfilled in his master’s stead the dream his master had failed to achieve. I had heard all of this when I interacted with Geugak Soma before my regression.

“You cannot cast me out as you please, Martial Uncle.”

“Shut your mouth!”

Killing intent erupted from Yang Cheogi’s body. It was not the sort of killing intent released to frighten an opponent, but one filled with the genuine resolve to kill.

“Well, you killed even the Sword Emperor, so I suppose there is no reason for you to fear this martial uncle. When I first heard the news that you had killed the Sword Emperor, I could not believe it. Or I thought perhaps something had been wrong with the Sword Emperor. But seeing you walk out in such fine shape, I suppose there was nothing wrong with him after all.”

Even so, he showed no fear whatsoever. He clearly had something he believed in.

“If you are his martial uncle, why are you trying to kill Lord Soma?”

At my question, Geugak Soma answered calmly.

“Martial Uncle’s dream was to become the Alliance Leader of the Evil Path. He resents me, believing he failed to achieve that dream because of me.”

“Of course it was because of you! My lifelong wish collapsed because of you. A Demon Venerable and the Alliance Leader of the Evil Path cannot coexist from the same sect!”

He blamed Geugak Soma for his failure to become the Alliance Leader of the Evil Path. It had happened quite some time ago, but it seemed he had continued nurturing that resentment all this time.

“When my master was alive, you could not even move a finger, and now that he has passed, you act like this.”

“Shut up! I only endured it because you were trapped in the Demonic Cult under a restriction. It has nothing to do with my junior brother!”

Yang Cheogi had been a loser his entire life. In his youth, he too had thrown himself into the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult to become a Demon Venerable, but he failed to adapt and was driven out as good as expelled. He wanted to become the master of the White Mask Sect, but that position was inherited by Geugak Soma’s master. Finally, even his wish to become the Alliance Leader of the Evil Path went unfulfilled. His life had been one of dreaming only dreams far beyond his own ability.

“How pathetic.”

At my words, Geugak Soma and Yang Cheogi looked at me.

“Blaming others, using others to kill someone for you. Truly pathetic. How is it Lord Soma’s fault that you became unhappy? It is your own fault for ruining your life by doing nothing but blaming others.”

Cold killing intent poured out of Yang Cheogi, but I said everything I wanted to say.

“Do you really think you failed to become the Alliance Leader of the Evil Path because a Demon Venerable emerged from the same sect? I think that is an excuse. If you had shown the qualities and ability befitting that position, you surely would have become the Alliance Leader of the Evil Path. Is that not merely an excuse you fabricated and attached to yourself?”

Yang Cheogi could not refute me.

“With such a petty heart, even if you had become the Alliance Leader of the Evil Path, the alliance would have fallen into chaos because of you. In the end, you would have been assassinated by someone within the alliance. Instead of resenting Lord Soma, you should be grateful to him. Repeat after me. Thank you for saving my life. Soma, thank you!”

Yang Cheogi’s gaze sank coldly. From his eyes, I could feel his determination to kill me without fail. It seemed something I had said had struck a nerve.

Staring straight into those eyes that were struggling to hide his shame, I said,

“The reason you are trying to kill Lord Soma—it is not only because of that, is it?”

At my words, Yang Cheogi flinched. Geugak Soma, who was standing beside me, asked,

“What do you mean?”

“A petty great man like that always puts his own interests first. He would not risk his life for revenge. Who did you join hands with? What were you promised in return? Did they say they would grant even your final dream?”

Yang Cheogi could not deny my words.

“For a young brat, your tongue is quite sharp. The Demonic Cult has a bright future ahead of it!”

His shame turned into anger and turned toward Geugak Soma.

“They say you went to the Demonic Cult and became Geugak Soma. Let us see just how extremely evil you truly are.”

When Yang Cheogi snapped his fingers as a signal, the people who had been hiding began to reveal themselves one by one. Numbering in the dozens, they were all wearing masks, and all of them were disciples of the White Mask Sect.

“Now then, go ahead and kill all your fellow disciples of the sect.”

I was startled. I had not imagined that he would act like this.

Rather than having brought them here to kill us, he had brought them here to torment Geugak Soma. Since they were wearing masks, I could not tell their ages, but judging by their bearing, there were many young experts among them.

What choice would Geugak Soma make?

If they charged at us to kill us, I too would have no choice but to act. They were a sect of the Evil Path, but they were Geugak Soma’s own sect. Could I kill them freely? And how would Geugak Soma respond?

I glared at Yang Cheogi. The absolute evil I spoke of meant precisely people like him. Not someone forced into an unavoidable choice, but someone with the malicious intent to deliberately break another person’s heart, someone who used the disciples of his sect as sacrifices without the slightest pang of conscience. You will surely die by my hand.

Just then, Geugak Soma spoke in a ringing voice.

“My senior martial brothers, junior martial brothers, senior martial sisters, junior martial sisters, and martial nephews of the sect, listen. Though I left the main sect and entrusted myself to the Divine Cult, I am the chief disciple of the late former sect master. In life, the sect master did not expel me, so as a legitimate disciple of the White Mask Sect, I command you. All of you, withdraw!”

The disciples of the White Mask Sect looked at one another and exchanged glances.

Yang Cheogi shouted,

“What are you doing? Kill them at once!”

Geugak Soma shouted even louder.

“Yang Cheogi, the great criminal who moved the disciples of his sect as he pleased for his own personal desire, will die here today. So withdraw!”

“I said kill them!”

At that moment, someone released a finger wind.

Piiing.

Geugak Soma twisted his body and avoided the finger wind. It had not been fired to kill him, but as a warning.

“Senior Brother!”

Geugak Soma recognized at a glance that the man who had fired the finger wind was his senior martial brother.

“Did you recognize me?”

“How could I not?”

In the gazes that met in midair were memories and longing from their childhood.

“Senior Brother, take the disciples and return now.”

“I cannot defy Martial Uncle’s command.”

“Martial Uncle has already abandoned the sect. Can you not see that he joined hands with the Valley of Villains and is trying to use you and the disciples like shield bearers?”

Because they had heard everything I had said earlier to Yang Cheogi, they knew what kind of situation this was.

“There are only two outcomes to this fight. Either you die fighting me, or you kill us and have the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult destroy the sect. Is that what you want, Senior Brother? To drive everyone to their deaths?”

Yang Cheogi shouted for them to hurry up and kill us, but Geugak Soma and the man called his senior martial brother only stared at one another in silence.

“That old man who is trying to ruin the main sect by using his seniority as a weapon will die by my hand. So do not worry and go back. Do you not know what kind of person I am?”

“I do. Junior Brother was a man who always kept his word once he gave it.”

“Let us meet again next time, Senior Brother.”

Geugak Soma’s senior martial brother shouted to the disciples.

“We return!”

The disciples of the White Mask Sect leapt away, following him.

I was truly impressed by Geugak Soma’s calm response. It was the moment I saw a new side of him.

—To finish this without a single sacrifice. Since meeting you, Lord Soma, that was your most impressive moment.

—You think it was impressive that Geugak Soma did not see blood? That feels rather strange.

Perhaps he too was changing after meeting me.

“Those useless fools!”

As Yang Cheogi exploded in anger, Geugak Soma said as if mocking him,

“It seems I am more fortunate in senior martial brothers than our master was.”

“Shut up!”

At Yang Cheogi’s irritated reaction, I added a word of my own.

“They left because they hate you. They probably came here only because they were forced to obey your command. How much must they have hated you to listen to Lord Soma, who left the sect long ago? How could they not know that you were trying to drag the disciples to their deaths merely because you are an elder of the sect? You had best simply die here today.”

Yang Cheogi gave a sneer.

“I knew you killed the Sword Emperor. Did you think I would face you alone? I have kept you waiting long enough. Eccentric Demon, please come out now.”

Then a person appeared from inside the building across the road. He was an old man who had not aged gracefully. Malice filled the gaps between his wrinkled folds, and his eyes were full of nothing but killing intent.

At the name Eccentric Demon, I was startled.

He was a demonic practitioner who had learned demonic arts, but did not belong to our cult.

He was famous for having used demonic arts to commit massacres wherever he went, until in the end, our cult and the Murim Alliance cooperated to form an execution squad to kill him. After that, he had vanished, and today he had revealed himself.

I asked Yang Cheogi,

“Just who have you joined hands with? Who did you join hands with for even the Eccentric Demon to come crawling out?”

But before I could hear an answer, the Eccentric Demon stepped forward with a cold gaze.

“Crawling out? You little bastard has quite a foul way of speaking. Is that how your father taught you?”

He mentioned my parents in this situation? And my father, no less?

“It is because old men act so shamelessly like this that the old masters of murim have fewer and fewer places to stand. You came out to kill us, yet you expect courtesy too? Very well, from now on, I will give you all the courtesy in the world. It is an honor to meet you, Senior. Please, for the sake of our bond as fellow demonic practitioners, spare this insignificant junior! Now then, will you spare me?”

The Eccentric Demon could not answer, and the corner of one eye twitched.

“So please stop spouting nonsense about courtesy. Where did you suddenly crawl out from to start this bullshit?”

I deliberately provoked him strongly. They had come here to kill us anyway. In that case, the more I provoked him, the more advantageous it would be in the fight. The more it was a fight between experts, the more one lost if emotions interfered. The reason he had mentioned my father to make me flare up was probably along the same lines.

Yang Cheogi said to the Eccentric Demon,

“Break every bone in his body. Let us see if he can still act that arrogant then.”

“Annoying. Watch carefully in advance. In a little while, he will disappear without even leaving a shape behind.”

No matter how terrible their characters were, in terms of martial arts, both of them were close to the Sword Emperor Baek Manggi. If the two of us faced one person together, it might be one thing, but facing two of them was no easy matter.

It was right then.

Clank, clank.

The sound of chains came from somewhere.

When I turned, a man with a sturdy build appeared, a blood-red chain wrapped around his wrist and hanging down as he dragged it along the ground.

“Iron Chain Master!”

He was a villain called Iron Chain Master, a man who had once plunged murim into terror. Who knew how many experts had lost their lives with their bodies bursting apart under that red chain of his? His aura was also no less than that of the Eccentric Demon who had appeared earlier.

At his appearance, my heart sank. Facing the two before him would not have been easy to begin with. But now even Iron Chain Master had appeared, placing us in an absolutely disadvantageous situation.

I said to Geugak Soma with a sigh,

“This is all my fault.”

“What do you mean?”

“Elder Blood Heaven Saber Demon said it. That I would stir up storms and wake up all sorts of hidden masters. I really did not think I would end up meeting the Eccentric Demon and Iron Chain Master here.”

“This time, I think it was I, not the Second Young Master, who woke them.”

“Then please take the stronger one, Lord Soma.”

Seeing that I did not forget to joke even in this situation, the two eyes behind the mask smiled. It was the worst possible situation, but perhaps because I was with Geugak Soma, my heart felt strangely reassured.

“Ah, before that, I must ask this first.”

I looked at Yang Cheogi and said,

“Which side will you join to attack us together?”

Of course, I had thrown out the question with the intention of stopping him from joining in.

Yang Cheogi smiled leisurely.

“You do not have to prod my pride like that. I will not step forward. Now, we should look well at our Second Young Master’s face and body. Soon, he will become a bloody pulp and disappear.”

Since the Eccentric Demon intended to face me, Geugak Soma’s opponent naturally became Iron Chain Master.

I thought it had worked out well. Both the Eccentric Demon and Iron Chain Master were formidable foes, but I expected the Eccentric Demon to be slightly stronger.

I looked at Geugak Soma.

I did not ask if he was confident. That would have been an insult to Geugak Soma. Until the very end, I displayed my composure.

“Let us make a bet on who kills first.”

At my words, the Eccentric Demon laughed. Iron Chain Master laughed, and Yang Cheogi laughed as well.

“I do not know why those things are laughing when even Lord Soma is staying still.”

Only then did Geugak Soma laugh too, and lastly, I laughed as well.

When I drew my sword and let it hang, Geugak Soma lightly flicked the blade of the Black Demon Sword with his finger.

Tiiing!

Listening to the clear metallic sound that carried Geugak Soma’s wish for us to fight well, I walked forward toward the most difficult battle I had faced since my regression.

Chapter 148: If I Feel Like I’m Going to Die
--------------------------------------------
I controlled my mind.

With a powerful enemy before me, I couldn't afford to worry about Geukak Soma. I had to focus solely on my own fight. I would trust Geukak Soma. Instead, I had to keep my eye on Yangcheogi.

Because he was someone who could jump in and launch a surprise attack at any moment.

Goema donned gloves fitted with claws split like fingers on both hands. It was his exclusive weapon, the Heavenfall Flying Claws. A chilling azure gleam ran along the blades.

"Shall we see if your blade can keep up with that sharp tongue?"

The moment Goema finished speaking, the fight began.

Kaack! Kak! Kaaaack!

With bizarre whistling sounds, the Heavenfall Flying Claws tore through the air. It was an attack that would rip away flesh and bone with just a graze.

I advanced with Shadow Step footwork and launched my attack.

Clang! Clang! Chaeng! Clang!

My sword and the Heavenfall Flying Claws clashed countless times. I struggled to widen the distance, while he desperately tried to close in. After all, since the Heavenfall Flying Claws were shorter than my sword, the closer he got, the more advantageous it was for him.

Amidst the bone-chilling exchange of offense and defense, I met that moment.

Just as when I had fought Baekmanggi, I saw only Goema.

The surroundings fell silent, and I heard only our movements and sounds. I felt the illusion that even time was flowing slowly. But time never flowed slowly. My focus had merely heightened to such a degree.

Right then, a short scream tore through the silence!

"Keuk!"

The instant I realized the one who had screamed was Geukak Soma.

Flash.

Instinctively unleashing my Vanishing Step, I was already behind Cheolsweja, slashing with my sword.

Swhiiik.

The iron chain that had been aimed at the staggering, retreating Geukak Soma turned toward me.

Swhiiiik!

Taeng!

The moment my sword struck Cheolsweja's iron chain and deflected away, I sensed a rushing force from behind and twisted my body to dodge.

Goema's Heavenfall Flying Claws raked through the space where I had just been.

Geukak Soma, having narrowly escaped danger thanks to me, launched his Hyeorangji at Goema, who was pressing a second attack on me.

Swhiiiing.

Goema likewise twisted his body to evade the Hyeorangji.

Seizing that opening, I leaped away together with Geukak Soma, putting distance between us and the two men. If I hadn't intervened, Geukak Soma would have been injured. Cheolsweja's martial arts were stronger than I had thought.

As the battle briefly settled into a lull, Yangcheogi burst out laughing. He was confident of victory. Moreover, Goema and Cheolsweja also seemed brimming with composure.

Regulating my breath, I asked Yangcheogi.

"Are you the Cheonmyeong Hoeju?"

While he was caught up in triumphant arrogance, I tried my utmost to extract a secret.

"I am not the Cheonmyeong Hoeju."

"Then are you the Akingokju?"

"I am not the Akingokju either."

"Those two must be among the top experts in the Valley of Villains; just how were you able to make them come out?"

Yangcheogi didn't answer that. I quickly inferred.

"Then the Cheonmyeong Hoeju must have joined hands with the Akingokju as well, just as he recruited you."

A hint of admiration flickered in Yangcheogi's eyes. From that reaction, I knew my guess was correct. It was a secret not even the Tongcheongak had uncovered yet.

I had known the Cheonmyeong Hoe was no ordinary organization, but it was far greater than I had realized.

In the past, failing a mission simply meant the end. Since the restriction on Geukak Soma hadn't been released, such a situation couldn't have occurred in the first place.

But one thing was certain. Judging by how they were orchestrating matters now, the Cheonmyeong Hoe must have been active in the shadows even then.

Before fighting again, I looked at Geukak Soma. He silently nodded. His eyes told me that he was fine and not to worry.

The fight started again.

The Heavenfall Flying Claws ripped space apart, and Cheolsweja's iron chain reared its head like a living serpent and lunged.

The Heavenfall Flying Claws grazed Geukak Soma's arm and side, while the Hyeorangji grazed Cheolsweja's forehead.

Sword qi flew at close range, finger winds were unleashed, an iron chain laden with gang qi soared, and the Heavenfall Flying Claws carved dozens of claw marks. All manner of secret techniques were unleashed in rapid succession.

The battle was neck and neck. Geukak Soma and I had fought together only once before, yet we moved in perfect harmony, as if we had spent our entire lives mastering combined attack techniques. Strangely, when I fought alongside Geukak Soma, my mind was at ease, and our teamwork flowed naturally.

Then, at one moment, Geukak Soma's second scream burst out.

He crashed down onto his rear, and the iron chain flew at him.

I rushed in and blocked the chain with my back.

Tung!

I withstood it with protective gang qi, but the impact felt as though my shoulder was being torn from its socket.

Teong! Tung!

The chain fell in rapid succession.

Though the chain battered my body, the protective gang qi surrounding me deflected it. It was consuming an enormous amount of internal energy.

Yangcheogi, who had been watching, exclaimed in admiration with a surprised expression.

"To raise protective gang qi at that age—you truly are something else."

But this situation was exactly what we had intended. I had sent a telepathic message to Geukak Soma moments ago, telling him to pretend to be struck once more.

While turned away from them, my sword was splitting apart before me.

It was the execution of the Flying Sky Sword Technique, Seventh Form, Floating Sky Form.

Swish-rip!

I didn't split it into many. Only two, right before my chest where they couldn't see.

The moment the sword qi blades were completed!

Swhiiing! Swish!

The two blades of sword qi shot toward the men behind me as if alive.

Fwaak!

One pierced through the shoulder of the off-guard Goema, and the other sliced the iron chain in half as it flew past.

"Damn it!"
