Goema scowled, pressing on the blood point in his shoulder. If he hadn't reflexively twisted his body to dodge, his throat would have been pierced.

Cheolsweja discarded the severed chain and gripped the remaining portion short.

"Shit!"

We too let out a sigh. Our sure-kill strike had failed to kill them. Fortunately, at least we had pierced Goema's shoulder and damaged Cheolsweja's weapon.

I reached out my hand to Geukak Soma, who had fallen. He took it and stood up. Geukak Soma's eyes burned even hotter. Both he and I were blazing in this fight.

Goema and Cheolsweja chose a war of attrition. They intended to exhaust our internal energy and kill us, judging that they held the advantage. They also intended to fight carefully since Goema was injured and Cheolsweja's weapon was damaged.

As such, Geukak Soma's internal energy, who used finger wind and palm force, was inevitably the first to deplete.

At that critical moment when his internal energy ran out and his movements grew drastically sluggish.

Sssut.

Geukak Soma was looking at me with a surprised face.

We were now inside the world of the Spacetime Transference Technique.

"We are in the space of the Spacetime Transference Technique."

"The Spacetime Transference Technique, even!"

"There is no time to explain. This is a Swift Recovery Pill—take it and recover your internal energy first."

I gave him the Swift Recovery Pill I had received from Hyeolcheondoma.

"You should take it, Igongja."

"I still have internal energy left. There is no time. Hurry and take it to recover. Even in this moment, my internal power is being consumed."

Geukak Soma immediately took the Swift Recovery Pill and circulated his true qi.

Their voices could be heard from outside.

"It's an illusion technique."

"For a brat to use even illusion techniques? He truly continues to surprise."

"If it's such a supreme illusion technique, the internal power consumption will be severe, so he won't be able to maintain it for long and will reveal himself. In short, it's an act of suicide."

If I hadn't reversed and absorbed Geungnak Yohui's internal energy, I wouldn't have been able to unfold the Spacetime Transference Technique at this moment.

Shortly after, Geukak Soma, having rapidly recovered his internal energy with the Swift Recovery Pill, opened his eyes and rose from his spot.

"We must finish this within a hundred moves."

"Yes! Let's go!"

I thrust my sword forward, and Geukak Soma tapped it once more.

Tiiing!

With a clear metallic sound, the Spacetime Transference Technique unraveled.

The moment we burst out, I rushed at Cheolsweja like a madman.

Clang-clang-clang-clang-clang-clang-clang!

I unleashed extreme focus.

The surroundings disappeared, and only Cheolsweja and his iron chain were visible to my eyes.

And soon, even Cheolsweja disappeared. Only the iron chain moved brilliantly.

I couldn't hear any sound. I heard only the clash of my sword and the iron chain.

If I'm struggling, you must be struggling too. If I feel like I'm dying, you must feel like you're dying too. I don't believe it. That you worked harder than me. I believed in myself, and I believed in all my effort.

And I experienced something astonishing once more.

I was fighting at the extreme speed I was capable of, yet the speed of my sword began to increase further. It was the result of desperation. A miracle born from my desperation not to let Geukak Soma die.

I could feel the chain's movements growing frantic.

Driven into a corner, he unleashed a hidden secret technique. He trapped my sword between the chains. At the very moment it seemed like a coiled snake was wrapping around my blade, I lost the Black Demon Sword.

I saw Cheolsweja's gleeful expression.

Shick! Puoook!

The next moment, a dagger was embedded in Cheolsweja's throat.

He had unleashed his own trump card, but so had I. The instant my sword was snatched away and he let his guard down, I had thrown the dagger.

"Keuk."

The dagger embedded in Cheolsweja's throat was a ten-thousand-year cold iron dagger. Since I hadn't used dagger arts even once since the battle began, he couldn't block this sudden attack.

Cheolsweja, like someone who absolutely cannot endure an itch and must scratch it, yanked the dagger out of his throat.

The moment he pulled out the dagger, blood gushed like a waterfall, and he toppled over. Not even bothering to waste internal energy pulling it back through the air, I ran forward and grabbed the Black Demon Sword entangled in the chains.

And without hesitation, I rushed at Goema.

Geukak Soma was facing Goema, who was stronger than himself, with extreme focus.

We now fought as we had when facing Baekmanggi.

The two of us pressed him like madmen. I had been wary of Yangcheogi jumping in, but for some reason, he didn't intervene.

With the two of us pressing the attack, the battle instantly tilted in our favor. They were strong, but ultimately not as strong as Sword Emperor Baekmanggi. On the other hand, our combined assault was far more in sync than it had been then.

At the very moment Goema spat out words asking Yangcheogi for help.

As my blade passed between the slightly disordered blades of the Heavenfall Flying Claws.

Puoook!

My sword embedded itself in Goema's shoulder, and.

Piing! Peoek!

Simultaneously, the Hyeorangji launched by Geukak Soma pierced through Goema's forehead.

Goema fell backward, eyes wide with resentment and fury.

"Haa, haa,"

Geukak Soma and I gasped roughly for breath. Geukak Soma's internal energy was completely exhausted, and I too had very little left.

"You really are tenacious bastards!"

I spoke to him in response to Yangcheogi's exclamation.

"You are the more tenacious one. You watched without helping them?"

"If your internal energy is exhausted, I can kill you more safely and easily. Why would I interfere for no reason?"

I felt that wasn't the reason. The variable of being left alone to face the two of us was just as dangerous.

'He didn't stop those two experts from dying? Like someone who wanted them to die. Why?'

Then, a thought flashed like lightning.

"Could it be that what you were promised by the Cheonmyeong Hoeju was the position of Akingokju?"

"!"

Since he had desired to be the Demon Lord, the Sect Leader, and the Alliance Leader, I had wondered if perhaps he wanted the Valley Lord position.

From Yangcheogi's eyes and reaction, asking how I knew, I could tell my guess was certain. He had undoubtedly used this opportunity to cut off the limbs of the Akingokju. To take over the Valley of Villains more comfortably. The villains had gathered together only to stab each other in the back.

"You truly are a person who only dreams of things that can never be achieved in a lifetime."

"That mouth of yours will be your downfall."

"You are trash."

"Yes. Save your cursing for hell."

Geukak Soma stepped in front of me.

—Igongja, now is the time to display your running skills. I should be able to hold out for a few moves.

He thought we couldn't defeat Yangcheogi. Because his internal energy had already bottomed out. He had judged that it was impossible to face him with the little internal energy I had left.

—That running skill is for when I have a rematch with Soma-nim.

—Stop being stubborn and go!

—The demonic path I walk may not even shatter a table, but neither does it abandon a friend to danger.

—!

I felt Geukak Soma tremble at the word 'friend.'

—What kind of Geukak Soma is so sentimental?

Geukak Soma's gaze, which had been directed at me, turned toward Yangcheogi. And then, truly unexpected words flowed out.

"Let Igongja go, Sabaek."

He was making a request he absolutely hated in order to save me.

Then Yangcheogi spoke with a mocking gaze.

"If you remove your mask and kneel, I will spare him."

I saw Geukak Soma's conflicted back.

Ah! Even if the whole world pointed fingers at him, calling him evil, I alone could never do so.

I quickly went to him and grabbed his arm.

"He won't let us live even if you do."

Geukak Soma shook off my hand and spoke.

"I had no intention of kneeling. I know better than anyone what kind of person Sabaek is."

"Then why?"

"I was trying to buy a little more time. Igongja is clever, so I thought he might find some way out."

Geukak Soma smiled silently. A deep regret flashed in his pupils behind the mask. Regret that we didn't have a little more time. He was sensing the end.

Yangcheogi raised internal power in one hand. I too channeled power into the Black Demon Sword. How many moves could I truly endure? Could I kill him with this internal energy?

"Go together in good company!"

He unleashed a palm strike. It was the Demonic Lightblast Palm imbued with full internal energy.

At the exact moment I tried to block it by emitting sword qi.

Kwaang!

The incoming Demonic Lightblast Palm was neutralized and vanished.

I wasn't the one who blocked it. Something that had flown in from somewhere blocked Yangcheogi's attack.

It was a dao. A great blade brimming with gang qi was embedded before us like a shield.

Astoundingly, it was Hyeolcheondoma's Annihilating Heaven Greatblade.

When I raised my head, Hyeolcheondoma was riding the wind down from the sky and spoke.

"I knew it would come to this."

Chapter 149: The Trial Continues.
---------------------------------
“Elder!”

The moment I saw the Blood Heaven Blade Demon, I leapt for joy and shouted. Had I ever been so glad to see him since the day we met?

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon descended to the ground and pulled out the Heaven-Destroying Great Saber, which had been half-buried in the floor.

“A man old enough to know better—why are you bullying your juniors?”

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon sternly rebuked him.

In reply, a Hyeorang Finger shot toward him.

Boom!

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon thrust out a palm and dispersed the Hyeorang Finger. In terms of inner force, he was not pushed back in the slightest. In particular, the aura of the Blood Heaven Blade Demon, who had been training diligently in martial arts of late, was sharper than ever.

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon roared.

“You ugly old bastard! Do you want to die?”

The Heaven-Destroying Great Saber swung in a wide arc.

Fwoooooosh!

The saber wind unleashed from the blade tore through space as it swept forward.

Yang Cheogi did not dodge. He thrust out both palms and released his palm force.

Kwaaaaang!

With an explosion that seemed to split the ears, both men were pushed sliding backward. The number of steps they retreated was exactly the same. The Blood Heaven Blade Demon’s cultivation was truly profound; he was not inferior to Yang Cheogi at all.

I immediately joined the fight as well. If the Blood Heaven Blade Demon fought alone and happened to get hurt, that would be disastrous.

Chwararararak.

When I unleashed the Seventh Form of the Flying Heaven Sword Art, the Flowing Heaven Form, the Black Demon Sword split apart and rose into the air as twelve strands of sword qi. Though I had little inner force left, I deliberately flaunted what I had and divided it as much as possible.

Seeing that even I was about to join the battle, Yang Cheogi judged the situation unfavorable, kicked off the ground in an instant, and vanished from the place.

“Please don’t chase him.”

I worried that the Blood Heaven Blade Demon might pursue him, but he did not move an inch.

“I wouldn’t go even if you told me to. When we exchanged that palm just now, I thought my arm was going to fall off. He’s as strong as he is ugly.”

“Compared to that bastard, Elder, you’re a peerless beauty!”

“Of course.”

“Elder!”

I ran over to throw my arms around him, but the Blood Heaven Blade Demon used his footwork and dodged.

“Disgusting.”

“How did you come here?”

“I happened to be passing by.”

As if that could be true. The Blood Heaven Blade Demon had been worried about me and had been keeping an eye on my movements.

“Thank you. If not for you, Elder, we would have died here.”

It was not mere empty courtesy. If he had not come, there was truly no telling how the fight would have ended.

The Extreme Evil Young Demon collapsed heavily to the ground. His mask was covered in blood, and his entire body was wounded and drenched in it.

“Elder, please stand guard for a moment.”

“Very well.”

I placed my hand on the Extreme Evil Young Demon’s back and injected a stream of inner force. Slowly, I tended to his meridians, bolstered his true qi, and treated his internal injuries. As the pure inner force soothed his body, the Extreme Evil Young Demon soon opened his eyes. Fortunately, his internal injuries were not very deep.

“What a relief. Your internal injuries aren’t that severe.”

The Extreme Evil Young Demon looked at me. I could not forget it—the look in his eyes earlier when he had sensed the end, the regret that had filled them.

We forget so easily. We forget those intense moments that could change me and my life. Once that moment passes, we fail to live as we did in that instant.

But I would not do that. Now, nothing else was needed in my relationship with him. As long as I did not forget the regret of that moment when we had sensed death, that would be enough.

Extreme Evil Young Demon, what about you? Do you feel the same as I do?

The Extreme Evil Young Demon simply smiled. For the first time, he smiled with all of his emotions laid bare. I smiled too.

That’s right. Drink with the Drunken Demon, laugh with the Young Demon. That was enough.

Next, the Extreme Evil Young Demon expressed his gratitude to the Blood Heaven Blade Demon. Not with words, but with a slight bow of his head.

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon likewise accepted the greeting with nothing more than a single nod.

Seizing the chance, I threw my arms around the Blood Heaven Blade Demon.

“Elder! I missed you.”

“I told you it’s disgusting. Why are you doing this?”

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon shook me off as I acted like a child and fled backward. But how could I not know? He was truly happy that he had saved me.

I retrieved the ten-thousand-year cold iron dagger that had fallen beside Iron Chain Master’s corpse and spoke honestly.

“This fight was quite hard.”

Watching me, the Blood Heaven Blade Demon said,

“Didn’t I tell you? You’re waking up the murim.”

“Should I go somewhere and learn a lullaby?”

“Then in the process of learning it, you’d wake up even those who didn’t need to.”

At the Blood Heaven Blade Demon’s joke, I spoke with confidence.

“Since it’s come to this anyway, tell them all to wake up. Tell them not to wake up late later and talk in their sleep—tell them all to wake up. Instead…”

After meeting both their eyes, I said,

“From now on, please fight alongside me.”

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon stared at me for a moment, then abruptly turned and walked away. The Extreme Evil Young Demon followed behind him.

“There you go again. There you go again.”

I walked after them and left the place.

* * *

Sima Myeong entered the Heavenly Demon Hall with an urgent message in hand. The fact that his steps were quicker than usual told of the importance of the contents he had brought.

Even seeing that urgency, the Heavenly Demon, Geom Ujin, remained as calm as ever.

When Sima Myeong reached the end of the red carpet, he swiftly delivered the news.

“This time, during the mission, the Second Young Master came into conflict with the villains of Evil Valley.”

“Evil Valley?”

“The Ecstasy Enchantress, the Three Lords of the Blood Road, the Yama Divine Lord, and the Blood Wolf Saber.”

“Some fairly capable ones came out, then.”

“They were not the only ones. The Monster Demon and Iron Chain Master appeared as well.”

At the mention of those two names, Geom Ujin flinched in surprise. The first four were not easy opponents either, but the two mentioned afterward were certainly not people to be taken lightly.

“They clashed with the Second Young Master and the Extreme Evil Young Demon.”

“The Extreme Evil Young Demon was there as well?”

“Yes. It seems the Extreme Evil Young Demon is also involved in this matter.”

“And?”

After a brief pause, Sima Myeong reported,

“The Second Young Master and the Extreme Evil Young Demon killed them all.”

“The first four, perhaps, but the latter two could not have been easy.”

“They say the Extreme Evil Young Demon sustained internal injuries in the process. Fortunately, they are not deep.”

“And Mugeuk?”

“He is unharmed.”

“The two of them killed the Monster Demon and Iron Chain Master?”

Unlike the composed Geom Ujin, the Grand Strategist Sima Myeong was slightly flushed.

“To be honest, I was greatly surprised when I saw the message. Previously, the Second Young Master and the Extreme Evil Young Demon did join forces to kill the Sword Emperor, but at that time their opponent was only one man. This time, however, the number of enemies was great, so it could not have been easy.”

If he had not sparred with Geom Mugeuk at the Pungryu Tavern before, Geom Ujin would have been just as surprised. Geom Ujin had already been surprised in advance that day.

“Who is behind this matter?”

“The true mastermind is the leader of the Cheonmyeong Society, and those who took the lead in carrying it out were Yang Cheogi and the Master of Evil Valley.”

“Yang Cheogi? That Yang Cheogi of the White Mask Sect?”

Geom Ujin knew of him.

“Yes. It appears he did this because of his grudge with the Extreme Evil Young Demon.”

“That seems far too grand a lineup to mobilize for such a reason.”

“According to the Second Young Master’s report, the leader of the Cheonmyeong Society won over the Master of Evil Valley and Yang Cheogi. However, while recruiting Yang Cheogi, it seems he secretly offered him the position of Master of Evil Valley.”

“Why would he do that? From the perspective of the Cheonmyeong Society’s leader, the Master of Evil Valley would be more useful than Yang Cheogi in many ways.”

“There must be some hidden intention.”

“Look into it.”

“Yes! The Cheonmyeong Society is more troublesome than we had thought.”

Until now, they had regarded the Cheonmyeong Society as little more than a group that impersonated the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult to seek illicit profit. But now that they saw more clearly, it was an organization far larger and stronger than that.

“What will you do? It may be too much for the Second Young Master to handle. Shall we call him back now?”

But Geom Ujin shook his head.

“That mission is one the child chose himself, is it not? Then he must bear it. Instead, overlook the Demon Venerables’ involvement this time. And increase the cult’s support from one instance to three.”

“Understood.”

Originally, it was a mission he was supposed to carry out alone, so it should not have been permissible for the Demon Venerables to intervene like this.

But since a special situation beyond the scope of the trial had unfolded, they would adjust the difficulty accordingly.

“I will focus our pavilion’s information network on monitoring the Cheonmyeong Society.”

Geom Ujin nodded and said,

“Tell my second son that the trial continues.”

* * *

We stayed at one of the cult’s safe houses not far from the Yellow River Inn, where the fight had taken place.

For several days, we rested, recovered our inner force, and treated our wounds. We ate proper meals, circulated our qi, regulated our breathing, and rested fully.

And today, a message arrived from the Heavenly Demon Hall telling me to continue the mission.

As expected of Father, I thought. Even if I sent a message saying I was about to wage a battle against the Murim Alliance, he would be the kind of man to reply, “Fight well.”

That night, as I strolled through the garden of the safe house, enjoying the lonely moonlight, the Blood Heaven Blade Demon was training in martial arts in the yard off to one side.

“You train even here?”

“Do you think training is a game? Why would I care about the place?”

“These days, whenever I see you, Elder, I find myself thinking that becoming a Demon Venerable is no joke.”

“Don’t lie. You look down on us.”

“That’s a misunderstanding!”

To be honest, when I first resolved to win over the Demon Venerables, I had looked down on them. But as I grew closer to them, I had long since abandoned that thought. These people were absolutely not simple.

That was probably why Yang Cheogi had failed to become a Demon Venerable.

Separate from his martial skill, he lacked something decisive that would allow him to become a Demon Venerable. Should I say he had no charm? No presence? That he was not of that caliber? There was something missing in him.

“I suppose because I’ve only been seeing the Drunken Demon drinking every day, I’d forgotten how frightening Demon Venerables are.”

“You mustn’t look down on that drunkard either.”

“Why have you been worrying about me so much lately?”

“Do you look like someone I shouldn’t worry about? Every chance you get, you fall into danger like this. At this rate, I’ll have to empty my pocket money and buy you another protective robe. By the way, what about the Sokgong Pill?”

“I took it.”

I did not say that I had fed it to the Extreme Evil Young Demon. If I did, our old man might fume all night and be unable to sleep.

That was when it happened.

“I was the one who took it.”

I turned and saw the Extreme Evil Young Demon walking out into the garden.

“Thanks to it, I survived.”

Then he bowed his head lightly. At that respectful attitude, the Blood Heaven Blade Demon merely shot me a look and did not say anything to needles the Extreme Evil Young Demon for no reason.

“As long as you’re safe, that’s enough.”

How desperate must the situation have been for him to swallow that and keep fighting? How could the Blood Heaven Blade Demon not understand? Behind his back, he cursed the Extreme Evil Young Demon as a sinister bastard all the time, but he still acknowledged his skill.

“I’ll repay this debt next time.”

“Before that, you should wipe the blood off that mask.”

“I intend to wear it as is until this matter is over.”

I sensed that the Extreme Evil Young Demon was slightly excited. It was not merely anger. It was closer to the exhilaration one felt after surviving a life-and-death battle for the first time in a long while.

I felt the same. In the final fight, I had experienced my sword moving faster than before. It had come from my desperation to save the Extreme Evil Young Demon, but in any case, I had surpassed the limit I could reach. That was what mattered. The fact that I had broken through a limit. Since the Flying Heaven Sword Art had already reached the great completion of the twelfth star, this improvement must be related to the overall realm of my martial learning.

“I brushed against death more than once, but honestly, it felt incredible.”

At my words, the eyes behind the Extreme Evil Young Demon’s mask showed agreement.

Even the Blood Heaven Blade Demon nodded.

“It’s that taste that drives everyone mad.”

It seemed the Blood Heaven Blade Demon’s blood had also begun to boil when he exchanged that palm strike with Yang Cheogi.

“First, we have to catch Yang Cheogi.”

Yang Cheogi had not only tried to kill us; he had also attempted to drive the disciples of the White Mask Sect, the Extreme Evil Young Demon’s own sect, to their deaths. Since he would continue targeting the Extreme Evil Young Demon and me in the future, he had to be dealt with first.

“Moreover, he must have met the leader of the Cheonmyeong Society in person, so he will also be useful in finding him.”

At that, the Blood Heaven Blade Demon asked,

“Do you know where that Yang Cheogi fellow is?”

“He’ll be hiding in Evil Valley. The people he brought out from there are dead, so he’ll have to report it, and it’s also the safest place for him. Besides, Yang Cheogi is aiming for the position of Master of Evil Valley, so he must have returned there.”

“I’ll help you.”

When the Blood Heaven Blade Demon said he would help, the Extreme Evil Young Demon unexpectedly spoke up.

“I know Evil Valley well.”

After a brief pause, he added,

“I was there once.”

I was startled and asked,

“When were you there?”

“When I was young. My master sent me there, saying I should cultivate a wicked heart. He came to retrieve me three years later.”

“What if you had died there?”

“Then he would have found another disciple.”

“You survived in Evil Valley at such a young age?”

The Extreme Evil Young Demon nodded.

“Evil Valley is ultimately a place where people live. If there are experts like the Monster Demon, there are also people who know no martial arts at all. There are even people who are not from Evil Valley but bring goods in bulk and do business there, because the profit margin is high. Still, you must not let your guard down. If you enter without any information, there is no place as terrifying as Evil Valley.”

I relayed to the two of them the contents of the message that had come from the cult.

“The cult contacted me today. The mission to eliminate the leader of the Cheonmyeong Society remains in effect, and instead, support has been strengthened so that I may receive aid from the cult three times. I think we should use one of those chances now. The three of us could handle it, but in the process, the Master of Evil Valley may step forward. There is no need to take unnecessary risks. In any case, it only means using one opportunity.”

With the spirit of wiping out Evil Valley entirely if need be, I spoke powerfully.

“Let’s call the One Flower Sword Venerable and the Great Drunken Demon too, and go catch Yang Cheogi.”

Chapter 150: The Good Must Die.
-------------------------------
The Master of Villains’ Valley, Ji Fangyin, was so furious he could hardly bear it.

“You expect me to believe that?”

“What’s there to believe or not believe? It’s simply what happened.”

“The two great masters of Villains’ Valley do not simply die at the same time.”

Short in stature, with gloomy eyes, Ji Fangyin had always possessed the sort of presence that dampened a person’s spirits. Now that his temper had turned, the air around him grew so stifling it was hard to breathe.

“Can you defeat the Strange Demon? Are you confident you can defeat the Iron Chain Master? I’m not. And neither are you, are you? So how am I supposed to believe that those two were killed by the Extreme Evil Young Demon? In the same place, no less! Together!”

“It wasn’t because of the Extreme Evil Young Demon. It was because of the Second Young Master of the Demon Cult.”

“You call that an explanation?”

It was only natural that voices were rising. The Master of Villains’ Valley knew nothing of Geom Mugeuk’s strength. Only those in the murim who dealt in the most secretive information knew that Geom Mugeuk’s martial prowess was far greater than expected; his true abilities had yet to become known to the world.

“And was it only them? Did we not send the Paradise Enchantress, the Three Lords of the Bloody Road, the Blood Wolf Blade, and the Yama Divine Lord as well? Yet you say they were all annihilated, and you alone came back alive!”

“I barely made it back alive! I nearly died, I tell you.”

“Do you know something? Your words are so absurd that they almost sound like the truth. Surely you wouldn’t be stupid enough to offer such a ridiculous excuse.”

The Master of Villains’ Valley simply could not believe this situation. Through this affair, he had lost the two strongest limbs at his disposal. He had sent them all out precisely so that no one would die.

“When they died, what were you doing, Great Hero Yang?”

“When I was about to step in, the Blood-Heaven Blade Demon appeared. That old man’s internal energy was so profound that there was nothing I could do.”

Yang Cheogi thoroughly concealed the fact that he had only stepped in after the Strange Demon and the Iron Chain Master had died. The Valley Master would suspect him and investigate every which way, but in the end he would have no choice but to believe him. After all, it was an undeniable fact that on that day, only Geom Mugeuk, the Extreme Evil Young Demon, and the Blood-Heaven Blade Demon had been there.

“I, too, have ties with the Lord of the Heavenly Mandate Society. Why would I ruin this? Please believe me.”

The Master of Villains’ Valley regretted it. Had he known things would turn out this way, he would never have joined hands with the Lord of the Heavenly Mandate Society. Only now did it occur to him that the sweet thing the man had offered might in fact have been poison. Greed was always the root of disaster.

What was past was past. The present problem was this: with their deaths, his own position as Valley Master had become precarious.

Of all the organizations in the murim, Villains’ Valley was the one whose leader changed the quickest. It was filled with people who would bare their fangs the moment they sensed weakness in their head.

‘If you’ve been scheming for my seat, you won’t die a clean death.’

Ordinarily, even if Ji Fangyin harbored such thoughts, he would not reveal them on the outside. He was the sort of man who always smiled at enemies he intended to kill.

But today, he could not suppress his anger.

“I don’t want to see your face. Withdraw for now. We’ll speak again later.”

“Very well.”

Yang Cheogi was about to turn away when the Master of Villains’ Valley spoke.

“Do you know what my byname was before I entered this place?”

“What was it?”

“Hundred Evils. They called me that because I possessed every evil in the world.”

“Is that so?”

That indifferent response only angered the Master of Villains’ Valley further. Whether he knew that or not, Yang Cheogi threw out a new concern.

“They’ll guess that I’m here.”

“What are you trying to say? That they may even come charging all the way into this valley?”

“It’s not impossible.”

In this way, Yang Cheogi blocked off the possibility that the Master of Villains’ Valley might try to kill him.

“Since you came back after cutting off both our arms, we’ll have to fight with only our feet. If what you say is true, then we’re all dead now.”

Of course, he did not mean it sincerely. If Villains’ Valley would collapse simply because those two were gone, then it would have disappeared long ago. This was a place teeming with countless villains driven to the end of the road.

No one in the jianghu would immerse themselves in this filthy water. Just as it had always been.

After Yang Cheogi left, the Master of Villains’ Valley summoned a subordinate and issued an order.

“Investigate this matter thoroughly. And from this moment on, kill every person who enters the valley. Let everyone know! We’ll pay fifty thousand taels for each intruder’s head.”

* * *

After sending a letter to the main cult requesting the Drunken Demon and the One-Flower Sword Venerable, we headed straight for Villains’ Valley.

Lightness skill was faster than riding horses, so we ran using lightness skill.

A journey running alongside the Blood-Heaven Blade Demon and the Extreme Evil Young Demon? It was something I had never imagined, not before my regression, and not even after it.

I had traveled with the Extreme Evil Young Demon before, but this was my first time with the Blood-Heaven Blade Demon. On this journey, I learned something new about him.

The Blood-Heaven Blade Demon, whom I had thought would only like shutting himself in a room and reading books, had unexpectedly been to almost every corner of the Central Plains. Whenever we came upon an area he knew, he would stop mid-run and tell us about it.

“If you go over that mountain, you’ll reach a large place called Joyapyeong. There, I exchanged five hundred moves with a wandering swordsman who challenged me. That was when I was in the prime of my youth.”

Stories of battles he had fought, like this.

“In that village over there, there is a sect made up solely of women. Its sect master and her chief disciple both fell in love with me.”

He also spoke such absurd, lie-like stories with perfect nonchalance. At that moment, the Extreme Evil Young Demon and I sent voice transmissions to each other at the exact same time: He’s lying.

“From beyond that side lies the sphere of influence of the Evil Alliance. If you travel eight hundred li in that direction, you’ll reach the territory of the Murim Alliance.”

He was also well-versed in geography and the state of the murim.

There were many moments when I wanted to chime in because his words overlapped with what I knew, but I forced myself to hold back.

“By the way, was your internal energy always this profound?”

Seeing that I was not falling behind at all while running with them, the Blood-Heaven Blade Demon looked puzzled.

“I once took a spiritual medicine gifted to me by a very noble person.”

That spiritual medicine was none other than the Heaven-Outside Divine Pill the Blood-Heaven Blade Demon had given me before.

“That alone shouldn’t allow you to display this level of internal energy.”

“This time, while facing the Paradise Enchantress, I absorbed that temptress’s internal energy.”

Of course, there had been several other fortuitous encounters besides that, but I did not explain each one.

“Ah, truly, the heavens are helping you.”

“Then the two of you should help me a little too.”

At that, the Blood-Heaven Blade Demon and the Extreme Evil Young Demon once again shot forward.

To think they disliked each other, yet were able to coordinate like that with the sole intention of teasing me. Perhaps the two of them did not actually dislike each other that much.

As I ran after them, I shouted.

“Where do you think you’re running off to, when you’re both slower than me?”

* * *

We ran and ran like that until we arrived at the entrance to Villains’ Valley.

In the distance, I could see the One-Flower Sword Venerable and the Drunken Demon already there waiting for us. I had only ever seen them drunk and swimming about, or tending flowers in a garden, so seeing them outside felt different.

The Drunken Demon, arms crossed as he looked up at the sky, somehow seemed both lonely and handsome. The Sword Venerable, standing there in clean white robes with a sword at her waist, looked proud and elegant. Both of them felt extraordinary.

“Hyungnim!”

When I called him hyungnim even in the presence of the Demon Venerables, the Drunken Demon’s expression brightened radiantly.

“My dear younger brother!”

I focused on my relationship with the Drunken Demon without being conscious of any other ties.

“Hyungnim, thank you for coming.”

To feel awkward or mind others because of my relationships with the other Demon Venerables would be disrespectful to the Drunken Demon—and to the other Demon Venerables as well.

I am someone who calls the Drunken Demon brother. I believe that only when I clearly show what kind of person I am can I properly form other relationships as well.

“Unless it is something extraordinary, my closed-door training is never disturbed, but Yeobin brought me the news. She said my younger brother wanted my help. Just how did you make Yeobin take your side?”

All I did was look after your health, hyungnim. So Yeobin is the one who is truly on your side.

I could not answer like that, so I smiled and replied.

“Once this matter is over, I’ll buy you a drink.”

I then immediately greeted the One-Flower Sword Venerable politely as well.

“Seeing you outside, your beauty shines all the more.”

“I feel this every time, but your gilding is the most pleasing to hear. I heard you fought the villains of Villains’ Valley this time. Are you injured anywhere?”

“No. The Blade Demon appeared at the decisive moment.”

The One-Flower Sword Venerable looked at the Blood-Heaven Blade Demon and said,

“He may have been waiting for it. The decisive moment, that is.”

At those words, the Blood-Heaven Blade Demon looked dumbfounded and said,

“That’s right. I hid and waited. I wondered, when will the decisive moment come? I should appear only when things become a bit more dangerous.”

“Still, I’m glad you arrived in time. Well done.”

For some reason, the One-Flower Sword Venerable concluded the conversation with kind words. The Blood-Heaven Blade Demon sent me a look as if to say, What’s suddenly gotten into that woman? But how would I know what was in her heart?

Perhaps, on the way here together, the Drunken Demon had said good things about the Blood-Heaven Blade Demon? If so, today might be the starting point of their reconciliation.

The One-Flower Sword Venerable asked me again.

“So? The one behind this hid himself here?”

“Yes. I confirmed it once more through Tongcheon Pavilion. He entered Villains’ Valley.”

“This place is rather troublesome.”

“Fortunately, the Young Demon knows Villains’ Valley well.”

“Is that so?”

The One-Flower Sword Venerable’s gaze turned toward the Extreme Evil Young Demon. The bloodstains on his mask spoke of how urgent the previous situation had been.

The Extreme Evil Young Demon bowed his head lightly, and the One-Flower Sword Venerable exchanged greetings with him as well. The Sword Venerable treated the Drunken Demon like a friend, but with the Extreme Evil Young Demon, they maintained mutual courtesy.

They seemed like they were as indifferent to each other as chickens and dogs, but in truth, the reason I had formed a connection with the Extreme Evil Young Demon was because of the One-Flower Sword Venerable. When Cheongseon was being established as the Soul-Capturing Demon Venerable, she had told me that the Extreme Evil Young Demon had cast an approving vote. That was why I had gone to find him.

“Our target is Yang Cheogi. The villains here have committed crimes that deserve death, but there is no need to deliberately carry out an unnecessary massacre. It should be enough to eliminate only those who stand in our way.”

At my words, everyone nodded with expressions of understanding.

“There is one more thing I must tell you, concerning Yang Cheogi. Though the goal is to capture him alive, his martial arts are so strong that if capturing him proves difficult, you may kill him. Capture if possible, but this absolutely does not mean you must capture him alive at the cost of taking risks. The safety of everyone here comes first, no matter what.”

At that, the Drunken Demon said to the One-Flower Sword Venerable,

“Just who among us is my younger brother so worried about?”

“His drunken hyungnim, I suppose?”

“Ah, is it me?”

Even though they were Demon Venerables, charging into Villains’ Valley was something that could be burdensome and unpleasant. Yet they did not show the slightest sign of it. They did not even make a joke of claiming credit. I believe that was because of their feelings for me. It was a gratitude I must not forget.

“Thank you for coming like this for my sake. To be able to carry out a mission with four Demon Venerables is truly an immense honor. I will never forget today. If there were a painter here, I would have them paint us as we are today… Ah, where are you going? Listen to the end! I’m coming too!”

I followed after the Demon Venerables, who had gone ahead first, and entered Villains’ Valley.

After walking a little way inside, we found a warning written in red letters.

—The good shall surely die.

At that, the Drunken Demon looked around at us and said,

“Fortunately, there’s no one among us who will die.”

At those words, everyone laughed together.

“I’m in danger.”

When I protested at the injustice, the Extreme Evil Young Demon said,

“By that phrase, the safest person here is the Second Young Master.”

The gazes directed at me all seemed to agree with those words. Only the Drunken Demon took my side.

“I believe in my younger brother’s innocence. My kindhearted younger brother.”

“That makes me sound even more like a villain, doesn’t it?”

The Blood-Heaven Blade Demon, who had been listening, could not hold back and threw in a remark.

“I’m sick to death of hearing that damned ‘younger brother, younger brother.’”

Worried that he might pointlessly start a war of nerves with the Drunken Demon, I quickly took up his words.

“Exactly. How is a man without any sworn brothers supposed to live with the sorrow of it?”

At that, the Drunken Demon laughed and took a sip of liquor. I had been watching him since the day we first met, and it was certain that he drank less than before.

I sent a voice transmission to the Drunken Demon.

—You’ve cut down on drinking a lot. Impressive, brother.

—Thanks for noticing.

We could hardly have had this sort of conversation in front of the other Demon Supremes.

How long had we walked like that?

We came across a man with a basket of medicinal herbs on his back. He was just about to bow to us and pass by when—

Whoosh! Thud!

His forehead was pierced by the Blood Ang Finger unleashed by the Extreme Evil Demon, and he toppled over on the spot.

“He’s a scout guarding the entrance. If anyone comes in, his role is to take a shortcut and report it. Besides him…”

Swish! Shaaaak!

Before he could even say that others were hiding up in the trees and behind the rocks, the One Blossom Sword Sovereign sent out sword qi, and the Blood Heaven Blade Demon sent out blade qi.

The man hiding in the tree fell dead, and the rock was split in two, the man behind it meeting the same fate. Moving with Demon Supremes meant words were unnecessary. One gesture was enough.

“It’s been a very long time since I came back, but this place hasn’t changed at all. Back then, too, three of them guarded this spot.”

At the Extreme Evil Demon’s words, I smiled and replied.

“Isn’t the Cult the same? It doesn’t seem as if much has changed from decades ago to now.”

Then the Drunken Demon took a sip of liquor and said,

“That’s why our little brother is trying to establish a new demonic path, isn’t it?”

Instead of answering, I smiled as I looked at the Demon Supremes. I was growing closer to them by the day, but I still did not know just how much they would understand of the demonic path I pursued.

When we went a little farther in, several thatched huts came into view, with rice paddies and fields in front of them. They grew fruit trees and raised cows and pigs as well. It was truly no different from the scenery of any ordinary village.

But beside the fields were terrifying traps, and the flower beds blooming with splendid flowers were teeming with venomous insects.

A swarm of poisonous bees flew at us from somewhere, but from the start, it was impossible for bees to sting Demon Supremes. With a single wave of the hand, they were swept away all at once.

If we had not had someone who knew this place as well as the Extreme Evil Demon did, the road here would have been far more perilous. There were paths where it seemed there could be none, and traps where the way seemed safe.

The Extreme Evil Demon guided us only along shortcuts that avoided the traps.

At last, a village appeared in the distance. It was the first village we had seen since entering the Valley of Villains.

“From here to the tree at the village entrance over there, there are quite a few dangerous traps scattered about. Let’s just leap over them.”

The four Demon Supremes and I launched ourselves into the air at once.

We crossed in a single flight a distance no ordinary master could ever traverse. It was graceful and splendid, a supreme level of lightness skill that no one else could imitate.

Tap! Tap! Tap! Tap! Tap!

We landed in a line at the same time.

The moment we touched the ground, sword qi, blade qi, finger winds, and palm force flew out in every direction. The enemies lying in ambush around us fell one after another without even getting the chance to attack. Though we had not decided our targets in advance, not a single attack overlapped. These were the Demon Supremes of the Cult.

“Now, let’s go in.”

To my left stood the Blood Heaven Blade Demon, and to my right the One Blossom Sword Sovereign. At the far ends on either side were the Extreme Evil Demon and the Drunken Demon.

With the strongest wings in the world spread beside me, I walked into the village of villains.
